### [1] [Following the Error Through a Compressed Network:An Angular Measure for FFN Weight Compression in ViT-L/16](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28067)
*Paolo D'Alberto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究 ViT-L/16 的 48 个 FFN 矩阵在 2–8 比特下四种量化方案的压缩表现，并提出残差流角误差与块输出谱角度量来解释量化方案的排序从何而来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察不同量化方案不仅要在最终精度上比较，还要理解误差如何在网络中逐块累积、以及为何单块最优方案在全网中未必最优，从而需要可移植、可解释的误差度量。

Method: 对 48 个 FFN 矩阵采用四种方案：逐 token 网格、固定高斯 Lloyd–Max 表、按矩阵拟合的表、随机符号 Hadamard 旋转加固定表；在 50,000 张图像上评估；以压缩与 float32 残差流之间的角误差逐块追踪，并引入块输出谱的角度量。

Result: 4 比特下四种方案 top-1 精度与 float32 相差 0.8 点以内，预测一致率为 94.4%–98.4%；旋转方案的角误差随块数近似按平方根增长（独立累积误差特征），其他方案在后期块被放大；谱角度量在方案分歧最大处能复现全网排序。

Conclusion: 残差流角误差可作为与最终分类排序一致的便携度量并揭示排序来源；谱角度量可缓解单块与全网排序不一致的问题。结论限于单模型、仅 FFN 权重、浮点评估压缩权重，不声称加速，但度量与样本生成方法可推广到其他模型与精度。

Abstract: We study the compression of the 48 feed-forward network (FFN) matrices of the Vision Transformer ViT-L/16 to 2--8 bits with four schemes: a per-token grid, a fixed Gaussian Lloyd--Max table, a table fitted to the matrix, and a random-sign Hadamard rotation followed by the fixed table. We evaluate on 50,000 images. At 4 bits the top-1 accuracy of the four schemes lies within 0.8 points of the float32 model, while the agreement with its predictions ranges from 98.4\% to 94.4\%. We use the angular error between the compressed and the float32 residual stream, followed through the blocks, as a portable measure that ranks the schemes as the final classification does and shows where the ranking is built: with rotation the angle grows close to the square root of the number of blocks, the signature of independently accumulating errors, while the other schemes are amplified in the later blocks. The same measure shows that a scheme that is better on one block in isolation can be worse in the whole network. To address this inconsistency we introduce an angular measure of the spectrum of the output of a block, which in isolation orders the schemes as the whole network does where they diverge most. We support these observations with real samples and with statistically generated ones. The scope is one model, the FFN weights only, and float evaluation of compressed weights; no speed-up is claimed. The error measures and the sample generation, however, can be applied to other models and precisions.

</details>


### [2] [Fairness Evaluation for Diffusion and Flow Models Should Account for the Generation Path: Implications for Clinical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28077)
*Dharini Raghavan,Amritpal Singh,Anant Madabhushi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文主张医学图像生成模型的公平性评估不应假定在单一采样过程下得到的结果可移植到其他采样过程，因为采样步数、调度与数值求解器的改变会差异化影响不同患者组的临床任务误差；作者给出数学例子并进行了探索性实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像生成模型的评估通常只使用单一采样过程产生的图像；但扩散与流模型在不改变训练参数的前提下可采用不同采样步数、采样调度或数值求解器，这些变化会改变生成图像及由其导出的临床测量，且可能对不同患者组产生不同的临床任务误差。因此"公平性结果在某一采样过程下成立"不应被默认为普遍成立。

Method: 基于医学图像质量、加速采样、生成动力学与生成模型公平性的已有工作，论证采样过程应被视为被评估系统的一部分；定义跨采样过程的子组临床任务误差差异；构建数学例子，说明在精确动力学与参考采样器下两组输出相同、但在替代采样器下误差不同，并将结论推广到共享噪声下的随机生成；使用年龄条件图像生成模型进行探索性实验，改变采样步数与采样时点安排。

Result: 数学例子表明，两组在精确动力学与参考采样器下可具有相同输出，但在替代采样器下呈现不同的临床任务误差；该结论在共享噪声的随机生成设定下依然成立。探索性实验显示，改变采样步数与采样时序会改变图像导出的测量值，但作者明确指出这些差异尚不足以证明临床不公平。

Conclusion: 公平性评估应针对实际部署中所用的采样过程进行，并在有助于解释患者组间临床相关误差差异时考察中间生成状态；不应将单一采样过程下的公平性结论直接外推。

Abstract: Medical image generation models are often evaluated using images produced by a single sampling procedure. However, diffusion and flow models can use different numbers of sampling steps, sampling schedules, or numerical solvers without changing their trained parameters. These changes may affect the generated images and the clinical measurements derived from them. We argue that fairness results obtained under one sampling procedure should not be assumed to hold under another, particularly when changes in sampling affect clinical task errors differently across patient groups. Building on previous work on medical image quality, accelerated sampling, generation dynamics, and generative model fairness, we examine why the sampling procedure should be considered part of the evaluated system. We define subgroup differences in clinical task error across sampling procedures and provide a mathematical example showing that two groups can have identical outputs under the exact dynamics and a reference sampler but different errors under an alternative sampler. We also extend this result to stochastic generation under shared noise. An exploratory experiment with an age-conditioned image generation model illustrates how changing the number and timing of sampling steps can alter image-derived measurements, although these differences do not establish clinical unfairness. We therefore argue that fairness evaluation should assess the sampling procedure used in practice and examine intermediate generation states when they help explain differences in clinically relevant errors across patient groups. Analysis code is available here https://github.com/rdharini2001/generation-path-fairness

</details>


### [3] [Adversarial Attacks Expose Path-Dependent Failures in Discrete Medical Image Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28078)
*Ahmed Loughzali,Akash Yallamati,Aadiv Reki,Aslan Shaukat Vaida,Jeyashree Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了离散图像tokenizer对不可感知对抗扰动的脆弱性，核心发现是"路径依赖"：同一tokenizer的输出在重建路径与token路径上呈现相反的失效模式——有监督攻击使重建路径AUC降至≤0.013但token路径几乎无损，无标签无监督攻击则反之（MedITok token路径AUC从0.919降至0.239），且可迁移至LLaVA-Med诱导错误临床结论。危险性取决于内部表示被移动的距离而非token改变数量，领域预训练不带来鲁棒性，六种防御均无法覆盖所有失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像正被离散tokenizer转换为token序列，供下游以"重建后分类""token直接分类""生成式多模态模型输入"三类路径使用。作者质疑：仅通过tokenizer这一环节，不可感知的输入扰动是否就能击穿这些系统；并指出现有鲁棒性评估未区分下游使用路径，可能掩盖真实风险。

Method: 在三种成像模态上评估四个tokenizer；设计两类攻击：有监督（标签驱动）攻击与无标签（unsupervised、label-free）攻击，并比较其对重建路径与token路径的影响；将最强的无标签攻击迁移至基于MedITok的冻结医学视觉语言模型LLaVA-Med（不访问问题、答案或语言模型梯度）；设置"改变近全部token但表示移动较小"的对照攻击以分离token改动量与表示位移的作用；与通用域对应模型UniTok对比；评估六种候选防御及针对防御的自适应攻击。

Result: 有监督攻击在ε=2/255时使四个tokenizer重建路径的ROC AUC均跌至≤0.013，而token路径基本不受影响；无标签攻击则相反，损伤集中于多码本（multi-codebook）tokenizer，MedITok最严重，token路径AUC由0.919降至0.239。该无标签攻击可零梯度迁移到LLaVA-Med，使其在健康视网膜上报告增殖性糖尿病视网膜病变、或将恶性病变判为良性。对照攻击改变几乎全部token但表示位移较小，所有路径与答案均保持完好。MedITok干净精度最佳却最脆弱，脆弱性高于通用域的UniTok。六种防御无一能修复全部失效模式，且部分在自适应攻击下失效。

Conclusion: 医学tokenizer的鲁棒性必须按下游路径分别评估，并针对自适应攻击者进行测试；领域特定预训练不等于鲁棒，token级扰动统计量不能作为风险代理指标，表示层位移才是关键。

Abstract: Discrete image tokenizers convert medical images into token sequences that downstream models either decode into an image for a classifier (the reconstruction path), classify directly (the token path), or feed to a generative multimodal model. We ask whether imperceptible input perturbations can break these systems through the tokenizer alone. We evaluate four tokenizers on three imaging modalities and extend the strongest attack to a deployed generative model. Our central finding is path-dependence: the same tokenizer fails in opposite ways depending on how its output is used. A supervised attack collapses diagnostic accuracy on the reconstruction path, with ROC AUC falling to $\leq 0.013$ at $ε=2/255$ on all four tokenizers, while sparing the token path. An unsupervised, label-free attack does the reverse; its damage concentrates in the multi-codebook tokenizers, worst in MedITok, whose token-path AUC falls from 0.919 to 0.239. The same label-free attack transfers to LLaVA-Med, a frozen medical vision-language model built on MedITok, making it report proliferative diabetic retinopathy in a healthy retina or call a malignant lesion benign, without access to the question, answer, or language-model gradients. Harm is driven by how far the attack moves the tokenizer's internal representation, not by how many tokens change: on the learned-codebook tokenizers, a control attack that changes nearly all tokens but moves the representation less leaves every path and answer intact. Domain-specific pretraining does not provide robustness: MedITok has the best clean accuracy yet is the most fragile, more so than UniTok, its general-domain counterpart. None of six candidate defenses repairs every failure mode, and several fail once the attacker optimizes against the defense directly. Medical tokenizer robustness must therefore be evaluated per downstream path, against an adaptive adversary.

</details>


### [4] [Vision-Language Truck Driving Advisories Grounded in Provincial Rules and Forecast Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28079)
*Xintong Jiang,Shucheng Huang,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Jincheng Xu,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出数据生成与蒸馏框架，结合结构化场景理解、风险预测与交通规则检索，生成卡车驾驶决策与自然语言提示并做一致性校验，用于将8B教师蒸馏为3B车载学生模型；构建加拿大四省行车记录仪数据集，学生在94.6%片段通过一致性校验，97.7%提示符合省级规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）生成的驾驶提示可能与道路状况或交通规则冲突，且通常仅在危险可见后才做出反应；这对长途卡车运输尤为关键——重卡需要较长制动距离，且单次行程可能跨越多个法规不同的司法辖区。

Method: 数据生成与蒸馏框架：整合结构化场景理解、风险预测与交通规则检索，生成卡车驾驶决策及自然语言提示，并对每条记录执行一致性校验；由8B教师模型生成记录，再蒸馏出3B参数学生模型用于车载部署；同时发布覆盖加拿大四省的行车记录仪数据集（11,142个车道标线标注帧、9,494个驾驶片段）。

Result: 在四省留出驾驶数据上，学生模型在94.6%的片段通过一致性校验（未调优的8B教师仅20.2%），并在风险出现前10–30秒提前提示26.2%的风险；生成提示中97.7%符合省级交通规则与指引。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为紧凑型驾驶提示模型生成符合规则的训练数据，并支持车载部署下的前瞻性风险提示。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate context-aware driving advisories, but their outputs may conflict with road conditions or traffic rules, and they typically address a hazard only once it is visible. This matters for long-haul trucking, where heavy trucks need long stopping distances and a single trip may cross several jurisdictions with different traffic regulations. This paper proposes a data generation and distillation framework that integrates structured scene understanding, risk forecasting, and traffic-rule retrieval to generate truck driving decisions and natural-language advisories, with a consistency check on every record. The records, written by an 8B teacher, are then used to distill a 3B-parameter student model for onboard deployment. We also introduce a dashcam dataset covering four Canadian provinces, comprising 11,142 annotated lane-marking frames and 9,494 driving segments. On held-out driving data across all four provinces, the student passes the consistency check in 94.6% of the segments, against 20.2% for the untuned 8B teacher, and announces 26.2% of the risks in the next 10 to 30 seconds before they appear. Of the generated advisories, 97.7% conform to the provincial traffic rules and guidance. These findings suggest that the framework can produce rule-conforming training data for compact driving advisory models.

</details>


### [5] [Multimodal Violence Understanding and Temporal Retrieval in Movies with Adaptive Audio-Visual Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28080)
*Jiaqian Li,Rohitash Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态视频理解框架，联合完成暴力严重程度分类、多标签暴力类型预测与长视频暴力事件时间定位，在XD-Violence上取得93.27%严重度准确率、88.60% micro-F1与0.5075平均tIoU，并揭示跨域性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视与流媒体中的暴力内容既涉及受众保护，又带来繁重的人工审核工作量；现有暴力检测方法多依赖单一模态或短视频片段，难以获取互补证据，也难以在长未剪辑视频中定位暴力事件。

Method: 使用预训练编码器提取时间对齐的视觉与音频特征，经基于注意力的自适应融合后，送入Transformer进行时序建模；框架同时支持三个互补任务：视频级暴力严重程度分类、多标签暴力类型预测、暴力事件的时序定位。

Result: 在XD-Violence基准上，多模态模型达到93.27%的严重程度分类准确率、88.60%的多标签暴力类型micro-F1，优于对应单模态配置；时序定位平均tIoU为0.5075。基于AIRTLab的跨域实验显示不同录制条件下性能下降；电影案例研究显示框架具备检索潜在暴力场景以供复核的能力。

Conclusion: 多模态自适应融合与Transformer时序建模能有效整合视觉与音频互补证据，在统一框架内实现分类、多标签预测与时序检索，可为影视及流媒体暴力内容的人工复核提供识别、分类与时间检索支持，但跨域泛化仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Violence in films and streaming media remains a concern for audience protection and creates substantial workload for manual content classification. Existing violence-detection methods often rely on limited modalities or short video clips, making it difficult to capture complementary evidence and localise violent events in long, untrimmed videos. In this study, we develop a framework for multimodal video understanding that classifies violence severity, predicts multiple violence types, and locates violent events in time. We combine temporally aligned visual and audio features extracted using pretrained encoders through attention-based adaptive fusion, followed by Transformer-based temporal modelling. Our framework supports three complementary tasks: video-level violence-severity classification, multi-label violence-type prediction, and temporal localisation of violent events. We evaluate the framework on the XD-Violence benchmark and examine its cross-domain performance using AIRTLab. On XD-Violence, our multimodal model achieves 93.27\% severity-classification accuracy and a micro-F1 score of 88.60\% for multi-label violence-type prediction, outperforming the corresponding unimodal configurations. We achieve a mean temporal intersection-over-union (tIoU) of 0.5075 for temporal localisation. Our cross-domain experiments reveal performance degradation under different recording conditions, while our movie case studies demonstrate the framework's potential to retrieve potentially violent scenes for further review. Our results highlight the practical value of multimodal intelligent systems in supporting human reviewers with the identification, classification, and temporal retrieval of violent content in films and streaming media.

</details>


### [6] [BehaviorICL: Task-Conditioned Decoder States for Visual In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28081)
*Chenyang Wang,Jiamo Hu,Chengrui Wu,Lingyue Chen,Jianguo Chen,Xun Xu,Xulei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BehaviorICL：用冻结VLM自身解码器状态（任务条件化的处理签名）来选择上下文示例，实现无需训练、兼顾图像与任务的示例检索，在两款VLM、八个数据集上优于现有免训练检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉上下文学习（VICL）的示例检索多基于图像相似度，忽略了示例有效性同时取决于查询图像与所问任务；因此作者探究冻结目标VLM在生成答案前的解码器内部状态能否作为选择示例的依据。

Method: BehaviorICL将每个图像-任务对表示为"任务条件化处理签名"，即所有解码器层在最后一个输入位置的隐藏状态。免训练变体BehaviorICL-Raw对对应层状态计算余弦相似度并取平均进行检索，将检索到的示例连同参考答案一并送入同一VLM。此外提出轻量共享投影BehaviorICL-Proj以进一步提升性能；并分析提取文本、解码器深度、示例库答案及注意力（任务文本如何使提取位置的注意力偏向被分类对象）。

Result: 对同一批NICO++图像分别询问对象与上下文时，Raw检索到的示例差异明显，且在两款VLM上均优于任务匹配下的图像检索器；在两款VLM、八个分类与VQA数据集上取得免训练检索器中的最高平均准确率，相比使用CLIP ViT-L/14编码器的RICES在Qwen上高3.83分、InternVL上高1.85分。

Conclusion: 目标VLM自身的内部状态可作为视觉上下文学习中实用且有效的示例选择依据；BehaviorICL在免训练条件下即取得领先表现，Proj版本还能带来额外增益。

Abstract: The usefulness of an in-context demonstration for a vision-language model (VLM) depends on both the query image and the task posed about it. We study whether the frozen target VLM's own decoder states, read before answer generation, can select such demonstrations. BehaviorICL represents each image and task by a task-conditioned processing signature, the hidden states at the final input position across all decoder layers. Its training-free variant, BehaviorICL-Raw, averages cosine similarities between corresponding layer states and passes the retrieved examples with their reference answers to the same VLM. When the same NICO++ images are asked about their object or their context, Raw retrieves largely different demonstrations for the two questions and outperforms an image retriever given task-matched demonstrations on both VLMs. Across two VLMs and eight classification and visual question answering datasets, Raw gives the highest average accuracy among training-free retrievers on both VLMs and exceeds RICES with a CLIP ViT-L/14 encoder by 3.83 points on Qwen and 1.85 on InternVL. A lightweight shared projection, BehaviorICL-Proj, brings further gains on classification. Analyses of extraction text, decoder depth and bank answers help explain these gains, and attention analysis describes how the task text shifts attention at the extraction position toward the classified object. These results support the target VLM's internal states as a practical basis for visual in-context learning.

</details>


### [7] [OSLA: Observation-Aware Local Alignment and Cross-Scene Relation Learning for Any-Scenario Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28083)
*Xinyang Zhang,Feiyu Han,Chenhao Wu,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Yingguang Yang,Feng Zhang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OSLA框架，通过局部身份对齐与跨场景关系学习，提升任意场景行人重识别的跨光谱、跨视角检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AS-ReID需用统一模型跨光谱模态与空地视角检索身份，但视角与光谱变化使局部对应不可靠，且常规采样可能遗漏训练所需的跨相机正样本关系。

Method: 基于监督CLIP-ReID构建观测感知框架，包含：局部身份对齐（LIA），利用历史身份观测与竞争身份信息筛选可靠的跨场景局部对应并允许未匹配区域，通过局部到全局蒸馏将正确分类的历史局部特征身份知识迁移至全局表示；跨场景关系学习（CSR），将观测单元采样与按光谱分组排序结合，双相机双光谱采样同时提供同谱与跨谱正样本，并在最终检索描述子上按画廊光谱分别约束正负得分。两模块仅在训练时生效，推理阶段保持原始视觉编码器与检索描述子不变。

Result: 在WHU-MARS-1000与WHU-MARS-2337上，AS-ReID协议下相较UAD分别取得5.0%与4.8%的Rank-1绝对提升。

Conclusion: OSLA在无需改动推理结构的前提下有效应对跨光谱与跨视角的局部对应不可靠和正样本采样不足问题，显著提升统一模型的行人重识别性能。

Abstract: Any-Scenario Person Re-Identification (AS-ReID) aims to retrieve identities across spectral modalities and aerial-ground viewpoints using a unified model. However, changes in viewpoint and spectrum can make local correspondences unreliable, while conventional sampling may omit cross-camera positive relations needed for training. To address these challenges, we propose OSLA, an observation-aware framework built on supervised CLIP-ReID that combines Local Identity Alignment (LIA) and Cross-Scene Relation Learning (CSR). Specifically, LIA uses historical identity observations and competing-identity information to select reliable cross-scene local correspondences while allowing unmatched regions. Local-to-global distillation transfers identity knowledge from correctly classified historical local features into the global representation. CSR couples observed-cell sampling with spectrum-grouped ranking to jointly organize and optimize cross-camera relations. Sampling across two cameras and two spectra provides same- and cross-spectrum positives, while ranking constrains positive-negative scores separately by gallery spectrum on the final retrieval descriptor. Both modules operate only during training, preserving the original visual encoder and retrieval descriptor at inference. Experiments on WHU-MARS-1000 and WHU-MARS-2337 demonstrate that OSLA achieves absolute Rank-1 gains of 5.0% and 4.8% over UAD, respectively, under the AS-ReID protocol. The code will be available.

</details>


### [8] [ReSUL: Learning Regional Sensor Utility for Robust Multisensor Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28084)
*Yiwen Wang,Jiahao Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出区域传感器效用学习（ReSUL），通过学习“保留/排除/修复”受损传感器证据对分割风险的增益来动态分配空间混合权重，在MFNet十一项条件下10项排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受损传感器观测并非无用：错位会把有信息量的场景结构放到错误位置。鲁棒多传感器分割需要判断何处保留该证据、何处排除、何处可通过重配准修复其价值，而现有方法缺乏对这种区域级取舍的可度量监督。

Method: ReSUL以分割风险为学习信号，比较三条路径：全传感器预测、传感器排除路径、以及针对位移热红外输入的重配准路径。路由器预测各路径相对固定参考的类别平衡Brier风险的带符号下降量，并据此分配空间混合权重；该监督将空间分配与可测量的任务收益关联，推理时无需故障标签。

Result: 在MFNet上对比十一项干净与扰动输入条件，ReSUL在ImageNet-1k与ImageNet-22k两组比较中均于10/11条件排名第一。严重热红外位移下mIoU达54.58%与55.63%，分别领先各组最强竞争方法7.19与5.63个百分点。固定backbone的MFNet消融显示效用监督使全部十一项条件均值优于仅任务路由。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过对区域预测收益的学习来决定如何使用受损观测，为鲁棒多传感器分割提供了以任务风险为导向的证据取舍框架。

Abstract: A corrupted sensor observation is not necessarily useless: misalignment can place informative scene structure at the wrong location. Robust multisensor segmentation must determine where to retain this evidence, where to exclude it, and where repair can recover its value. We introduce Regional Sensor Utility Learning (ReSUL), which learns these choices through their effect on segmentation risk. ReSUL compares full-sensor predictions, sensor-exclusion paths, and a re-registration path for displaced thermal inputs. A router predicts each path's signed reduction in category-balanced Brier risk relative to a fixed reference, then uses these estimates to allocate spatial mixture weights. This supervision connects spatial allocation to measurable task benefit without requiring fault labels at inference. MFNet experiments compare project-trained models under eleven clean and disturbed input conditions. ReSUL ranks first in 10 of 11 conditions in both ImageNet-1k and ImageNet-22k comparisons. Under severe thermal displacement, it reaches 54.58% and 55.63% mIoU, exceeding the strongest competing method in each group by 7.19 and 5.63 percentage points. Fixed-bank MFNet ablations show that utility supervision improves all eleven condition means over task-only routing. These results support learning how to use disturbed observations through their regional prediction benefit. Code is available at https://github.com/D-ST-Sword/ReSUL.

</details>


### [9] [UbiEnlight: User-Perception-Aware Real-Time Low-Light Video Enhancement on Mobile Devices and Smartglasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28085)
*Minfan Wang,Sicong Liu,Teng Li,Zimu Zhou,Sixun He,Bin Guo,Junzhao Du,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UbiEnlight：首个基于扩散Transformer的端侧实时、时序稳定的低光视频增强系统，借助频域先验分离光照与结构并结合感知自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机与可穿戴设备的低光视频拍摄普遍存在，动态光照叠加用户运动导致噪声、模糊与闪烁，尤其影响夜盲症用户和近眼显示体验。已有方法或采用逐帧复原后的事后时序约束，或直接优化整段视频，但低光与运动退化在时空上强耦合，事后一致性难以消除闪烁与运动伪影。

Method: 提出两项关键洞察并据此设计系统：（1）频域中光照与结构分离更稳健，故采用频谱引导的扩散Transformer，注入Fourier幅度/相位先验以校正光照并锚定结构，且无需光流；（2）用户感知容忍度随场景上下文变化，故设计感知感知控制器，在运行时自适应调整采样深度、注意力复用与帧缓存。系统在智能手机与智能眼镜上实现。

Result: 在动态/极端条件下将时序稳定性最高提升54.76%，同时在端侧保持实时性能；并与当前最优基线进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: 频域先验驱动的扩散Transformer与上下文感知的运行时控制相结合，可有效解耦低光视频的时空退化，在移动/可穿戴平台上兼顾实时性、时序一致性与感知质量。

Abstract: Low-light video capture is now common on smartphones and wearables, yet dynamic illumination coupled with mobile/werable user motion causes noise, blur, and flicker that make videos hard to perceive and use, especially for users with night blindness and near-eye displays. Prior work enhances frames with post-hoc temporal constraints or optimizes videos directly; however,low-light and motion degradations are spatiotemporally coupled, so post-hoc consistency after per-frame restoration often leaves flicker and motion artifacts. We present UbiEnlight, the first diffusion-transformer-based on-device system for real-time, temporally stable low-light video enhancement. UbiEnlight builds on two insights: (1) illumination and structure separate more robustly in the frequency domain, enabling a spectrum-guided diffusion transformer that injects Fourier amplitude/phase priors to correct illumination while anchoring structure without optical flow; and (2) user perceptual tolerance varies by context, motivating a perception-aware controller that adapts sampling depth, attention reuse, and frame caching at runtime. We implement UbiEnlight on smartphones and smartglasses, and evaluate it against state-of-the-art baselines. UbiEnlight improves temporal stability by up to 54.76% under dynamic/extreme conditions while sustaining real-time on-device performance.

</details>


### [10] [Efficient Memory-Constrained Video Object Segmentation via Subspace Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28087)
*Hai-Anh Le,Le-Trung Nguyen,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的插件 Memory Subspace Iteration (MSI)，利用流式记忆特征的时间子空间稳定性压缩记忆库，在保持分割精度的同时将记忆库占用最多降低 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于记忆的 VOS 架构需在流式 Memory Bank 中保存历史帧的稠密特征表示，随时间的存储带来显著内存开销与计算成本，因此需要在不损失精度的前提下压缩记忆特征。

Method: 观察到相邻帧的记忆表示存在大量时间冗余，其低秩子空间高度重叠且随时间保持稳定；据此提出 MSI，以子空间迭代方式压缩流式记忆特征，无需训练、与模型架构无关，可作为插件接入。

Result: 在标准 VOS 基准上的大量实验表明，MSI 在多种架构上保持分割精度，并将记忆库占用最多减少 25%。

Conclusion: 记忆特征的时间子空间稳定性可被有效利用于记忆压缩，MSI 提供了一种通用、低成本且精度友好的 VOS 记忆库优化方案。

Abstract: Memory-based architectures for video object segmentation (VOS) have achieved remarkable performance by maintaining dense feature representations from past frames in a streaming Memory Bank. However, storing these features over time incurs substantial memory overhead and computational cost. In this work, we identify significant temporal redundancy in memory representations, where consecutive frames exhibit highly overlapping low-rank subspaces that remain stable over time. Building on this observation, we propose Memory Subspace Iteration (MSI), a training-free, model-agnostic plugin that exploits temporal subspace stability to efficiently compress streaming memory features. Extensive experiments on standard VOS benchmarks demonstrate that MSI preserves segmentation accuracy across multiple architectures while reducing the memory bank footprint by up to 25%. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/Hai-Anh-LE/MSI_WACV2027.

</details>


### [11] [Shallow may be better: Physics-Constrained Lightweight Networks for High-Throughput Particle Image Velocimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28088)
*Claudio Mucignat,Kamila Zdybal,Ivan Lunati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过空间支撑（spatial support）的逐层分析，给出PIV可容许位移对应的网络深度上界，据此提出轻量金字塔位移估计器LIMA3（589K参数），在精度持平的条件下实现数量级更低的显存占用与更高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光流卷积网络默认"感受野越大越好"，采用越来越深的金字塔编码器；但PIV中示踪粒子帧间位移受采集几何约束而有界，过深结构带来的感受野远超实际需求，反而损害训练稳定性、粒子丢失下的空间分辨率与推理效率。

Method: 对空间支撑的逐层增长进行解析分析，推导其显式上界，并由物理可容许位移反推所需网络深度；据此设计轻量金字塔位移估计器LIMA3，含589,000个可训练参数。

Result: 在合成与实验基准上，LIMA3精度与既有学习型方法及经典方法相当，显存占用至少低一个数量级、吞吐显著更高；保留随示踪信息丢失而分辨率粗化的物理特性；显存线性扩展使其可在嵌入式显存预算内处理百万像素图像，RTX 5090上640×480可达900+图像对/秒，Jetson AGX Orin上达99图像对/秒。

Conclusion: 空间支撑分析为紧凑光流架构与有界窗口注意力提供了可迁移的设计原则，可作为"压缩超大通用网络"的替代路径，并支持与图像采集同步的高效在线运行。

Abstract: Convolutional networks for optical flow typically adopt progressively deeper pyramidal encoders under the assumption that larger receptive fields improve motion estimation. For particle image velocimetry (PIV), however, tracer-particle displacement between frames is bounded by the acquisition geometry, allowing network depth to be derived from the physically admissible displacement rather than inherited from general-purpose architectures. Using a layer-by-layer analysis of spatial-support growth, we derive its explicit upper bound and show that deeper architectures substantially exceed the support required for PIV, compromising training stability, spatial resolution under particle loss, and inference efficiency. Based on this analysis, we develop LIMA3, a lightweight pyramidal displacement estimator with 589,000 trainable parameters. Across synthetic and experimental benchmarks, LIMA3 matches the accuracy of established learned and classical methods with at least an order of magnitude less memory and substantially higher throughput, while retaining the physically expected coarsening of spatial resolution as tracer information is lost. Its linear memory scaling enables megapixel processing within embedded memory budgets, such that LIMA3 can reach over 900 image pairs per second at $640\times480$~pixel on an RTX 5090 and 99 image pairs per second on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. The spatial-support analysis provides a transferable design principle for compact optical-flow architectures and bounded-window attention, offering an alternative to compressing oversized general-purpose networks and enabling efficient operation alongside image acquisition.

</details>


### [12] [Every IVUS Frame in Its Place: A Zero-Install Browser Tool for Registering Intravascular Ultrasound to the Coronary Angiogram, and a Path to Angiography-Only Plaque Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28089)
*Prahlad G. Menon,Srilakshmi Adhyapak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全在浏览器内运行的半自动IVUS–造影配准工具，无需安装与上传，可将归档影像逐帧映射到血管并导出帧级训练记录，且与服务器端分析结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IVUS可显示斑块成分、钙化与支架贴壁信息，但将每一IVUS横断面定位到造影上的对应位置仍主要依赖术者主观判断；商用配准受厂商控制台与采集时配置限制，无法处理归档的造影与回撤数据，导致AI训练所需的配对数据稀缺。

Method: 开发纯浏览器端半自动配准工具：临床医生选取含造影剂的参考帧（辅以自动造影剂与心动周期分析），选择或描绘血管中心线（辅以Frangi血管增强与最小路径），标注回撤端点与可见标志点；用单调分段线性映射将每个IVUS帧置于血管上，逐例导出帧级训练记录。原始Python分析经WebAssembly原样运行，视频经WebCodecs逐帧精确解码。

Result: 在6条造影序列上，浏览器端分析与服务器流程逐比特一致；浏览器解码灰度差异不超过2个灰阶；推荐参考帧在全部序列中一致，默认自动中心线在4/6中一致。546帧回撤解码耗时5.3秒。在36个已知真值的体模中，医生引导描迹在87–99%的血管长度内位于管腔内，而全自动中心线随噪声升高、对比度下降由83%降至0%。在一例左主干配准中，单一分支标志点使回撤中段位置偏移达该段的17.5%。

Conclusion: 该工具实现了可及、可复现的IVUS–造影配准并生成AI训练数据；文中梳理了AI可介入的各环节，直至仅凭造影预测虚拟IVUS，并将工具免费在线开放并提供一键演示。

Abstract: Intravascular ultrasound (IVUS) shows plaque composition, calcium and stent apposition that the coronary angiogram cannot, but relating each IVUS cross-section to its position on the angiogram is still largely done in the operator's head. Commercial co-registration is tied to vendor consoles and to set-up at acquisition, so archived angiograms and pullbacks cannot be co-registered, and paired data for training AI models remain scarce. We present a semi-automatic registration tool that runs entirely in a web browser, with no installation and no upload. The clinician picks a contrast-filled reference frame (aided by automatic contrast and cardiac-phase analysis), selects or traces the vessel centerline (aided by Frangi vesselness and minimal paths), and pins the pullback ends and visible landmarks; a monotone piecewise-linear map then places every IVUS frame on the vessel, and each case exports as a frame-level training record. The original Python analysis runs unchanged in WebAssembly and videos are decoded frame-accurately with WebCodecs. On 6 angiographic runs the in-browser analysis was bit-identical to the server pipeline on identical frames; browser decoding differed by at most 2 grey levels, suggested reference frames agreed in all runs and the default automatic centerline in 4 of 6. A 546-frame pullback decoded in 5.3 s. On 36 phantoms with known ground truth, the clinician-guided trace stayed inside the lumen along 87-99% of the vessel, whereas the fully automatic centerline fell from 83% to 0% as noise rose and contrast fell. In a registered Left Main case, one side-branch landmark moved mid-pullback positions by 17.5% of the segment. We map where AI can enter each step, up to predicting a virtual IVUS from the angiogram alone, and make the tool freely available online with a one-click demo.

</details>


### [13] [Teacher-Guided Multimodal Dual Representation Reconstruction for Egocentric Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28090)
*Yuzhe Hao,Ikuro Sato,Rei Kawakami,Koichi Shinoda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出教师引导的多模态双重表征重建预训练框架MDR2，用冻结的视频/惯性单模态教师分别为多模态学生提供模态特定密集目标，在EvIs-Kitchen上以稀疏输入取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Ego-HAR预训练方法通常用单一联合目标监督融合表征，弱化了视频与惯性模态间的互补性，限制了模型从模态特有信号中学习的能力。

Method: 教师-学生式预训练框架MDR2：冻结的、含标签信息的视频教师与惯性教师分别输出密集的、模态特定的重建目标，多模态学生基于时间稀疏输入进行学习（双重表征重建）。

Result: EvIs-Kitchen数据集上，相对监督M-Baseline，K=4关键帧时名词/动词F1-macro分别提升9.20/6.17点；K=32时提升1.21/1.82点。统一骨干设置下各视频观测设定均值均更优，稀疏输入下增益更明显。

Conclusion: 保留模态特定监督信号的双重表征重建优于单一联合目标，在稀疏观测场景收益最大；结论限于EvIs-Kitchen及统一骨干设置，其他数据集与模态组合的泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Multimodal Egocentric Human Activity Recognition (Ego-HAR) leverages complementary cues from first-person vision (V) and inertial sensor data (S) to recognize daily activities, where video mainly captures appearance and object-related context while inertial signals capture motion cues that video may miss. However, existing pre-training methods typically supervise the fused representation with a single joint objective, which may underemphasize the complementarity between the two modalities and limit the model's ability to learn from distinct modality-oriented signals. To address this issue, we propose Multimodal Dual Representation Reconstruction (MDR2), a teacher-guided pre-training framework in which frozen, label-informed video and inertial teachers each provide dense, modality-specific targets to a multimodal student trained on temporally sparse inputs. On the EvIs-Kitchen dataset, MDR2 improves noun/verb F1-macro over the supervised M-Baseline by 9.20/6.17 points at K=4 video key frames and by 1.21/1.82 points at K=32. These results show that MDR2 consistently achieves higher mean recognition performance across the evaluated video-observation settings under the unified backbone setting, with more pronounced gains under sparse inputs.

</details>


### [14] [When Recognition Meets Discovery: Learning Need Not Be Entangled in One Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28092)
*Chenglin Liu,An Tian,Mingxuan Du,Shang Rui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双空间谱路由（Dual-Space Spectral Routing），通过在线SVD将表征分解为主奇异子空间与残差子空间，分别路由监督识别与无监督发现，缓解二者对表征容量的竞争，并用双空间新颖性信号引导子空间关系聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法在共享表征空间中同时优化识别与发现，隐含假设同一表征几何可服务两个目标。作者通过受控谱分析发现显著的非对称性：旧类判别性集中于主奇异方向，新类结构更多保留在残差空间，且强化前者会损害后者，说明识别与发现竞争的是表征容量而非仅优化权重。

Method: 1）通过受控谱分析揭示判别信息与新颖结构的子空间非对称分布；2）提出双空间谱路由，利用在线SVD动态分解表征，将监督识别与无监督发现路由至互补的判别子空间与通用子空间；3）利用两空间对旧/新样本的不同响应构造双空间新颖性信号；4）以该信号引导子空间关系聚合（SRA），组织候选新类。

Result: 在多种GCD基线、骨干网络与基准数据集上的大量实验表明，方法持续改善识别—发现平衡，且不引入额外推理参数。

Conclusion: 识别与发现对表征容量的竞争可通过子空间解耦得到有效缓解；谱路由与双空间新颖性信号为GCD提供了一种无额外推理开销、即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to recognize known classes while discovering novel categories from unlabeled data containing both old and novel samples. Existing methods typically optimize recognition and discovery within a shared representation space, implicitly assuming that the same representation geometry can serve both objectives. Through controlled spectral analysis, we uncover a striking asymmetry: old-class discrimination is increasingly concentrated in the dominant singular directions, while novel-class structure is better preserved in the complementary residual space. More importantly, strengthening the former can even degrade the latter, revealing that recognition and discovery compete for representation capacity rather than merely for optimization weight. Motivated by this observation, we propose Dual-Space Spectral Routing, which dynamically decomposes the representation via online SVD and routes supervised recognition and unsupervised discovery to complementary discriminative and general subspaces. We further exploit their distinct responses to old and novel samples to construct a dual-space novelty signal, which guides Subspace Relation Aggregation (SRA) for organizing candidate novel categories. Extensive experiments across multiple GCD baselines, backbones, and benchmarks demonstrate a consistently improved recognition-discovery balance without introducing additional inference parameters. Code will be open source.

</details>


### [15] [VTree: Question-Driven Growth of Hierarchical Memory for Agentic Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28093)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Lianyu Hu,Zixuan Hu,Zhizhen Wu,Zeqin Liao,Shengqian Qin,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTree：一次性构建视频的粗粒度分层记忆树，按问题在需要深度处生长分支，并写回记忆实现跨问题复用，在五个长视频基准上提升准确率并降低上下文长度与执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频（数十分钟至数小时）远超当前视觉语言模型的上下文窗口；稀疏均匀帧采样会丢弃细粒度证据；现有多轮探索代理每题从头探索且作答后丢弃探索状态，单题成本随视频长度增长；分层记忆方法预先固定各层深度范围，沿预设定金字塔加深而非朝问题所指方向加深。核心观察：视频的粗粒度结构（场景/主题起止）与问题无关，而问题需放大哪一部分、放大到多深则与问题有关。

Method: VTree 先对视频一次性构建粗粒度分层记忆树；对每个问题仅在需要深度处生长分支。扩展具备三重性质：全局路由（时间轴上任意部分可达）、充分性门控（证据足够即停止下钻）、查询条件化（分支展开为能回答当前问题的子区间）。每次扩展均写回视频记忆，后续问题可复用已发现的结构与证据，无需重复扩展。

Result: 在五个长视频理解基准上，VTree 一致提升多种视觉语言主干模型的准确率，同时显著减少每题所需的上下文长度与执行时间。

Conclusion: 将问题无关的粗结构预建与问题相关的按需加深相分离、并以可写回的记忆树实现跨问题复用，是缓解长视频理解中精度—成本矛盾的有效范式。

Abstract: Long videos spanning tens of minutes to hours far exceed the context window of today's vision-language models, and the standard remedy of sparse uniform frame sampling discards exactly the fine-grained evidence that specific questions depend on. Existing video agents explore on demand but re-explore from scratch for every question and discard their exploration state once an answer is produced, so per-question cost scales with video length. Hierarchical-memory methods instead fix the scope of each depth in advance and deepen along a pre-committed pyramid rather than toward the question. We observe that a video's coarse structure (where its scenes and topics begin and end) is question-independent, whereas which part a question must zoom into, and how deep, is question-dependent. We propose VTree, which builds a coarse hierarchical memory tree over a video once and then, for each question, grows branches only where that question needs depth. The growth is globally routed so any part of the timeline is reachable, sufficiency-gated so drilling stops when the evidence suffices, and query-conditioned so a branch unfolds into the sub-intervals that answer \emph{this} question. Crucially, every expansion is written back into the video memory, allowing later questions to reuse previously discovered structure and evidence without repeating the corresponding expansion process. Across five long-video understanding benchmarks, VTree consistently improves the accuracy of diverse vision-language backbones while substantially reducing the context length and execution time required for each question.

</details>


### [16] [LightOnOCR for AMNLT: Reading Chant Notation and Lyrics in One Pass, from Single Staves to Whole Pages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28094)
*Robert Klugseder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以1B参数的页面OCR模型LightOnOCR-3-1B端到端微调（含视觉编码器）完成“对齐乐谱–歌词转写”（AMNLT），在Solesmes上将最佳公开AMLER由21.0%降至9.9%，并可整页识别而无需谱表检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMNLT要求同时输出音符、歌词及其音节–音符对齐。任务提出者Fuentes-Martínez等发现其测试的单一视觉-语言模型远落后于专用识别器，说明如何高效地把通用视觉模型用于该任务仍是开放问题；同时现有基准对未见手稿的泛化能力缺乏度量。

Method: 端到端微调LightOnOCR-3-1B（10亿参数页面OCR模型），视觉编码器一并更新，使模型直接输出GABC字符串，其字母表示谱表位置从而编码音高；仅在公开AMNLT训练集上训练。此外，在包含多谱表页面的图像上训练以实现无谱表检测的整页识别；并逐步加入经CantusDB对齐的圣咏、Transkribus真值，以及仅在两名标注者一致时保留的silver谱表，以考察未见手稿上的泛化。

Result: 在唯一可直接比较的Solesmes数据集上，AMLER由21.0%降至9.9%（误差减半以上）；Einsiedeln与Salzinnes在另一编码下指标亦更低。泛化测试：AMNLT验证集音高错误率1.7–1.9%，而在五份未见手稿的200个谱表上由24.9%降至1.3%（随数据增补）。整页识别模式下，验证/测试页音高错误率3.3–3.8%，未见手稿页1.9%。残余错误包括：哥特式F谱号被读作C谱号、文字缩写或不可辨时漏音符、小尺寸谱表页面精度较低。

Conclusion: 大规模页面OCR模型的端到端微调可显著超越专用识别器在AMNLT上的表现，且无需显式谱表检测即可整页转写；泛化到未见手稿高度依赖对齐与silver数据，但各增益来源尚需受控消融实验予以解释。

Abstract: Aligned music notation and lyrics transcription (AMNLT) asks for the notes of a chant, its sung text and the assignment of syllables to notes in one output. Fuentes-Martínez et al., who defined the task, found that the one vision-language model they tested fell far behind specialised recognisers. We fine-tune LightOnOCR-3-1B, a page OCR model with one billion parameters, end to end, vision encoder included, to write a staff as a GABC string whose letters denote staff positions. Trained on the published AMNLT training split alone, it more than halves the best published aligned music and lyrics error rate (AMLER) on Solesmes, the one directly comparable dataset (9.9 against 21.0 %); on Einsiedeln and Salzinnes, measured in a different encoding, its figures are also lower. The benchmark does not measure generalisation to unseen manuscripts: while the pitch error on AMNLT validation stays between 1.7 and 1.9 %, on 200 staves from five unseen manuscripts it falls from 24.9 to 1.3 % as we add chant aligned through CantusDB, Transkribus ground truth and silver staves kept only where two readers agree. Trained also on multi-staff images, it reads whole pages without staff detection (pitch error 3.3 to 3.8 % on validation and test pages, 1.9 % on pages of unseen manuscripts). Remaining errors include Gothic F clefs read as C clefs, notes dropped with abbreviated or illegible text and lower accuracy on pages with small notation. Controlled ablations are needed to explain the gains.

</details>


### [17] [ORDO: Fine-Grained Motion Understanding in Video LLMs via Order-Dependent Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28097)
*Jiaye Luan,Chaoning Zhang,Pengcheng Zheng,Dongshen Han,Sheng Zheng,Ping Guo,Caiyan Qin,Yang Du,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ORDO框架，通过构造"顺序必要性监督"数据（无序帧集在各竞争标签下同分布），结合时间反转配对、空间范围匹配、均匀间隔覆盖三种构造与六种运动基元课程，用LoRA训练视频LLM，显著提升细粒度运动（如加速/减速）判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在细粒度运动（如物体加速或减速）上表现薄弱。现有方法依赖合成运动片段、乱序片段重排等前置任务，或用有序帧与扰动帧对比的奖励，但均未要求"丢弃帧序后标签不可从帧中恢复"，模型可仅凭静态线索拟合数据而不学习利用帧顺序。

Method: 定义"顺序必要性监督"：无序帧集合在竞争标签下具有相同分布。针对运动属性用三种构造实现：时间反转配对、匹配空间范围、均匀间隔覆盖。用六种运动基元的程序化课程实例化这些构造，仅对语言模型低秩适配器（LoRA）以答案级目标训练，并在真实视频上做自蒸馏回放。

Result: 仅用4,000个合成片段，ORDO在四个运动基准上的平均准确率在Qwen3.5-9B、Qwen3-VL-8B和Molmo2-8B上分别提升+7.6、+4.9、+5.1个点；而相同训练改用乱序帧时变化不足1个点，验证收益来自帧顺序的使用。

Conclusion: 将"顺序必要性"直接编码进训练数据构造，是提升视频LLM细粒度运动理解的有效且数据高效的途径；乱序帧消融证明性能增益确由顺序信息驱动。

Abstract: Video large language models (video LLMs) answer a wide range of questions about real videos, yet they remain weak at fine-grained motion such as whether an object speeds up or slows down. Existing methods train these models on synthetic motion clips, on order pretext tasks such as reordering shuffled clips, or with rewards that compare ordered and perturbed frames. None of them requires that the label be unrecoverable from the frames once their order is discarded, so a model can fit such data from static cues without learning to use frame order. We propose ORDO, a framework that builds this requirement into the training data. We define order-necessary supervision as data in which the unordered set of frames has the same distribution under competing labels, and we realise it for motion attributes with three constructions, time-reversal pairing, matched spatial extent and uniform interval coverage. A procedural curriculum of six motion primitives instantiates these constructions, and low-rank adapters of the language model are trained on it with an answer-level objective and self-distillation replay on real videos. With 4,000 synthetic clips, ORDO raises the mean accuracy over four motion benchmarks by +7.6, +4.9 and +5.1 points on Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3-VL-8B and Molmo2-8B, while the same training with shuffled frames changes it by less than one point. Code and generator will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [18] [Causal Dioramas: Controllable Video Generation, Revision and Editing via Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28098)
*Yifan Shen,Xinyi Yang,Peiyuan Zhu,Yichang Jian,Jinyuan Hu,Xilin He,Fengming Zhu,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Causal Dioramas——一种将视频分解为可单独干预的变量概念图的模块，把指令转化为概念图上的干预，经连接器传入视频生成器并复用原始噪声，实现可控生成、生成视频的反事实修正与真实视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽接近电影级画质，但缺乏可控性。根本原因是文本与像素之间存在语义鸿沟：提示词只能粗略描述视频，其细节与元素间关系无法被精确控制。

Method: 将视频分解为由可单独干预变量构成的概念图（concept graph）；每条指令被翻译为对概念图的干预，修改指定概念及其依赖项；再由连接器把该干预传递给视频生成器，生成器复用原始噪声生成反事实视频，同时保留其余内容。基于此构建统一框架，支持可控生成、生成视频的反事实修正和真实视频编辑。

Result: 在T2V-CompBench、CoVEBench和OpenVE-Bench上，可控性优于开源与商业模型。

Conclusion: 以因果/概念图级别的干预替代粗粒度文本提示，可在保留原视频其余内容的前提下实现细粒度、局部化的视频控制，并统一生成、反事实修正与真实视频编辑三类任务。

Abstract: Two centuries ago, an early cinema-like theater called the Diorama could already control its scenes at will. Today's video generation models produce videos of near-cinematic quality, yet they still lack this controllability. This limitation stems from the inherent semantic gap between text and pixels, since a prompt can describe a video only coarsely and leaves its details and relations beyond control. We bring this ability to video generation with Causal Dioramas, modules that decompose a video into a concept graph of variables that can be intervened on individually. Each instruction is translated into an intervention on the concept graph, modifying the specified concepts and their dependents. A connector then conveys this intervention to the video generator, which reuses the original noise to produce a counterfactual video while preserving all other content. Building on Causal Dioramas, we develop a unified framework for controllable generation, counterfactual revision of generated videos and editing of real videos. We outperform both open-source and commercial models in controllability on T2V-CompBench, CoVEBench and OpenVE-Bench.

</details>


### [19] [VidRouteBench: A Benchmark for Budgeted Video-Generation Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28099)
*Dayu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 VidRouteBench 视频生成路由基准（16 个 arm、121 倍成本跨度、27,110 个片段），证明逐 prompt 特化可预测，并提出 OPMU 方法：仅用 K 个探针 prompt 标注新 arm 的 9 个质量维度，随后通过标量化服务任意效用方向，在 94% 的效用方向上帕累托支配 prompt-agnostic 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成器价格相差两个数量级，强模型的廉价配置往往已足够，因此按 prompt 在 (模型×采样配置) 的 arm 间路由可在固定预算下提升质量。但现有公开视频偏好数据集只含闭源模型且每个模型仅一种配置，无法支撑此类研究。

Method: 构建 VidRouteBench：7 个开源家族共 16 个 arm，实测成本跨 121 倍（$0.0025–$0.298/片段），1,625 个分组 prompt 上生成 27,110 个片段（1,421 A800 小时）；质量分数经人类偏好校准后冻结（68.7% 交叉验证准确率），含 9 个可识别质量维度、重复随机种子、加密测试集与冷启动密封家族；评测以最佳 prompt-agnostic arm 混合为基线。提出 OPMU：用 K 个探针 prompt 标注新 arm，一次性预测其 9 维质量，再按场景效用标量化。

Result: 逐 prompt 特化真实且可预测：oracle gap 0.642，47% 可跨种子复现，prompt-arm 交互 F=4.47（p=1.6e-63）；而在 LLM 路由 arm 上同一流程未发现此类效应。OPMU 在 215 个效用方向中对 94% 帕累托支配 prompt-agnostic 基线（逐场景路由器为 90%），且无需逐场景重训。三类基线失败：词袋特征在低数据区几乎丧失全部路由增益（0.009 vs 0.168）；以探针均值信用新 arm 有害（-3.32 recovery），尽管在 RewardBench 类 arm 上是强基线（0.786）；Chebyshev 标量化无益，因成本-效用前沿为凸。

Conclusion: 视频生成存在可学习的 prompt 级路由增益，OPMU 以极低标注成本实现跨效用方向泛化，是固定预算下质量-成本权衡的有效方案。

Abstract: Text-to-video generators differ by two orders of magnitude in price, and a cheap configuration of a strong model is often good enough for a prompt. Routing prompts across such arms (model x sampling configuration) should buy quality at fixed budget, but no dataset supports studying it: public video-preference sets contain only closed models, one configuration each.
  We release VidRouteBench: 16 arms from 7 open families spanning 121x in measured cost ($0.0025-$0.298 per clip), 27,110 clips (1,421 A800-hours) over 1,625 grouped prompts, a quality score frozen after calibration against human preferences (68.7% CV accuracy vs 66.2% for the best single scorer), per-clip scores on 9 identifiable quality dimensions, repeated seeds, an encrypted test split, and a sealed newest family for cold start. The evaluator compares a router against the best prompt-agnostic mixture of arms, not the best single arm. Per-prompt specialisation here is real and predictable (oracle gap 0.642, 47% reproducible across seeds, prompt-arm interaction F=4.47 at p=1.6e-63), unlike LLM-routing arms, where the same pipeline finds none.
  We then propose OPMU (one probe, many utilities): label a new arm on K probe prompts, predict its 9 dimensions once, and serve any scenario utility by scalarising afterwards. Over 215 utility directions OPMU Pareto-dominates the prompt-agnostic baseline for 94% of directions versus 90% for per-scenario routers, with no per-scenario retraining. Three baselines fail instructively: bag-of-words features lose almost all routing gain in the low-data regime (0.009 vs 0.168); crediting a new arm with its probe average is harmful (-3.32 recovery) although it is the strong baseline on RewardBench-style arms (0.786); Chebyshev scalarisation does not help, our cost-utility front being convex.

</details>


### [20] [You Only Think Once: Rescuing Hybrid Vision-Language Models Lost in Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28100)
*Tianyi Tan,Wentao Wang,Xiaohan Li,Zining Ren,Siyuan Li,Xutao Mao,Rui Qian,Luoxi Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YOTO方法：混合VLM先直接作答，仅在投票未收敛时思考一次，并由失败预测头提前终止，20k预算下优于early-exit基线最多5.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合式VLM的长思考常陷入循环或耗尽预算而无法输出答案，使thinking表现反低于直接作答；现有early-exit方法依赖"答案已就绪"的时刻，但在混合VLM中该时刻常常不出现，故需改变退出对象的定义。

Method: 核心思想由"更早停止失败的思考"转为"停止采纳其答案"。YOTO先进行多次直接作答，用probe加权投票判定是否收敛；仅对未收敛问题给予一次thought，并由作用于隐藏状态的failure head实时监控，一旦预测失败即终止思考并回退到直接答案。权重冻结，仅新增两个线性探针，跨模型尺寸共用单一失败阈值。

Result: 同等20k token预算下，较最强early-exit基线最高提升5.9个点；MathVision上Qwen3.5-2B由40.1%升至57.3%，Qwen3.5-4B由68.3%升至73.7%，超过两倍规模Qwen3.5-9B的72.8%；增益可迁移至另外五个基准及第二个模型族。

Conclusion: 混合式VLM的推理提升应来自"先答后想一次"的自适应计算分配，而非延长思考长度；失败信号可用于决定是否采纳思考结果。

Abstract: Thinking at length before answering is expected to strengthen the visual reasoning of vision-language models (VLMs), yet hybrid VLMs often get lost in thought: their thoughts loop or exhaust the budget without an answer, so thinking can score below answering directly. Existing early-exit methods watch a thought for the moment its answer is ready and force the answer out of it, but in a hybrid VLM that moment often never comes. We find that the remedy is not to stop a failing thought earlier, but to stop taking its answer: once a thought is given up, the model's own direct answers decide. Based on this, we propose **YOTO (You Only Think Once)**, a method that answers first and thinks at most once. YOTO draws direct answers until a probe-weighted vote settles; only an unsettled problem receives a single thought, which a failure head on the hidden state monitors and stops as soon as it predicts failure, handing the problem back to direct answers. At a matched 20k-token budget, YOTO is up to 5.9 points more accurate than the strongest early-exit baseline, using one failure-head threshold across model sizes. Keeping the released weights frozen, it adds only two linear probes. On the full MathVision benchmark, YOTO improves Qwen3.5-2B from 40.1% to 57.3% and Qwen3.5-4B from 68.3% to 73.7%, above the 72.8% that Qwen3.5-9B, a model more than twice its size, reaches with thinking. Its gains carry over to five further benchmarks and a second model family. These results suggest that hybrid VLMs reason better by answering first and thinking once than by thinking longer.

</details>


### [21] [Scale-Aware Reference Attention for Mannequin-to-Model Gown Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28101)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出尺度感知参考注意力SARA，使扩散Transformer感知输出与参考图的缩放级别，从而在虚拟试穿中保留礼服的织物细节，并引入Kiss3000数据集与缩放裁剪评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线上礼服销售依赖买家放大查看亮片、蕾丝与珠饰等细节，但现有图像编辑模型在生成模特穿着图时无法感知参考照片的放大倍率，输出在高倍查看时丢失精细细节；即便是4K的商用编辑器也会在多数测试礼服上丢失或重绘局部细节。

Method: 提出尺度感知参考注意力（SARA）：向开放扩散Transformer显式输入输出图与每张参考图的缩放级别，使输出注意力偏向同尺度参考；采用织物微距照片作为参考；在整衣与放大目标混合数据上训练。构建Kiss3000数据集（915张高分辨率人体模特照与2274张上身照，含跨域配对与按款式划分），并设计缩放裁剪评测：在整幅、半幅、局部三种尺度比较服装裁剪区域，无需像素对齐，用两个视觉语言模型在控制裁剪门控下评判局部保真度。

Result: 在4K下Nano Banana Pro在20件测试礼服中有14件丢失或重绘局部细节；在1536x2048分辨率下，SARA在18件测试项上优于LoRA微调基线、6件不敌（p=0.023），在26件上优于基座模型、4件不敌（p=5.9e-5），同时保持礼服设计不变的比例与基线相当。

Conclusion: 显式建模尺度信息可有效提升扩散编辑模型的局部细节保真度，所提SARA与配套数据及评测协议为高分辨率服装细节保持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Formal gowns are sold online with photos of models wearing them, and buyers zoom in to inspect sequins, lace and beading. Image editors can turn a photo of a gown on a mannequin into a convincing photo of a person wearing it, but they receive every reference photo without any notion of its magnification, and the fine detail of the gown is lost when the output is viewed up close. We propose scale-aware reference attention (SARA), which tells an open diffusion transformer the zoom level of the output and of every reference photo, biases the attention of the output toward references of a similar zoom level, uses macro photos of the fabric as references, and is trained on a mix of whole-garment and zoomed targets. To train and evaluate it, we introduce Kiss3000, a dataset of 915 high-resolution mannequin photos and 2274 on-model photos of formal gowns with cross-domain pairs and splits by style, and zoom-crop evaluation, which compares garment crops at full, half and local scale without pixel alignment and judges local fidelity with two vision-language models gated on control crops. With this protocol, Nano Banana Pro at 4K, the highest-resolution commercial editor we test, loses or redraws local detail on 14 of 20 test gowns. At 1536x2048 pixels, both judges prefer SARA over a LoRA fine-tuning baseline on 18 test items and the baseline on 6 (p=0.023), and over the base model on 26 against 4 (p=5.9e-5), while SARA keeps the gown design as often as the baseline.

</details>


### [22] [VideoGym: An Adaptive RL Environment for Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28102)
*Jing Wang,Yi Ke Yun,Yifeng Yao,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoGym：一个基于课程学习的强化学习环境，联合构建视频任务与分配 rollout，用 EXP3 对抗赌博机在线调整训练分布以缓解长尾模态分布，长视频基准平均准确率从 60.4% 提升到 64.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 训练长视频问答智能体时数据存在长尾模态分布（源池中文本类占 58%，图类 15%、视觉类 17%、时间区间类 10%），固定分布 RL 会给表现差的问题类型分配更少 rollout；单纯重加权会反复复用有限的原始问题库，无法真正扩充样本。

Method: 提出 VideoGym：1) 环境内训练记忆增强的 VideoAgent，协调文本/图/视觉三种记忆检索与时间重观测；2) 将每周期样本选择建模为 (modality, mode) 臂上的对抗赌博机，用 EXP3 更新把采样推向低准确率臂；3) TaskGenerator-Verifier 流水线生成更难的变体问题，且保证在同一视频内可回答，为增强模式提供臂。

Result: 在五个长视频基准上，RL 准确率平均由 60.4% 提升至 64.8%，在欠代表的图密集型问题上最多提升 10.5 个百分点，样本效率提升 3.3 倍。

Conclusion: 任务扩展与自适应分配相结合，能改善视频智能体在有限且不均衡训练数据上的学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) trains video agents to locate sparse evidence for long-video question answering through structured memory retrieval and temporal re-observation. This training, however, faces a long-tail modality distribution in the data. In our source pool, text-heavy questions account for 58% of samples, compared with 15% graph-heavy, 17% visual-heavy, and 10% time-range questions. Fixed-distribution RL therefore allocates fewer rollouts to underperforming question types. Reweighting these types could increase their sampling frequency but repeatedly reuses the same finite banks of original questions. To solve this problem, we introduce VideoGym, a curriculum-based RL environment that jointly constructs video-grounded training tasks and allocates rollouts among them. Within the environment, a memory-augmented VideoAgent is trained to coordinate retrieval from text, graph, and visual memories with temporal re-observation. Across episodes, VideoGym adapts VideoAgent's training distribution online: it casts per-period sample selection as an adversarial bandit over (modality, mode) arms and applies the EXP3 update to shift sampling toward arms with low answer accuracy. To supply the augmented-mode arms, a TaskGenerator-Verifier pipeline produces harder variants of existing questions that remain answerable within the same video. Across five long-video benchmarks, VideoGym lifts RL accuracy from 60.4% to 64.8% on average and by up to 10.5 pp on underrepresented graph-heavy questions with 3.3x sample efficiency. Together, these results show that task expansion and adaptive allocation can improve video agent learning from limited, imbalanced training data.

</details>


### [23] [Intervene or Trust CLIP? Competence-Gated Adaptation for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28104)
*Haoyue Liu,Chenhao Zhu,Yu Huo,Haoran Shou,Shuguang Cui,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Competence-Gated Adaptation (CoGA)：用支持集上冻结 CLIP 的准确率与 margin 判断是否干预视觉 token，无额外参数，在 BSCD-FSL 取得最佳平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SF-CDFSL 方法对 CLIP 采用统一的 token 级干预，忽视干预效果依赖于冻结 CLIP 对目标域本身的识别能力：支持集分类差时干预有益（+0.72），已识别良好时干预反而有害（−1.31）。

Method: 基于单一 episode 的支持集即可区分上述两种情形，作者提出以支持集上冻结 CLIP 的准确率与预测 margin 作为"能力门控"信号，在训练前决定：若能力低则对视觉 token 进行干预式微调，若能力高则信任 CLIP，将适配后预测与冻结预测融合。方法不引入可训练参数，不使用查询标签。

Result: 在 BSCD-FSL 四个目标域上取得最佳平均精度 59.56%（1-shot）与 68.96%（5-shot），在相同 episode 上较 ATHA 与 TIR 分别至少领先 2.82 与 1.39 个百分点；并在三种额外骨干网络、三种参数高效微调框架及五个额外目标域上稳定提升。

Conclusion: token 级干预是否有效取决于冻结 CLIP 的目标域胜任度，据此进行条件化门控与预测融合可无参数、低成本地获得稳健且可泛化的 SF-CDFSL 性能。

Abstract: Source-free cross-domain few-shot learning (SF-CDFSL) adapts a pre-trained vision-language model such as CLIP to a distant target domain from a handful of labeled images, without access to source data. Recent methods improve the fine-tuning of CLIP with token-level interventions and apply them in the same way to every target domain. In this paper, we find that whether an intervention helps depends on how well frozen CLIP already recognizes the target domain: in 5-way 1-shot tasks, intervening improves accuracy by 0.72 points where frozen CLIP misclassifies most support images, but lowers it by 1.31 points where frozen CLIP already classifies them well, and the support set of a single episode suffices to tell the two cases apart; a bias-variance analysis explains both. Based on this finding, we propose Competence-Gated Adaptation (CoGA), which measures frozen CLIP's accuracy and margin on the labeled support set and decides, before training, whether to intervene on the visual tokens or to trust CLIP by fusing the adapted prediction with the frozen one. CoGA adds no trainable parameters and uses no query labels. On the four BSCD-FSL target domains, CoGA achieves the best average accuracy, 59.56% (1-shot) and 68.96% (5-shot), outperforms ATHA and TIR by at least 2.82 and 1.39 points on the same episodes, and consistently improves three further backbones, three parameter-efficient fine-tuning frameworks and five further target domains.

</details>


### [24] [Gradient-Free Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision Models via Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28105)
*Khayrul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"Agent edit"：用编码智能体写程序直接改写冻结检测器分类头的新类权重，无需梯度；仅在五样本、头内比较时可部分替代梯度微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本（few-shot）下用梯度微调视觉模型适配新类不稳定，作者探究编码智能体能否替代梯度下降完成该适配任务。

Method: 冻结COCO预训练检测器，智能体有限次运行检测器并观察mAP变化，反复编写程序设定分类头中新类权重（无梯度步）；最终程序可脱离智能体重建权重。作者还提炼出多数程序共享的三部分结构并写成无需智能体的公式。

Result: 同头同标签下，5-shot时在4个细粒度集中的2个上分别超全解码器微调11.2与12.3 F1点，其余持平；10-shot时微调持平或更优。新类与COCO类联合评分时，所属COCO类AP最多下降33.3点，抑制该损失会降低新类F1。冻结骨干特征上的简单分类器在所有集合上F1均高于头内所有方法。

Conclusion: 编码智能体只能部分替代梯度下降——限于分类头内部与5-shot设置；联合评分下的旧类遗忘与骨干特征分类器的优势表明其适用边界明确。

Abstract: Adapting a vision model to new classes from a few labeled images usually relies on gradient fine-tuning, which is unstable with so few images. We ask whether a coding agent can replace gradient descent for this task. We propose the Agent edit, a method in which a coding agent writes a program that sets the new class weights in the class head of a frozen COCO-trained detector, with no gradient step. The agent runs the detector on the labeled images a limited number of times and sees how much its edit lowers COCO mAP; the program rebuilds the weights without the agent. In the same head and with the same labels, the Agent edit outperforms full-decoder fine-tuning at five shots on 2 of four fine-grained sets, by 11.2 and 12.3 F1 points, and ties on the rest. At ten shots, fine-tuning ties with or outperforms it. Moreover, when the new classes are scored jointly with the COCO classes, the AP of the COCO class to which they belong falls by up to 33.3 points, and limiting that loss lowers F1 on the new classes. Most of the agent's programs share three parts, which we write as a formula that needs no agent. A classifier on frozen backbone features reaches a higher F1 than every method inside the head on every set. Hence a coding agent can replace gradient descent only in part: inside the head and at five shots.

</details>


### [25] [Cyclic Discrete Diffusion: A Novel Multi-Class Segmentation Refinement Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28107)
*Libo Chen,Souvik Ghosh,Vinay P. Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出循环离散扩散（CDD），以“跳转或保持”的离散标签马尔可夫过程实现多类分割掩码的联合精化，兼具解析可逆动态与有限步收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割精化方法多基于二值化的逐类分解，需对每个类别单独处理，难以在原生多类标签空间中一次性联合精化；同时连续扩散注入高斯噪声的方式与离散标签空间不匹配。

Method: 将图像分割精化建模为离散扩散过程。设计带循环标签拓扑的前向-反向公式，把离散标签集映射到均匀分布再映射回标签；前向过程不注入高斯噪声，而是每步让每个像素以固定速率“保持当前类”或“跳转到下一个标签”，形成有限状态马尔可夫过程，其正反向步数有界且反向动态可解析、可处理。CDD 直接在多类标签空间运行，单次前向即可联合精化所有类别；并通过谱隙分析刻画有限且可控精化步数下的收敛性质。

Result: 在多类语义分割、实例级分割与二值分割精化等多样任务上评估，跨多种骨干网络与数据集，方法在区域精度与边界质量两方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 离散循环扩散为掩码精化提供了原生多类、解析可逆且步数可控的统一框架，可稳定提升分割的区域与边界性能。

Abstract: We cast image segmentation refinement as a discrete diffusion process. To solve this task we introduce Cyclic Discrete Diffusion (CDD), a novel forward-reverse formulation with a cyclic label topology that maps a discrete label set to the uniform distribution and back. Instead of injecting Gaussian noise as is done for continuous diffusion, CDD evolves segmentation masks through label jumps: at each step, every pixel either remains in its current class or jumps to the next label with a fixed rate. This jump-or-stay mechanism defines a finite-state Markov process with bounded forward and reverse steps, featuring analytically consistent and tractable reverse dynamics. CDD operates natively in a multi-class label space, enabling joint refinement of all classes in a single pass without requiring class-wise decomposition, as commonly needed in prior binary refinement approaches. The proposed formulation admits a finite and controllable number of refinement steps, for which we analyze the convergence properties through spectral gap analysis. We evaluate CDD across diverse segmentation tasks, including multi-class semantic, instance-level, and binary segmentation refinement. Our method consistently improves both region accuracy and boundary quality across multiple backbones and datasets.

</details>


### [26] [Tangent incompleteness under coupled image transformations: margin reconstruction and decision consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28110)
*Karim Bounja,Abdeljalil Sakat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究对单个几何图像变换的响应能否可加地还原其联合效应对分类器决策的影响，指出恒等对齐双线性重采样下一阶坐标可加装配会失效，并给出耦合修正表示与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为回答"单项几何变换的准确响应能否恢复其组合效应对分类器决策的影响"这一核心问题，并解释为何按坐标（轴）分别装配的分析在一阶就会失败。

Method: 将实现的变换投影到固定的 logit-margin 梯度上，推导线性项加带符号绝对值脊（ridge）的展开；给出精确的偶-奇恒等式与脊抵消条件刻画缺失信息；在独立参数律下分析非抵消的非坐标脊对偶数无约束加性拟合的阻碍，并比较耦合修正的 O(s²) 投影误差与轴式方法的 Θ(s) 缺陷；借助局部光滑性把结果迁移到真实网络 margin；构造可精确最小化的三参数威胁代理，并给出条件选择遗憾保证。

Result: 投影 RMS 误差在 CIFAR-10 上降低 82.96%、ImageNetV2 上降低 82.38%；真实固定对 margin RMS 误差分别降低 39.75% 与 42.73%。该表示前瞻性识别出九种严格非可加的网络 margin 符号配置。独立 CIFAR-10 搜索中，耦合选择发现 51 个 top-1 变化，轴式方法为 41 个。在 256 张 ImageNetV2/ConvNeXt-Tiny 图像、s=.40 下 16,384 次变换决策中，多类 top-1 翻转预测错误率从 11.97% 降至 10.23%（配对改进 1.74 个百分点，95% 区间 [0.93, 2.61]）；方向有限差分略更准确（10.00%）但模型构建成本更高。

Conclusion: 一阶采样交互与 margin 重构、决策预测及威胁选择存在内在联系；耦合修正显著优于轴式装配，但作者明确不主张对有限尺度网络给出认证保证。

Abstract: Can accurate responses to individual geometric image transformations recover their combined effect on a classifier's decision? For identity-aligned bilinear resampling, coordinatewise assembly may fail at first order. Projecting the implemented transformation onto a fixed logit-margin gradient, we derive a linear term plus signed absolute-value ridges. An exact even--odd identity and ridge-cancellation conditions characterize the missing information. Under independent parameter laws, a noncancelling off-coordinate ridge obstructs even unrestricted additive fits. The coupled correction has $O(s^2)$ projected error, versus a possible $Θ(s)$ axiswise defect; transfer to realized network margins requires local smoothness. Projected RMS errors fall by 82.96% on CIFAR-10 and 82.38% on ImageNetV2 relative to axiswise assembly; realized fixed-pair margin RMS errors fall by 39.75% and 42.73%. The representation prospectively identifies nine strict nonadditive network-margin sign configurations and permits exact minimization of a three-parameter threat surrogate, with conditional selection-regret guarantees. In an independent CIFAR-10 search, coupled-selected transformations uncover 51 top-1 changes versus 41 for axes. On 256 independent ImageNetV2/ConvNeXt-Tiny images and 16,384 transformed decisions at the prespecified scale $s=.40$, multiclass top-1 flip-prediction errors fall from 11.97% to 10.23% (paired improvement 1.74 percentage points; 95% interval [0.93, 2.61]). Directional finite differences are slightly more accurate (10.00%) at higher measured model-construction cost. These results connect first-order sampling interactions to margin reconstruction, decision prediction, and threat selection, without claiming finite-scale network certification.

</details>


### [27] [GC-GS: Optimizing 3D Gaussian Splatting with Gradient Conflict as a Geometry Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28111)
*Chenhao Zhang,Fengze Zhang,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将3DGS训练重构为多目标优化问题，利用位置梯度冲突作为几何感知信号指导位置更新与致密化/剪枝，在提升渲染质量的同时增强表示紧凑性与渲染效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS通常使用多个重建损失联合优化，引入感知损失后各目标对高斯原语的更新方向相互竞争，难以同时兼顾保真度、感知质量与表示效率；作者观察到梯度冲突贯穿训练全过程且在高斯位置参数上最严重，说明目标冲突与几何优化紧密相关。

Method: 把3DGS训练表述为多目标优化问题，推导出选择性的冲突感知位置更新（selective conflict-aware position updates），在保留各损失贡献的同时减少竞争目标间的干扰；并将位置梯度冲突作为几何感知信号，用于引导致密化（densification）与剪枝（pruning）。

Result: 实验表明，与现有3DGS变体相比，该方法在获得更好渲染性能的同时，提升了表示紧凑性和渲染效率。

Conclusion: 目标冲突是3DGS几何优化的关键因素，基于位置梯度冲突的多目标优化框架可有效协调保真度、感知质量与表示效率三者的权衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is commonly optimized with multiple reconstruction losses, which naturally forms a multi-objective optimization problem. This issue becomes more pronounced when perceptual losses are introduced to improve visual quality, as different objectives may impose competing update directions on Gaussian primitives and make it difficult to simultaneously improve fidelity, perceptual quality, and representation efficiency. Based on this view, we reformulate 3DGS training as a multi-objective optimization problem and derive selective conflict-aware position updates that reduce interference between competing objectives while preserving the contribution of each loss. We further observe that gradient conflicts persist throughout training and are most severe in Gaussian position parameters, indicating that objective conflict is closely tied to geometry optimization. We therefore treat position-gradient conflict as a geometry-aware signal to guide densification and pruning. Experiments show that our method achieves better rendering performance while improving representation compactness and rendering efficiency over existing 3DGS variants.

</details>


### [28] [ROSE-SAM3: Response-Oriented Prompt Selection for Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28112)
*Yanghui Song,Chengfu Yang,Yongpeng Wen,Zhanxuan Hu,Wei Lu,Qi Ming,Nanqing Liu,Heng-Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架ROSE-SAM3，基于冻结SAM 3响应筛选描述提示以构建PromptBank，并用SICH头细化语义响应，在八个遥感数据集上取得免训练方法SOTA（57.3% mIoU），并新增RS-OVSS-C鲁棒性基准（54.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇遥感语义分割中，类名提示难以覆盖外观多样的目标，而更丰富的描述又会激活竞争类，导致提示设计与类别判别之间存在矛盾；同时缺乏对损坏鲁棒性的评测。

Method: 免训练流程：1）利用冻结SAM 3在无标注目标域图像上的响应筛选候选描述，要求在超出原生提示覆盖的同时保护可靠的native类响应，且需在五折中至少四折通过；2）离线DAG搜索评估候选组合，为每类构建紧凑PromptBank（原生提示+residual prompts）；3）推理时固定bank，由Semantic-Instance Cooperative Head（SICH）利用实例证据细化语义响应后再分配像素标签。

Result: 八个干净遥感基准上平均mIoU 57.3%；在自建RS-OVSS-C（六类图像损坏、八个数据集、保留类词表与参考标签）上54.7%；免训练方法中达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 以响应驱动的提示选择替代人工提示工程，可在不训练的情况下提升开放词汇遥感分割的精度与鲁棒性；代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: In open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) of remote-sensing imagery, class-name prompts can miss objects with varied appearances, while richer descriptions can activate competing classes. We propose Response-Oriented Prompt Selection for SAM 3 (ROSE-SAM3), a training-free framework that selects descriptions using frozen SAM 3 responses on unlabeled target-domain images. We screen candidate descriptions for coverage beyond the native prompts while protecting reliable native class responses, retaining candidates that pass these checks on at least four of five image folds. An offline directed acyclic graph (DAG) search then evaluates candidate combinations and builds a compact PromptBank for each class. Each bank retains the native prompts and adds selected descriptions, which we call residual prompts. At inference, the bank is fixed, and Semantic-Instance Cooperative Head (SICH) uses instance evidence to refine semantic responses before assigning pixel labels. We also introduce RS-OVSS-C to evaluate robustness under six image corruption families across eight remote-sensing datasets, preserving their class vocabularies and reference labels. ROSE-SAM3 achieves 57.3% mean mIoU across eight clean benchmarks and 54.7% on RS-OVSS-C, achieving state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/YanghuiSong/ROSE-SAM3.

</details>


### [29] [Benchmarking Foundation Models for Bone Scaffold Pore Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Few-Shot Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28113)
*Tri Tin Vo,Vu Thien An Ngo,Nhi Kieu,Cuong Hung Luu,Giang V. H. Phan,Kien Nguyen,Clinton Fookes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文发布首个专家像素级标注的骨支架SEM图像数据集，在12-shot微调下对比SAM3与YOLOv26，发现两模型性能瓶颈由孔隙密度而非放大倍率决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨支架力学性能评估依赖破坏性测试或昂贵的有限元分析；深度学习替代方案受生物材料标注数据稀缺所限，因此需构建高质量标注数据集并明确预训练基础模型在何种成像条件下可靠。

Method: 构建覆盖五个放大倍率序列的专家像素级标注骨支架SEM数据集；在12-shot微调设定下基准测试实例分割器SAM3与框检测器YOLOv26，将其预测的孔隙几何输入回归头以估计杨氏模量与压缩屈服强度。

Result: 44张图像评测集上，YOLOv26平均孔隙率误差更低（MAE 19.9% vs 23.1%），精确率与F1更高；YOLOv26出现3次灾难性失败，SAM3为8次。YOLOv26在拥挤帧上孔隙计数饱和、孔隙率估计崩溃；SAM3在稀疏孔隙场中过度分裂、在密集场中合并相邻孔隙。SAM3可提供YOLOv26边界框无法复现的像素级实例分割。

Conclusion: 模型选择应依据孔隙密度而非仅SEM放大倍率；YOLOv26更适用于孔隙率定量统计，SAM3适用于需要像素级实例几何的场景。

Abstract: Evaluating the mechanical properties of bone scaffolds conventionally requires destructive testing or costly Finite Element Analysis, while deep learning alternatives are constrained by the scarcity of labeled biomaterial imaging data. This study introduces the first expert-annotated, pixel-dense dataset of biopolymer bone-scaffold SEM images, spanning five magnification series, and uses it to benchmark two state-of-the-art pre-trained foundation models under a 12-shot fine-tuning regime. SAM3, an instance segmenter, and YOLOv26, a bounding-box detector, feed the predicted pore geometry to regression heads that estimate Young's modulus and compressive yield strength. Our central finding is that both models fail by pore density rather than magnification: YOLOv26's predicted pore counts saturate and porosity estimation collapses on the most crowded frames, whereas SAM3 over-fragments sparse pore fields and merges adjacent pores in dense ones, producing eight catastrophic failures against three for YOLOv26. Averaged across the 44-image evaluation set, YOLOv26 achieves lower porosity error (19.9% vs. 23.1% MAE), higher precision, and a higher F1 score, whereas SAM3 offers pixel-level instance segmentation that YOLOv26's bounding boxes cannot reproduce. We conclude that model selection should be driven by pore density rather than SEM magnification alone.

</details>


### [30] [NOVA: Normalization-Aware Output-Guided Attention Acceleration for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28115)
*Wenxuan Miao,Haosong Liu,Aiyue Chen,Yiwu Yao,Zihan Liu,Anbang Wu,Yiming Gan,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA：面向视频扩散Transformer的粗到细稀疏注意力框架，用尺寸加权质心和残差代理联合刻画分子-分母误差来选择需精算的块，在五个vDiT上取得最佳生成质量与最高1.82×端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vDiT自注意力为二次复杂度，高分辨率和长时长下效率受限；现有稀疏注意力方法以保留高注意力权重为准则，但该准则无法完整反映最终value加权输出的保真度，因为输出误差同时取决于注意力分子与softmax分母的变化。

Method: 提出粗到细稀疏注意力：先用尺寸加权的key/value质心近似完整归一化输出；再构建残差代理，通过将value贡献围绕估计输出进行中心化，耦合地计入分子与分母误差；该代理在每个query簇的预设块预算下指导query-key块选择；被选块进行精确token级计算，其余块在共享softmax归一化下以质心方式贡献。

Result: 在五个vDiT上，NOVA在稀疏注意力方法中取得最佳PSNR、SSIM与LPIPS，PSNR最高比最强基线提升1.55 dB，在H100 GPU上实现最高1.82×端到端加速。

Conclusion: 以输出保真度（分子-分母联合误差）而非注意力权重幅值作为块选择依据，是提升视频扩散稀疏注意力效率-质量权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (vDiTs) have become the main backbone for video generation. The quadratic cost of self-attention limits their efficiency at high resolutions and long durations. Existing sparse attention methods often rely on preserving high attention weights. However, these criteria do not fully capture the fidelity of the final value-weighted output, whose error depends jointly on changes to the attention numerator and the softmax denominator.
  We propose NOVA, a coarse-to-fine sparse attention framework that first approximates the full normalized output using size-weighted key/value centroids. Building on this estimate, we construct a residual proxy that accounts for coupled numerator-denominator errors by centering value contributions around the estimated output. This proxy guides the selection of query-key blocks for refinement under a predefined block budget for each query cluster. Selected blocks are evaluated through exact token-level computation, while the remaining blocks contribute through their centroids under a shared softmax normalization. On five vDiTs, NOVA achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among sparse attention methods, improving PSNR by up to 1.55\,dB over the strongest baseline, while delivering up to 1.82$\times$ end-to-end speedup on an H100 GPU.

</details>


### [31] [Is It the Agent or the Retry? Separating Selection from Agency in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28116)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将"单工具拒绝重试"(SRR)基线用于审计四个 agentic 文生图系统，发现其宣称的大部分增益可被简单的"最强生成器+现成验证器+预算内重试"复现；剩余差异主要来自验证器似然比上限与路由带来的算力节省，而非真实精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic 文生图系统（规划、路由、验证、精炼）报告的组合作图基准增益几乎总是相对于"单次采样"这一弱基线，缺乏与同等预算下更强简单基线的对照。作者希望检验：这些增益中有多少能在同等预算、更强的单工具基线下存活，并区分真实 agentic 收益与基线选择造成的假象。

Method: 提出 SRR（Single-tool Rejection Retry）基线：使用系统池中最强生成器、现成验证器、在系统自身生成预算内重试至首次接受。用官方评估器审计 OctoT2I、GEMS、GenPilot 等四个系统，并在非针对基准调优的生成器与 T2I-CompBench++ 上复现。辅以闭式分析预测任意预算下 SRR 精度、推导验证器似然比上界、证明跨生成器路由的精度增益上限，并进行离线蒸馏实验与信息论解释。

Result: GenEval 上 SRR 与 OctoT2I 精度相当（0.962 vs. 0.96），恢复 GEMS 增益的 63%–90%（依验证器而定），以 2.3 次生成/图超越 GenPilot 12 点；效应在非调优生成器与 T2I-CompBench++ 上持续。闭式分析可精确预测各预算下 SRR 精度，且其增益受验证器似然比封顶。反例方面：GenEval 2 上即使由评估器从五个样本中选最优，仍比 GEMS 报告值低 15 点（95% 区间上端低 12 点），表明存在真实 agentic 增益。路由精度增益被证明有上限，在 OctoT2I 自身池内为 1–3 点；OctoT2I 的路由换来的是算力（等精度下约为 SRR 的 1/2.5），而非精度。离线蒸馏中，被选样本与随机样本对生成器的教学效果无差异，可由简短信息论论证解释。

Conclusion: 多数 agentic 文生图系统的表观增益可被同等预算下的简单拒绝重试基线复现，真正稳健的 agentic 收益仅出现在更强基准（如 GenEval 2）上；路由的价值在于算力效率而非精度，选择样本的蒸馏并不比随机样本更有效。作者据此提出 agentic 生成论文的报告清单（reporting checklist）。

Abstract: Agentic text-to-image systems wrap generators in loops of planning, routing, verification and refinement, and report large gains on compositional benchmarks, almost always relative to a single sample. We ask how much of the gain survives against Single-tool Rejection Retry (\ours): the strongest generator already in the system's pool, an off-the-shelf verifier, and retries that stop at the first accepted image under the system's own budget. Auditing four recent systems with official evaluators, we find that on GenEval SRR matches the accuracy of OctoT2I (0.962 vs.\ 0.96), recovers 63 to 90% of the gain of GEMS depending on the verifier, and exceeds GenPilot by 12 points with 2.3 generations per image; the effect persists with generators not tuned to the benchmark and on T2I-CompBench++. A closed-form analysis predicts the accuracy of SRR at every budget and shows that its gain is capped by the verifier's likelihood ratio. The same analysis also exposes genuine agentic gains: on GenEval 2, even the best of five samples chosen by the evaluator stays 15 points below the score GEMS reports (12 at the upper end of its 95% interval). We prove a ceiling on the accuracy that routing across generators can add, bracket it between one and three points on OctoT2I's own pool, and show that what OctoT2I's routing buys is compute (about 2.5
  less than SRR at equal accuracy), not accuracy. In our offline distillation experiments selected samples teach the generator no more than random ones, which a short information argument explains. We end with a reporting checklist for agentic generation papers.

</details>


### [32] [PhoneQC: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Order-Level Smartphone Trade-in Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28117)
*Zihan Deng,Xinpei Lin,Du Tiantian,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhoneQC——一个含335个订单（以 iPhone 为主）的基准，用0–100的表单分数（Score100）在匹配协议下（仅图像 / 图像+检测器 sidecar）对系统排序，并声明一个不被当作同侪排名的上限；同时以字段级 LoRA 作为案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机以旧换新的远程评估中，卖家已上传六视角图像和诊断屏，容易被误认为模型可替代人工报价。但报价是员工表单，模型外观填写率仅约0.25，闭源模型略高的分数只体现在其"愿意作答"的订单上，人工评估仍不可少。现有单视角缺陷集与属性基准无法在固定输入契约下评估订单级完整表单，框或泄漏字段会被误当作完成的报价；且表单中未被读取的员工字段，模型在仅输出框或描述时学不到。

Method: 构建 PhoneQC：335订单、iPhone 占比高的数据切分，按 Score100（0–100 表单分数）在匹配协议内排名，并声明非排名的分数上限。案例研究采用 field-only LoRA：冻结主干、仅适配 attention 投影层、监督员工字段同时保持缺陷列表为空；适配器与零样本模型看到相同图像。

Result: 在仅图像的 T1 协议下，3B 模型分数从 26.6 提升至 33.3，覆盖率 0.928；去掉空 blocker 项后，开放排名仍倾向适配器；但与人工作业之间的差距在该切分上依然很大。

Conclusion: 订单级表单评分需要在受控协议下评估，单视角缺陷或属性指标不足以代表完整报价；轻量字段适配可带来可测增益，但远不能替代人工鉴定，人机差距仍显著。

Abstract: As vision-language models enter remote smartphone trade-in, sellers already upload six views and a diagnostic screen, which makes it easy to treat the human quote as a step a model can take over. A model may mark a crack or read the screen, yet the quote is a staff form: appearance fill stays near 0.25, and the closed models that score a little higher do so only on the orders they answer, so human appraisal remains necessary. Two obstacles stand in the way. (1) Single-view defect sets and attribute benchmarks do not score an order-level form under a fixed input contract, so a box or a leaked field can be mistaken for a finished quote. (2) The unread half of the form is the staff fields, and a model left to emit boxes or captions does not learn them. We present PhoneQC, a 335-order iPhone-heavy cut that ranks systems by a 0 to 100 form score (Score100) only inside matched protocols (images alone; images plus a detector sidecar), with a declared upper bound that is not ranked as a peer. As a case study on that board, field-only LoRA freezes the backbone, adapts attention projections, and supervises staff fields while leaving the defect list empty; the adapter sees the same images as the zero-shot model. On image-only T1 the 3B model rises from 26.6 to 33.3 at coverage 0.928; without the empty-blocker item the open ranking still favors the adapter, while the remaining gap to a person stays large on this cut.

</details>


### [33] [StateBind: Exposing the Symbol-to-State Binding Gap in Visual Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28118)
*Qing Mu,Jiani Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StateBind-200基准与Factorial StateBind验证器，将数值视觉测量中"符号→物理状态"的绑定识别为独立可靠性瓶颈，并给出可解释的高精度证据门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠视觉系统应把数值主张与可见证据对应起来；视觉测量中需将候选数值映射到图像特定的物理状态（如标尺刻度位置），但现有评估未区分"定位"与"绑定"两类失败。

Method: 构建StateBind-200（200张真实图像+人工精修局部几何，配套Numeric/Calibrated/Physicalized三种接口）；在128张留出集、6696次受控调用上比较接口效果并做校准与推理消融；另做40张几何审计与分歧敏感性分析；提出免训练选择性验证器Factorial StateBind：两个VLM分别提出target与lattice分量，独立VLM验证证据，有效组合确定性求解，精度模式在69张开发集上融合分量有效性、四方一致性与状态分离度，原样迁移至留出集。

Result: 物理化相同候选值使三模型多数投票准确率由75.54%升至91.13%（源图像均衡增益+15.30点）；校准与额外推理未能弥合大部分差距；目标定位可在值-格绑定错误时仍成功，双端点标尺跨度的负担更大；几何审计中位目标偏差0.08 tick且在分歧敏感性下主效应保持；验证器达91.67%候选精度，覆盖36/128张图像（28.12%）与9.68%有序单元格。

Conclusion: 实例特定的符号-状态绑定是区别于定位的独立可靠性瓶颈；所提供的可解释证据门控适用于数值视觉主张，可高精度但覆盖率有限地筛选可信候选。

Abstract: Reliable vision systems should connect numerical claims to visible evidence. In visual measurement, this requires mapping candidate values to image-specific physical states. StateBind-200 augments 200 real images with human-refined local geometry and matched Numeric, Calibrated, and Physicalized interfaces. Across a 128-image held-out evaluation and 6,696 controlled calls, physicalizing identical candidates raises three-model majority accuracy from 75.54% to 91.13%, a source-image-balanced gain of +15.30 points. Calibration and additional reasoning leave most of the gap intact; target localization can succeed despite incorrect value-lattice binding, with a larger burden for two-endpoint ruler spans. An independent 40-image geometry audit yields a 0.08-tick median target discrepancy and preserves the main effect under disagreement sensitivity.
  We further propose Factorial StateBind, a training-free selective verifier. Two VLMs propose target and lattice components, an independent VLM validates their evidence, and valid combinations are resolved deterministically. A precision mode selected on 69 development images combines component validity, four-way agreement, and state separation. Applied unchanged to held-out evaluation, this high-precision evidence gate reaches 91.67% candidate precision across 36/128 images (28.12%) and 9.68% of ordered cells. The results identify instance-specific symbol-to-state binding as a distinct reliability bottleneck and provide an interpretable evidence gate for numerical visual claims.

</details>


### [34] [Seeing Beyond Pixels: Radar Feature Generation from Images and Sparse Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28119)
*Hongyu Zhou,Yubo Peng,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VRTG，利用视觉信息为稀疏雷达数据生成任务相关的雷达token，在每帧仅4个雷达点下接近全雷达融合模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-雷达融合虽兼具视觉与几何互补优势，但通常依赖完整雷达表征，带来显著计算开销；视觉信息能在多大程度上补偿有限的雷达测量尚缺乏充分探索。

Method: VRTG分三步：(1) 利用图像目标线索筛选少量有信息量的雷达点；(2) 建立视觉目标与雷达回波之间经标定的一对一关联，用匹配测量修正目标距离，对未匹配目标保留视觉估计；(3) 将关联后的目标特征与视觉上下文转换为与现有相机-雷达融合网络兼容的雷达token。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上，每帧仅用4个雷达点即达到90.76%车辆检测F1与86.68%自由空间mIoU，接近全雷达模型性能。

Conclusion: 视觉信息可有效补偿稀疏雷达测量，VRTG以极少雷达点实现接近完整雷达表征的融合效果，显著降低计算开销。
