Conclusion: DMD梯度的运动—布局耦合与信号缺失是少步视频蒸馏质量差、成本高的关键症结；仅通过针对性的目标偏移式训练期修正即可在不改动损失的前提下显著提升运动质量并大幅降低训练成本，且方案具备跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) enables fast video generation by distilling a multi-step teacher into a few-step student. However, DMD-based methods often suffer from unnatural motion and costly training. We trace these limitations to the DMD gradient, which jointly optimizes appearance, layout, and motion without distinguishing their learning dynamics. Its static component, which carries appearance and layout, has a much higher signal-to-noise ratio than its motion component. As a result, per-frame quality converges within a few hundred iterations, whereas motion lags far behind. Beyond being noisy, the gradient also lacks two signals. (1) Video VAEs encode several frames into one latent frame (four in Wan2.1), and the gradient provides little guidance for spreading motion over these frames, causing periodic stutter. (2) DMD does not constrain the final sampling step to preserve the layout set by earlier steps, so objects merge and drift once the video moves. We propose HorizonFlow, which decouples the correction of motion from that of layout. It keeps the DMD loss and only shifts its regression target, with one training-only correction in the motion component and one in the orthogonal layout component. Chunk Evenness addresses (1) by spreading the motion of each latent chunk evenly over its frames, and Semantic Commitment addresses (2) by anchoring the coarse layout to the teacher's completion of the student's last step. In only 600 iterations, HorizonFlow distills Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B into a 4-step model using 8.4k training samples, 4.6-304$\times$ fewer than prior work. On VBench, HorizonFlow outperforms all released few-step Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B models. The same recipe transfers to Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B, where our student outperforms NVIDIA's distilled model. Code: https://github.com/XIANGLONGGUO/HorizonFlow-repo

</details>


### [203] [Ride on Anything: Contact-Coupled Monocular 4D Reconstruction of a Rider and Horse in Camera and World Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29435)
*Zehang Weng,Ci Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoA：免训练方法，在测试时将冻结的人体与马匹估计器耦合，联合优化有界修正，从单目视频重建骑手骑马的 3D 网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立应用人体估计器（SAM 3D Body）与马匹估计器（4DEquine）时，两者虽各自贴合轮廓，但骑手会悬浮于马旁、穿透马体、沿马背滑动并相对抖动；这些误差沿相机轴向分布，在输入视角下不可见，因此需要显式建模人-动物交互约束。

Method: 提出通用的免训练框架 Ride on Anything（RoA）：将两估计器对齐到统一帧索引；从马匹估计器初始化单一像素各向同性相机，将两个主体投射至同一相机；在马体坐标系下定义交互项（含基于广义环绕数的碰撞项，适用于非水密马网格），联合优化有界修正；再借助背景估计的相机旋转与 MoGe-2 的视场角将结果提升至水平世界坐标系。以骑马为实例（SAM 3D Body + 4DEquine）。

Result: 骑手骨盆与马背的平均间距由 15 cm 降至 1 cm；座位沿脊柱方向与横向漂移分别降低 64% 与 89%；90 分位穿透深度降低 57%；骑手表面位于马体内的比例由 12.1% 降至 7.9%；相对加速度降低 94%、马匹 jerk 降低 80%，运动显著更平滑；代价是骑手轮廓 IoU 从 0.59 降至 0.52。定性上骑手稳定乘坐并随马同步运动。

Conclusion: RoA 以训练无关方式有效消除沿相机轴的单目交互歧义，显著改善座位贴合、穿透与相对运动稳定性。局限：尚缺乏校准录制数据的三维精度定量评估；方法依赖已拟合估计器并牺牲部分轮廓精度；在任务外场景（如牵马步行）中存在失效模式，需进一步分析。

Abstract: We address the reconstruction of a horse and its rider from a single video. When a human estimator and a horse estimator are applied independently, both meshes can fit their silhouettes well while the rider hovers beside or passes through the horse, slides along its back and jitters relative to it. These errors lie along the camera axis and are not visible in the input view. We present Ride on Anything (RoA), a general, training-free methodology for reconstructing a person riding an animal, which couples frozen estimators of the rider and the mount at test time. We instantiate it for horseback riding with SAM 3D Body and 4DEquine. RoA aligns both estimators on a common frame index, projects both subjects through one pixel-isotropic camera initialized from the horse estimator, and jointly optimizes bounded corrections under interaction terms defined in the horse's body frame, including a collision term based on generalized winding numbers that remains valid on the non-watertight horse mesh. The result is lifted to a levelled world frame using the camera rotation from the background and the field of view from MoGe-2. On our riding clips, the raw outputs of the two estimators leave the rider's pelvis 15 cm from the horse's back on average. RoA reduces this seat gap to 1 cm, the drift of the seat along and across the spine by 64% and 89%, the 90th-percentile penetration depth by 57%, and the share of rider surface inside the horse from 12.1% to 7.9%. The motion is markedly smoother (94% lower acceleration of the rider relative to the horse, 80% lower horse jerk), while the rider's silhouette IoU decreases from 0.59 to 0.52. Qualitatively, the rider stays seated on the horse and moves together with it. A quantitative evaluation of 3D accuracy awaits calibrated recordings of ridden animals. We also test a clip outside the task, a horse led on foot, and analyse the failure modes.

</details>


### [204] [Seeing Through Preferences: Recovering Hidden Local Preference Neighborhoods for Text-to-Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29436)
*Pulkit Bansal,Vivek Srivastava,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrefLens 从稀疏的人类偏好标注中恢复"局部偏好邻域"：在人类偏好图像周围生成保留语义身份的受控感知变体，并借助预训练奖励模型诱导的偏好空间筛选出紧凑的信息性邻居，从而把每条偏好标注转化为一组结构化比较，在不增加人工标注的前提下丰富监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I 扩散模型对齐人类偏好通常依赖大规模成对标注，成本高昂且监督稀疏；同时并非所有偏好比较对学习贡献相同——语义差异大的图像对比往往难以揭示细粒度感知因素。作者认为真正有信息量的监督来自人类偏好图像周围的局部偏好邻域，在那里语义对齐的替代样本能暴露有意义的感知与结构差异。

Method: 提出 PrefLens 框架：(1) 在人类偏好图像周围生成受控感知变体，使其大体保留语义身份；(2) 在预训练奖励模型诱导的、由奖励定义的偏好空间中，筛选出紧凑的信息性邻居集合；(3) 将每条人类偏好标注转化为结构化的邻域比较集合，实现监督扩充而无需额外人工标注。

Result: 实验与诊断性评估表明，恢复出的邻域能够捕获与人类相关的偏好特征，并在多种设置下为偏好对齐提供有效监督。

Conclusion: 局部偏好邻域是一种高效、可扩展的偏好监督来源；通过受控感知扰动与偏好空间筛选，可将稀疏成对标注放大为信息密集的结构化比较，提升 T2I 偏好对齐的数据效率。

Abstract: Aligning text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models with human preferences typically relies on large-scale pairwise annotations, which are expensive to obtain and provide only sparse supervision. Moreover, not all preference comparisons contribute equally to learning: comparisons between semantically distant images often reveal little about the fine-grained perceptual factors underlying human judgments. We argue that informative supervision can arise from local preference neighborhoods surrounding human-preferred images, where semantically aligned alternatives expose meaningful perceptual and structural differences. Motivated by this observation, we propose PrefLens, a framework that recovers local preference neighborhoods from sparse human feedback. Specifically, PrefLens generates controlled perceptual variations around preferred images that largely retain semantic identity and selects compact sets of informative neighbors in a reward-defined preference space induced by pretrained reward models. By transforming each human preference annotation into a structured neighborhood of informative comparisons, PrefLens enriches supervision without requiring additional human labeling. Our experiments and diagnostic evaluations show that the recovered neighborhoods capture human-relevant preference characteristics and provide effective supervision for preference alignment across diverse settings.

</details>


### [205] [Exploring Attention-Driven Deep Learning for Accurate Lung Nodule Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29450)
*Nima Shafiei Rezvani Nezhad,Meysam Mansouri,Saeid Ghayour,Ruhollah Abolhasani,Shahla Azizi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在LIDC-IDRI数据集上，仅用356个筛选样本训练多种深度学习模型进行肺结节分割，其中DPL nkNet50（通道注意力桥+ResNet主干）取得最高Dice 0.86，证明了数据高效利用与定制化架构的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肺结节分割研究通常使用完整数据集训练，数据冗余且计算成本高；作者希望验证在精选小规模子集上训练也能达到稳健的分割性能，从而提升数据效率并推动临床计算机辅助诊断应用。

Method: 采用LIDC-IDRI数据集，构建356个样本的筛选子集；引入注意力机制（如Channel Attention Bridge）与ResNet主干等新型网络配置及先进预处理策略，对多种深度学习分割模型进行对比评估。

Result: DPL nkNet50（Channel Attention Bridge + ResNet主干）表现最佳，Dice分数达0.86，显著优于传统架构。

Conclusion: 数据效率与定制化架构相结合可实现鲁棒的肺结节分割性能，为临床计算机辅助诊断的改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This study explores the application of various deep learning models for the segmentation of lung nodules using LIDC-IDRI. Unlike traditional approaches that utilize the full dataset, our work emphasizes the efficacy of training models on a filtered subset of 356 samples. Novel configurations, including attention mechanisms and advanced preprocessing strategies, were employed to optimize segmentation accuracy. Among the models evaluated, the DPL nkNet50 with a Channel Attention Bridge and ResNet backbone demonstrated the highest performance with a Dice score of 0.86, significantly outperforming conventional architectures. This work underscores the potential of leveraging data efficiency and tailored architectures in achieving robust segmentation performance, paving the way for improved computer-aided diagnosis in clinical settings.

</details>


### [206] [Fill in the Blanks: Non-Autoregressive Schema-Parallel Key Information Extraction with CTC Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29460)
*Shaked Perek,Avihu Dekel,Ben Wiesel,Sivan Harary,Eli Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出非自回归结构化提取器：由schema直接写出结构、用计数通道确定列表长度、并行预测所有值，以CTC按槽块解码值，固定前向次数且不随文档长度增长。零样本在VAREX/CORD/FUNSD上比扩散VLM平均快3.5倍（最高5.6倍）且更准，仅需4–6次前向；亦可用于投机解码，比自回归快8.3倍（最高10.4倍），精度差0.4点内，比扩散投机解码快3.3倍且高13.0点。开源模型与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM逐token自回归生成JSON抽取文档结构化数据，解码步数随答案长度线性增长，效率低；而KIE任务的schema已预先确定键、嵌套与列表结构，只有值未知，这种结构先验未被利用。

Method: 非自回归提取器：直接从schema写出结构；通过一次计数通道确定每个列表的长度；并行预测所有值，前向次数固定，不随文档长度增长。每个值在其专属槽块内以CTC解码，无需预测长度；对槽块结束后仍未闭合的值分配更大token预算。同一提取器还可作为自回归基座的投机解码草稿模型。

Result: 零样本下，4B模型在VAREX、CORD、FUNSD上平均比扩散VLM竞品快3.5倍（最高5.6倍）且精度更高，每文档仅4–6次前向，而block diffusion需12–74次；投机解码中比自回归解码快8.3倍（最高10.4倍），精度下降不超过0.4点，比投机扩散解码快3.3倍且高13.0点。

Conclusion: 利用KIE的schema结构先验可显著缩短解码路径，实现固定次数的并行结构化抽取，在速度与精度上同时优于扩散VLM与自回归基线，并可作为高效的投机解码草稿器；模型与代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) extract structured data from documents by generating the requested JSON one token at a time, so their sequential steps grow with the length of the answer. Yet in key information extraction (KIE) the schema already fixes the answer's structure: keys, nesting and lists are known before decoding, and only the values are unknown. We exploit this with a non-autoregressive extractor that writes the structure from the schema, sizes every list with a count pass, and predicts all values in parallel, in a fixed number of passes that does not grow with the document. Each value is decoded with connectionist temporal classification (CTC) in its own block of slots, so its length never has to be predicted, and a value that is still open after its block gets a larger token budget. Zero-shot on VAREX, CORD and FUNSD, our 4B extractor is 3.5$\times$ faster than the diffusion-VLM competition on average (up to 5.6$\times$) and more accurate, with 4--6 forward passes per document where block diffusion needs 12--74. The same extractor also drafts for speculative decoding with its own autoregressive base: it runs 8.3$\times$ faster than autoregressive decoding (up to 10.4$\times$) within 0.4 points of its accuracy, and 3.3$\times$ faster than speculative diffusion decoding while 13.0 points more accurate. We release the model and code.

</details>


### [207] [RSWP: Robust SO(3)-Equivariant Watermarking for Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29468)
*Pengzhen Chen,Yanwei Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Jinxia Liu,Siwei Ma,Weiping Wang,Wu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSWP：基于二十面体群理论的球面全景图像鲁棒水印框架，将SO(3)旋转分解为局部平面旋转与面片置换，通过等变到不变解码实现视口提取与水印恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像水印受球面信号与平面表示间拓扑失配的阻碍：连续SO(3)视角旋转带来非线性几何畸变，视口提取通常需昂贵的逆向重映射，常规方法难以应对。

Method: 利用二十面体群I60作为SO(3)最大离散子群的性质，将球面旋转分解为可处理的局部平面旋转与组合面片置换；提出解耦架构，将水印嵌入离散面片；设计等变到不变的解码范式——先由等变特征提取器提取特征，再经置换不变排序模块同步旋转畸变特征；双提取器设计支持无需几何标定、直接从部分2D渲染图中盲提取水印。

Result: 大量实验表明，RSWP在连续SO(3)旋转与视口变换下达到最先进鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 以群论为指导的解耦嵌入与等变-不变解码可有效解决全景水印的几何同步问题，实现盲提取与高鲁棒性，为球面域水印提供理论化范式。

Abstract: Robust watermarking for panoramic imagery is impeded by the topological mismatch between spherical signals and planar representations. Conventional methods fail under $SO(3)$ viewing rotations due to non-linear geometric warping and struggle with viewport extraction, which typically necessitates computationally expensive inverse remapping. To address these challenges, we propose RSWP, a watermarking framework grounded in icosahedral group theory. Leveraging the property that the icosahedral group ($\mathcal{I}_{60}$) serves as the largest discrete subgroup of $SO(3)$, we decompose spherical rotations into tractable local planar rotations and combinatorial facet permutations. Accordingly, we introduce a decoupling architecture that embeds watermarks into discrete facets and a theoretically guaranteed Equivariance-to-Invariance decoding paradigm. By employing an equivariant feature extractor followed by a permutation-invariant sorting module, this paradigm rigorously synchronizes rotation-distorted features. Moreover, the dual-extractor design inherently supports direct, blind watermark retrieval from partial 2D renderings without geometric calibration. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RSWP achieves state-of-the-art robustness against continuous $SO(3)$ rotations and viewport transformations while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [208] [Separating Action Allocation from Image Matching in Visual Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29477)
*Chenxi Wang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过固定各任务内的动作次数、并将路由器的分配与"同一动作-图像集合的随机配对"对比，把视觉token路由的平均分增益分解为"匹配质量"与"动作频率"两部分；结论是当前自适应路由器的主要瓶颈在动作频率的选择而非匹配本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应视觉token路由器以节省计算为目标，同时需保留回答所需图像细节。但平均分提升可能来自两方面：(1) 对单张图像做出更优的分辨率/token选择（匹配更好）；(2) 在需要高分辨率的任务上更频繁地启用高分辨率（动作频率偏移）。已有评测无法区分二者，因而难以判断路由器真正的能力来源与改进方向。

Method: 提出受控分解评测：在每个任务内固定动作次数（保持各分辨率/动作的使用频次不变），仅将路由器的"动作—图像"分配与同分布下的随机配对进行比较，从而隔离出纯匹配效应；剩余差异归因于动作频率。实验在等权重的ChartQA与TextVQA上进行，模型为Qwen2.5-VL-3B与-7B，并与外部选择器CARES所选的连续分辨率进行交换实验，另比较带任务标签时的任务查找表与离散路由器的表现（精度与延迟）。

Result: 在等权重ChartQA+TextVQA上，3B与7B均从匹配中获益，但相对任务查找表因动作频率而损失更多：7B上匹配带来+1.71分，频率带来−3.95分。交换CARES所选连续分辨率后，7B质量同样下降。当请求自带任务标签时，任务查找表比离散路由器高1.92分，并在7B上平均每完整请求节省31.76 ms。

Conclusion: 自适应路由器的平均分增益主要不来自更精细的逐图匹配，而受制于次优的动作频率分配；简单任务查找表在具备任务标签时反而更优、更快。因此未来路由器设计应重点优化各任务的动作预算/频率决策，而非仅提升匹配粒度；同时，连续分辨率选择（如CARES）未必带来质量收益。

Abstract: Adaptive visual token routers aim to save computation while preserving the image detail needed to answer each question. A higher average score could reflect better choices for individual images or more frequent use of high resolution on tasks that need it. We distinguish the two by keeping action counts fixed within each task and comparing the router's assignments with random pairings of the same actions and images. On equally weighted ChartQA and TextVQA, both Qwen2.5-VL-3B and -7B gain from matching but lose more from their action frequencies relative to a task table, with a 1.71-point gain and a 3.95-point loss on 7B. Exchanging the continuous resolutions chosen by the external selector CARES also lowers quality on 7B. When requests arrive with task labels, the table outscores the discrete router by 1.92 points and saves 31.76 ms per complete 7B request on average.

</details>


### [209] [Neutron Imaging Enhancement for Lithium Batteries Based on Swin-Transformer and Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29479)
*Hansheng Ding,Fanxu Meng,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出改进Swin-Transformer结合空域融合的锂电池中子图像增强方法，在去噪、边缘与对比度提升上优于BM3D/DnCNN/GAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子成像对锂等轻元素敏感，适于锂电池无损检测与内部结构演化分析，但受中子源通量低与探测器性能限制，图像存在混合噪声、低对比度和边缘模糊，影响电池内部状态的准确评估。

Method: 三步流程：①改进Swin-Transformer，引入MDTA（多深度卷积头转置注意力）与GRN（全局响应归一化）进行特征提取与重建，抑制高密度白斑及高斯-泊松混合噪声；②引导滤波结合梯度自适应掩膜实现选择性边缘增强，CLAHE提升局部对比度；③基于空间频率（SF）的局部块加权融合机制，自适应融合边缘增强图与对比度均衡图。

Result: 与BM3D、DnCNN、GAN对比，在全参考与无参考评价上均表现良好：最佳PSNR 33.0517 dB、SSIM 0.9637，NR-IQA 0.1794、NIQE 4.0974。

Conclusion: 该方法减轻伪影与过度平滑，提升锂电池中子图像的视觉保真度与结构可辨识性。

Abstract: Neutron imaging is highly sensitive to light elements such as lithium and is therefore valuable for non-destructive testing (NDT) and analyzing internal structural evolution in lithium batteries. However, limited neutron-source flux and detector performance often result in images with mixed noise, low contrast, and blurred edges, hindering accurate assessment of battery internal states. To address these issues, this paper proposes a lithium-battery neutron image enhancement method combining an improved Swin-Transformer with spatial-domain image fusion. First, an improved Swin-Transformer incorporating Multi-Dconv Head Transposed Attention (MDTA) and Global Response Normalization (GRN) extracts and reconstructs image features to suppress high-density white spots and Gaussian-Poisson mixed noise. Second, guided filtering and a gradient-adaptive mask are used for selective edge enhancement, while Contrast Limited Adaptive Histogram Equalization (CLAHE) improves local contrast. Finally, a spatial-frequency (SF)-based local block-weighted fusion mechanism adaptively combines the edge-enhanced and contrast-equalized images to preserve microscopic details while suppressing noise. Experiments show that, compared with BM3D, DnCNN, and GAN, the proposed method achieves strong performance in full-reference and no-reference evaluations. Its best PSNR and SSIM reach 33.0517 dB and 0.9637, respectively, while the NR-IQA and Natural Image Quality Evaluator (NIQE) scores are as low as 0.1794 and 4.0974. The method reduces artifacts and over smoothing, improving the visual fidelity and structural identifiability of lithium-battery neutron images.

</details>


### [210] [CAR: Reducing Decoder Output Shift in Visual Token Pruning via Coverage Selection and Attention Reallocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29489)
*Xixi Jiang,Pingcheng Dong,Chen Yang,Xin Yang,Kwang-Ting Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAR视觉token剪枝框架，通过覆盖选择与注意力重分配分别降低图像摘要误差与注意力分配误差，在90%剪枝率下显著优于现有方法，尤其在视觉定位任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在激进剪枝下性能严重下降，尤其是视觉定位（VG）任务。作者从剪枝引起解码器层输出偏移的角度分析退化来源。

Method: 将解码器输出偏移闭式分解为两项：图像摘要误差（视觉信息丢失）与注意力分配误差（视觉token注意力占比下降）。据此提出CAR：(1) 覆盖选择——保留兼具显著性与非冗余性的token，显著性由无标签探针选择的编码器头给出，以收紧摘要误差上界；(2) 注意力重分配——通过闭式logit偏置将丢弃token的注意力重新分配给保留token。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上剪除90%视觉token时，CAR保留VQA准确率93.0%、VG准确率77.9%，而最佳现有方法在VG上仅26.5%；仅logit偏置即可使现有方法VG准确率提升最多24个点。多模型上VQA、VG与视频理解实验均显示良好泛化与一致优势。

Conclusion: 通过闭式误差分解指导的覆盖选择与注意力重分配可有效缓解激进剪枝下的性能退化，机制通用性强、可跨模型与任务泛化，且注意力重分配模块可作为插件提升既有方法。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is an effective way to accelerate multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing methods degrade substantially under aggressive pruning, especially on visual grounding (VG). We analyze the sources of this degradation from the perspective of the output shift that pruning causes in decoder layers, and decompose this shift in closed form into two terms, an image summary error and an attention allocation error. The summary error stems from the loss of visual information, and the allocation error from the drop in the share of attention assigned to visual tokens. To reduce both errors, we propose CAR (Coverage selection and Attention Reallocation), a token pruning framework that restores attention share while maintaining information. Specifically, to minimize the summary error, whose upper bound tightens as the retained tokens better cover all visual tokens, our coverage selection retains tokens that are both salient and non-redundant, with saliency from encoder heads chosen by a label-free probe. To minimize the allocation error, our attention reallocation reassigns the attention of the discarded tokens to the retained ones through a closed-form logit bias. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B with $90\%$ of the visual tokens pruned, CAR retains $93.0\%$ of the unpruned accuracy on visual question answering (VQA) and $77.9\%$ on VG, against $26.5\%$ for the best existing method on VG; the logit bias alone raises the VG accuracy of existing methods by up to $24$ points. Experiments on multiple MLLMs across VQA, VG and video understanding show that CAR generalizes well and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [211] [STAGE: Structured Test-Time Adaptation with Global Entropic Soft-coupling for Cross-Subject Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29538)
*Yihang Zou,Ruixi Liang,Chunmei Qing,Junpeng Tan,Haichuan Wei,Wanxiang Luo,Gao Wu,Haodong Li,Xiangmin Xu,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAGE，一个基于结构化测试时自适应的跨被试脑-图像检索框架，利用无标签目标批次的全局熵软耦合排序，缓解被试间差异与查询排序过度集中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑到图像检索需在未见被试的脑信号上识别所观看的图像。被试间变异性阻碍了脑-图像表征的对齐；同时独立查询排序易将预测集中在少数候选上，在无目标标签条件下难以实现准确检索。

Method: STAGE包含四个组件：(1) 矩校准减少特征间统计差异；(2) 耦合加权初始化建立初始对齐；(3) 耦合引导自适应进一步修正残余特征失配；(4) 软耦合排序通过全局熵协调各查询间的候选竞争，避免匹配过度集中。方法仅使用无标签目标批次，冻结两个编码器。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG2上达到68.25% Top-1与90.47% Top-5，在相同冻结NICE特征下相比SCORE恢复加CSLS提升Top-1 18.52个百分点；在200个查询与200张图像组成的图库上校准后，冻结变换条件下对1,854张图像排序仍保持42.05% Top-1。三个公开数据集验证了检索性能。

Conclusion: 利用无标签目标批次的结构化测试时自适应可在冻结编码器条件下从特征校准贯穿到图像检索保持一致匹配，有效恢复跨被试对齐并显著提升检索精度与泛化稳定性。

Abstract: Cross-subject brain-to-image retrieval aims to identify viewed images from unseen subjects' brain signals. However, inter-subject variability hinders alignment of brain-image representations, while independent query ranking tends to concentrate predictions on a few candidates, making accurate retrieval difficult without target labels. We propose STAGE (Structured Test-Time Adaptation with Global Entropic Soft-coupling), a structured test-time adaptation framework for recovering cross-subject alignment from uncertain brain-image correspondences. Moment calibration reduces statistical discrepancies between features, while coupling-weighted initialization establishes initial alignment. Coupling-guided adaptation further refines residual feature mismatches, and soft-coupling ranking coordinates candidate competition across queries to prevent overly concentrated matching assignments. Using only unlabeled target batches, STAGE maintains consistent matching from feature calibration to image retrieval while keeping both encoders frozen. Experiments on three public datasets validate STAGE's retrieval performance. On THINGS-EEG2, STAGE achieves 68.25% Top-1 and 90.47% Top-5, improving Top-1 by 18.52 percentage points over SCORE recovery and CSLS on identical frozen NICE features. Calibrated on 200 queries and a 200-image gallery covering their identities, STAGE retains 42.05% Top-1 when ranking 1,854 images with the transform frozen.

</details>


### [212] [Seeing vs. Knowing: Counterfactual Supervision and Correction for 3D-Aware Object Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29542)
*Jian Zahng,Zhijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实状态监督与对称反事实校正两种训练策略，使图像编辑器学会区分"照片可见外观"与"3D状态提供的未见内容"，在无需改架构或增加推理步的情况下提升空间编辑的几何一致性与内容保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对照片中物体进行旋转、平移、缩放需补全未见表面、保持可见外观并更新阴影与反射。以目标位姿渲染的重建3D资产可提供显式几何引导，但其纹理不完美会与照片冲突；而常规成对训练并未显式教会编辑器该信任哪一来源。

Method: 1) 反事实状态监督：通过隐藏面重绘、隐藏几何（重渲阴影与反射）、可见面纹理替换，构造受控的"照片—状态"冲突样本，训练编辑器对未见内容遵循3D状态、对可见外观保持照片。2) 对称反事实校正：构造状态侧与照片侧的孪生样本（分别共享照片与状态），构建指向条件不一致目标的有向隐空间扰动，并训练其向当前条件指定目标恢复。二者仅用于训练，无需架构修改或额外推理步骤。

Result: 在OBJect与SpatialEdit-Bench上取得较强的空间编辑性能与内容保持效果；受控消融实验表明源选择能力提升，且采样过程中对条件的响应性得以持续保持。

Conclusion: 显式区分并训练"何时信任照片、何时信任3D状态"，并辅以采样期的隐空间一致性校正，可有效提升基于3D状态引导的图像编辑质量，且不引入推理开销。

Abstract: Rotating, translating, or rescaling an object in a photograph requires revealing unseen surfaces, preserving visible appearance, and updating shadows and reflections. Reconstructed 3D assets, rendered at the target pose as a 3D state, provide explicit geometric guidance, but their imperfect textures may conflict with the photograph, while standard paired training does not explicitly teach the editor which source to trust. To address this, we propose counterfactual state supervision, which constructs controlled photo--state conflicts through hidden-surface repainting, hidden geometry with re-rendered shadows and reflections, and visible-surface texture replacement. This teaches the editor to follow the 3D state for unseen content while preserving photograph-visible appearance. However, learning this distinction does not ensure that it is maintained during sampling. We therefore introduce symmetric counterfactual correction, which uses state-side and photo-side twins, which share the photograph and the state respectively, to construct directed latent perturbations toward condition-inconsistent targets and train recovery toward the target specified by the current condition. Both strategies operate only during training, requiring no architectural changes or additional inference steps. Experiments on OBJect and SpatialEdit-Bench demonstrate strong spatial editing performance and content preservation, while controlled ablations show improved source selection and sustained responsiveness to conditioning during sampling.

</details>


### [213] [SCRA-CLIP: Spherical-Contextual Representation Adaptation for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29545)
*Peng Chen,Chao Huang,Wenqiang Wang,Mingbo Yang,Yannan Chen,Yunkang Cao,Wei Wang,Li Shen,Fangjun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCRA-CLIP框架，通过球面原型条件化提示与上下文偏差引导的视觉适配，联合增强异常感知文本语义与上下文敏感视觉表示，实现零样本细粒度异常检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型虽支持零样本异常检测（ZSAD），但对多样异常模式及其空间上下文建模不足，导致细粒度异常检测仍具挑战性。

Method: 1) 球面原型条件化提示（SPCP）：用von Mises–Fisher混合模型刻画正常/异常patch特征的方向分布，将状态特定原型注入文本上下文以学习更具判别力的文本表示；2) 上下文偏差引导的视觉适配（CGVA）：捕获patch特征相对多尺度邻域上下文的局部偏差，并通过自适应门控精炼patch表示。

Result: 在11个工业与医学基准上进行了大量实验，验证了SCRA-CLIP在多样未见类别与跨域场景下的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将分布感知的语义建模与上下文敏感的视觉适配相结合，可实现更具判别力的跨模态匹配，从而提升细粒度异常检测与定位性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models have facilitated zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) through transferable visual-semantic representations. However, detecting fine-grained anomalies remains challenging due to inadequate modeling of diverse anomaly patterns and their spatial context. To address these challenges, we propose SCRA-CLIP, a Spherical-Contextual Representation Adaptation framework that jointly enhances anomaly-aware textual semantics and context-sensitive visual representations. Specifically, we introduce Spherical Prototype-Conditioned Prompting (SPCP), which employs von Mises--Fisher mixtures to model the directional distributions of normal and abnormal patch features. These state-specific prototypes are incorporated into textual contexts to guide the learning of more discriminative textual representations. Furthermore, we develop Context-Deviation Guided Visual Adaptation (CGVA), which captures local feature deviations relative to multi-scale neighborhood contexts and refines patch representations through adaptive gating. By integrating distribution-aware semantic modeling with context-sensitive visual adaptation, SCRA-CLIP facilitates more discriminative cross-modal matching for fine-grained anomaly detection and localization. Extensive experiments on 11 industrial and medical benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization capability of SCRA-CLIP across diverse unseen categories and domains. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [214] [Align the Pair, Not the Sample: Representation Alignment for Faster and Better One-Step Generato](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29547)
*Mingxiao Li,Xuantuo Huang,Encheng Su,Aoran Wang,Han Zhang,Jinouwen Zhang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究一步生成器中"损失应施加于哪条训练路径"的问题，发现对齐配对重建路径优于对齐生成路径，提出加速两阶段训练的\method方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征对齐能加速扩散训练，但一步生成器存在两类输入（带干净配对的损坏真实图像、无对应真值的纯噪声样本），"损失应加在哪条路径"尚不清楚。

Method: 在Priming（重建损坏图像+最优传输匹配特征分布）框架上系统对比：对齐生成路径 vs. 用冻结DINOv2编码器对齐配对重建路径；并给出理论分析、随机同类图像对照实验及生成器内部"接口块"机制分析。

Result: 对齐生成路径在全部17种配置下均导致召回崩塌（0.45→0.15–0.25）；对齐配对路径则在所有设置下均有增益。ImageNet 256×256上，Priming epoch减半即可匹配160-epoch基线，后训练更新减少4倍即超越最终质量，标准预算下FID降至1.30 (B)、1.155 (L)；单阶段变体以115M参数达到FID 1.22。

Conclusion: 对齐目标若不含输入的实例信息，最优对齐特征类内为常数，会把样本拉向类原型而损害召回；配对路径对齐则形成承载语义的接口块并预测样本质量，据此得到的\method可同时加速两阶段训练。

Abstract: Representation alignment markedly accelerates diffusion training, but one-step generators raise a question that diffusion does not: \emph{which training path should receive the loss?} A one-step generator sees two kinds of inputs. Corrupted real images come with their clean counterpart, which provides an instance-paired target. Samples generated from pure noise have no corresponding real image, yet this is where the final model operates. We study this question on Priming, which trains one-step generators from scratch by reconstructing corrupted images and then matching feature distributions with optimal transport. Aligning the generative path, the natural choice, collapses recall ($0.45\!\to\!0.15$--$0.25$) in all 17 configurations we test, including five target constructions and loss weights down to $0.05$. Aligning the paired reconstruction path with a frozen DINOv2 encoder instead helps in every setting we test. A short analysis explains this asymmetry: when the target carries no instance information about the input, the optimal aligned feature is constant within each class, so the loss pulls samples toward class prototypes. A control confirms this: aligning the paired path to a random same-class image instead of the input's own image removes most of the gain. Inside the generator, paired-path alignment creates an interface block through which post-training routes noise, and the image semantics carried through it predict sample quality across 16 models. The resulting recipe, \method, accelerates \emph{both} training stages on ImageNet $256\!\times\!256$: it matches the 160-epoch baseline with $2\times$ fewer priming epochs, surpasses its final quality with $4\times$ fewer post-training updates, and lowers FID to 1.30 (B) and 1.155 (L) at the standard budget. A joint single-stage variant trains native one-step generators and reaches FID 1.22 with 115M parameters.

</details>


### [215] [Inspect Before You Decide: Evidence-Guided Visual Search for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29551)
*Peng Chen,Chao Huang,Yangyang Liu,Maodong Wu,Jinyi Xu,Mingbo Yang,Yunkang Cao,Fangjun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出证据引导的视觉搜索框架EVAD，使MLLM主动获取并核验细粒度视觉证据以进行工业异常检测与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM方法难以获取并核验超越全局观测的细粒度视觉证据，导致细微异常识别不可靠。

Method: 先进行全局视觉评估定位可疑区域；再用MCTS引导的探索策略，通过语义细化（细粒度检查）与空间扩展（上下文验证）两种互补操作逐步采集证据；最后对证据评估整合以支撑异常决策。

Result: 在多项工业异常检测任务上，多数评测设置达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 主动证据探索对细粒度异常理解与推理有效，可提升MLLM异常检测的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential for industrial anomaly detection and understanding. However, reliably identifying subtle anomalies remains challenging, as existing approaches often struggle to acquire and verify fine-grained visual evidence beyond global observations. To address this challenge, we propose EVAD, an evidence-guided visual search framework that enables MLLMs to actively seek and examine diagnostically informative visual evidence for reliable anomaly reasoning. Specifically, EVAD begins with a global visual assessment to identify potentially anomalous regions for further inspection. Building upon this observation, we introduce a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-guided exploration strategy that progressively acquires visual evidence through two complementary operations: semantic refinement for fine-grained inspection and spatial expansion for contextual verification. The acquired evidence is subsequently evaluated and integrated to support more informed and reliable anomaly decisions. Extensive experiments across multiple industrial anomaly detection tasks demonstrate that EVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance in most evaluated settings, highlighting the effectiveness of active evidence exploration for fine-grained anomaly understanding and reasoning. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [216] [GravHMR: Coupling SLAM and Human Mesh Recovery in a Shared Gravity Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29614)
*Hanglei Jin,Chenglu Sun,Junchen Ge,Yuchao Wang,Siqi Chen,Yuzhi Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GravHMR：无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过共享重力对齐参考耦合SLAM与HMR，并用YAOO与GATO分别修正朝向与锚定根轨迹，在EMDB-2上取得SOTA世界坐标人体运动估计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频在动态相机下恢复全局一致的3D人体运动仍困难；SLAM与人体网格恢复（HMR）虽互补，但直接结合会产生朝向不一致与轨迹累计漂移。

Method: 以重力对齐作为SLAM与HMR的共享参考，重力估计独立于HMR骨干，兼容有无显式重力视角表示的模型。Yaw-Aware Orientation Optimization（YAOO）绕共享重力轴修正人体与速度朝向以解决航向不一致；Gravity-Aware Trajectory Optimization（GATO）在修正朝向后，联合2D重投影、人体隐含的度量尺度与自身运动，将根轨迹锚定在SLAM度量世界坐标中。

Result: EMDB-2上：ViPE相机下达154.0 mm W-MPJPE100，masked DROID-SLAM下达189.8 mm；相同masked DROID-SLAM相机估计下优于TRAM（222.4 mm）；相同HMR骨干下较GVHMR（276.5 mm）降低44.3%，较GVHMR+HTD-Refine（192.4 mm）降低20.0%。

Conclusion: 以重力对齐为共同参考的解耦式耦合策略可有效消除朝向不一致与轨迹漂移，实现训练免费的全局一致人体运动恢复，并具即插即用性与骨干无关的通用性。

Abstract: Recovering globally consistent 3D human motion from monocular videos remains challenging under dynamic camera motion. Although SLAM and human mesh recovery (HMR) provide complementary camera and body estimates, directly combining them often leads to inconsistent orientations and accumulated trajectory drift. We present GravHMR, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that couples SLAM and HMR through a shared gravity-aligned reference. By allowing gravity to be estimated independently of the HMR backbone, GravHMR accommodates world-grounded HMR models with or without explicit gravity-view representations. Specifically, Yaw-Aware Orientation Optimization (YAOO) resolves heading inconsistencies by correcting body and velocity orientations about the shared gravity axis. Conditioned on these corrected orientations, Gravity-Aware Trajectory Optimization (GATO) anchors root trajectories in the metric SLAM world by jointly leveraging 2D reprojection, the metric scale implicit in the human body, and the body's own motion. On EMDB-2, GravHMR achieves state-of-the-art performance with 154.0 mm W-MPJPE100 using ViPE and 189.8 mm using masked DROID-SLAM, outperforming TRAM (222.4 mm) under the same masked DROID-SLAM camera estimates. It further reduces W-MPJPE100 by 44.3% over GVHMR (276.5 mm) and by 20.0% over GVHMR + HTD-Refine (192.4 mm) with the same HMR backbone.

</details>


### [217] [Mass Forcing: On-Policy Mass-Aware Distillation for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29618)
*Yiyu Li,Fei Wei,Yong Wang,Zaiquan Yang,Gerhard Petrus Hancke,Xiangxiang Chu,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Mass Forcing，一种概率质量感知框架，将 on-policy DMD 监督从整段视频细化到单个状态，通过密度比重加权与参考分布对齐缓解 mode collapse，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升视频保真度与动态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD 的反向 KL 目标是 mode-seeking 的，会把学生的条件视频分布坍缩到少数模态上，导致样本多样性下降、时序动态减弱与颜色过饱和。已有工作主要靠覆盖导向的学生初始化来缓解，但作者观察到这种覆盖是瞬态的：在三种初始化下，动态视频比例在后续 DMD 训练中急剧下降，说明造成退化的主因是 mode-seeking 目标本身，而非初始化。

Method: Mass Forcing 将 on-policy DMD 监督从整段视频转移到单个状态：每次更新从共享的学生生成前缀分叉出多条续写，从而可度量学生在该状态处的概率质量并对照教师。其一，通过潜空间 score integration 估计这些续写之间教师相对学生的密度比，据此重新平衡条件 DMD 更新，放大相对教师被低估的 rollout；其二，由于模态概率失配未必产生足够强的局部 score 修正，进一步将生成的 rollout 集合与由同一质量估计定义的加权参考分布对齐，形成跨 rollout 监督。

Result: 在 MovieGen 与 VBench 上的实验表明，Mass Forcing 提升了视频保真度并在整个训练过程中维持时序动态：在对比方法中取得最高 Total 分数；经过 1,000 次生成器更新后 Dynamic Degree 达 83%，而基线仅为 12–32%；且不引入额外推理时开销。

Conclusion: 视频蒸馏中的模态退化由 DMD 的 mode-seeking 目标驱动而非初始化，将监督粒度下沉到状态级、结合概率质量重加权与跨 rollout 对齐，可有效抑制 mode collapse 并保持动态性，是一种无推理开销的实用改进。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for real-time video synthesis, distilling pretrained diffusion models into few-step autoregressive generators. However, its reverse-KL objective is mode-seeking and can collapse the student's conditional video distribution onto a narrow subset of modes, leading to reduced sample diversity, diminished temporal dynamics, and color oversaturation. Existing methods mitigate this issue mainly via coverage-oriented student initialization, but we observe that such coverage is transient: across three initializations, the fraction of dynamic videos drops sharply during subsequent DMD training, indicating that the mode-seeking objective, not the initialization, drives this degradation. We propose Mass Forcing, a probability-mass-aware framework that moves on-policy DMD supervision from whole videos to individual states. Each update branches several continuations from a shared student-generated prefix, making the student's probability mass at that state measurable against the teacher. First, Mass Forcing estimates relative teacher-to-student density ratios among these continuations through latent-space score integration and uses them to rebalance conditional DMD updates, amplifying rollouts underrepresented relative to the teacher. Second, since mismatched mode probabilities do not necessarily induce strong local score corrections, it aligns generated rollout sets with a reweighted reference distribution defined by the same mass estimates, providing cross-rollout supervision. Experiments on MovieGen and VBench show that Mass Forcing improves video fidelity and sustains temporal dynamics throughout training, achieving the highest Total scores among compared methods and a Dynamic Degree of 83% after 1,000 generator updates, versus 12-32% for the baselines, with no additional inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [218] [Contamination at the Operating Point: Train-Test Identity Overlap Inflates Low-FMR Face Recognition Accuracy by an Order of Magnitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29629)
*Fu Dai,Kamal Taha,Naoufel Werghi,Maregu Assefa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出人脸验证基准中训练-测试重叠导致的评估偏差并非小且恒定的加性误差，而是随FMR下降而放大的乘性误差；在FMR=10⁻⁶下剔除污染身份会使FNMR增大13.9倍，并揭示IJB-C在FMR=10⁻⁶时仅有270万有效独立冒名比对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署的人脸验证系统工作在FMR≤10⁻⁶，但常用基准（如MS1MV3、IJB-C）与模型训练语料（WebFace42M/260M）存在身份重叠，导致报告的小FMR性能被乐观偏置。已有工作（Wu et al., 2024）将偏差量化为10折准确率的小幅加性变化（≤1.01个百分点），低估了问题严重性，作者希望重新刻画该偏差的本质与量级。

Method: (1) 使用基于零分布校准（而非手工阈值）的重叠检测器审计训练-测试重叠；(2) 在固定模型下仅移除测试集中的污染身份，对比同规模随机移除的FNMR变化，并分析冒名尾部分布与阈值移动；(3) 首次直接测量IJB-C中有效独立冒名比对数量；(4) 基于已知训练语料构建经审计零重叠的真实人脸协议，将评测分辨率推进至FMR=10⁻⁸。

Result: 以WebFace42M为参照，检测出MS1MV3中88.6%的身份、IJB-C中14.3%的受试者存在重叠（尽管WebFace260M声称已对IJB-C去重叠）；人工复核100个被标记IJB-C受试者确认69.2%，残余重叠约9.9%。剔除污染身份后，FMR=10⁻⁴时FNMR增至3.6倍，10⁻⁵时为6.3倍，10⁻⁶时为13.9倍，而等量随机移除无此效应；机制在于污染缩短了冒名尾部从而降低阈值。偏差幅度依赖训练语料，不会在模型间相互抵消，将本模型与公开基线的差距缩小了一半。IJB-C在FMR=10⁻⁶下仅约270万有效独立比对，低于所有常用报告规则要求。

Conclusion: 训练-测试重叠引致的评估偏差是随FMR降低而放大的乘性误差，低FMR下的报告性能被严重高估；常用基准（含IJB-C）在FMR=10⁻⁶的统计分辨率不足。作者呼吁采用零重叠审计协议并在FMR=10⁻⁸级别评测，以得到可信的低误匹配率结论。

Abstract: Face verification systems are deployed by selecting a decision threshold that achieves a target false match rate (FMR); performance is then reported as the corresponding false non-match rate (FNMR). In practice, deployed systems operate at FMR $\le 10^{-6}$, but widely used benchmarks can share identities with the models' training corpora. Prior work (Wu et al., 2024) measures the resulting optimistic bias as a small additive change in 10-fold accuracy (up to 1.01 percentage points). We show that the bias is neither small nor constant: it is a multiplicative error that grows as FMR falls. First, we audit train-test overlap with a detector calibrated against a null distribution (rather than a hand-tuned threshold). Relative to WebFace42M, we flag 88.6% of MS1MV3 identities and 14.3% of IJB-C subjects as present, despite WebFace260M's published de-overlap explicitly naming IJB-C. Manual review of 100 flagged IJB-C subjects confirms 69.2%, implying a residual overlap of roughly 9.9%. Second, holding the model fixed, removing only the contaminated identities from the test set increases FNMR by 3.6$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-4}$, 6.3$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-5}$, and 13.9$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-6}$, while a same-size random removal does not. The effect is traced to a shorter impostor tail that lowers the threshold. The magnitude of the shift depends on the training corpus and does not cancel betweent margin between our model and a publicbaseline by half. Third, we give the first direct measurement of the effective number of independent impostor comparisons in IJB-C: only 2.7 million effectively independent comparisons at FMR=$10^{-6}$, which is below all commonly accepted reporting rules. Finallyth a known training corpus, we build areal-face protocol with audited zero overlap that resolves FMR=$10^{-8}$.

</details>


### [219] [SuperFine3D: Efficient Global-Local Recursive Refinement for Extremely Detailed 3D Geometry Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29630)
*Lihan Li,Junwei Zhou,Yu-wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SuperFine3D通过全局-局部递归细化，用仅在512³–1024³表示上训练的共享细化算子，生成4096³解码器网格分辨率的高细节3D几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像蕴含丰富细节，需精细几何结构才能忠实重建；但从图像生成极细节3D几何需要大量算力与训练数据，现有SOTA模型难以实现高分辨率几何生成。

Method: 从粗到细配对表示中学习共享细化算子并递归复用以合成超出训练分辨率的更细几何（目标分辨率无需额外配对监督）；提出空间单元注意力将高分辨率交互限制在局部邻域并保持相邻单元间有界通信；提出空间锚定的跨尺度引导，用全局上下文化的粗几何引导局部细节合成并保持结构一致性；辅以几何感知训练目标。

Result: 可在4096³解码器网格（对应稀疏256³潜在格）生成几何，共享算子仅用512³–1024³表示训练；相比全注意力递归基线，端到端推理9.4倍加速、峰值GPU显存降低23.0%，跨尺度结构一致性提升，表面更干净且几何细节更精细。

Conclusion: 递归复用共享细化算子配合局部注意力与跨尺度引导，可在无需目标分辨率配对监督的情况下高效实现超高分辨率3D几何生成。

Abstract: High-resolution images contain rich visual details that require fine geometric structures for faithful 3D reconstruction. However, generating extremely detailed 3D geometry from images requires substantial computational resources and training data, making high-resolution geometry generation challenging for existing state-of-the-art models. In this paper, we present SuperFine3D, an efficient framework for generating extremely detailed 3D geometry through global-local recursive refinement. SuperFine3D learns a shared refinement operator from paired coarse-to-fine representations and recursively reuses it to progressively synthesize finer geometry beyond the training resolutions, without requiring additional paired supervision at target resolutions. To make recursive refinement computationally efficient, we introduce spatial-cell attention, which restricts high-resolution interactions to local spatial neighborhoods while maintaining bounded communication across neighboring cells. We further propose spatially anchored cross-scale guidance, which leverages globally contextualized coarse geometry to guide local detail synthesis and preserve structural consistency. In addition, geometry-aware training objectives encourage the refinement process to preserve stable geometric structures while introducing fine-grained surface details. Experiments demonstrate that SuperFine3D generates geometry at a $4096^3$ decoder-grid resolution, corresponding to a sparse $256^3$ latent lattice, using a shared refinement operator trained only on $512^3$--$1024^3$ representations. Compared with a full-attention recursive baseline, SuperFine3D achieves a $9.4\times$ speedup in end-to-end inference and reduces peak GPU memory usage by $23.0\%$, while improving cross-scale structural consistency and producing cleaner surfaces with finer geometric details.

</details>


### [220] [PROPS: Can Visual Generators Keep the Current Look of a Prop?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29665)
*Xiangzhao Hao,Dianyi Wang,Keyan Zhou,Yiqian Zhang,Zefeng Zhang,Bailin Li,Kun Zhan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PROPS 基准（2,257 对"双生历史"），用于隔离并度量生成模型在视觉历史生成任务中的"道具漂移"（prop drift）失败；全部 34 个被测系统均存在该问题，并发布 PROPS-Train 训练集以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故事板、插图叙事、多轮编辑等生成任务要求从视觉历史中取出物体的"当前外观"，但新请求往往只描述"接下来发生什么"而不重述物体样子。生成器通常仅凭请求重绘物体或沿用过期外观，且误差会向后续步骤累积（prop drift）。现有评测无法隔离该失败：与历史相似度会奖励"照抄"，而只看单条历史的评判者无法区分"从历史中恢复的外观"与"生成器自身偏好的外观"。

Method: 构建 PROPS 基准：2,257 对孪生历史，两条历史以相同请求结尾，但其中一个道具呈现两种不同外观；一对样本须两侧各自画出匹配外观并执行请求才算通过，因此生成器或评判者偏好的外观至多只能通过一侧。进一步分析 agent 的 planner 与 editor 互换，并构建 PROPS-Train（80,250 条训练链，四条轨迹，目标为恢复早期状态或延续脚本化变化）用于微调。

Result: 34 个开源与闭源系统全部出现 prop drift；即使多数样本能正确执行动作的系统，也常丢失道具的当前外观。planner/editor 互换表明历史理解与图像生成共同限制 agent 表现。在 PROPS-Train 上微调可明显提升全部 4 个被测模型。

Conclusion: prop drift 是视觉历史生成中普遍且被现有评测掩盖的失败模式；PROPS 通过对偶设计实现了无偏、可判别的外观一致性评测，PROPS-Train 证明该问题可通过训练缓解。两个数据集将公开。

Abstract: Many generation tasks draw the next image from a visual history, such as storyboards, illustrated stories and multi-turn editing. A new request often states what happens next without restating how earlier objects look. Instead of recovering an object's current look from the history, generators often redraw it from the request alone or bring back an outdated look, and the error can carry into later steps. We call this failure prop drift. Existing evaluations rarely isolate it, because similarity to the history rewards copying and a judge that sees a single history cannot tell a look recovered from that history from one the generator happens to prefer. We introduce PROPS, a benchmark of 2,257 pairs of twin histories that end on the same request but show one prop in two different looks. A pair passes only if the generator draws the matching look on each side and carries out the request, so a look preferred by the generator or the judge passes at most one side. All 34 open and closed systems we test show prop drift, and even systems that carry out the requested action on most sides often lose the current look of the prop. Swapping the planners and editors of agents further shows that both history understanding and image generation limit agent performance. We also build PROPS-Train, 80,250 training chains in four tracks whose targets recover an earlier state or carry scripted changes forward, and fine-tuning on it clearly improves all four tested models. Both datasets will be publicly released.

</details>


### [221] [DeclareViT: Decoupling Early Classification from Feature Refinement with Exit Query for Efficient ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29674)
*Xinrui Gong,Yao Rong,Chuangtao Chen,Daniel Ochs,Ulf Schlichtmann,Bing Li,Kristian Kersting,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeclareViT通过解耦早期分类与特征精炼，在ViT/DeiT/Swin上实现1.58–2.57倍MAC加速且精度仅下降0.39–1.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有early-exit方法将早期分类与共享骨干耦合，中间表征需同时服务于早期分类与后续特征精炼；联合训练时两类目标共同更新共享骨干参数，导致梯度方向冲突，限制早退效果。

Method: 提出DeclareViT框架，解耦早期分类与特征精炼。每个出口使用专用query通过单向cross-attention读取中间特征，不修改骨干token流；仅优化query分支用于早期分类，骨干与最终分类器保持冻结。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-1K、Food-101上，跨ViT、DeiT、Swin均取得SOTA精度-效率权衡：基于MAC达1.58×–2.57×加速，精度仅降0.39–1.91个百分点；CPU与GPU的wall-clock测量亦验证实际推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过将早期分类从骨干中解耦并冻结主干，可有效规避梯度冲突，在多种Transformer架构与数据集上稳定获得显著加速与实际部署收益。

Abstract: Vision Transformers achieve remarkable recognition performance but incur substantial computational cost during inference, limiting their use in latency- and compute-constrained deployment scenarios. Early-exit inference offers a promising solution by allowing easy samples to terminate at intermediate layers. However, existing approaches typically couple early classification with the shared backbone, requiring intermediate representations to support both early classification and subsequent feature refinement. Under joint training, the corresponding optimization objectives jointly update shared backbone parameters, which can lead to conflicting gradient directions. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DeclareViT, a framework that decouples early classification from feature refinement. Each exit uses a dedicated query to read intermediate features through one-way cross-attention without modifying the backbone token stream. The query branches are optimized for early classification while the backbone and final classifier remain frozen. Across ViT, DeiT, and Swin on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-1K, and Food-101, DeclareViT consistently establishes a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off: It achieves 1.58 times to 2.57 times multiply-accumulate (MAC)-based speed-up with only a 0.39-1.91 percentage-point accuracy drop. Wall-clock measurements on CPU and GPU further demonstrate practical inference acceleration. Code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DeclareViT.

</details>


### [222] [Ref2Light: From One Image to Interactive Glasses-Free 3D Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29678)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Haoyang Wang,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ref2Light 是一个前馈框架，可将单张 A-pose 插画与连续姿态/表情控制映射为光场显示原生动画，无需逐角色三维重建、绑定或测试时优化，并在消费级 GPU 上以 34–40 FPS 输出 1440×2560 原生栅格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场显示上的角色动画同时要求相干双目视差、实时姿态驱动和细粒度表情控制，但现有 2D 动画与 3D 重建流程无法兼顾这些需求，且难以支持角色快速切换（逐角色重建/绑定代价高）。

Method: 核心思路是将光场合成解耦为静态身份、视角相关视差与控制驱动关节三部分：1) 共享编码器对每个角色仅缓存一次多尺度身份特征以支持快速切换；2) 条件解码器通过反向纹理传输与直接颜色合成生成有序角度锚点视图；3) 全局-局部控制方案用头身控制形变锚点，并利用更新后的对应场跨视角追踪面部区域进行掩码局部表情精修；4) 标定的可微子像素交织将锚点转换为面板原生栅格，实现端到端原生监督，无需全分辨率渲染所有视图。

Result: 在留出 VRoid 身份上，头身 LPIPS 相较 2D/3D 基线降低 20.1%–55.6%；嘴部闭合的非目标区域变化 MAE 相较单体式解码降低 95.6%–96.5%；1440×2560 原生栅格在消费级 GPU 上达到 34.0–40.1 FPS。

Conclusion: 通过身份-视差-关节的解耦与可微原生交织监督，Ref2Light 在无需逐角色重建的前提下同时满足光场动画的视差一致性、实时性与可切换性，并实现高保真、局部可控的表情编辑。

Abstract: Animating character illustrations on light-field displays demands coherent binocular parallax, real-time pose articulation, and fine-grained expression control. However, existing 2D animation and 3D reconstruction pipelines cannot combine these with rapid character switching. We present Ref2Light, a feed-forward framework that maps a single A-pose illustration and continuous pose and expression controls to display-native light-field animation, without per-character 3D reconstruction, rigging, or test-time optimization. Our key insight is to decompose light-field synthesis into static identity, view-dependent parallax, and control-driven articulation. A shared encoder caches multi-scale identity features once per character for rapid switching. A conditional decoder then synthesizes ordered angular anchors from these features via backward texture transport and direct color synthesis. A global-local control scheme deforms the anchors with head-body controls and uses the updated correspondence fields to track facial regions across views for masked local facial refinement. Finally, calibrated differentiable subpixel interlacing converts the anchors into the native panel raster, enabling end-to-end native supervision without rendering all views at full resolution. On held-out VRoid identities, Ref2Light reduces head-body LPIPS by 20.1%-55.6% over 2D and 3D baselines and lowers non-target change MAE in mouth closure by 95.6%-96.5% versus monolithic decoding, while running at 34.0-40.1 FPS for 1440x2560 native rasters on a consumer GPU. Code and demo are in the Supplementary Material.

</details>


### [223] [HandiVid: A High-Quality Dataset for Hand-Driven Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29693)
*Chaoyang Zhang,Shaohao Rui,Xiaofeng Mao,Yuanxun Xu,Haoyuan Xia,Xiaohong Liu,Qinghe Du,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HandiVid，一个面向手部驱动的第一人称交互视频生成的高质量数据集，含 20 万条经过筛选与标注的第一人称交互视频片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频数据集难以同时满足场景多样性、有意义的手-物交互和可靠运动标注三项要求。

Method: 构建统一数据处理流水线，整合公开数据集、网络视频与合成视频；通过视频质量评估、手部检测过滤，以及基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的多阶段交互验证筛选高质量片段；将 SAM 3 融入手部检测流程以减少漏检、提升 3D 手部标注完整性；每段视频配以高质量手部渲染、相机轨迹与交互描述。

Result: 数据集分析与下游视频生成实验表明，HandiVid 在数据多样性、标注质量和可控视频生成方面均表现有效。

Conclusion: HandiVid 可有效支撑手部驱动的第一人称交互视频生成研究，弥补现有数据集在多样性与标注可靠性上的不足。

Abstract: Hand-driven egocentric interactive video generation aims to generate dynamic scenes with realistic interaction responses conditioned on hand movements and camera motion. However, existing egocentric video datasets struggle to provide scene diversity, meaningful hand-object interactions, and reliable motion annotations simultaneously. To address these limitations, we introduce HandiVid, a high-quality dataset comprising 200K curated and annotated egocentric interaction video clips for hand-driven interactive video generation. We develop a unified data processing pipeline that integrates public datasets, online videos and synthetic videos. Through video quality assessment, hand detection filtering, and multi-stage interaction validation using vision-language models (VLMs), we select high-quality video clips with meaningful hand-object interactions. Since 3D hand reconstruction relies on initial hand detections, we integrate SAM 3 into the hand detection pipeline to reduce missed detections and improve the completeness of 3D hand annotations. Each video clip is provided with high-quality hand renderings, camera trajectories, and interaction descriptions. Comprehensive dataset analysis and downstream video generation experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of HandiVid in terms of data diversity, annotation quality, and controllable video generation.

</details>


### [224] [UniDR: Learning To Generate Anatomical Deformations From Images via Unified Diffusion Modeling and Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29712)
*Swakshar Deb,Tonmoy Hossain,Tom Fletcher,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniDR 提出将扩散建模与配准统一的条件扩散框架，在形变场上联合优化，生成拓扑保持的多样化解剖形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将生成过程与基于配准的形变估计分离建模，导致配准阶段的误差传播到生成环节。

Method: 构建以单张图像为条件、作用于形变场的条件扩散模型，并与配准过程联合优化；提出配准引导的 score-matching 目标函数，使扩散过程对齐配准诱导的形变分布，从而在生成与配准间建立显式联系。

Result: 在多个真实应用中，UniDR 生成高保真样本优于现有最先进方法，且所生成样本可提升下游医学图像分析与视觉任务性能。

Conclusion: 将扩散生成与配准统一可避免误差传播，兼顾形变多样性与解剖合理性，并作为数据增强有效服务下游任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework, Unified Diffusion modeling and Registration (UniDR), which learns to generate arbitrary anatomical deformations from images with preserved topology. Existing approaches often model the generative process separately from registration-based deformation estimation; hence making them prone to propagated errors from the registration stage. To address this limitation, we propose a unified paradigm based on a conditional diffusion model over deformation fields that are jointly optimized from the registration process. A principled registration-guided score-matching objective function is then introduced to align the diffusion process with the deformation distribution induced by registration. As a result, our UniDR establishes an explicit connection between generation and registration: the diffusion model enables exploration of the space of plausible anatomical deformations, whereas registration provides structured supervision for learning transformations consistent with observed images. Once trained, UniDR is able to sample diverse, anatomically plausible transformations conditioned on any single input image. We evaluate UniDR across multiple real-world applications and demonstrate its effectiveness in generating high-fidelity image samples compared to the state-of-the-art. Additionally, we demonstrate that these generated samples improve performance in downstream medical image analysis and vision tasks.

</details>


### [225] [ApexID: Hierarchical Learning for Multi-Species Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29723)
*Seifeldin Elkerdany,Mohamed ElBassat,Gamal Abouelhamd,Assem Elkady,Jana Ghoneim,Matin Mahmood,Abigail Allen Martin,Gerard de Melo,Antonio Rueda-Toicen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层SphereFace2损失ApexID，将全局个体判别与动物类目平衡监督结合，在19个公开数据集共132,533张图像上训练，于4个外部数据集上取得优于预训练基线的跨数据集与跨物种Re-ID检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物重识别需同时区分不同物种的个体并适应多变的拍摄条件，现有方法对未见数据集与未见物种的迁移能力不足，亟需引入类目层面的结构化监督以提升泛化性。

Method: 提出ApexID——一种基于SphereFace2的分层损失，联合优化全局身份判别与类别平衡的动物类目监督；训练集由19个公开数据集整理为132,533张图像，评估涵盖3类模型族、4种目标函数，并在4个外部数据集上采用观测感知（observation-aware）的检索协议。

Result: ConvNeXt-Small配合ApexID取得数据集平均Rank-1 64.89%，MIEW-ID取得mAP 32.54%，均超过其预训练参考模型；在训练中完全未出现的大象类别上，ConvNeXt-Small变体在14种评估配置中检索性能最高；分数随骨干网络与数据集变化，开放集评估显示仍有发现性挑战。

Conclusion: 分层类别-身份学习有助于提升对未见数据集与未见物种的迁移能力；同时性能对骨干与数据集具有依赖性，开放集场景下的个体发现仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Animal re-identification (Re-ID) must distinguish individuals across species and capture conditions. We introduce ApexID, a hierarchical SphereFace2-based loss that integrates global identity discrimination with balanced animal-category supervision. Using 132,533 images curated from 19 public datasets, we evaluate three model families and four objectives on four external datasets with observation-aware retrieval protocols. ApexID achieves a dataset-averaged Rank-1 accuracy of 64.89% with ConvNeXt-Small and an mAP of 32.54% with MIEW-ID, outperforming their pretrained reference models. On elephants, a category absent from animal Re-ID training, our ConvNeXt-Small variant achieves the highest retrieval performance among the fourteen evaluated configurations. Scores vary by backbone and dataset, and open-set evaluation reveals remaining discovery challenges. These findings support hierarchical category-identity learning to improve transfer to unseen datasets and species.

</details>


### [226] [Reasoning Beyond Imitation: Observation Conditioned Distillation for Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29728)
*Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang,Gang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对视频大模型推理蒸馏中师生采样管线不同导致的观测不一致问题，形式化为“条件目标不匹配”，并提出“获取到执行”框架（先蒸馏推理先验、再跨多观测优化执行能力）及执行感知参考正则化，在多个Video-LLM骨干与六个视频推理基准上优于监督蒸馏和标准RL优化，并泛化至域外基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强大Video-LLM的时序推理能力可通过推理蒸馏迁移到开源学生模型，但师生采用不同的视频采样管线，会从同一视频构造出不同的时序观测；教师观测下有效的推理轨迹可能依赖学生无法获取的证据，因而无法直接在学生观测上执行，形成“推理获取-执行鸿沟”。

Method: 将上述问题形式化为条件目标不匹配（conditional target mismatch）；提出获取到执行（acquisition-to-execution）框架：先将教师推理轨迹蒸馏为学生推理先验，再跨多种观测优化该先验的执行能力；引入执行感知参考正则化（Execution-Aware Reference Regularization），在当前观测下先验可执行时保留先验，不可执行时允许更大程度自适应调整。

Result: 在多个Video-LLM骨干和六个视频推理基准上，显式优化观测条件下的执行能力持续优于监督蒸馏和标准RL-based优化，且增益可迁移到留出的域外基准。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏需显式建模观测条件差异，仅对齐轨迹不足；解耦“先验获取”与“执行适配”并以参考正则化控制调整幅度，可有效弥合获取-执行鸿沟并提升泛化性。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation offers a promising approach to transfer temporal reasoning capabilities of powerful Video-LLMs to open-source student models. However, teacher and student Video-LLMs may construct different temporal observations from the same video due to the adoption of distinct video sampling pipelines. A reasoning trajectory valid under the teacher's observations may rely on evidence inaccessible to the student and thus cannot be directly executed based on the student's observations. We formalize this as a conditional target mismatch, thereby revealing the Reasoning Acquisition-Execution Gap. To bridge this gap, we propose an acquisition-to-execution framework that first distills teacher trajectories into a student reasoning prior and then further optimizes the execution capability of this prior across multiple observations. We further introduce Execution-Aware Reference Regularization, which retains the existing prior when it is executable under current observations, while allowing a greater degree of adaptive adjustment when it is not. Experiments across multiple Video-LLM backbones and six video reasoning benchmarks show that explicitly optimizing observation-conditioned execution consistently outperforms supervised distillation and standard RL-based optimization, with gains extending to held-out out-of-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [227] [Seeing Beyond Names: Image-Grounded Concept Linking for Robust Vision-Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29735)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Tahir Qasim Syed,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 零样本视觉语言分类器对类别名称极为敏感，同义/缩写/本体术语替换会使CLIP ViT-B/16 top-1精度下降14.8–17.6个百分点；作者提出免训练的"图像接地概念链接"方法，在四个数据集上一致优于模板集成，汇总提升1.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本分类器的预测依赖所接收的类别名称文本，即使图像不变，词汇替换也会显著改变预测。作者将这一"词汇漂移"现象作为研究对象，旨在量化其影响并加以缓解。

Method: 采用无泄漏评估协议，在Tiny ImageNet、ImageNet-R、ImageNet-A、ImageNetV2上测量词汇漂移；提出免训练的image-grounded concept linking：将类别名称映射到视觉概念，并用可靠性门控（reliability gate）融合以改进预测；在5个视觉语言骨干×4个数据集上验证，并进行文本链接增强、直推式精炼与随机种子复现等消融/鲁棒性分析。

Result: CLIP ViT-B/16在替代类别名下top-1下降14.8–17.6个百分点；所提方法在四个数据集上均优于模板集成，汇总增益1.8个百分点，20个骨干—数据集组合中16个提升且无显著负结果，并在全部5个骨干上降低汇总负对数似然；更强文本链接与直推式精炼无一致收益。

Conclusion: 词汇漂移是零样本分类的实质性缺陷；免训练的图像接地概念链接可稳定带来小幅但一致的提升，且准确地将类别名称与视觉概念链接仍是核心难点。第二个随机种子在ImageNetV2上复现提升，但在ImageNet-A上未复现，提示结果存在一定数据集依赖性。

Abstract: A zero-shot vision-language classifier is highly sensitive to the class names it receives. Even when images remain unchanged, replacing familiar labels with synonyms, abbreviations, or ontology terms can significantly alter predictions. We investigate this \emph{vocabulary shift} using a leakage-free evaluation protocol across Tiny ImageNet, ImageNet-R, ImageNet-A, and ImageNetV2. With CLIP ViT-B/16, alternative class names reduce top-1 accuracy by $14.8$--$17.6$ percentage points. To address this issue, we propose \emph{image-grounded concept linking}, a training-free method that maps class names to visual concepts and uses a reliability gate to improve predictions. The method outperforms a template ensemble across all four datasets, achieving a pooled gain of $1.8$ points. We further evaluate five vision-language backbones across four datasets. Our method improves performance in $16$ of $20$ backbone--dataset combinations, with no significantly negative results. It also reduces pooled negative log-likelihood across all five backbones. Stronger text linking and transductive refinement provide no consistent improvements, suggesting that accurate visual class-name linking remains the main challenge. A second CLIP ViT-B/16 seed confirms the improvement on ImageNetV2, but not on ImageNet-A.

</details>


### [228] [MISeg-Agent: A Human-in-the-Loop Agentic Framework for Error Diagnosis and Executable Prompt Review in 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29741)
*Delin An,Mingqi Xu,Jian-Xun Wang,Pengfei Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MISeg-Agent，一个human-in-the-loop的智能体框架，用于诊断医学图像分割中的局部错误并给出有证据支撑的交互式修正建议，在CT数据集上显著改善初始分割结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割模型仍存在局部错误，影响分割质量与可靠性；交互式分割虽可修正，但"定位错误、决定干预位置、评估修正效果"仍具挑战，且每次修正都会改变当前掩码，导致后续诊断与建议需随之自适应调整。

Method: 框架先依据基准性能与计算需求选择预训练分割模型；基于其预测掩码识别潜在缺失前景与多余前景区域，推荐可疑切片和修正点；再用交互式分割模型评估候选点提示，生成修正结果并交人工核验后再应用；诊断直接基于图像与预测掩码，与底层模型架构无关。

Result: 在CT数据集上的评估表明，MISeg-Agent可提升初始分割结果，最终掩码与参考标注高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过将自适应错误诊断与经验证的修正建议相结合，MISeg-Agent为识别和减少现有医学图像分割中的残余错误提供了一种可解释的途径。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of modern medical image segmentation models, their predictions can still contain localized errors that compromise segmentation quality. Identifying and correcting these errors is therefore essential for improving the reliability of existing segmentation results. While interactive segmentation models enable targeted corrections, identifying potential errors, determining where to intervene, and evaluating corrective actions remain challenging. Moreover, each correction changes the current mask, requiring subsequent error diagnosis and recommendations to adapt accordingly. To address these challenges, we propose MISeg-Agent, a human-in-the-loop agentic framework that diagnoses segmentation errors and provides evidence-based correction recommendations. MISeg-Agent first selects a suitable pretrained segmentation model based on benchmark performance and computational requirements. Given its predicted mask, the framework identifies potential missing and excess foreground regions and recommends suspicious slices and corrective points for review. The framework then evaluates candidate point prompts using an interactive segmentation model and presents the resulting corrections for human verification before applying them. By operating directly on images and predicted masks, the error diagnosis process remains independent of the underlying segmentation model architecture. Evaluations on CT datasets demonstrate that MISeg-Agent improves initial segmentation results and produces final masks closely aligned with reference annotations. By integrating adaptive error diagnosis with validated correction recommendations, MISeg-Agent provides an interpretable approach to identifying and reducing residual errors in existing medical image segmentations.

</details>


### [229] [DREAM: Dual-Memory Reasoning for Anticipatory 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29753)
*Sriram Mandalika,Athira Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DREAM：面向持续LiDAR语义分割的双记忆推理框架，在严格在线设定下用"潜记忆+错误记忆"检索式调制特征，冻结主干、不回放历史扫描，在多个大规模LiDAR基准上取得SOTA，并在动态目标与小尺度目标上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR语义分割方法多为帧中心（frame-centric）范式，逐帧独立处理并丢弃历史上下文经验，在遮挡、稀疏观测与重复性失败模式下鲁棒性不足，缺乏对感知挑战的预判能力。

Method: 将感知重构为经验驱动、可预判的过程；在严格在线设定中维护两个互补记忆库：潜记忆存储既往场景的紧凑语义抽象，错误记忆记录与不确定预测相关的表示。每个时间步以余弦相似度检索相关记忆条目，并通过轻量调制机制融入特征空间，强化一致的语义模式、抑制反复出现的失败模式；主干网络保持冻结，不回放历史扫描。

Result: 在多个大规模LiDAR基准上的大量实验表明达到SOTA性能，且在动态目标与小尺度目标上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 持续且错误感知的记忆机制对长时程鲁棒感知有效，并在保证计算效率与有界内存增长的前提下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must not only interpret the current scene but also anticipate perceptual challenges such as occlusions, sparse observations, and recurring failure patterns. However, most existing LiDAR semantic segmentation methods operate in a frame-centric manner, processing each scan independently and discarding past contextual experience, which limits robustness under dynamic scene changes. We propose DREAM, a Dual-memory REAsoning framework for continual LiDAR seMantic segmentation that reframes perception as an experience-driven and anticipatory process. DREAM maintains two complementary memory banks in a strictly online setting: a latent memory that stores compact semantic abstractions of previously observed scenes, and an error memory that records representations associated with uncertain predictions. At each timestep, relevant memory entries are retrieved via cosine similarity and integrated into the feature space through a lightweight modulation mechanism, enabling the model to reinforce consistent semantic patterns while suppressing recurring failure modes. The backbone remains frozen and no past scans are replayed, ensuring computational efficiency and bounded memory growth. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale LiDAR benchmarks demonstrate that DREAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with consistent improvements on dynamic and small-scale objects, highlighting the effectiveness of persistent and error-aware memory for robust long-horizon perception.

</details>


### [230] [DictaRashi: A State-of-the-Art OCR Model For Rashi Script](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29760)
*Ometz Shmidman,Yonah Taurog,Aryeh Sanders,Shlomo Bluth,Moshe Koppel,Joshua Geudalia,Avi Shmidman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布DictaRashi——首个面向"Rashi"字体希伯来语印刷古籍的高精度Tesseract OCR模型，CER 0.86%、WER 3.38%，并公开模型与测试基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Rashi"字体在拉比及后拉比文献中广泛使用，但现有公开希伯来语Tesseract模型在该字体上精度不足，缺乏专用模型与公开评测基准。

Method: 基于Tesseract框架，用来自100余部书籍、逾一百万行人工校正文本训练DictaRashi；在1,120行人工转写、来自扫描Rashi字体页面的留出集上评测，并与另外31个公开希伯来语Tesseract模型及当前前沿LLM进行对比。

Result: DictaRashi取得CER 0.86%、WER 3.38%；表现最好的其他公开模型为CER 2.98%、WER 11.86%；DictaRashi亦优于前沿LLM。

Conclusion: DictaRashi刷新公开Tesseract模型的最优水平，且优于LLM；作者以CC BY NC 4.0许可公开模型与测试集，为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: We release DictaRashi, a Tesseract OCR model for scanned Hebrew books printed in the so-called "Rashi" font. We trained DictaRashi on over one million manually corrected lines of text from over 100 different books of Rabbinic and post-Rabbinic literature. On a held-out set of 1,120 manually transcribed lines from scanned Rashi-script pages, DictaRashi reaches a character error rate (CER) of 0.86% and a word error rate (WER) of 3.38%, setting a new state-of-the-art for publicly-available Tesseract models. We provide a full comparison of our results to those of the 31 other publicly available Hebrew Tesseract models; the best of those models achieves a 2.98% CER and 11.86% WER. We further demonstrate that DictaRashi outperforms the current frontier LLMs on this OCR task. We release the test set of Rashi-script documents so that it may be used as a benchmark for future work in this area. We are pleased to release the model and test set to the community under a CC BY NC 4.0 license.

</details>


### [231] [CivicBench: Robust Detection of AI-Generated Political Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29766)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CivicBench 基准（10,800 条虚构政治人物生成视频 + 2,632 条真实公共领域视频，且均可被攻击），揭示现有检测器在对抗扰动下性能急剧退化（≤0.781 AUC），并提出 Tau-Anchor 方法达到 0.941 AUC 并显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造政治视频既可能被伪装成真实素材，也可能被攻击者用来"构陷"真实素材使其被判定为假。现有政治视频数据集规模小、仅含真实政治人物、且不测试任何攻击，无法评估检测器在对抗条件下的可靠性。

Method: 构建 CivicBench：含 8 个开源生成器生成的 10,800 条虚构人物视频与 2,632 条真实视频，每条样本均可施加对抗攻击。提出"攻击可达范围（reach）"指标——攻击可移动样本分数的幅度；若样本距阈值裕度超过该可达范围，则标签保持稳定，并实证其随扰动预算近似线性增长。据此设计 Tau-Anchor：在受攻击图像上预训练的编码器上进行微调，训练中同时对真实与生成片段施加攻击，并在部署阈值处加入边际项。

Result: 通用基准训练的公开检测器在 CivicBench 上 AUC 最高仅 .781；每像素 1 灰阶的对抗改动即可使非抗攻击检测器几乎漏检所有生成片段。Tau-Anchor 达到 .941 AUC；在 2 灰阶攻击下仍保持 .668 的生成片段检出率（公开方法至多 .289）；真实片段被构陷时误报率仅从 8% 升至 14%，而四个公开检测器在仅 1 灰阶攻击下几乎将全部被构陷的真实片段判为伪造。外部政治素材与两个通用基准呈现相同规律。

Conclusion: 上传者对像素的控制权使检测器面临根本性对抗风险；CivicBench 提供了可攻击评测的政治视频基准，Tau-Anchor 通过对抗训练与阈值边际约束显著提升检测鲁棒性与对称公平性（兼顾防漏检与防构陷），为平台部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: AI-generated video of politicians can pass for real footage, and once such fakes are common, real footage can be dismissed as fake. Platforms address both problems with detectors, but whoever uploads a clip controls its pixels. Existing datasets of political video are small, show real politicians, and test no attacks. We introduce CivicBench, with 10,800 generated clips of invented politicians from eight open generators and 2,632 public-domain or openly licensed real clips. Each clip can be attacked, to hide a fake or to frame real footage. Published detectors trained on a general benchmark reach at most .781 AUC on CivicBench. Adversarial changes of 1 gray level per pixel hide nearly every generated clip from the detectors not built to resist attacks. An attack's reach is how far it can move a clip's score. A clip keeps its label when its margin from the threshold exceeds this reach, and we find that the reach grows almost linearly with the budget. On this basis, we propose Tau-Anchor, which fine-tunes an encoder pretrained on attacked images. It attacks real and generated clips during training and adds a margin term at its deployed threshold. It reaches .941 AUC on CivicBench. It also keeps .668 of generated clips flagged at 2 gray levels, against at most .289 for published detectors. When real clips are framed at the same 2 gray levels, Tau-Anchor's false alarms rise only from 8% to 14%, whereas four published detectors flag nearly every framed real clip at just 1 gray level. External political footage and two general benchmarks show the same pattern under attack. Code and data are available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/CivicBench.

</details>


### [232] [S1Arena-MM: Which Multimodal Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29773)
*Lang Lin,Tianyu Chen,Yi Gu,Yang Zhao,Xinran Song,Xinyang Liu,Jianpeng Cheng,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S1Arena-MM基准（51,060题、94任务、多模态、含102个闭环游戏环境），统一比较12个System-1模型与21个MLLM的离散决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知系统常需做离散决策（如判定仇恨梗、预测行人过街、检测伪造语音、选择游戏动作）。System-1模型单次前向返回选项概率，MLLM生成文本，二者在何种多模态决策上各具优势缺乏统一评测。

Method: 构建统一基准：对所有模型给出相同决策请求并统一评分其返回的概率；覆盖图像、视频、音频输入，94个任务、51,060题，源自94个公开数据集及3个程序生成数据集；另设102个闭环游戏环境（截图输入、实时模式下游戏在模型决策时持续推进）；评测33个开源模型（12个System-1、21个MLLM，后者在答案token处读取）。

Result: 静态决策中最佳System-1在图像上领先最佳MLLM 4.9个百分点，视频与音频上差距在1个百分点内；面向决策的后训练主要改善概率质量，视频上12对中有10对System-1的Brier分数低于其来源MLLM；每题至多2,048个思考token时最准确的MLLM仅小幅超越最佳System-1，且思考普遍抬高多数MLLM的Brier分数；闭环中三个基于27B MLLM的System-1在BALROG取得22.0–33.3%进度，其余模型（关闭思考）均不超9.7%；实时GameWorld中每决策最高1.1s延迟对System-1进度无可测损失。

Conclusion: System-1模型在静态离散决策上具竞争力且概率校准更优，闭环与实时场景优势显著；MLLM的思考增益有限且常损害概率质量，表明决策专用模型+低延迟架构更适配感知决策任务。

Abstract: Perception systems often make discrete decisions: flag a hateful meme, predict whether a pedestrian will cross, detect a spoofed voice, or select the next move in a game. System-1 models, such as OneJev and Jev-Omni, read the media and return probabilities over the options in a single forward pass, while multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate text. Which multimodal decisions can System-1 models handle, and where do MLLMs perform better? We introduce S1Arena-MM, a benchmark that gives every model the same decision requests and scores the probabilities it returns. It contains 51,060 questions across 94 tasks with image, video, and audio input, drawn from 94 public datasets and three program-generated ones. It also includes 102 closed-loop game environments that a model plays from screenshots; in real-time mode, the game continues while the model decides. We evaluate 33 open models: 12 System-1 models and 21 MLLMs read at the answer token. On static decisions, the best System-1 model leads the best MLLM by 4.9 percentage points on images and is within one point of it on video and audio. Post-training for decisions mainly improves the probabilities: on video, a System-1 model has a lower Brier score than the MLLM that it is post-trained from in 10 of 12 pairs. With up to 2,048 thought tokens per question, the most accurate MLLMs pass the best System-1 models only slightly, and thinking raises the Brier score of most MLLMs. Closed loops separate the models: three System-1 models built on a 27B MLLM reach 22.0-33.3% progression in BALROG, and no other model exceeds 9.7% with thinking off. In real-time GameWorld, latencies of up to 1.1 s per decision cause no measurable loss of progress for System-1 models. Code and data: https://github.com/zhaoyang02/S1Arena-MM.

</details>


### [233] [Beyond Language Optimization: Enhancing Visual Representations for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29783)
*Rui Zuo,Qinyue Tong,Ziqian Lu,Zheming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性区域视觉蒸馏框架，在保留原有视觉能力的前提下增强困难异常的视觉表征，并引入空间门控残差适配器（SGRA）实现验证后特征的蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO虽提升报告临床准确性，但对异常识别的视觉表征改进有限，缺乏对困难异常区域的有效视觉建模。

Method: 训练针对困难解剖区域做定向监督的区域感知教师模型以获取互补视觉知识；通过区域特征替换验证教师特征效用，仅选择能提升学生性能的特征；设计SGRA在蒸馏这些验证特征时减少对原有视觉表征的干扰。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR与IU-Xray上，跨多种多模态大模型骨干，临床报告准确率与区域级异常识别均提升，且非目标发现性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 选择性、经效用验证的区域视觉蒸馏可在不损害已有能力的情况下针对性增强困难异常的视觉表征，为RRG提供有效改进路径。

Abstract: Accurate chest X-ray radiology report generation (RRG) requires recognizing clinically relevant abnormalities and accurately describing them. Although Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves the clinical accuracy of generated reports, our analyses suggest limited improvements in visual representations for abnormality recognition. To address this limitation, we propose a selective regional visual distillation framework that enhances visual representations of challenging abnormalities while preserving existing visual capabilities. Specifically, we train a region-aware teacher with targeted supervision on challenging anatomical regions to acquire complementary visual knowledge. Rather than transferring all teacher features, we verify their utility through regional feature replacement and select only those that improve the original student's performance. We further introduce a Spatially Gated Residual Adapter (SGRA) to distill these verified features while minimizing interference with the student's existing visual representations. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray demonstrate improved clinical report accuracy and region-level abnormality recognition across different multimodal large language model backbones, while largely preserving performance on non-target findings.

</details>


### [234] [A Cascaded Framework with a Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN Model for Pre- and Post-Treatment Glioma Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29792)
*Siavash Taremi,Mahmoud Heidari,Seyed Mohammad Hossein Hashemi,Mohsen Hooshmand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出级联框架下的 Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN（CMC-Seg）模型，兼顾治疗前与治疗后弥漫性胶质瘤分割的精度与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤手工分割耗时、主观性强；其异质性强。CNN 擅长局部特征但长程依赖弱，ViT 全局建模强但计算复杂；且多数研究集中于治疗前病例，术后含切除腔（RC）的分割研究不足。

Method: 两阶段级联框架：先用粗分割模型提取以肿瘤为中心的 patch，再输入 CMC-Seg 进行精细分割；CMC-Seg 融合跨注意力与多方向扫描 Mamba-CNN。数据为 BraTS 2025 弥漫性胶质瘤 mpMRI。

Result: 测试集平均 Dice 81.20%、NETC Dice 77.09%、RC Dice 78.63%、HD95 4.39 mm（最低）；与 CNN 及 Mamba 基线相当，显著优于 Transformer 基线（P<0.05）；推理 GFLOPs 最低（381.49）。消融显示多方向扫描与跨注意力提升性能，尤其对 RC（P<0.05）；Grad-CAM 显示关注诊断相关肿瘤区域。

Conclusion: CMC-Seg 在治疗前后胶质瘤分割中实现精度与计算效率的良好平衡，尤其改善切除腔分割，具备临床可用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate glioma segmentation is vital for diagnosis and follow-up, but manual segmentation is time-consuming, subjective, and variable across interpreters. Gliomas are heterogeneous in size, shape, and location. Deep learning has therefore emerged as a potential solution. CNNs excel at local features but may struggle with long-range dependencies, whereas Vision Transformers capture global context efficiently but incur higher computational complexity. Most previous studies focused on pre-treatment gliomas; post-treatment cases with resection cavities (RCs) remain less explored. This study proposes a Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN (CMC-Seg) model within a cascaded framework for pre- and post-treatment diffuse glioma segmentation, aiming to balance accuracy and computational cost. Experiments used BraTS 2025 mpMRI of diffuse glioma patients. After preprocessing, a coarse segmentation model extracted tumor-centered patches, which were fed to CMC-Seg for fine-grained segmentation. CMC-Seg was compared with 3D U-Net, SegResNet, SwinUNETR, UNETR, nnFormer, and SegMamba. On the test set, CMC-Seg achieved the highest mean Dice (81.20%) and non-enhancing tumor core (NETC) Dice (77.09%). It also achieved an RC Dice of 78.63% and the lowest 95% Hausdorff distance (HD95; 4.39 mm). Results were comparable to CNN and Mamba baselines and significantly better than Transformer-based baselines (P < 0.05). CMC-Seg required the fewest inference GFLOPs (381.49). Ablation experiments indicated that multi-direction scanning and cross-attention improved performance, particularly for RC (P < 0.05). Grad-CAM explainability experiments showed that CMC-Seg focuses on diagnostically relevant tumor regions.

</details>


### [235] [Revisiting RadioGalaxyNET: modern detectors for radio galaxy detection and host identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29798)
*Argo Saakyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在RadioGalaxyNET基准上重新评测三个当前检测器（D-FINE-seg、YOLO26-pose、RF-DETR），均超越2023年公开最优结果，并揭示该基准评测协议中的若干数据与评分偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RadioGalaxyNET是EMU先导巡天图像中搜寻延展射电星系、分类并定位红外宿主天体的公开基准，但其最优结果仍停留在2023年（Gal-YOLOv8 54.5 box AP、Gal-DINO 48.1 host keypoint AP），缺乏用当代检测器做的可比复现与审计。

Method: 用基准自带的评测代码（可精确复现Gal-DINO已发表数值）在同一数据上训练并评测D-FINE-seg、YOLO26-pose和RF-DETR，比较AP与速度；同时分析评分协议、标签几何与图像可重建性。

Result: D-FINE-seg M取得67.1 box AP与72.4 host AP，较最优已发表结果分别提升12.6与24.3点，宿主AP为所测最高，box AP与YOLO26持平；端到端最快为D-FINE-seg S（1.9 ms/图），同尺寸引擎对引擎时YOLO26更快。发现评分协议虚增5–10 AP、宿主标签位于图像中心、box标签相对图像偏移1.5像素，且图像可由公开巡天数据重建，从而恢复天球坐标并扩充训练数据。建议改用更贴近编目用途的宿主识别F1：D-FINE-seg M 71.8、YOLO26-pose M 70.5、RF-DETR 63.8、Gal-DINO 59.2。

Conclusion: RadioGalaxyNET的已发表最优结果已被当代检测器明显超越，但基准自身的评分与标签特性会系统性抬高AP，未来工作宜报告宿主识别F1等更贴近实际编目任务的指标，并注意标签偏差与数据可重建性。

Abstract: RadioGalaxyNET is the public benchmark for finding extended radio galaxies in EMU pilot-survey images, classifying them and locating their infrared hosts. Its best published results date from 2023: 54.5 box AP (Gal-YOLOv8) and 48.1 host keypoint AP (Gal-DINO). We train three current detectors, D-FINE-seg, YOLO26-pose and RF-DETR, on the same data and score them with the benchmark's own evaluation code, which reproduces the published Gal-DINO numbers exactly. All exceed the published results. D-FINE-seg M reaches 67.1 box AP and 72.4 host AP, 12.6 and 24.3 points above the best published results, and the highest host AP of the detectors we tested; on boxes it is level with YOLO26. Its host lead over YOLO26 comes from host-centered images, which make up most of the benchmark. End to end, D-FINE-seg S is the fastest model at 1.9 ms per image; engine against engine, YOLO26 is faster at the same size.
