We also report what matters for anyone using the benchmark: its scoring protocol adds 5 to 10 AP, its host labels sit at the image center, its box labels are offset from the image by 1.5 pixels, and its images can be rebuilt from the public survey data, which recovers sky positions and doubles as extra training data. We suggest that future work report host identification F1, as a measure closer to catalog use: D-FINE-seg M 71.8, YOLO26-pose M 70.5, RF-DETR 63.8, Gal-DINO 59.2.

</details>


### [236] [Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head for Tiny Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29813)
*Aditya Makineni,Amitesh Sahoo,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层推理动作头(HR-AH),在单次骨干前向之后通过高、低层双潜状态的分频嵌套迭代实现轻量化的层级推理,显著优于参数量匹配的单状态头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时机载机器人控制需要轻量VLA模型,但缩小骨干会削弱推理能力;CoT解码、双系统架构和加大骨干虽可恢复推理却重新带来延迟与显存开销。现有循环动作头在潜空间中迭代虽更轻,却仅精炼单一未分化状态,该状态须同时编码缓慢演化的任务上下文与快速变化的运动指令,存在表征冲突。

Method: HR-AH维护两个以不同频率更新的潜状态:低层状态快速更新,以动作token和本体感知为接地;高层状态缓慢更新,以视觉理解和经指令调制的低层状态为接地;两状态通过嵌套推理循环交换信息。全部迭代在单次骨干前向后的紧凑动作头内完成,不增加骨干规模。

Result: 动作头仅22.5M参数,在相同骨干下比RD-VLA与VLA-Adapter小3–4.3倍;LIBERO成功率97.0%,CALVIN ABC→D平均链长4.40,在对比方法中延迟最低。消融显示该层级结构优于参数量匹配的单状态头;将不同模态分别路由至不同层级可进一步提升精度并降低推理开销。

Conclusion: 分层潜空间推理可在不扩大骨干的前提下为轻量VLA补充 deliberation 能力;状态分频与模态分层路由是提升精度—效率权衡的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Real-time onboard robot control calls for a lightweight vision-language-action (VLA) model, yet shrinking the backbone leaves less capacity for deliberation at inference time. Chain-of-thought decoding, dual-system architectures, and larger backbones can restore deliberation but reintroduce the latency and memory costs that motivated going small. Recurrent action heads offer a lighter alternative by iterating in latent space before committing to an action, but existing designs refine a single undifferentiated state that must simultaneously encode slowly evolving task context and rapidly changing motor commands. We propose the Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head (HR-AH), which maintains two latent states updated at different frequencies: a low-level state that updates fast, grounded in action tokens and proprioception, and a high-level state that updates slowly, grounded in visual understanding and the instruction-informed low-level state. The two states exchange information through nested reasoning cycles. Because all iteration occurs in the compact head after a single backbone pass, HR-AH adds hierarchical reasoning without increasing the backbone size. With a 22.5M-parameter head, 3-4.3x smaller than those of RD-VLA and VLA-Adapter on the same backbone, HR-AH reaches 97.0% success rate on LIBERO and a 4.40 average chain length on CALVIN ABC->D, at the lowest latency among compared methods. We show that this hierarchy improves performance over a parameter-matched single-state head, and that routing distinct modalities to each level yields further gains in both accuracy and inference cost.

</details>


### [237] [Data-Quality-Aware Fundus Classification with Anatomy-Guided Glaucoma Assessment and Cross-Dataset Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29834)
*Imdadul Haque Sourav,Marjana Jannat Miti,Md. Jehadul Islam Mony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 九分类眼底分类器需经数据审计、解剖引导建模与多队列外部验证来评判；解剖引导融合可无损提升迁移，但增益因队列而异且阈值不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习眼底分类器内部表现良好，但重复图像、标注不一致、采集差异和阈值迁移不良会削弱其可靠性，仅凭内部指标无法证明真实性能。

Method: 对5,318张图像做精确重复与跨标签冲突审计；训练ConvNeXt-Small、EfficientNetV2-Small、DenseNet169三种九分类模型（各3个随机种子）；构建解剖引导的青光眼专家模块，读取视盘中心裁剪图并结合血管及视盘/视杯分割图与杯盘比特征；冻结模型在RFMiD、JSIEC上外部评估，REFUGE上的预设比较在ORIGA、G1020、PAPILA上复现。

Result: 审计删除464个冲突组（942张）及6张同标签副本，保留4,370张。ConvNeXt-Small内部准确率0.910、macro-F1 0.917、AUC 0.982，视盘/视杯Dice为0.966/0.915。解剖引导固定混合相对等权软投票内部准确率变化-0.001，外部闭集准确率在RFMiD提升4.9个百分点（3个种子中2个为正）、JSIEC提升2.3；REFUGE（800张）平衡准确率提升7.4个百分点（95% CI 5.1–9.5），ORIGA提升2.1，G1020与PAPILA亦有正增益。阈值迁移差（REFUGE敏感度0.950、特异度0.411）。

Conclusion: 应以数据质量审计、解剖信息建模和多队列评估评判视网膜分类器；解剖引导融合在无内部代价下改善迁移，但增益因队列而异且阈值未能迁移，内部指标不足以确立可靠性能。

Abstract: Background: Deep learning fundus classifiers often perform well internally, but duplicated images, inconsistent labels, acquisition differences and poor threshold transfer can undermine their reliability. We assess nine-class classifiers beyond internal accuracy, using a data audit, frozen-model external evaluation and an anatomy-guided specialist. Methods: Nine-class experts (ConvNeXt-Small, EfficientNetV2-Small, DenseNet169) were trained with three seeds. Auditing 5,318 images for exact duplicates and cross-label conflicts removed 464 conflict groups (942 images) and six same-label copies, leaving 4,370 images. An anatomy-guided glaucoma specialist read disc-centred crops with vessel and disc/cup segmentation maps, plus cup-to-disc features. Frozen models were evaluated on RFMiD and JSIEC, and a prespecified comparison on REFUGE was replicated on ORIGA, G1020 and PAPILA. Results: ConvNeXt-Small reached an internal accuracy of 0.910, macro-F1 of 0.917 and AUC of 0.982; disc and cup Dice scores were 0.966 and 0.915. Across seeds, the fixed anatomy-guided mix left internal accuracy unchanged relative to equal soft voting (-0.001) but raised mean external closed-set accuracy by 4.9 percentage points on RFMiD (positive in two of three seeds) and 2.3 on JSIEC. On REFUGE (800 photographs) it improved balanced accuracy by 7.4 percentage points (95% CI 5.1 to 9.5), and by 2.1 on ORIGA, with positive gains on G1020 and PAPILA. Thresholds transferred poorly (REFUGE sensitivity 0.950, specificity 0.411). Conclusions: Retinal classifiers should be judged by data-quality auditing, anatomy-informed modelling and multi-cohort evaluation. Anatomy-guided fusion improved transfer at no internal cost, but gains varied by cohort and thresholds did not transfer, so internal metrics alone do not establish reliable performance.

</details>


### [238] [Continuous-time 4D Reconstruction via Event-guided Latent Feature Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29838)
*Gwangtak Bae,Seunggu Kang,Junho Kim,Eun Sun Lee,Changwoon Choi,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续时间4D重建方法，在潜特征空间插值并辅以事件相机图像空间损失，实现任意时刻的3D结构与相机位姿预测，并用自监督框架解决配对数据缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型可将视频转为配准点云帧，但无法预测超出观测时间分辨率之外的时刻；事件相机提供微秒级连续证据，但难以直接融入基于RGB的基础模型。

Method: 将问题建模为潜特征空间中的插值，并在图像空间以事件损失作为辅助任务；利用基础模型的几何先验与事件损失的物理约束。针对配对数据缺失，提出自监督训练框架：以高帧率RGB视频估计的几何作为伪真值标签，并生成模拟事件。

Result: 大量实验表明该流程优于对输入RGB视频或估计几何进行时间插值的基线方法，并可泛化到未见过的真实场景；合成退化的控制实验显示其在运动模糊与低光照条件下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 在潜空间插值结合事件监督，可有效突破基础模型的时间分辨率限制，实现连续时间4D重建且具备鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Recent geometric foundation models can successfully convert a video sequence into frames of registered point clouds, but cannot predict moments beyond the observed temporal resolution. We propose a continuous-time 4D reconstruction method that extracts 3D structure and camera poses at any time instant, with additional guidance from event-camera measurements. While event cameras provide evidence at microsecond resolution to continuously fill the temporal gap, integrating them into existing RGB-based foundation models remains challenging. We formulate the problem as interpolation in the latent feature space and augment event-based losses in the image space as an auxiliary task to boost accuracy. Operating in the latent space leverages the powerful geometric priors of foundation models, while the event loss provides physically grounded guidance. To effectively address the lack of paired training data, we further propose a self-supervised training framework that uses pseudo-ground-truth labels of estimated geometry and simulated events generated from high-frame-rate RGB videos. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed pipeline outperforms baselines that temporally interpolate either the input RGB videos or the estimated geometry, and that it generalizes to unseen real-world scenarios. Controlled experiments with synthetic corruptions show that it remains robust under motion-blur and low-light conditions.

</details>


### [239] [Dynamics Forcing: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Causal Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29839)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamics Forcing 训练方案，在仅用真实视频监督、无需教师模型/蒸馏的前提下，通过重塑历史呈现方式缓解因果视频世界模型的误差累积与"复制上一帧"捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果视频世界模型逐块自回归生成，早期块的小误差被作为历史回灌并不断放大，最终导致场景与机器人崩坏。教师强制（teacher forcing）训练时使用干净真值历史，模型从未见过推理时真正条件到的历史；同时模型会学到"复制上一帧"的捷径，而非预测场景变化。

Method: 扰动历史（随机加噪、模型自重采样、联合去噪——预测下一块时最近的块仍部分含噪）；并将推理期使用历史引导与首帧锚点引导迁移到训练阶段，迫使模型利用历史与参考帧；无需教师模型、蒸馏，推理期不增加额外模型。在单 GPU 上以 LoRA 微调 5B 视频模型，使用 77 小时操作视频。

Result: 在未见过的真实续写视频上匹配度更高，并降低了误差累积。

Conclusion: Dynamics Forcing 是一种轻量、可扩展的训练方案，通过训练-推理一致性（exposure bias 缓解）与引导内化，有效抑制因果视频世界模型的误差累积和复制捷径，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Causal video world models generate block by block from their own previous outputs. Small errors in early blocks are fed back as history and grow until the scene and the robot fall apart. Teacher forcing trains on clean ground-truth history, so the model never sees the history it will actually condition on, and it also finds a shortcut: copying the previous frame instead of predicting how the scene changes. We present Dynamics Forcing, a training scheme that keeps real video as the only supervision and reshapes how history is presented. History is perturbed in three ways: random noising, self-resampling by the model itself, and joint denoising, in which the most recent blocks are still partly noisy when the next block is predicted. On top of this we move history guidance and first-frame anchor guidance from inference into training, so that the model is pushed to use its history and its reference frame rather than ignore them. Dynamics Forcing needs no teacher model, no distillation, and no additional model at inference. Applied to a 5B video model with LoRA on 77 hours of manipulation video on a single GPU, it better matches unseen real continuations and reduces error accumulation.

</details>


### [240] [Ego2Wrist: Bootstrapping Wrist-View Synthesis from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29850)
*Ziyao Zeng,Xiatao Sun,Hao Wang,Ziyu Guo,Youheng Yao,Jingcheng Ni,Shenyuan Gao,Fengyu Yang,Renrui Zhang,Bangya Liu,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego2Wrist 从人类头戴式自我中心视频与任务描述中合成同步的腕部相机视角视频，用于机器人操作策略训练，并提出无真值评估指标 HCC 以实现递归自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部相机视角与机器人操作直接相关，但大规模采集人类腕部相机视频因设备笨重、干扰自然手部动作而困难；相比之下头戴式自我中心视频更易规模化。因此需要将易获取的自我中心视频转化为腕部视角数据。

Method: 提出 Ego2Wrist，在约940小时成对头戴-腕部相机数据上训练，联合生成三路视角；提出手中心跨视角一致性（HCC）指标，在无真值条件下度量生成腕部视角与输入头部视角中手部区域的一致性。

Result: HCC 的模型检查点排序与基于真值的 LPIPS 相当，人类评测在77%情况下偏好 HCC 选出的生成结果；在四个数据集上较微调的 WorldWander 降低 LPIPS 28%，获得76%的人类偏好；并支持在野外自我中心视频上递归自改进，三轮迭代中质量持续提升。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了可扩展地生成真实世界腕部视角人类操作视频的途径，可用于机器人策略训练。

Abstract: Wrist cameras provide an action-centric view that is directly relevant to robot manipulation. However, collecting human wrist-camera videos at scale is challenging due to cumbersome setups that can interfere with natural hand movements. In contrast, head-mounted human egocentric videos are much easier to scale. We introduce Ego2Wrist, which synthesizes synchronized wrist-camera views from human egocentric videos and task descriptions. Trained on approximately 940 hours of paired head- and wrist-camera recordings, Ego2Wrist jointly generates three views. To evaluate generated wrist views without ground truth, we propose hand-centric cross-view consistency (HCC), which measures consistency between generated wrist views and corresponding hand regions in the input head view. HCC ranks model checkpoints comparably to ground-truth-based LPIPS, and human evaluators prefer HCC-selected generations in 77% of responses. Evaluated on four datasets, Ego2Wrist outperforms fine-tuned WorldWander, reducing LPIPS by 28% and receiving human preference in 76% of responses. More importantly, HCC facilitates recursive self-improvement on in-the-wild egocentric videos by selecting generated wrist views for further training. Generation quality progressively improves over three rounds. These results demonstrate a scalable approach to generating real-world wrist-view human manipulation videos for robot policy training.

</details>


### [241] [LexiDet-OVD: Benchmarking Lexical Sensitivity in Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29851)
*Amit Thakur,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LexiDet-OVD 基准与指标套件，系统研究开放词汇目标检测中的提示词（词汇）敏感性，发现强平均精度与词汇稳定性是两种独立属性，且无聚合方法全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇检测器依赖用户自由文本描述，但标准评测通常只用固定类别名，导致提示词引发的失败模式研究不足。

Method: 构建 LexiDet-OVD：将提示集分为等价、条件、宽泛语义三类范围；在固定 COCO（800 张，30 类）与 LVIS（1,000 张，30 类）样本上评测四个检测器家族。除均值与最差提示 AP50 外，还报告 P10、20% CVaR、留一提示影响、检测一致性、框一致性与置信度方差，采用一对一多实例匹配；并比较最大分数选择、加权框融合、Prompt Consensus 及一种探索性融合规则，另做 Detic/SHiNe 对照实验。

Result: 等价提示最优-最差差距在 COCO1000 为 5.3–23.4 点、在 LVIS1000-30c 为 5.1–29.9 点；聚合可部分挽回性能，但无方法在所有模型-数据集组合上占优；语义层级增强（SHiNe）并不能单独消除词汇敏感性。

Conclusion: 应在报告常规开放词汇检测精度的同时，报告提示分布稳健性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detectors localize objects from free-form text prompts, but their reliability depends on how users describe the target. Standard evaluations usually test a fixed class name, leaving prompt-induced failure modes underexplored. We introduce LexiDet-OVD, a benchmark and metric suite for studying lexical sensitivity in open-vocabulary object detection. We organize prompt sets into equivalent, conditional, and broad semantic scopes, and evaluate four detector families on fixed COCO and LVIS samples, each covering 30 classes (800 held-out COCO images and 1,000 LVIS images). Beyond mean and worst-prompt AP50, we report P10, CVaR at 20%, leave-one-prompt-out influence, detection agreement, box consistency, and confidence variance using one-to-one multi-instance matching. Equivalent-prompt best-to-worst gaps range from 5.3 to 23.4 points on COCO1000 and from 5.1 to 29.9 points on LVIS1000-30c, showing that strong average accuracy and lexical stability are distinct properties. We compare maximum-score selection, weighted-box fusion, Prompt Consensus, and an exploratory fusion rule. Aggregation recovers performance in several settings, but no method dominates all model-dataset pairs. A controlled Detic/SHiNe comparison further shows that semantic hierarchy enrichment does not by itself eliminate lexical sensitivity. These results support reporting prompt-distribution robustness alongside conventional open-vocabulary detection accuracy.

</details>


### [242] [Towards Agentic Video Corpus Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29877)
*Chenwei Xu,Shang Wu,Zhenyu Pan,Jianshu Zhang,Pranav Maneriker,Lie Lu,Fan Du,Manling Li,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RELAY：一个无需训练的VCMR框架，通过跨查询共享内存（查询库、视频笔记、提示缓存）实现检索与定位，并在三个VERIFIED基准上超越训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCMR方法依赖训练，且逐查询独立处理、重复计算；同时基准标注的时刻区间与人类标注存在偏差。

Method: 三阶段批处理：1）搜索阶段用嵌入模型按最佳匹配帧打分，并用查询库对在批次中对多查询高分的视频施加惩罚；2）排序阶段用VLM一次性为候选视频撰写场景/人物/物体/动作笔记，再由LLM重排；3）定位阶段用VLM预测时刻区间，多查询选中同一视频时通过提示缓存复用帧。另引入在少量标注查询上校准的单一margin以对齐基准标注惯例。

Result: 在三个VERIFIED基准上，无需训练即在视频检索R@1上超越训练基线；预测区间短于标注区间；VCMR在一个基准上与VideoSearch-R1相当，另两个基准在IoU 0.5上略逊；加入校准margin后，IoU 0.5下超越训练基线。

Conclusion: 跨查询内存共享与免训练流程对VCMR有效；区间长度差异源于标注惯例而非理解失败，简单margin校准即可弥合。

Abstract: Video corpus moment retrieval (VCMR) requires finding the video and time interval that match a text query in a corpus of videos. We present RELAY, a training-free framework for VCMR that shares memory across queries. RELAY processes a batch of queries in three stages: search with a query bank, ranking with video notes, and localization with video prompt caching. In the search stage, an embedding model scores each video by its best-matching frame. The query bank penalizes videos that score highly for many other queries in the batch. In the ranking stage, a vision-language model (VLM) writes notes on each candidate video's scenes, people, objects, and actions once. Then, a large language model (LLM) reranks the candidates from these notes. In the localization stage, a VLM predicts the time interval of the moment in the top-ranked video. When multiple queries select the same video, prompt caching lets the VLM reuse its frames. On the three VERIFIED benchmarks, RELAY outperforms the trained baselines in video retrieval R@1 without training. Without any labeled moment, its predicted intervals are shorter than the annotated ones. In VCMR, it achieves similar performance to VideoSearch-R1 on one benchmark and trails it at IoU 0.5 on the other two. Human annotators also mark shorter moments than the original annotations of the benchmarks. A single margin, calibrated on a few labeled queries, widens the predicted interval to the annotation convention of the benchmarks. With it, RELAY outperforms the trained baselines in VCMR at IoU 0.5.

</details>


### [243] [Reparameterizing Your Object Detectors for Weight Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29882)
*Chien-Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IR-ODWSP：用函数保持算子将检测器层提升到解耦子空间中进行免反向传播的权重编辑，诱导各向异性黎曼度量，实现零参数/零FLOPs增量下多检测器、多任务的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) 无需反向传播即可编辑网络权重，但直接编辑已收敛的目标检测器会出现停滞或崩溃：一方面集合预测会把重复框判为假阳性而产生惩罚，另一方面紧凑参数化使权重被困在各向同性欧氏极小值中。

Method: 提出IR-ODWSP，通过精确函数保持算子(E, C)将检测器层提升（lifting）到解耦的矩、2D-DCT、PCA与Stiefel-Procrustes子空间中，求解winner-exclusive更新后折叠回原空间（ΔParams=0%，ΔFLOPs=0%）；该过程诱导各向异性黎曼度量。支持桥接收缩、1.00x折叠、动态(IR-ODWSP-DYN)与静态(IR-ODWSP-SG)图，覆盖-100%至+100%延迟预算。

Result: 在17个YOLO26、EdgeCrafter、RF-DETR检测器上全部26/26个MS COCO设置均提升（最高+0.31 APs）；跨盆地COCO-O +1.42 mAP；零图像修复+2.95 mAP；无标签天气自适应+2.79 AP。-10%延迟保留51.62 AP/33.48 mAP；正预算单调扩展（+10%时+0.30 AP，RF-DETR-N上+1.12；+100%时54.83 AP；IR-ODWSP+达55.91 AP/35.44 mAP）。

Conclusion: IR-ODWSP在免重训练、零参数与零计算开销前提下，稳定实现目标检测器的权重空间编程，并在精度、跨域鲁棒性与延迟可调性上取得一致收益。

Abstract: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) edits network weights without backpropagation, yet directly editing converged object detectors stalls or collapses because set prediction penalizes duplicate boxes as false positives while compact parameterizations trap weights in an isotropic Euclidean minimum. We propose Inverse-Reparameterized Object-Detection WSP (IR-ODWSP), lifting detector layers via exact function-preserving operators (E, C) into decoupled moment, 2D-DCT, PCA, and Stiefel-Procrustes subspaces. Solving winner-exclusive updates and folding back (Delta Params=0%, Delta FLOPs=0%) induces an anisotropic Riemannian metric, improving all 26/26 MS COCO settings across 17 YOLO26, EdgeCrafter, and RF-DETR detectors (up to +0.31 APs), +1.42 mAP cross-basin COCO-O, zeroimage repair (+2.95 mAP), and label-free weather adaptation (+2.79 AP). Without retraining, IR-ODWSP supports -100% to +100% latency budgets across bridged contraction, 1.00x folding, dynamic (IR-ODWSP-DYN), and static (IR-ODWSP-SG) graphs: -10% retains 51.62 AP / 33.48 mAP, while positive budgets scale monotonically (+0.30 AP at +10%, +1.12 on RF-DETR-N; 54.83 AP at +100%; 55.91 AP / 35.44 mAP in IR-ODWSP+). Source code is released at https://github.com/WongKinYiu/ODWSP.

</details>


### [244] [Segment Anything In-Context via Visual Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29896)
*Yuzhu Wang,Manni Duan,Hongliang Ye,Shu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAYD——基于视觉示范（提示图像及其掩码）的上下文分割基础模型，通过提示与查询图像间的视觉对应分割连贯区域，在24个域外基准上超越现有上下文分割与开放词汇分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割任务中，许多目标依赖难以用语言明确表述的隐性视觉知识，语言提示存在歧义与表达瓶颈，因此需要以视觉示范替代文本提示来实现对新任务的泛化分割。

Method: 在12个原用于全景分割的公开数据集上训练；提出两项技术：(1) 轻量级掩码解码器实现与提示无关的掩码生成，提升计算效率；(2) 学习机制以识别提示中未出现的新查询类别，增强域外泛化；通过建立提示与查询图像间的视觉对应完成分割。

Result: 在覆盖自然、生物、生态、宇宙、医学、病理及卫星影像的24个域外基准上，显著优于现有上下文分割与开放词汇分割方法。

Conclusion: 以视觉示范为条件的上下文分割可作为一种通用基础模型范式，兼顾效率与跨域泛化，适用于语言难以描述的分割任务。

Abstract: We present the Segment Anything Model via Visual Demonstrations (SAYD), a foundation model for in-context segmentation. Given visual demos comprising prompt images and their segmentation masks, SAYD segments coherent regions (e.g., objects or their parts) in a query image by establishing visual correspondences between the prompts and the query. SAYD generalizes to previously-unseen segmentation tasks, including those requiring tacit visual knowledge or involving concepts that are difficult to articulate unambiguously in language. We train SAYD on 12 public datasets originally curated for panoptic segmentation using our proposed techniques, including a lightweight mask decoder for prompt-agnostic mask generation and a learning mechanism for recognizing novel query classes absent in the prompts. These innovations enhance both computational efficiency and out-of-domain generalization. Consequently, SAYD significantly outperforms existing methods of in-context segmentation and open-vocabulary segmentation across 24 out-of-domain benchmarks, spanning natural, biological, ecological, cosmological, medical, pathological, and satellite imagery.

</details>


### [245] [SpectralRoute: Adaptive Spectral Routing for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Hyperspectral Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29919)
*Qingmei Li,Xiaoya Fan,Juepeng Zheng,Jianxi Huang,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架SpectralRoute，通过构建互补光谱视图、引入外部光谱知识增强类别描述、类自适应光谱路由与物理光谱先验，实现开放词汇高光谱语义分割，在三个高光谱基准上免训练超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割工作多基于三通道光学影像或有限波段多光谱数据；预训练视觉-语言模型难以直接利用高光谱影像的丰富光谱信息，需额外适配；且不同地物类别依赖不同的光谱线索，统一融合会削弱这些判别性线索。

Method: 免训练框架：(1) 从HSI构建互补光谱视图，并用外部光谱知识丰富类别描述，使冻结的VLM提取多样化光谱-语义响应；(2) 类自适应光谱路由机制，为每个类别选择性放大判别性光谱响应；(3) 结合物理光谱先验，细化视觉相似地物类别的预测。

Result: 在三个高光谱基准数据集上的实验表明，SpectralRoute无需额外训练即可持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 面向高光谱影像的开放词汇分割可通过互补光谱视图、知识增强描述与类自适应路由，在不训练VLM的前提下有效激活其光谱感知能力，为遥感开放词汇分割提供免训练新范式。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation enables the recognition of arbitrary categories through natural language descriptions and has recently been extended from natural images to remote sensing. However, existing approaches primarily focus on three-channel optical imagery or limited-band multispectral data, while pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to directly exploit the rich spectral information of hyperspectral imagery (HSI) without additional adaptation. More importantly, different land-cover categories rely on distinct spectral cues, which may be weakened by uniform fusion. To address these challenges, we propose SpectralRoute, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary hyperspectral segmentation. Specifically, we construct complementary spectral views from HSI and enrich category descriptions with external spectral knowledge, enabling frozen vision-language models to extract diverse spectral-semantic responses. We then introduce a class-adaptive spectral routing mechanism that selectively amplifies discriminative spectral responses for each category, overcoming the limitations of uniform fusion. Furthermore, physical spectral priors are incorporated to refine predictions for visually similar land-cover categories. Experiments on three hyperspectral benchmarks demonstrate that SpectralRoute consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods without additional training.

</details>


### [246] [Dynamic Gaussian Scene Reconstruction by Integrating Events and Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29920)
*Zihao Yu,Guozhen Liu,Jie Yan,Jialiang Liu,Panlong Tan,Qinglin Sun,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出事件-结构协同动态高斯重建方法 ESC-DGR，利用事件观测摆脱对现有高斯支撑与光度收敛的依赖，在 E-D3DGS 基准上取得最优重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动的动态高斯重建依赖两个假设：现有高斯可吸收重建误差；光度优化已充分收敛从而可用解析方差估计可靠性。快速运动下两者均失效——前者导致无支撑区域无法生成稠密化候选，后者使未收敛的运动区域高斯被排除作为运动参考。

Method: 利用与上述两条件无关的事件观测：事件首先识别需要额外支撑的变化，且仅在渲染深度有效处放置；再通过在线渲染统计与历史事件证据，将位移估计从可靠高斯传播至弱观测邻域，获得帧间时间尺度的主导运动参考；目标时刻事件进一步约束活跃高斯的有界位置残差。支撑放置、参考选择与残差预测在同一优化过程内作用于同一演化表示。

Result: 在 E-D3DGS 基准的事件驱动单目动态场景重建中取得所评估方法中最优质量：合成与真实场景动态区域 PSNR（dPSNR）分别提升 1.02 dB 与 1.45 dB；八个合成场景动态高斯总数减少 8.5%；平均帧率相对 E-D3DGS 下降约 21%–25%，但十二个场景均保持 80 FPS 以上。

Conclusion: 以事件信息替代失效假设，可在单一优化流程中协同完成支撑增广、参考选择与残差预测，实现质量提升与表示紧凑，且保持实时渲染性能。

Abstract: Existing approaches to event-based dynamic Gaussian reconstruction rely on two assumptions for support augmentation and reliability estimation. First, existing Gaussians can absorb reconstruction errors. Second, photometric optimization has converged sufficiently to estimate reliability using analytical variance. Both assumptions can fail under fast motion. When the first fails, regions without Gaussian support cannot generate densification candidates. When the second fails, Gaussians in moving regions where optimization has not converged may be excluded as motion references. We propose Event-Structure Collaborative Dynamic Gaussian Reconstruction (ESC-DGR), using event observations independent of both conditions. Events first identify changes that require additional support, placed only where rendered depth is valid. The augmented representation still needs reliable motion references. Online rendering statistics and historical event evidence therefore propagate displacement estimates from reliable Gaussians to weakly observed neighborhoods. These references describe dominant motion at the frame-interval timescale. Target-time events therefore further condition bounded position residuals for active Gaussians. Support placement, reference selection, and residual prediction thus operate on the same evolving representation within one optimization run. For event-driven monocular dynamic scene reconstruction, our method achieves the best reconstruction quality among the evaluated methods on the E-D3DGS benchmark. Relative to E-D3DGS, dynamic-region PSNR (dPSNR) improves by 1.02 and 1.45 dB on synthetic and real scenes, respectively. The total dynamic Gaussian count across eight synthetic scenes decreases by 8.5%. Although mean frame rate decreases by about 21%-25% relative to E-D3DGS, rendering remains above 80 FPS in all twelve scenes. Code: https://github.com/zh-yu1/ESC-DGR.

</details>


### [247] [Benchmarking VLM Medical Hallucination across 5 Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29934)
*Zhixing Cao,Yusen Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multi-Med，一个包含 1000 个图像-问题对、覆盖五种医学影像模态的幻觉评测基准，并对 10 个常用视觉语言模型进行了跨模态、跨问题类型的系统实验研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学领域视觉语言模型（VLM）的幻觉问题仍难以理解，模型究竟在何处失效尚不清楚；已有医学幻觉基准普遍缺乏对不同医学影像模态的广泛覆盖。

Method: 构建 Multi-Med 基准，含 1,000 个图像-问题对，覆盖五种影像模态；在此基础上，对 10 个广泛使用的 VLM 在五种模态和四类问题类型下开展深入实验分析。

Result: 模型表现随影像模态和问题类型的不同而差异显著；表现最好的若干模型之间的差异在统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 医学 VLM 幻觉受模态与问题类型强烈影响，需按维度细粒度评估；顶尖模型间的性能差距有限，提示当前评测与模型改进仍有较大空间。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) within the medical domain remain a difficult and not well-understood problem, and it is still unclear where these models break down. Although several benchmarks targeting medical hallucination have been introduced, they often lack broad coverage across different medical imaging modalities. We introduce Multi-Med, a medical hallucination benchmark containing 1,000 image-question pairs spanning five distinct modalities. Using this benchmark, we perform an in-depth experimental study of 10 widely used VLMs across five modalities and four question categories. Our findings indicate that model performance differs substantially depending on the imaging modality and question type, and that the differences among the best-performing models are not statistically significant.

</details>


### [248] [KGLP-Net: Kalman-Reliable Global--Local Learning With Group Fisher Pruning for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29944)
*Wenshuai Deng,Wen Xie,Abdel Hamid Soliman,Qianzheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KGLP-Net框架，将不确定性感知的全局-局部特征融合与可靠性引导的结构化剪枝统一起来，实现高精度且轻量的高光谱图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSI分类需同时整合大范围空间上下文与目标像素邻域内的细粒度光谱-空间信息，但这两类互补表示在异质场景中的可靠性差异较大；此外，特征融合过程中产生的不确定性估计很少被用于指导模型压缩。

Method: 1）非对称双视图架构：Global Mamba Encoder捕获长程空间依赖，Local CNN Encoder提取邻域细粒度光谱-空间特征；2）Kalman-Reliable Fusion（KRF）在特征空间估计两路表示的相对不确定性，执行Kalman式后验更新，将全局特征视为上下文先验、局部特征视为观测；3）Kalman-Reliable Group Fisher Pruning（KRGF）以归一化后验精度量化组级可靠性，并与Group Fisher重要性结合，识别并剪除Kalman耦合通道组，实现结构一致的压缩。

Result: 在四个公开HSI数据集上，隐藏宽度为48的Compact48模型在十个代表性对比分类器中取得最高总体精度；相较未剪枝的Dense64，参数量、FLOPs与推理时间平均分别降低38.5%、34.8%和37.6%，平均总体精度仅下降0.03个百分点。

Conclusion: 将融合阶段的后验不确定性显式用于剪枝重要性度量，可在近乎无损精度的前提下实现显著的结构化压缩，验证了"不确定性引导压缩"思路在HSI分类中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires both the effective integration of broad spatial context and fine-grained spectral--spatial information surrounding target pixels. However, the reliability of these complementary representations may vary considerably across heterogeneous scenes, and uncertainty estimates obtained during feature fusion are rarely exploited to guide subsequent model compression. To address these challenges, we propose Kalman-Reliable Global--Local Learning with Group Fisher Pruning (KGLP-Net), a unified framework that couples uncertainty-aware feature fusion with reliability-guided structured pruning. Specifically, an asymmetric dual-view architecture employs a Global Mamba Encoder to capture long-range spatial dependencies and a Local CNN Encoder to extract fine-grained spectral--spatial features from target neighborhoods. To adaptively integrate these complementary representations, Kalman-Reliable Fusion (KRF) estimates their relative uncertainties in feature space and performs a Kalman-inspired posterior update, treating global features as a contextual prior and local features as an observation. Building upon the resulting posterior uncertainty, Kalman-Reliable Group Fisher Pruning (KRGF) quantifies group-level reliability through normalized posterior precision and integrates it with Group Fisher importance to identify and prune Kalman-coupled channel groups, enabling structurally consistent model compression. Experiments on four public HSI datasets demonstrate that the resulting Compact48 model, with a hidden width of 48, achieves the highest overall accuracy among ten representative competing classifiers. Compared with the unpruned Dense64 model, Compact48 reduces the number of parameters, FLOPs, and inference time by 38.5%, 34.8% and 37.6% on average, respectively, while incurring only a 0.03-percentage-point decrease in mean overall accuracy.

</details>


### [249] [When Should We Trust Medical Image Models? Evaluating Confidence Reliability under Cross-Dataset Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29955)
*Haoran Zhang,Sarah Lam,Yuting Lin,Nuo Chen,Mingyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在CheXpert训练、MIMIC-CXR测试的胸部X光跨数据集迁移中，域内最优模型未必跨域最优；AUC下降7-13个百分点，源域温度缩放改善ECE有限却系统性提高错误预测的置信度，且DenseNet依赖的集中空间先验迁移性差于ResNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片深度学习模型在基准数据集上表现良好，但跨医院部署时的可靠性与置信度行为尚不清楚；分布与标注差异会同时影响预测准确性和模型置信度，需要受控的实证研究。

Method: 在CheXpert上训练ResNet-50与DenseNet-121，在MIMIC-CXR上测试，覆盖五种实验设置，聚焦气胸与胸腔积液。用AUC衡量预测性能，用ECE、Brier分数及错误预测平均置信度衡量置信可靠性，均给出95% bootstrap置信区间；并评估在源域验证集上拟合的事后温度缩放，用Grad-CAM分析跨域差异机制。

Result: 跨域AUC下降7-13个百分点；域内最优模型并非跨域最优；源域温度缩放对ECE仅带来边际变化，但在全部四种模型-病理组合上系统性提高错误预测的置信度；Grad-CAM显示DenseNet依赖集中空间先验而迁移失效，ResNet的分布式注意力泛化更好。

Conclusion: 医学AI评估应纳入跨站点验证以及面向安全性的校准指标，而非仅报告域内基准性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models for chest X-ray analysis often achieve strong performance on benchmark datasets, but their reliability under cross-dataset deployment remains unclear. In clinical practice, models trained in one hospital system may be applied to data from another, where distributional and labeling differences can affect both prediction accuracy and model confidence.
  We conduct a controlled empirical study of model confidence under cross-dataset transfer. We train ResNet-50 and DenseNet-121 on CheXpert and evaluate them on MIMIC-CXR across five experimental settings, focusing on Pneumothorax and Pleural Effusion. We measure predictive performance using AUC and confidence reliability using Expected Calibration Error (ECE), Brier score, and average confidence on incorrect predictions, all reported with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. We further evaluate post-hoc temperature scaling fit on the source validation set and use Grad-CAM to examine the mechanism behind cross-domain performance differences.
  Cross-domain AUC drops 7-13 percentage points, and the best in-domain model is not the best cross-domain model. Source-domain temperature scaling produces marginal ECE changes but systematically increases confidence on incorrect predictions across all four model-pathology combinations. Grad-CAM reveals that DenseNet relies on a concentrated spatial prior that fails to transfer, while ResNet's more distributed attention generalizes better. These findings argue for cross-site validation and safety-specific calibration metrics in medical AI evaluation.

</details>


### [250] [FASCIA: Seeing Beneath the Surface with Queryable, Personalized Anatomy in Any Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29970)
*R. James Cotton,Tim Unger,Lucinda Williamson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FASCIA：以MHR为锚点，由外表面体形与骨骼比例预测并摆姿个体内部解剖结构（290结构群体模板经平滑可逆warp映射）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一外形的人内部解剖可存在显著差异，而现有外部体形/骨架拟合无法给出个体化内部解剖；需要可从图像、视频或任何输出MHR的系统直接驱动的内部解剖建模方法，以支撑个体化数字孪生。

Method: 提出FASCIA框架：将单一290结构群体模板通过平滑可逆warp映射到个体；用依赖体形的高斯先验预测平均warp坐标及受试者间变异性；warp形变学习得到的基模板生成骨骼、肌肉、器官与脂肪库；将MHR蒙皮（skinning）扩展到内部以实现直接监督；在4个自动标注CT队列与1个全身MRI队列上训练。

Result: 82例留出CT上，命名骨骼平均体积Dice 0.48、肌肉0.63，多数骨骼与肌肉保持单连通，并给出去校准的体素概率；33例留出全身MRI上，逐点场在全部腔室上组织重叠优于HIT；全身脂肪百分比个体间差异预测R²=0.88（女性，对比0.38）、0.54（男性，对比−0.01）；节段质心恢复误差在1.1 mm内。

Conclusion: FASCIA仅需MHR拟合即可工作，可由图像、视频或任何输出MHR的系统驱动，为基于视频运动数据的个体化校准肌骨数字孪生奠定基础。

Abstract: We present FASCIA (*Functional Anatomical Structures and Composition via Implicit Atlas*), a Momentum Human Rig (MHR)-anchored framework that predicts and poses a person's internal anatomy from their external body shape and skeletal proportions. FASCIA maps a single 290-structure population template onto each individual through a smooth, invertible warp. As people with the same shape can differ internally, a body-dependent Gaussian prior predicts both average warp coordinates and their inter-subject variability. The warp deforms a learned base template into bones, muscles, organs, and fat depots. Extending MHR skinning into the interior enables direct supervision of this model across four automatically labeled CT cohorts and one whole-body MRI cohort. On 82 held-out CT scans, FASCIA reaches a mean volumetric Dice (volume agreement) of 0.48 on named bones and 0.63 on muscles, keeps the majority of bones and muscles in one piece, and yields calibrated voxel probabilities. On 33 held-out whole-body MRI subjects, FASCIA's pointwise field improves tissue overlap over HIT across all compartments, predicts between-person differences in whole-body adipose percentage ($R^2 = 0.88$ vs. $0.38$ in women and $0.54$ vs. $-0.01$ in men), and recovers segment centers of mass within 1.1 mm. Because it needs only an MHR fit, FASCIA can be driven from images, video, or any system that outputs MHR. This opens the door to fitting individualized calibrated musculoskeletal digital twins to video motion data.

</details>


### [251] [One Size Doesn't Fit All: Input Adaptive Class Activation Mapping for Improved Visual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29993)
*Ayesha Sahar,Tahir Mustafa Madni,Tehseen Zia,David Windridge,Uzair Iqbal Janjua,Waleed Umar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADAPT-CAM是一种基于梯度的参数自适应类激活图框架，融合一阶梯度能量与激活加权二阶曲率计算通道贡献，并引入自适应局部对比度归一化与knee引导的软通道选择，在ImageNet和Oxford 102 Flowers上跨CNN与ViT架构取得竞争性或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN与ViT可解释性有限，影响安全关键场景的信任与部署；现有CAM方法在通道加权、定位精度和跨架构适应性上存在不足，易产生噪声大或不精确的解释。

Method: 提出ADAPT-CAM：以梯度能量与激活加权的二阶曲率共同推导通道贡献；加入自适应局部对比度归一化与knee引导的软通道选择精化解释；关键参数对每个输入独立估计，以适应不同网络架构的特征表示差异。

Result: 在ImageNet与Oxford 102 Flowers数据集、CNN与ViT架构上验证，多项定量指标上达到与主流CAM方法相当或更优的性能；定性对比显示其能有效突出判别性区域；参数分析与消融实验考察了各自适应组件的行为与贡献。

Conclusion: ADAPT-CAM在不同架构与数据集上具有良好的适用性，能够生成更精准、自适应的视觉解释。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use in tasks such as image classification, object detection and semantic segmentation, the limited interpretability of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) can undermine trust and hinder adoption in safety-critical applications. Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods seek to address this by providing human-understandable explanations for model predictions. Class Activation Maps (CAM) constitute one such widely used XAI technique for visualizing discriminative regions. However, existing CAM methods may exhibit limitations in channel weighting, localization, and adaptability across network architectures potentially resulting in noisy or imprecise explanations. To address these issues, we propose ADAPT-CAM, a gradient-based, parameter-adaptive CAM framework. The method combines first-order gradient energy with activation-weighted second-order curvature to derive channel contributions. It further incorporates adaptive local contrast normalization and knee-guided soft channel selection to refine the resulting explanations. Importantly, key parameters are estimated independently for each input, allowing the framework to adapt to variations in feature representations across network architectures. We validate ADAPT-CAM on ImageNet and Oxford 102 Flowers across CNN and ViT architectures. Quantitative results demonstrate competitive or superior performance against established CAM methods across multiple evaluation metrics. In parallel, qualitative comparisons illustrate its ability to highlight discriminative image regions. Further parameter analysis and ablation experiments examine the behaviour and contributions of the individual adaptive components. These findings demonstrate the applicability of ADAPT-CAM across different architectures and datasets.

</details>


### [252] [Antiphony: A Dual-Process Agent for Deepfake Video Detection with Real-Video Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29999)
*Xinyu Yan,Yurong Hao,Zherui Li,Yebo Wu,Tiantong Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Zihao Zeng,Zheng Lin,Zijie Wang,Pengpeng Qiao,Yang Cao,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Antiphony 框架：以真实视频为共同参考，实现四个视觉专家的校准融合与近阈值样本的选择性调查，在未见生成器上取得显著优于基线（平均 88.3%）的检测性能，并提供有限样本虚警控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频深度伪造检测需要在未见生成器上泛化，同时控制真实视频的误报。现有检测器虽能捕获多样视觉线索，但在生成器偏移下易产生过度自信的错误，削弱基于置信度融合的可靠性；阈值校准又受真实采集域偏移及缺乏未来生成器样本的困扰。

Method: Antiphony 由两阶段构成：(1) 专家融合阶段，以真实视频作为分数融合与阈值校准的共享参考，无需目标深度伪造样本即可更新阈值；并在评分程序独立于校准、真实样本可交换的假设下给出有限样本虚警控制；(2) 对落入校准阈值附近的视频，由工具调用的多模态调查器（investigator）进一步审查并输出额外融合分数。调查器在生成器留出预测与反事实专家输出上训练，以增强对不可靠专家证据的鲁棒性。

Result: 在六个基准协议上，Antiphony 平均基准得分 88.3%，分别比最强 MLLM 基线与传统检测器基线高 14.7 与 11.5 分；平均优于 ARGUS 20.2 分，在 FaceVid 的 20 个未见生成器上平衡准确率高 3.6 分。

Conclusion: 以真实视频为锚的校准融合结合选择性调查，可在无目标伪造样本条件下实现阈值更新与虚警可控，并在跨生成器泛化上显著超越现有 MLLM 与传统检测器方法。

Abstract: Reliable video deepfake detection requires generalization to unseen generators while limiting false alarms on authentic footage. Although existing detectors capture diverse visual cues, they may produce overconfident errors under generator shift, limiting the reliability of confidence-based fusion. Threshold calibration is further complicated by shifts in authentic capture domains and the lack of future-generator examples. To address these challenges, we present Antiphony, a framework that combines calibrated fusion of four visual experts with selective investigation. Authentic videos serve as a shared reference for score fusion and threshold calibration, enabling threshold updates without target deepfakes. For this expert-fusion stage, we establish finite-sample false-alarm control when the scoring procedure is fixed independently of calibration and authentic samples are exchangeable. To refine decisions near the calibrated threshold, a tool-using multimodal investigator examines these videos and contributes an additional fusion score. We train the investigator on generator-held-out predictions and counterfactual expert outputs, to improve robustness to unreliable expert evidence. Across six benchmark protocols, Antiphony achieves a mean benchmark score of 88.3%, exceeding the strongest evaluated MLLM and conventional detector baselines by 14.7 and 11.5 points, respectively. It also outperforms ARGUS by 20.2 points on average and by 3.6 balanced-accuracy points on FaceVid's 20 unseen generators.

</details>


### [253] [MORSE: Optimizing Streaming Memory Design via Open-World Auto-Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30011)
*Ce Zhang,Jianshu Zhang,Jinxi He,Xiyuan Yang,Pan Wang,Han Liu,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORSE 是一个开放世界自动研究框架，通过将流式视频中的反复出现的失败按原因分组，在线检索论文、模型卡和代码以适配已有方法或预训练感知模型，并同步修订 harness 的存储与检索机制，在冻结的 video-language 模型上提升答案质量与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频系统必须在单次通过、帧存储受限的条件下决定保留何种证据，而未来问题所需细节事先未知。现有递归自我改进方法虽能自动化设计存储与检索 harness，但其编辑仍只能依赖自身先验知识和预定义工具集来构造修复方案，难以应对反复出现的失败模式。

Method: 提出 MORSE（Memory Optimization with Recursive Self-Evolvement）。其编辑器先将训练轨迹中的失败按原因聚类，随后检索论文、模型卡与代码，寻找可适配的方法或可用的预训练感知模型；当引入新的感知能力时，同步修订 harness 对相应证据的存储与检索方式，且保持视频-语言模型冻结。在留出验证流上，先选择提升答案质量的修订，再在给定容差内寻找保持增益的更快修订。

Result: 在三个流式视频基准上，MORSE 在每个冻结骨干对比中均取得最高答案准确率。在 OVO-Bench 上，基于 Qwen3-VL-8B 的 harness 平均 0.30 秒完成查询，准确率达 75.1%，比同冻结骨干的次优 harness 高 3.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以反复失败为驱动的开放世界在线检索式自我进化，能在不更新底层模型的前提下有效改进流式视频的记忆 harness，并在准确率与延迟上同时取得优势。

Abstract: A streaming video system must decide in a single pass what evidence to retain under bounded frame storage, before it knows which details a future question will require. Recent recursive self-improvement methods automate the design of the harness that stores and retrieves this evidence. They revise the harness in response to failures, but the editor must still devise each repair from its prior knowledge and a predefined set of tools. A failure that recurs across videos, however, may point to a problem that prior work has already addressed. We introduce MORSE (Memory Optimization with Recursive Self-Evolvement), an open-world auto-research framework that uses recurring failures to guide an online search for solutions. Its editor groups failures in training traces by cause, then searches papers, model cards, and code for methods it can adapt or pretrained perception models it can use. When the editor introduces a new perceptual capability, it can also revise how the harness stores and retrieves the resulting evidence for a frozen video-language model. On held-out validation streams, MORSE first selects revisions that improve answer quality, then seeks faster revisions that preserve those gains within a specified tolerance. Across three streaming video benchmarks, MORSE consistently achieves the highest answer accuracy within each frozen-backbone comparison. On OVO-Bench, our Qwen3-VL-8B harness answers queries in 0.30 seconds on average while achieving 75.1% accuracy, 3.1 percentage points ahead of the next-best evaluated harness using the same frozen backbone.

</details>


### [254] [FlexGaze: Weakly Supervised Gaze Estimation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30012)
*Zongjian Li,Mohammad Soleymani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexGaze 利用现成模型自动生成约324万头实例的伪3D注视标签进行弱监督预训练，仅凭头部裁剪图即可估计3D注视，在八个基准上以更少参数达到或超越公开模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视估计模型多依赖专用设备采集的数据训练，其被试、场景与头姿多样性不足，导致对新条件泛化能力差。

Method: 用现成模型预测2D注视目标与场景深度，融合生成伪3D注视标签（约324万头实例）做弱监督预训练；推理阶段仅输入头部裁剪图，无需人脸检测与头姿估计，可处理侧脸与后脑视角；训练时采用数据增强提升对裁剪位置与尺度的鲁棒性；随后在公开注视数据集上监督微调。

Result: 仅用自动生成标签预训练即可泛化到未见基准；微调后在统一评测协议下的八个基准上，多数基准上匹敌或超越参数量为其数倍的公开注视估计器。

Conclusion: 弱监督伪标签预训练是提升注视估计泛化性的有效途径；作者公开生成标签数据集、全部训练模型与代码。

Abstract: Most gaze estimation models are trained on data collected using specialized equipment. The limited diversity of subjects, scenes, and head poses in these datasets hinders generalization to new conditions. We present FlexGaze, a family of 3D gaze estimators pretrained with weak supervision from gaze labels (pseudo-labels) automatically generated from in-the-wild images. We use off-the-shelf models to predict 2D gaze targets and scene depth, then combine these predictions to generate 3D gaze labels for approximately 3.24 million head instances. At inference, FlexGaze predicts gaze from a head crop alone and can handle profile and rear views without requiring face detection or head pose estimation. We use data augmentation during training to improve robustness to variations in crop position and scale. Pretrained using only these generated gaze labels, FlexGaze generalizes to unseen benchmarks. After supervised fine-tuning on public gaze datasets, we evaluate FlexGaze on eight benchmarks under a unified evaluation protocol. Our models match or outperform publicly available gaze estimators with several times as many parameters on most benchmarks. We release our dataset of generated gaze labels, all trained models, and code via https://github.com/ihp-lab/FlexGaze.

</details>


### [255] [3AGameFactory-Motion: Agentic Rigging and Reliable Complex Motion Generation for Diverse 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30013)
*Bohan Zeng,Kaixin Zhu,Yifan Yang,Dongge Yu,Zihao Wu,Yifan Dai,Zhou Liu,Junbo Niu,Xiaochen Ma,Shiran Ge,Zimo Meng,Junzhu Liang,Huanyao Zhang,Jinhang Liu,Tianqi Xiao,Jinhao Zhang,Tianyi Bai,Zhixiong Zeng,Penghao Zhao,Yuanbo Peng,Key Zhang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DivDynBench 基准（六类形态、含多阶段部件协调与显式时序/角度/重复/轨迹约束的指令），系统对比三类动作生成范式，并提出 Agentic Motion Framework：由智能体编写紧凑参数化骨架与动作程序，确定性执行器完成绑定、运动学求解与几何修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从指令驱动任意 3D 资产动画化需要结构推理与运动控制，但现有运动基准多局限于常规人/动物骨架与简单指令，缺乏对跨形态、复杂约束指令下绑定与运动生成能力的评估。

Method: 构建 DivDynBench：覆盖六种形态类别，指令含多阶段部件协调及显式时序、角度、重复次数、轨迹约束。基于大语言模型系统比较三种范式——专用运动生成、视频生成后运动恢复、直接由智能体驱动生成，分析其控制能力、跨形态适应性与生成成本。据此提出 Agentic Motion Framework：智能体输出紧凑的参数化骨架与动作程序，确定性执行器负责绑定、运动学求解与几何修正。

Result: 对比分析揭示了三种范式在控制精度、跨形态泛化与生成开销上的差异；所提框架借助紧凑参数控制降低了生成开销，并提升了绑定可靠性与运动控制精度。

Conclusion: 紧凑参数化程序 + 智能体规划、确定性执行的组合为复杂 3D 运动生成的实际部署提供了可行路径与实践指导。

Abstract: Animating arbitrary 3D assets from instructions requires both structural inference and motion control, yet existing motion benchmarks mostly cover conventional human or animal skeletons with simple instructions. We introduce DivDynBench, which evaluates rigging and motion generation across six morphology categories, with instructions requiring multi-phase part coordination and explicit timing, angle, repetition, and trajectory constraints. With large language models, we systematically compare three common paradigms: specialized motion generation, video generation followed by motion recovery, and direct agent-driven generation. We analyze their control capabilities, cross-morphology adaptability, and generation costs. Guided by this comparison, we propose an Agentic Motion Framework in which an agent writes compact parametric skeleton and motion programs, and a deterministic executor performs rigging, kinematic solving, and geometric correction. Compact parameter control reduces generation overhead and improves rigging reliability and motion-control accuracy, providing practical guidance for deploying complex 3D motion generation.

</details>


### [256] [ReplaySAM: Zero-Shot 3D Particle-Pack Segmentation with SAM 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30016)
*Bogong Wang,Yulai Zhang,Philipp D. Lösel,Andrew Kingston,Mark Knackstedt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReplaySAM 无训练地将 SAM2 适配到三维颗粒堆积的实例分割：用前景几何自动生成点提示，并融合正交切片序列上的预测以恢复颗粒实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XCT 可获得颗粒堆积的非破坏性三维图像，但从中提取颗粒级测量需先识别并分离单个颗粒；高密度、不规则堆积中该任务仍困难。现有流程依赖人工修正或任务特定训练，可提示模型又缺乏自动定位个体颗粒并恢复实例掩码的策略。

Method: 基于预训练 SAM2，不额外训练：从前景几何生成点提示以自动定位颗粒，沿正交切片序列分别推理，并融合跨序列的 SAM2 预测得到三维颗粒实例。

Result: 在真实石英与铁矿石颗粒堆积的 XCT 体数据上，平均实例 Dice 高于专家辅助的分水岭方法和 nnInteractive（在所评估协议下）。

Conclusion: ReplaySAM 提供了免训练的三维颗粒堆积实例分割框架，几何驱动提示消除了人工提示放置。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (XCT) provides non-destructive three-dimensional (3D) images of particle packs, making their internal structure accessible for quantitative analysis. Extracting particle-level measurements from these volumes first requires identifying and separating individual particles, a task that remains challenging in dense, irregular packs. Existing workflows often require manual correction or task-specific training, while promptable models need a strategy for automatically locating individual particles and recovering their instance masks. We present ReplaySAM, which adapts the pretrained Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to three-dimensional particle-pack segmentation without additional training. It generates point prompts from foreground geometry and fuses SAM2 predictions across orthogonal slice sequences to recover particle instances. On XCT volumes of real quartz and iron-ore particle packs, ReplaySAM achieves higher mean instance Dice scores than expert-assisted watershed and nnInteractive under the evaluated protocols. ReplaySAM thus provides a training-free framework for 3D particle-pack instance segmentation, with geometry-based prompting that eliminates manual prompt placement.

</details>


### [257] [MOVER: Planning Beyond Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30022)
*Xiangyu Li,Yu Jiang,Tianyi Wang,Liang He,Qixing Huang,Zhaomiao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOVER：利用"历史依赖但残差证据弱"的事件（如等待行人、静止车辆）为驾驶场景规划提供补充路由，代价仅4.1ms/帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差世界模型仅通过时间变化预测场景，导致时间上恒定（变化微弱）却对规划关键的证据（等待的行人、停止的车辆）在残差通路中被抹除。作者追问此类弱时序变化证据能否被更有效用于规划。

Method: 提出互补的事件路由机制MOVER。训练期教师：将过去帧从共享历史中移除，度量每个空间落地的event query的响应敏感度，并与该事件的池化时间残差对比，优先选择"依赖历史但残差证据弱"的事件；学生网络从无扰动历史中预测这些优先级，故教师不增加推理开销。在固定总路由质量约束下，用同一分布调节低秩未来BEV更新，并偏置轨迹条件的事件读取。

Result: nuScenes上，MOVER将ResWorld的UniAD式平均L2从0.65 m降至0.55 m，碰撞率从0.23%降至0.12%（无直接自车状态；含自车状态时为0.50 m / 0.09%），每帧增加4.1 ms。匹配对照实验表明互补教师优于单一信号，且该分配主要在训练期起作用。迁移至BridgeDrive-LEAD，在220路由的Bench2Drive闭环基准上三个随机种子取得96.52±0.65驾驶得分，每个配对种子均超过匹配对照（差距处于种子波动范围内）。

Conclusion: 残差通路与历史依赖证据具有互补性，显式路由弱残差但强历史依赖的事件可显著提升开环与闭环规划性能，且以极低推理开销实现。

Abstract: Residual world models forecast a driving scene through its temporal change, so feature components that stay constant over time vanish from the residual pathway. We ask whether evidence with weak temporal change, such as a waiting pedestrian or a stopped vehicle, can be used more effectively for planning, and introduce MOVER, a complementary event-routing mechanism. A training-time teacher measures how each spatially grounded event query responds when a past frame is removed from shared history and contrasts that sensitivity with the event's pooled temporal residual, prioritizing events that depend on history but receive weak residual evidence. A student predicts the resulting priorities from unperturbed history, so the teacher adds no inference cost. Under fixed total routing mass, one distribution scales a low-rank future bird's-eye-view update and biases trajectory-conditioned event reading. On nuScenes, MOVER reduces ResWorld's UniAD-style average L2 from 0.65 to 0.55 m and its collision rate from 0.23% to 0.12% without direct planner ego status (0.50 m and 0.09% with it), adding 4.1 ms per frame. Matched controls show that the complementary teacher outperforms either signal alone and that the allocation acts mainly during training. Adapted to BridgeDrive-LEAD, MOVER reaches a 96.52 +/- 0.65 driving score over three seeds on the 220-route Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, exceeding matched controls in each paired seed by a margin within seed variation.

</details>


### [258] [QSRadar: Query-Conditioned Sparse Radar-Camera Fusion for Efficient 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30023)
*Chenhao Yan,Hongyu Liu,Xiang Cai,Yifei Sun,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 QSRadar：一种以查询为条件、稀疏采样原始雷达点的雷达-相机融合框架，用于自动驾驶卡车长距离高效 3D 检测，在 MAN TruckScenes 验证集上以更低算力与内存取得更优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶卡车需同时利用相机的语义细节与 4D 雷达的几何/运动线索。现有雷达-相机检测器多构建全局雷达 BEV 表征，计算与显存开销随感知距离增长；稀疏查询式检测器虽高效，但全局特征构建会掩盖点级雷达几何与运动信息。

Method: 基于 Sparse4Dv3 风格的视觉-时序检测器：围绕每个演化中的目标假设检索一小簇原始雷达点；将选中点表征于锚点对齐坐标系，并加入运动残差、RCS 与扫描时序信息；由查询条件化的集合编码器（set encoder）聚合；在每次锚点精化后再重新检索雷达邻域，使证据选择与目标估计耦合。

Result: 在 MAN TruckScenes 官方验证协议下，QSRadar 取得 38.09 mAP / 52.20 NDS，优于复现的 Sparse4D-Radar 全局 Radar-BEV 基线（37.08 mAP / 50.78 NDS）；RTX 4090 D 匹配测试中，延迟降低 45%，峰值显存降低 49%，GFLOPs 降低 43%。

Conclusion: 以查询为条件的稀疏点级雷达采样可替代全局雷达 BEV，在保持点级几何与运动信息的同时显著提升长距离检测的效率与精度，验证了"证据选择—目标估计"迭代耦合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable 3D object detection for autonomous trucks requires both the semantic detail provided by cameras and the geometric and motion cues measured by 4D radar. Existing radar-camera detectors commonly construct global radar bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations, whose computational and memory costs grow with the perception range. Sparse query-based detectors offer greater efficiency, but global feature construction can obscure point-level radar geometry and motion information. We present QSRadar, a query-conditioned sparse radar fusion framework for efficient long-range 3D detection. Built on a Sparse4Dv3-style visual-temporal detector, QSRadar retrieves a compact set of raw radar points around each evolving object hypothesis. The selected points are represented in an anchor-aligned coordinate frame, augmented with motion residuals, radar cross section, and sweep timing, and aggregated by a query-conditioned set encoder. Radar neighborhoods are retrieved again after each anchor refinement, coupling evidence selection with object estimation. Under the official MAN TruckScenes validation protocol, QSRadar achieves 38.09 mAP and 52.20 NDS, compared with 37.08 mAP and 50.78 NDS for our reproduced Sparse4D-Radar global Radar-BEV baseline. In matched tests on an RTX 4090 D, QSRadar reduces latency by 45%, peak memory usage by 49%, and GFLOPs by 43%.

</details>


### [259] [Teaching How to Reason, Beyond What to Answer: Evidence-Faithful On-Policy Distillation for streaming video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30040)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Jiaxuan Lin,Gang Hua,Le Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角辅助表示（MPAR）框架，用多个由视觉线索诱导的辅助表示弥补单一文本查询与视频细粒度视觉内容之间的语义鸿沟，从而提升时刻检索的定位判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时刻检索（MR）需根据文本描述在视频中定位对应片段，但文本查询通常只提供稀疏的语义描述，而视频包含丰富细粒度的视觉变化，造成文本语义与视觉内容之间的固有错位；单一文本输入难以实现精确的跨模态对齐，当存在时间相邻或语义相似的候选片段时，模型难以区分目标时刻与混淆项。

Method: MPAR 包含三个组件：(1) 辅助表示初始化（ARI）：从原始查询构建多个可学习的表示槽，在保留查询意图的同时为后续视觉线索注入提供灵活的载体；(2) 视觉诱导语义结构化（VISS）：向每个表示注入目标特定的视觉属性，同时保持与原始查询的语义连贯性；(3) 多视角语义对齐器（MPSA）：将每个辅助表示与对应的视频区域对齐，增强定位的判别能力。

Result: 在时刻检索基准上的大量实验表明，MPAR 优于现有最先进方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过构建多视角辅助表示并引导模型在这些表示上关注相关视频区域，可获得更精确的时刻定位；多视角辅助表示是缓解文本-视觉对齐鸿沟的有效途径。

Abstract: Moment Retrieval (MR) aims to localize the temporal segment in a video that corresponds to a given textual description. However, a textual query often provides only a sparse semantic description of the target event, whereas the video contains rich and fine-grained visual variations. This mismatch creates an inherent gap between textual semantics and visual content, making a single textual input insufficient for precise cross-modal alignment. As a result, when multiple temporally adjacent or semantically similar segments exist, the model may struggle to distinguish the target moment from confusing candidates. To address this challenge, we propose a Multi-Perspective Auxiliary Representation (MPAR) framework, which aims to bridge this alignment gap by enriching a single textual input with complementary visual cues from multiple perspectives. Specifically, MPAR consists of three key components: (1) Auxiliary Representation Initialization (ARI) constructs multiple learnable representation slots from the original query, preserving the query intent while preparing flexible carriers for subsequent visual cue injection; (2) Visual-Induced Semantic Structuring (VISS) injects target-specific visual attributes into each representation while preserving semantic coherence with the original query; and (3) Multi-Perspective Semantic Aligner (MPSA) aligns each auxiliary representation with corresponding video regions to enhance discriminative localization. By constructing multi-perspective auxiliary representations and guiding the model to attend to relevant visual regions across these representations, our model achieves more accurate moment localization. Extensive experiments on MR benchmarks demonstrate that our MPAR outperforms state-of-the-art methods, validating the effectiveness of MPAR.

</details>


### [260] [Bi-Refine: Semantic Verification-Guided Progressive Refinement for Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30055)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Jiaxuan Lin,Yuxuan Wu,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Bi-Refine框架，将时刻检索（MR）建模为闭环的"验证-精炼"过程，通过语义验证分支与可靠性门控的渐进精炼循环提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MR方法仅在定位通路内部精炼时间预测，缺乏对候选时刻的候选级语义一致性显式验证；且直接将语义分数用作反馈，在验证信号不可靠时会导致过度修正（over-correction）。

Method: 1) 启发式定位分支生成候选时刻；2) 语义验证分支在语义增强的隐空间中评估候选与查询的一致性，并引入查询监督：用同一候选时刻搭配不同查询训练验证器，使其区分匹配查询与语义相似的错配查询；3) 将验证特征注入渐进精炼循环，同时更新查询表示与多模态特征；4) 轻量级可靠性估计器对查询更新进行门控并对特征反馈中的候选贡献重加权，以抑制有害更新；5) 推理阶段采用基于收敛的自适应停止策略提前终止精炼。

Result: 在三个标准MR基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 将候选级语义验证与渐进精炼闭环结合，并配合可靠性门控与自适应停止，可有效提升时刻检索性能，同时避免不可靠验证信号带来的过度修正。

Abstract: Moment Retrieval (MR) aims to identify the temporal segment in an untrimmed video that corresponds to a natural language query. Existing approaches commonly refine temporal predictions within the localization pathway, without explicitly verifying candidate-level semantic consistency. Moreover, directly reusing semantic scores as feedback can cause over-correction when the verification signal is unreliable. To address these issues, we propose Bi-Refine, a semantic verification-guided progressive refinement framework that formulates MR as a closed-loop verification-and-refinement process. A Heuristic Localization Branch first generates candidate moments, while a Semantic Verification Branch evaluates their query consistency in a semantically enriched latent space. Query supervision further trains the verifier with the same candidate moment but different queries, encouraging it to distinguish the matched query from semantically similar mismatched queries. The resulting verification features are incorporated into a Progressive Refinement Loop that updates both query representations and multimodal features. To suppress potentially harmful updates, a lightweight reliability estimator gates query updates and reweights candidate contributions in feature feedback. A convergence-based adaptive stopping strategy additionally terminates unnecessary refinement during inference. Experiments on three standard MR benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and state-of-the-art performance of our method.

</details>


### [261] [FireDesmoke: Fire Smoke Removal via Real Smoke Transfer and Semantic Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30057)
*Chih-Chung Hsu,Rong-Lin Jian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FireDesmoke借助火灾烟雾的空间局部性，提出真实烟雾迁移与语义校正两个模块，在首个真实火灾烟雾配对基准上同时提升浓/中烟雾去除效果并保持清晰区域不变，且无人物幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火灾烟雾去除需在恢复浓/中烟雾区域的同时保留无烟清晰内容；现有方法或残留烟雾、或改变可见内容，生成式模型还可能幻觉出人物或抹除火焰。核心观察是真实火灾烟雾退化及其对语义特征的破坏均集中于烟雾空间区域，即具有空间局部性。

Method: 方法基于烟雾局部性构建：(1) Real Smoke Transfer，在真实火灾录像上校准烟雾场，并将其空间布局迁移到干净图像上以合成训练图像对；(2) Semantic Rectification，利用像素特征对冻结基础编码器中被烟雾污染的token进行校正。

Result: 同等训练预算下，用SmokeBench图像对替换迁移图像对使浓烟PSNR下降0.74 dB；去掉语义校正模块使清晰区域比输入低1.31 dB。在首个真实火灾烟雾配对数据集（留出视角的1500帧测试集）上，浓烟与中烟PSNR分别提升3.73 dB与2.63 dB，清晰区域保持输入水平（+0.41 dB）；对比方法中无一能在浓烟下取得≥1 dB增益且不降低清晰区PSNR，输出中未发现幻觉人物。在SmokeBench上训练亦取得最高PSNR、次优SSIM与LPIPS。

Conclusion: 验证了烟雾空间局部性作为设计先验的有效性，FireDesmoke在该基准上取得整体SOTA，并可作为跨数据集泛化的强基线。

Abstract: Fire smoke removal must restore dense and medium smoke regions while preserving the clear (smoke-free) content. Existing methods either leave the smoke or alter the visible content, and generative models may hallucinate people or remove flames. Real fire smoke is spatially local: the degradation and its corruption of semantic features are both concentrated in the smoke regions. We propose FireDesmoke, built on this locality: Real Smoke Transfer calibrates smoke fields on real-fire recordings and transfers their spatial layout onto clean images to synthesize training pairs, and Semantic Rectification uses pixel features to rectify the tokens of a frozen foundation encoder where smoke corrupts them. With matched training budgets, replacing the transferred pairs with SmokeBench pairs lowers dense-smoke PSNR by 0.74 dB, and removing Semantic Rectification leaves clear regions 1.31 dB below the input. On what is, to the best of our knowledge, the first paired dataset of real fire smoke for image restoration, with 1,500 test frames from a held-out viewpoint, FireDesmoke improves dense- and medium-smoke PSNR by 3.73 and 2.63 dB while keeping clear regions at the input level (+0.41 dB); no other compared method gains at least 1 dB in dense smoke without lowering clear-region PSNR, and no hallucinated person is found in its outputs. It achieves state-of-the-art overall results on this benchmark and, trained on SmokeBench, the highest PSNR with the second-best SSIM and LPIPS.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Binary Correctness: A Surrogate for Confidence Learning in Large Language and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30059)
*Chen Li,Xiaoling Hu,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种置信度学习框架：用查询级预测可靠性（重复采样下的经验成功率）与单个回答的正确性相结合，构建分级置信度代理目标，训练独立的置信度估计器，从而在推理时无需额外采样即可对单个回答打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对大语言模型与视觉语言模型的置信度估计旨在判断生成回答正确的可能性。然而二元正确性监督会把偶发错误与频繁失败、孤立成功与稳定成功赋予相同标签，而随机解码下这些结果对应的预测行为差异很大，导致监督信号不足。

Method: 在固定模型与解码配置下通过重复生成估计查询级可靠性（经验成功率），将其与单个回答的正确性结合，构造分级置信度代理目标：既区分正确性标签相同的不同回答，又保持采样回答池的平均预测可靠性。以查询及其完整生成回答为条件训练独立置信度估计器，联合优化主干LoRA适配器、投影层与标量置信度头，损失为均方误差；重复采样仅用于监督构造，推理时无需额外生成。

Result: 论文建立了该代理目标的排序性与均值保持性质，并推导出将总体代理预测误差与可接受回答错误率相联系的选择性风险界；在语言模型与视觉语言模型上评估了置信度校准与选择性预测性能。

Conclusion: 将查询级预测可靠性引入置信度监督，可在不增加推理成本的前提下获得更细粒度的置信度信号，并具备理论保证与跨模态适用性。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) aims to assess how likely a particular generated response is to be correct. We investigate how learning this assessment can benefit from the model's predictive reliability on the underlying query. Binary correctness supervision, however, assigns identical labels to occasional errors and frequent failures, as well as isolated successes and consistent success. These outcomes can arise from substantially different predictive behavior under stochastic decoding. We propose a confidence-learning framework that supplements realized response correctness with query-level predictive reliability. We estimate reliability through repeated generations under a fixed model and decoding configuration, then combine the empirical success rate with individual correctness to construct a graded confidence surrogate. The surrogate differentiates responses sharing the same correctness label while preserving the average predictive reliability of the sampled response pool. To learn this target, we condition an independent confidence estimator on the query and its full generated response, and optimize backbone LoRA adapters together with a projection and scalar confidence head using mean squared error. Repeated sampling is required only for supervision construction; inference scores an individual response without additional generations. We establish ordering and mean preservation properties of the surrogate and derive a selective-risk bound connecting population surrogate prediction error to the error rate among accepted responses. We evaluate the framework across language and vision-language models, examining confidence calibration and selective prediction.

</details>


### [263] [FolioWeaver: A LayerGraph-Grounded Agent for Layered Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30067)
*Wentao Lu,Shengzhe Cui,Xihao Wu,Dingxin Yan,Yixuan Qin,Pengfei Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FolioWeaver，一个以 LayerGraph 为支撑的智能体，通过持久化可执行场景状态统一分层图像生成与迭代编辑，并发布 LayerFolio 数据集及两种评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层图像生成虽实现了资产级可编辑性，但生成系统无法解读连续反馈、执行定向编辑并保留未受影响的内容，缺乏对场景的持久理解。

Method: 构建 LayerGraph 并在共享场景参考条件下生成独立视觉资产；由 Editing Agent 通过作用域化动作、选择性图层执行与视觉验证实现反馈驱动的修改与自我修复，从而复用未变更资产；同时发布含 100,377 个人工审核分层场景（覆盖六个视觉域）的 LayerFolio 数据集，以及 AtomicQA（衡量需求满足）与 CBLP（评估图层组织）两种评测协议。

Result: 实验表明 FolioWeaver 在图层组织与合成保真度上优于直接分层生成方法；消融分析验证了组件适配、执行接地的修复机制与跨域训练的贡献。

Conclusion: FolioWeaver 将分层图像生成推进至持久、可控且可迭代的视觉创作范式。

Abstract: Layered image generation separates images into compositable layers, allowing users to manipulate individual elements independently. However, such asset-level editability does not enable the generation system to interpret successive feedback, perform targeted edits, and preserve unaffected content. We introduce FolioWeaver, a LayerGraph-grounded agent that unifies layered-image generation and iterative editing through a persistent Executable Scene State. FolioWeaver constructs a LayerGraph and generates independent visual assets conditioned on a shared scene reference. An Editing Agent performs feedback-driven modifications and self-repair through scoped actions, selective layer execution, and visual verification, enabling targeted updates while reusing unchanged assets. We also publicly release LayerFolio, a dataset of 100,377 human-reviewed layered scenes spanning six visual domains, together with two evaluation protocols: AtomicQA for measuring requirement satisfaction and CBLP for assessing layer organization. Experiments demonstrate that FolioWeaver achieves superior layer organization and composite fidelity compared with direct layered-generation methods. Analyses validate the contributions of component adaptation, execution-grounded repair, and cross-domain training. FolioWeaver advances layered-image generation toward persistent, controllable, and iterative visual creation. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/FolioWeaver/LayerFolio.

</details>


### [264] [Pixel as the Common Language: Unified Multimodal Embedding via Visual Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30069)
*Xu Yuan,Pangjing Wu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“编码前统一”范式并实例化为 RenderEmbed：将异构输入渲染为统一视觉基底，用VLM编码，再经渐进层级锚点编码得到紧凑检索表示，并用干预式渲染学习训练，在MMEB与ViDoRe v2上同规模超越SOTA并具强域外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型将纯文本、纯图像、组合输入以不同格式呈现，并在模型内走不同路径，导致异构任务无法端到端联合优化同一编码过程，监督难以跨输入配置迁移。

Method: Visual Substrate Rendering将异构输入转为统一视觉基底供VLM编码；Progressive Hierarchical Anchor Encoding在视觉token中交错插入可学习锚点并跨层融合其状态，得到紧凑检索表示；Interventional Rendering Learning通过语义干预与渲染干预，使表示对内容变化敏感、对渲染变化稳定。

Result: 在覆盖多种检索任务的MMEB、ViDoRe v2上，同规模下优于先前SOTA；相较MetaEmbed，MMEB Overall Precision@1提升1.7点，域外任务提升2.1点；迁移与缩放分析显示共享视觉通路能更有效利用异构监督。

Conclusion: 统一视觉编码通路可缓解输入格式碎片化，实现异构任务联合端到端优化，提升检索性能与域外泛化。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding models map heterogeneous inputs into a shared representation space, yet text-only, image-only, and composed inputs are still presented in distinct formats and follow different routes through the model. This fragmentation prevents heterogeneous tasks from jointly optimizing the same end-to-end encoding process, limiting supervision transfer across input configurations. To address this issue, we propose unification before encoding and instantiate this paradigm in RenderEmbed. The framework uses Visual Substrate Rendering to convert heterogeneous inputs into a common visual substrate for encoding with a VLM. From the resulting visual tokens, Progressive Hierarchical Anchor Encoding produces compact retrieval representations by interleaving learnable anchors and fusing their states across layers, retaining broad context and fine-grained cues. Interventional Rendering Learning trains these representations to remain sensitive to content changes and stable across rendering variations through complementary semantic and rendering interventions. Extensive experiments on MMEB and ViDoRe v2, spanning diverse retrieval tasks, show that RenderEmbed outperforms prior state-of-the-art models at the same scale and exhibits strong out-of-domain generalization. Compared with MetaEmbed, RenderEmbed improves MMEB Overall Precision@1 by 1.7 points, with a larger 2.1-point gain on out-of-domain tasks. Transfer and scaling analyses further show that a shared visual pathway enables more effective learning from heterogeneous supervision.

</details>


### [265] [SalivaryHSI-Seg: A New Challenging Benchmark for Ex Vivo Salivary Gland Hyperspectral Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30071)
*Xiaoqi Guo,Xianran Wang,Xinquan Yang,Qinyi Lu,Fangqian Zhang,Jiangyi Chen,Ming Li,Wen Ma,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出唾液腺高光谱组织分割基准 SalivaryHSI-Seg（31例患者、119个离体样本、像素级多类标注、患者级五折交叉验证），并据此提出边界引导的光谱差异网络 HyBSD，实现全高光谱输入下的唾液腺组织分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 唾液腺肿瘤在口腔颌面部常见，准确识别肿瘤区域与周围唾液腺组织对组织评估、病理取样和临床决策支持至关重要；高光谱成像（HSI）可同时记录连续光谱响应与空间信息，具备捕获细粒度组织判别信息的潜力。但该方向缺乏带像素级标注的标准化基准与统一评测协议，制约了唾液腺HSI组织分割的发展。

Method: 1) 构建 SalivaryHSI-Seg 基准：31例患者的119个离体唾液腺HSI样本，提供多类像素级标注；2) 建立患者级五折交叉验证协议，系统评测不同输入表示（全HSI vs. 采样光谱）、不同模型架构，以及小肿瘤、复杂边界等挑战性子集；3) 提出 HyBSD 网络，利用边界引导的光谱差异线索，改善模糊过渡区域的组织轮廓划分。

Result: 全HSI输入比采样光谱提供更具判别性的信息；HyBSD 取得 74.97% mDice 与 87.76% Tumor Dice，并在小肿瘤与复杂边界等挑战性子集上表现出进一步优势。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了唾液腺高光谱组织分割在标准化基准与评测协议上的空白，验证了全光谱输入的价值与边界引导光谱差异建模的有效性；数据集与代码将公开，为后续研究提供可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Salivary gland tumors are common lesions in oral and maxillofacial practice, and accurate identification of tumor regions and surrounding salivary gland tissues is important for tissue assessment, pathological sampling, and clinical decision support. Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) records continuous spectral responses together with spatial imaging information, providing a promising way to capture fine-grained tissue-discriminative information. Nevertheless, the development of salivary gland HSI tissue segmentation remains limited due to the lack of standardized benchmarks with pixel-level annotations and unified evaluation protocols. To address this issue, we introduce SalivaryHSI-Seg, a hyperspectral tissue segmentation benchmark for ex vivo salivary gland specimens, containing 119 HSI samples from 31 patients with multi-class pixel-level annotations. We establish a patient-level five-fold cross-validation protocol and evaluate different input representations, model architectures, and challenging subsets with small tumor and complex boundary. Based on this benchmark, we further propose HyBSD, a hyperspectral boundary-guided spectral-difference network for full-HSI salivary gland tissue segmentation. HyBSD exploits boundary-guided spectral-difference cues to improve tissue delineation in ambiguous transition regions. Extensive experiments show that full-HSI input provides more discriminative information than sampled spectrums, and that HyBSD achieves 74.97% mDice and 87.76% Tumor Dice, with further advantages on the challenging subsets. Dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [266] [SF-Flow: Spectral Flow Matching with High Structural Fidelity for Hallucination-Suppressed Scientific Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30073)
*Zehua Wang,Patxi Fernandez-Zelaia,Mohamed Wahib,Xiao Wang,Isaac Lyngaas,Amir Koushyar Ziabari,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SF-Flow：一种仅在真实材料图像上训练、生成时施加频谱约束的流匹配模型，可在无配对监督下按预设布局生成高保真科学图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像生成需兼顾真实实验外观与指定空间布局，但面临严重数据稀疏问题：只能获得不配对的材料图像（真实图像与预设布局），无法直接监督。已有非配对翻译方法能复现纹理与强度变化，却极难在预设位置保留预期的相界（phase boundaries）。

Method: 提出SF-Flow（Spectral-Flow）流匹配（flow-matching）模型，仅用真实材料图像训练，在生成过程中引入频谱约束以强化布局一致性；由于布局仅在生成阶段输入，模型可泛化到训练分布之外的布局图。

Result: 在三个场景上评估：混凝土同步辐射断层成像、粉末透镜耦合实验室microCT、增材制造金属工业XCT。无需针对仪器专门建模，SF-Flow在模板一致性与联合保真度指标（structure score）上显著优于先前方法，包括对比非配对翻译（CUT）。

Conclusion: 频谱约束的流匹配为数据稀疏条件下的布局可控科学图像生成提供了有效方案，并具备跨仪器、跨布局分布的泛化能力。

Abstract: Scientific image generation focuses on reproducing experimental appearance while following a prescribed spatial layout. A key challenge is acute data sparsity, which requires operating with unpaired collections of materials (real images and prescribed layouts), where no direct supervision is feasible. Work on unpaired translation has shown that it is possible to reproduce the texture and intensity variations of real images, but it is extremely hard to retain the intended phase boundaries in the prescribed locations. This paper tackles this challenge and introduces SF-Flow, a flow-matching model trained only on real material images that imposes spectral constraints during generation, producing images that are high-fidelity and consistent with the prescribed layouts. Because layouts enter only at generation time, the approach extends to layout maps outside the distribution of any available training layouts. We evaluate the method on synchrotron tomography of concrete, lens-coupled laboratory microCT of powders, and industrial XCT of additively manufactured metal. Without instrument-specific modeling, we show that SF-Flow achieves substantially higher template agreement and joint fidelity--structure score than previous methods, including contrastive unpaired translation.

</details>


### [267] [RadarOSV: Unified 3D Scene and Motion Perception from mmWave Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30078)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadarOSV：首个仅用原始雷达数据、在统一体素中联合预测占据、动态目标语义与地面相对速度的框架，融合级联成像雷达与单片雷达的互补测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达占据方法以几何建模为主，语义与运动联合建模不足；级联成像雷达空间细节精细但无模糊速度范围有限，单片雷达速度范围大但空间分辨率粗，二者互补但未被联合利用。

Method: Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry (PCG) 主干通过固定与可变形采样将极坐标成像雷达特征映射到笛卡尔体素，引导跨雷达融合进行几何与属性预测；单片雷达检测输出提供类别证据，原始多普勒谱提供运动特征与径向速度候选；速度读出用融合特征从候选中消歧，再与学习运动结合；自车运动补偿使用雷达估计速度，无需外部里程计。

Result: K-Radar上单帧PCG在12.8 m内mIoU达40.7%（RadarOcc报告为34.3%）；BRIDGE-Liv上相比使用外部自车速度的级联雷达基线，四类动态目标在标注区域内的宏平均准确率由63.1%提升至75.5%，物体径向速度MAE由1.03降至0.51 m/s，同时保持几何占据mIoU。

Conclusion: 双雷达互补融合与雷达自估速度可实现无外部传感器的纯雷达统一占据—语义—速度预测，兼顾空间精度与速度消歧。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar offers weather-resilient sensing for 3D occupancy prediction. Existing radar occupancy methods mainly model geometry, with limited joint modeling of semantics and motion. Cascaded imaging radar provides fine spatial detail but has a limited unambiguous velocity range, whereas single-chip radar offers a wider range but coarser spatial resolution. We propose RadarOSV, to our knowledge the first radar-only framework that jointly predicts occupancy, dynamic object semantics, and ground-relative velocity from raw radar data in unified voxels, by exploiting complementary measurements from both radars. Its Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry (PCG) backbone maps polar imaging-radar features into Cartesian voxels through fixed and deformable sampling, guiding cross-radar fusion for geometry and attribute prediction. Single-chip detection outputs provide class evidence, while raw Doppler spectra supply motion features and radial-velocity candidates. The velocity readout selects from these candidates using fused features to resolve aliasing, then combines the selected radial estimate with learned motion. Ego-motion compensation uses radar-estimated velocity, so inference requires no external odometry or other sensors. On K-Radar, single-frame PCG achieves 40.7% mIoU within 12.8 m, compared with RadarOcc's reported 34.3%. On the BRIDGE-Liv dataset, compared with a cascaded-radar baseline predicting the same outputs with external ego velocity, RadarOSV improves macro-averaged accuracy across four dynamic object classes within annotated object regions from 63.1% to 75.5% and reduces object radial-velocity MAE against manual Doppler annotations from 1.03 to 0.51 m/s, while maintaining geometric occupancy mIoU.

</details>


### [268] [When "A or B" Is Less Likely than A: Coherent Probabilities for Vision Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30096)
*Seongrae Noh,SeongEun Hong,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉决策模型对多选题各选项输出单一概率，但该概率依赖选项分组方式：合并选项"A或B"的概率平均比A与B之和低11.5个百分点（次可加性），在0.85B–26B各规模及微调后均存在。作者提出CORE，将训练问题以不同分组方式重述，并令重新分组问题的答案（按其组求和）对齐同一目标分布。在7个基准的1,050题上，0.85B模型的总变差距离由14.8降至5.8（最佳同数据基线为9.4），五个模型规模下均在不降低准确率的前提下降低该距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件直接使用视觉决策模型输出的逐选项概率，但这些概率并非与分组方式无关的一致度量。作者发现合并选项的概率系统性低于其组成部分概率之和，说明模型概率不满足基本的可加性/一致性要求，从而威胁下游基于概率的使用可靠性。

Method: 首先实证刻画次可加性：比较合并选项"A or B"的概率与A、B概率之和，跨0.85B–26B多个模型规模及监督微调检验其稳健性。随后提出CORE：对每个训练问题构造多种选项重新分组的版本，将各版本问题的答案（在原始选项上按对应组求和）训练对齐到同一目标分布，从而施加分组一致性约束。

Result: 合并选项平均少11.5个百分点的概率，且在各模型规模与微调后持续存在。在7个基准共1,050题上，0.85B模型的重分组答案与原答案按组求和之间的总变差距离从14.8降至5.8个百分点，优于同问题训练的最佳基线9.4；在全部五个模型规模上，CORE均在不降低准确率的情况下减小该距离。

Conclusion: 视觉决策模型的选项概率存在系统性的分组依赖（次可加性），是一种可被训练校正的偏差。CORE通过分组重述的一致性训练有效提升概率的分组一致性，且不牺牲准确率，为下游直接使用模型概率提供了更可靠的校准手段。

Abstract: Vision decision models answer a multiple-choice question about an image with one probability per option, and software uses them directly. We show that the probabilities depend on how the options are grouped: a merged option "A or B" receives on average 11.5 points less probability than the sum of A and B. This under-additivity appears at every model size from 0.85B to 26B and remains after fine-tuning on labels. We propose CORE, which asks each training question with its options regrouped and trains the answers to all these questions toward one target distribution over the original options, summed into each question's groups. On 1,050 items from seven benchmarks, CORE reduces the total variation distance between the answer to a regrouped question and the original answer summed into the same groups from 14.8 to 5.8 points on the 0.85B model, against 9.4 for the best baseline trained on the same questions. At all five model sizes, CORE reduces this distance without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [269] [LMnOP: Pruning 3D Gaussian Splatting via Leave-Many-Out Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30102)
*Sunjae Jeong,Jaeseung Kim,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LMnOP提出"留多出"重要性剪枝，闭式计算随机移除下像素颜色的均值与方差，无需采样即可估计冗余互补高斯的重要性，在90%/99%高剪枝率下18项指标中16项最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅常需数百万高斯，存储与渲染成本高。常用剪枝分数为留一重要性（单独移除某高斯的损失增量），但在高剪枝率下大量高斯同时被移除，单独看冗余、组合起来却关键的高斯会被误删。

Method: 提出LMnOP：估计在其他高斯也被移除条件下每个高斯的重要性，即"留多出"重要性——某高斯被移除、其余高斯随机移除时的平均损失增量。闭式推导随机移除下每像素颜色的精确均值与方差，据此近似重要性而无需采样移除模式；再用该重要性迭代修正每个高斯的保留概率，最终一次性剪枝。

Result: 无需微调，在三个数据集、90%与99%移除率下的18项指标中有16项最优；在全部13个场景中SSIM与LPIPS均优于GaussianPOP。

Conclusion: 以留多出重要性替代留一重要性可有效避免高剪枝率下冗余互补高斯的误删，且闭式统计近似使方法免采样、免微调即可取得最优压缩-质量权衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting renders photorealistic views in real time. However, a single scene often needs millions of Gaussians, so these models are costly to store and render. Pruning reduces this cost by keeping a subset of the Gaussians. A common score is the leave-one-out importance, which is the loss increase when a Gaussian alone is removed. At high pruning ratios, many Gaussians are removed together, so Gaussians that are redundant alone but essential together are lost. We present LMnOP (Leave-Many-Out Pruning), which estimates the importance of each Gaussian under the removal of the other Gaussians. This leave-many-out importance is the average loss increase when a Gaussian is removed while the others are also removed at random. We compute the exact mean and variance of each pixel's color under random removal in closed form, and approximate the importance from them without sampling removal patterns. We refine each Gaussian's probability of remaining with its importance and prune once at the end. Without fine-tuning, LMnOP achieves the best results in 16 of 18 metrics on three datasets at 90% and 99% removal. It improves SSIM and LPIPS over GaussianPOP in all 13 scenes.

</details>


### [270] [Thermal-Guided Low-Light UAV Image Enhancement: A Reliability-Aware Guidance Network and a Unified Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30114)
*Zhicheng Zhao,Yu Wang,Jiangong Xu,Lixiang Xu,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知的多策略引导网络RMGNet，用于弱光-热对齐条件下的无人机低光照图像增强，并构建大规模UWOT数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
