Motivation: 夜间无人机感知中热成像在可见光严重退化时仍具信息量，但退化可见图像同时充当对齐参考与增强目标，在低照度、传感器差异和无人机运动下热引导的空间对应性与局部有效性不确定，错位或不合适的热线索会引入伪影，且空间变化的退化需要区域化恢复而非统一增强。

Method: RAGCM执行由粗到细的位移估计，并联合评估对齐后热引导的可靠性与退化可见证据的可用性；MSERM基于校准后的引导、退化与可靠性线索生成细节保持、高光保护重光照、热补偿三类候选，并对各自贡献进行空间路由。同时构建UWOT数据集（5,000组三元组）。

Result: 大量实验表明RMGNet在增强质量上优于代表性单模态与多模态SOTA方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率，并对实际跨模态错位具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模热引导可靠性与区域化多策略路由，RMGNet有效缓解弱光-热弱对齐下的引导不确定性，并提供大规模UAV光学-热低光照基准。

Abstract: Thermal-guided low-light image enhancement (LLIE) is promising for nighttime unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) perception because thermal imagery remains informative under severe visible degradation. However, the degraded visible image serves as both the alignment reference and the enhancement target, making thermal guidance uncertain in its spatial correspondence and local utility under low illumination, sensor differences, and UAV motion. Misaligned or unsuitable thermal cues may introduce artifacts, while spatially varying degradation requires region-specific restoration rather than uniform enhancement. To address these challenges, we propose a Reliability-Aware Multi-Strategy Guidance Network (RMGNet) for thermal-guided UAV LLIE under weak optical-thermal alignment. Its Reliability-Aware Guidance Calibration Module (RAGCM) performs coarse-to-fine displacement estimation and jointly evaluates the reliability of aligned thermal guidance and the usability of degraded visible evidence. Based on calibrated guidance, degradation, and reliability cues, the Multi-Strategy Enhancement Routing Module (MSERM) generates candidates for detail preservation, highlight-protected relighting, and thermal compensation, then spatially routes their contributions. We further construct the UAV weakly aligned Optical-Thermal dataset (UWOT), the largest UAV-oriented benchmark to date for weakly aligned optical-thermal low-light enhancement, containing 5,000 triplets. Extensive experiments show that RMGNet outperforms representative single-modal and multimodal state-of-the-art methods in enhancement quality while retaining competitive computational efficiency and greater robustness to practical cross-modal misalignment. The dataset and code will be publicly available at https://github.com/mmic-lcl/Datasets-and-benchmark-code.

</details>


### [271] [What Remains After Concept Erasure? Sparse Autoencoder Probes for Auditing Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30117)
*Zhuan Shi,Lu Wei,Yuta Nakashima,Noa Garcia,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SAE-Audit 白盒审计方法，用稀疏自编码器探针检验概念擦除是否真正移除概念，而非仅在输出上抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除评估仅衡量生成图像中目标概念的抑制与非目标概念的保留，但概念未出现在输出中并不等于其已从模型中被移除，缺乏对擦除真实性的检验。

Method: 利用从原始模型激活学习的共享 SAE 表示，识别擦除后目标相关响应减弱的特征；将源自原始模型响应的稀疏方向注入编辑后模型（方向在测试提示间固定、不改权重与提示），在留出场景族上评估恢复率；覆盖六种擦除方法与四类领域（物体、艺术风格、受版权内容、裸露内容）。

Result: 数十个选定 SAE 特征的干预平均恢复率高于匹配的随机特征干预；跨注意力编辑后的恢复集中于物体与裸露领域；多数艺术风格与受版权概念在测试设置下恢复有限。

Conclusion: 输出抑制可与对来自原始模型的稀疏概念方向的响应性共存，说明擦除未必真正移除概念，仅凭生成输出评估不足。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to remove selected visual concepts from text-to-image diffusion models while preserving the generation of non-target concepts. Evaluation typically measures target suppression and non-target preservation in generated images, but a concept's absence from these outputs does not establish its removal from the model. We introduce SAE-Audit, a white-box audit using sparse autoencoder (SAE) probes to test whether activation interventions can restore target-concept generation without changing model weights or test prompts. After verifying target suppression, we compare the original and edited models using a shared SAE representation learned from original-model activations, identifying features whose target-related responses weaken after erasure. We inject sparse directions derived from the original-model responses into the edited model, keeping these directions fixed across test prompts, and evaluate recovery on held-out scene families. Experiments span six erasure methods and four domains: objects, artistic styles, copyrighted content, and nudity. Interventions using a few dozen selected SAE features yield higher average recovery rates than matched random-feature interventions, with recovery concentrated in objects and nudity following cross-attention edits. Most styles and copyrighted concepts show limited recovery under the tested intervention settings. These findings show that output suppression can coexist with responsiveness to sparse concept directions supplied from the original model.

</details>


### [272] [AxE: Benchmarking Agent-Environment Generalization of Video World Models via Grounded Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30120)
*Haiyu Xu,Yiyang Chen,Jiayi Zhou,Jiaming Ji,Juntao Dai,Yaodong Yang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AxE Bench基准与AxE Score指标，系统评测条件视频生成世界模型在智能体与环境条件变化下的泛化和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统在真实部署中会频繁遇到训练数据中未出现的条件组合，边缘情况实为常态，因此需要能复用先验知识、预测后果并以极少训练自适应的世界模型。然而现有条件视频生成评测侧重视觉质量、提示词对齐或孤立物理合理性，缺乏对系统化泛化能力的可测量手段。

Method: 构建AxE Bench，将条件变化沿智能体与环境各八个因素组织，并为每个原始条件配对控制变体（单侧变化或双侧变化）；WBench-plus以同样结构扩展现有多轮基准WBench，为每个场景配对环境侧（世界设定或相机行为）或智能体侧（交互指令）变体。评分上开发评测harness，融合视觉语言推理与针对性计算机视觉证据，将视频转化为时间局部化事件，在时间与语义两个维度比较生成事件与参考事件；汇总指标为AxE Score，即按原始-变体配对稳定性加权的event Fidelity。

Result: 二十个模型中，生成事件在时间上匹配参考事件的频率远高于内容匹配：最高Fidelity不超过13.01，最高Stability为0.55，最高AxE Score为7.10。

Conclusion: 该工作将世界模型泛化从非正式期望转变为可证伪的评测框架，适用于真实与仿真交互场景，为物理智能研究提供系统化基准。

Abstract: Edge cases are not the long tail; they are the norm. An embodied system deployed across changing environments, bodies, objects, and tasks will routinely encounter combinations that were absent from its training data. General-purpose physical intelligence therefore requires world models that reuse prior knowledge, predict consequences, and adapt with little or no additional training. Yet current evaluations of conditional video generation emphasize visual quality, prompt alignment, or isolated physical plausibility, leaving systematic generalization difficult to measure. We introduce AxE Bench, a benchmark that organizes condition changes along eight factors of the acting agent and of its environment, and pairs every original condition with controlled variants that change one side or both. WBench-plus applies the same structure to an existing multi-turn benchmark, pairing each WBench scenario with variants on the environment side, through its world setting or camera behavior, or on the agent side, through its interaction instructions. Additionally, to score generated futures, we develop an evaluation harness that combines vision-language reasoning with targeted computer-vision evidence, converting each video into temporally localized events and comparing generated events with reference events in both time and semantics. We summarize each model with the AxE Score, event Fidelity weighted by its Stability across original-variant pairs. Across twenty models, generated events match reference events far more often in timing than in content: no model exceeds a Fidelity of 13.01, the highest Stability is 0.55, and the highest AxE Score is 7.10. Together, these components turn world-model generalization from an informal aspiration into a falsifiable evaluation program for real and simulated interactions. Our project website can be found at: AxEBench.github.io

</details>


### [273] [Separating Protocol Effects from Spatio-Temporal Evidence in Person Re-Identification Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30142)
*Mochamad Rizal Fauzan,Doan Pham Thao Nhu,Jeslin Konthoujam,Purit Hongjirakul,Lakshay Chawla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文质疑时空先验在行人重识别（ReID）上的增益来源：以课程项目级重排器为起点，通过控制先验与消融发现，Market-1501 上报到的增益很大程度来自评测协议本身（查询与真值不同摄像头）而非学习到的时空证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作报告时空先验（相机对＋时间差）在 Market-1501 上带来大幅提升，但作者怀疑这种增益有多少是编码了评测协议的结构性偏置，而非真实学到的时空证据。因此本文旨在量化解构该增益的来源。

Method: 从在余弦相似度上叠加对数相机转移项与对数时间差项的重排器出发；训练强 ResNet-50 基线（三随机种子，Rank-1 94.3%、mAP 86.2%）；在身份不相交验证集上选择融合权重，并用控制先验在官方测试集评估。设置三类对照：仅同摄像头对的单参数惩罚、似然比先验、以及时间戳打乱。并考察增益随视觉基线强弱的变化。

Result: 仓库形式的重排器提升 2.30 mAP；仅对同摄像头对加单参数惩罚即提升 2.19——因协议从不把真值放在查询摄像头中，惩罚可"免费"剔除干扰项，说明增益主要源于协议偏置。该形式中时间项几乎未被使用，成对诊断显示原因在于它只建模"给定匹配时的时间差"，未除以所有候选对的时间差分布。似然比先验在不加惩罚时提升 4.04，加惩罚后 5.62；打乱时间戳后额外增益消失，约 3.43 分与身份相关。先验为 4.2% 查询修正首位匹配，仅对 0.1% 造成破坏。增益随视觉基线增强而衰减（从 mAP 70.5% 到 86.2% 模型间，由 11.9 降至 5.62），且集中于真值与查询共享同一视频序列的查询。

Conclusion: 时空重排报告的增益大部分可由评测协议（查询与真值摄像头互斥）解释，真正的时空证据贡献有限，且随视觉模型变强而递减。研究受限于单一数据集、单一骨干网络与三个随机种子。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal priors re-rank person re-identification matches using the camera pair and the time gap, and are reported to add large gains on Market-1501. We ask how much of such a gain encodes the evaluation protocol rather than learned spatio-temporal evidence. Starting from a course-project re-ranker that adds log camera-transition and log time-gap terms to cosine similarity, we train a strong ResNet-50 baseline (Rank-1 94.3 %, mAP 86.2 % over three seeds), choose the fusion weights on an identity-disjoint validation split and evaluate on the official test set with control priors. The repository form adds 2.30 mAP points, but a one-parameter penalty on same-camera pairs alone adds 2.19: the protocol never places a true match in the query's camera, so the penalty removes distractors for free. The time term of that form is almost unused; a pair-level diagnostic traces this to its modelling the time gap given a match without dividing by the gap distribution of all candidate pairs. A likelihood-ratio prior adds 4.04 points without the penalty and 5.62 with it; shuffling the time stamps removes the extra gain, so about 3.43 points are tied to identity. The prior fixes the first-ranked match of 4.2 % of the queries and breaks it for 0.1 %. The gain shrinks as the visual baseline improves (11.9 to 5.62 points between a 70.5 % and a 86.2 % mAP model) and concentrates on queries whose true matches share the query's video sequence. The study covers one dataset, one backbone and three seeds.

</details>


### [274] [RMR-VLA: Recurrent Memory Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30145)
*Yiheng Du,Jingqi Tian,Xu Jiang,Jiexuan Zhang,Shuyu Wang,Zecheng Tang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出循环记忆推理（RMR），在VLA模型内部将记忆与动作通过显式推理步骤连接，无需外部推理模型，在RoboMME和LIBERO-Mem上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、依赖历史的机器人操作常需依据已消失的视觉线索行动（如先前指出的物体、移动杯子下隐藏的目标），难点在于场景变化时如何保持过去证据对下一步动作的意义。

Method: 在VLA内部加入显式循环推理步骤：每次更新先由视觉证据推断事件，再修正任务状态并在当前场景中定位子目标以引导动作生成；状态与子目标跨更新传递。单模型联合训练推理与动作。由于记忆决策为文本token、具有精确似然，可用GRPO以rollout奖励优化而不使动作生成随机化。

Result: RoboMME成功率相比无循环的相同模型翻倍；RMR-VLA在RoboMME上总体66.46%（Reference类别79.83%），LIBERO-Mem上90.08%，均为对比方法中最佳。

Conclusion: 显式循环记忆推理可在单一VLA内有效实现记忆到动作的衔接，并通过GRPO稳定优化记忆决策，验证了内嵌式记忆推理在长时程记忆依赖任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon, history-dependent robotic manipulation often requires acting on visual cues that have disappeared, such as an object indicated earlier or a target hidden under a moving cup. The key challenge is to maintain what past evidence means for the next action as the scene changes. We introduce Recurrent Memory Reasoning (RMR), an explicit reasoning step that connects memory to action inside a vision-language-action model (VLA), with no external reasoning model. At each update, the VLA first infers an event from visual evidence, then revises the task state and grounds a subgoal in the current scene to guide action generation. Carrying the state and subgoal across updates lets remembered identities and task progress determine the current target, doubling RoboMME success over an otherwise identical model without recurrence. Joint training lets one VLA both reason and act; because its memory decisions are text tokens with exact likelihoods, GRPO refines them from rollout rewards without making action generation stochastic. Among the compared methods, RMR-VLA achieves the best results on RoboMME (66.46% overall, 79.83% on the Reference category) and LIBERO-Mem (90.08%).

</details>


### [275] [Preserve to Compress: Beyond Low-Dimensional Latents in One-Step Diffusion Codec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30154)
*Chuqin Zhou,Xiaoyue Ling,Yuhang Wei,Yunuo Chen,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PICS 是一个一步式生成式图像压缩框架，通过信息保持适配器改造预训练潜扩散模型的潜空间接口以保留源图像信息，在极低码率下显著优于 StableCodec。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练潜扩散模型的潜空间存在"重建—生成"权衡：低维潜变量建模成本低但丢失细节，高维潜变量重建更好却使扩散优化在固定算力下更困难。采用这些预训练空间的生成式编解码器继承了该折中，而压缩需要忠实于特定源图像；低维潜接口会在编码前丢弃源细节，并限制解码信息传递到图像解码器。

Method: 提出 PICS（Preserving Information for Compression and Synthesis），一个一步式生成式图像压缩框架，用于适配潜空间接口以保留源信息。核心是信息保持适配器（Information-Preserving Adaptors, IPAs），为编码暴露更丰富的特征并在重建全过程保留解码信息；同时采用延迟解码器适配（deferred decoder adaptation）稳定训练，并帮助图像解码器利用扩展后的表示。

Result: 在 Kodak、DIV2K 和 CLIC 2020 数据集上，于匹配的 LPIPS 与 DISTS 指标下，相较 StableCodec 分别取得 52.64% 和 39.61% 的平均 BD-rate 节省。

Conclusion: 通过改造预训练潜扩散模型的潜空间接口以保持源信息，可在一步生成式压缩中兼顾重建保真与生成先验，实现在极低码率下的显著码率节省。

Abstract: Pretrained latent diffusion models bring powerful generative priors to ultra-low-bitrate image compression, but their latent spaces reflect a reconstruction--generation tradeoff. Low-dimensional latents reduce modeling cost but can discard image details, while higher-dimensional latents improve reconstruction yet make diffusion optimization harder under a fixed computational budget. Generative codecs that adopt these pretrained spaces also inherit this compromise, even though compression requires fidelity to a specific source image. In particular, low-dimensional latent interfaces can discard source details before coding and restrict how much decoded information reaches the image decoder. We propose Preserving Information for Compression and Synthesis (PICS), a one-step generative image compression framework that adapts these interfaces to preserve source information. Our Information-Preserving Adaptors (IPAs) expose richer features for coding and retain decoded information throughout reconstruction, while deferred decoder adaptation stabilizes training and helps the image decoder exploit the expanded representations. Experiments on Kodak, DIV2K, and CLIC 2020 show average BD-rate savings of 52.64% and 39.61% over StableCodec at matched LPIPS and DISTS, respectively.

</details>


### [276] [UUBench: Understanding the Unspoken toward Full-Duplex Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30157)
*Ye Pan,Chenfei Liao,Bin Ren,Hanqian Li,Haocong He,Jialei Chen,Jinlin He,Kunyu Peng,Mengyu Jin,Shijie Li,Wenqian Cui,Yuanqi Yao,Yuqian Fu,Kailong Ruan,Yuan Gao,Wei Wang,Xuming Hu,Irwin King,Nicu Sebe,Xu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UUBench基准，评估多模态交互系统对"未言明意图"（Unspoken intent）的理解与回应能力，并设计过程导向、结果导向及基于事实锚定的评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全双工多模态交互不仅需理解用户言语内容，还需捕捉韵律、表情、手势与上下文传达的沟通需求并生成恰当的音视频回应。现有评测难以判断系统是否将这种隐含需求的理解转化为有效回应，且隐含意图推断本身具有主观性，给评测带来挑战。

Method: 构建UUBench基准；提出过程导向评分（Process-Oriented Scoring），将意图满足度评估锚定在可观测的听觉与视觉线索（韵律、手势等）上，按每条适用原始维度的相关度加权聚合维度级分数；提出结果导向评分（Outcome-Oriented Scoring），在纯音频、纯视觉、音视频三种条件下评估回应的自然性、恰当性与拟人性；并设计事实锚定的JEV评分模块，利用带时间戳的结构化音视频观察逐维度/逐准则打分，使判断可溯源。

Result: 形成覆盖"未言明意图理解"与"回应质量"的评测框架，实现维度级、可溯源、多条件的评分，相较单一总分提供更细粒度的洞察。

Conclusion: UUBench为评估多模态交互系统能否超越字面语义、有效响应隐含沟通需求提供基础，助力上下文感知的实时全双工交互研究进展。

Abstract: Effective real-time, full-duplex multimodal interaction requires systems to understand not only what users say, but also the communicative needs conveyed through prosody, facial expressions, gestures, and context, and to generate audiovisual responses that appropriately address those needs. Existing evaluations offer limited insight into whether systems translate an understanding of such unspoken needs into effective responses. We introduce UUBench, a benchmark for assessing how well multimodal interactive systems Understand the Unspoken intent and address users' implicit communicative needs. The inherent subjectivity of implicit intent inference poses a key challenge for evaluation. To address this, we introduce Process-Oriented Scoring, which grounds the assessment of intent fulfillment in observable auditory and visual cues, such as prosody and gestures. Rather than relying solely on a single overall score, it evaluates how well a response addresses the communicative needs conveyed by each applicable primitive dimension. We aggregate the dimension-level scores according to each dimension's relevance to the annotated evidence. Beyond intent fulfillment, Outcome-Oriented Scoring evaluates the naturalness, appropriateness, and human-likeness of the reply itself under audio-only, visual-only, and audiovisual conditions. To make these judgments traceable to observable evidence, we propose a Fact-Grounded Scoring JEV module, which uses structured, timestamped audiovisual observations of the user and the digital human's reply to assess one dimension or criterion at a time. UUBench provides a foundation for assessing whether multimodal interactive systems respond effectively to meaning beyond literal wording, supporting progress toward context-aware, real-time, full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [277] [OpenViVE: New Benchmark and Dual-Stream Co-Attention Framework for Vietnamese Visual Entailment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30161)
*Bao Quoc Trinh,Tin Van Huynh,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个大规模越南语原生视觉蕴含基准OpenViVE（62,268对图文假设，10,378幅文化相关图像），并配套PhoBERT-ConvNeXt双向共注意力框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VE基准以英语为中心，缺乏越南语特有的语言区分与本土文化视觉语境，导致相关模型评估不足。

Method: 采用"伪影感知的LLM-人类混合流水线"（约束生成+人工校验+PMI引导精修）构建基准，以削弱假设-only捷径；并设计双流PhoBERT-ConvNeXt框架，通过双向共注意力对齐视觉证据与越南语假设。

Result: 精修后Contradiction准确率由96%降至66%，表明捷径显著减少；框架在测试集上达84.84%准确率与84.90%宏F1，PhoBERT一致优于多语言XLM-R变体。

Conclusion: 语言特定编码与显式跨模态交互对越南语VE至关重要；OpenViVE填补了低资源语言VE评测空白。

Abstract: Visual Entailment (VE) determines whether a textual hypothesis is entailed, contradicted, or unverifiable from an image. However, existing VE benchmarks are largely English-centric and do not capture Vietnamese-specific linguistic distinctions or culturally grounded visual contexts. To address this gap, we introduce OpenViVE, the first large-scale native Vietnamese VE benchmark, containing 62,268 balanced image-hypothesis pairs from 10,378 culturally grounded images. OpenViVE is constructed using an Artifact-Aware Hybrid LLM-Human Pipeline that combines constrained generation, human validation, and PMI-guided refinement. The pipeline substantially reduces hypothesis-only shortcuts, with Contradiction accuracy dropping from 96% to 66% after artifact refinement. We further evaluate a dual-stream PhoBERT-ConvNeXt framework with bidirectional co-attention to align visual evidence with Vietnamese hypotheses. The framework achieves 84.84% accuracy and 84.90% macro-F1 on the test set, while PhoBERT consistently outperforms multilingual XLM-R variants. These results highlight the importance of language-specific encoding and explicit cross-modal interaction for Vietnamese VE.

</details>


### [278] [WaDiS: Boundary-Preserving Diffusion Segmentation via Multi-Scale Wavelet Conditioning and Frequency-Aware Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30164)
*Qiang Gao,Yi Wang,Yong Zhang,Bin Fang,Yong Li,Lan Du,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WaDiS——基于小波的条件潜扩散分割框架，通过多尺度小波条件编码、频率感知门控与结构保持色彩增强，提升医学图像分割的边界精度与跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式医学图像分割方法在特征编码中易丢失空间与频率细节，难以保留精细解剖边界与多尺度结构信息，尤其在复杂临床成像条件下表现不佳；同时跨临床域的颜色偏置影响泛化。

Method: 1) 多尺度小波条件编码器，利用小波的无损特性保留丰富结构特征与语义先验；2) 在扩散U-Net跳跃连接中引入频率感知门控块（FAGB），实现自适应频谱滤波与残差融合，突出边界结构并抑制无关内容；3) 训练时采用结构保持色彩增强，在LAB空间进行全局颜色统计迁移，使外观与结构解耦。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，WaDiS 一致优于现有基于扩散和传统分割方法，在边界精度、噪声鲁棒性及颜色变化下的泛化性方面均有明显提升。

Conclusion: 小波域多尺度条件与频率感知滤波相结合的条件潜扩散框架可有效缓解细节丢失问题，配合结构保持色彩增强显著增强医学图像分割的边界质量与临床域泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown strong potential in medical image segmentation due to their generative capabilities and robustness. However, existing methods often struggle to preserve fine anatomical boundaries and to retain multi-scale structural information due to the loss of spatial and frequency details during feature encoding, particularly in complex clinical imaging conditions. This work presents WaDiS, a wavelet-powered conditional latent diffusion framework for medical image segmentation that addresses these limitations through multi-scale wavelet conditioning and frequency-aware information filtering for boundary preservation. The model adopts a multi-scale wavelet conditioning encoder to retain rich structural features, leveraging its lossless nature to better preserve semantic priors critical to the diffusion process. In addition, a Frequency-Aware Gating Block (FAGB) is introduced into the skip connections of the diffusion U-Net, enabling adaptive spectral filtering and residual fusion to better emphasize boundary structures while reducing irrelevant content. To improve generalization across clinical domains, we adopt a structure-preserving color augmentation strategy during training. Specifically, global color statistics are transferred between samples in the LAB color space, allowing the model to decouple appearance from structure and focus more on anatomical shape rather than color bias. Experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate that WaDiS consistently outperforms existing diffusion-based and conventional segmentation methods, with notable improvements in boundary accuracy, robustness to noise, and generalization under color variation. The code for this work will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [279] [Multimodal View Training: Learning More Through Different Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30165)
*Devang Kulshreshtha,Priyanka Kaswan,Minh Pham,Chinmay Hegde,Niv Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态视图训练（MVT）：将同一信息以文本、语音、图像三种形式呈现并训练模型输出相同目标，无需新事实或监督；配合轻量连接器可让纯文本LLM受益，在QA、机器遗忘与知识编辑上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否通过同一信息的不同呈现形式（多模态视图）更有效地学习，而无需引入新事实或额外监督信号。

Method: 从文本样本生成对应音频与渲染文本图像视图；训练多模态语言模型在三种视图下产生相同目标输出；为使纯文本LLM受益，训练轻量连接器模块接入预训练音频/视觉编码器，推理时仍保持纯文本架构；并给出理论分析说明跨模态训练如何将权重更新引向共享事实内容、降低对视图特有特征的依赖。

Result: 自适应MVT相比纯文本训练在QA召回率上提升15.2个百分点，遗忘质量提升40.7点，编辑泛化性提升25.2点；与标准数据增强结合可进一步提升QA与编辑泛化性。

Conclusion: 同一信息以多视图呈现即可带来实质收益；MVT是一种简单、无需新监督的通用训练策略，可迁移至纯文本LLM，且与常规增强互补。

Abstract: Can a language model learn more from the same information simply by encountering it in different forms? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing Multimodal View Training (MVT), a simple training approach that presents identical information through text, speech, and images, without requiring new facts or supervision. Starting from a text sample, MVT generates corresponding audio and rendered-text image views, and trains a multimodal language model to produce the same target output across all three views. To further bring these benefits to text-only large language models (LLMs), we train lightweight connector modules to pretrained audio and vision encoders, allowing the LLM to learn from multimodal inputs while retaining its text-only architecture at inference. Across question answering, machine unlearning, and knowledge editing, adaptive MVT improves QA recall by +15.2 percentage points, forget quality by +40.7 points, and editing generality by +25.2 points over text-only training. Combining MVT with standard augmentation further improves QA and editing generality. Our theoretical analysis illustrates how training across modalities can shift weight updates toward shared factual content and reduce their reliance on features specific to each view.

</details>


### [280] [PathStream: Towards Streaming Diagnostic Copilots for Whole-Slide Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30167)
*Dankai Liao,YuFeng Wu,Ziyue Wang,Guanxi Liu,Tianyi Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Linghan Cai,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文形式化"病理学家主导的流式诊断"任务，构建 PathStreamBench 基准与 PathStream 模型，让 copilot 在病理学家控制浏览轨迹的条件下逐步描述所见并整合证据给出最终诊断，性能超越 14 个 VLM 与 3 个领域微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断依赖病理学家沿观察轨迹在多区域、多放大倍数下整合形态学证据；现有病理 AI 多为从选定区域预测、固定视觉上下文内答题或自主导航切片，未能反映这一真实临床过程。

Method: 记录 5 位病理学家在 10 个器官、1,791 张 TCGA 全切片图像上的观察轨迹，并采集响应标签、形态学描述及会话级发现与诊断；据此构建 PathStreamBench，评估响应时机、形态学描述与会话级诊断（附辅助视频理解任务）；提出 PathStream，采用两阶段训练联合学习流式响应与诊断，并引入诊断注意力损失，引导诊断时关注随检查推进所累积的相关视觉与文本证据。

Result: 在核心任务上优于 14 个视觉语言模型和 3 个领域微调基线，轨迹 WSI 相关性得分达 77.87，显著高于最强的领域微调基线 62.76；无需额外训练即可泛化到新的教学病理视频、三个外部中心及一个未见器官。

Conclusion: 流式、病理学家主导的 copilot 范式能更贴合真实诊断工作流，并在多项核心任务与跨域泛化上取得领先，为病理 AI 提供了新的任务形式、基准与训练方法。

Abstract: Pathologists reach a diagnosis by integrating morphological evidence across regions and magnifications along viewing trajectories. Existing pathology AI rarely reflects this process: models predict from selected image regions, answer questions within fixed visual contexts, or navigate slides autonomously. We formalize pathologist-led streaming diagnosis, in which the pathologist controls navigation while a copilot selectively describes incoming views and synthesizes accumulated evidence into a final diagnosis. We record five pathologists' viewing trajectories on 1,791 TCGA whole-slide images (WSIs) spanning ten organs, with response labels, morphological descriptions, and session-level findings and diagnoses. We construct PathStreamBench to assess response timing, morphological description, and session-level diagnosis, alongside auxiliary video understanding. We introduce PathStream, which jointly learns streaming responses and diagnosis through two-stage training, with a diagnosis-attention loss guiding attention during diagnosis to relevant visual and textual evidence accumulated as the examination unfolds. PathStream outperforms 14 vision-language models and three domain-fine-tuned baselines on the core tasks, achieving a trajectory WSI Relevance score of 77.87, surpassing the strongest domain-fine-tuned baseline's 62.76. Without additional training, its diagnostic capabilities generalize to new educational pathology videos, three external centers, and an unseen organ.

</details>


### [281] [GUI-Q: Task-Sensitive Granularity and Relation-Preserving Quantization for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30170)
*Tianqi Li,Yang Wang,Xin He,Le Zhang,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GUI-Q，一种针对GUI智能体的W4A16训练后量化框架，通过任务敏感粒度分配与关系保持对称选择，在保留响应与关系的同时显著降低显存并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体参数量大、调用频繁，难以部署于资源受限设备；PTQ会损害GUI响应准确率，并扭曲指令-视觉亲和性与局部空间关系，而小的坐标误差会导致点击失败，早期动作错误还会改变后续界面状态。仅用局部重建误差无法直接评估这些响应与关系是否被保持。

Method: GUI-Q为W4A16的PTQ框架。TSGA（任务敏感粒度分配）在scale字节预算下，结合响应敏感性与实测的激活加权失真下降，分配更细的分组；RPSS（关系保持对称选择）在对称与非对称量化网格间选择，以保持指令-视觉亲和性与局部空间关系。

Result: 在三个具备GUI能力的模型上，GUI-Q相较最强4-bit配置将平均grounding TargetHit提升8.04–12.55点；静态峰值GPU显存相对BF16降低49.9%–56.2%；配合打包4-bit执行的固定输出重放实现1.7–2.8倍模型调用加速。

Conclusion: GUI-Q以W4A16量化在几乎不损失GUI定位精度的前提下大幅降低显存占用并提升推理速度，为资源受限设备上的GUI智能体部署提供可行方案，代码将开源。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents enable automated interactions with mobile, web, and desktop interfaces. However, their large memory footprint and repeated model calls hinder deployment on resource-constrained devices, motivating post-training quantization (PTQ) without full retraining. Quantization can degrade GUI response accuracy and distort the instruction-vision affinities and local spatial relations needed for GUI target selection. Small coordinate errors can cause missed clicks, while early action errors can alter subsequent interface states and disrupt task execution. Local reconstruction error alone does not directly assess whether these responses and relations are preserved. We propose GUI-Q, a PTQ framework that targets response and relation preservation with 4-bit weights and 16-bit activations (W4A16). Task-Sensitive Granularity Allocation (TSGA) allocates finer groups under a scale-byte budget by combining response sensitivity with the measured reduction in activation-weighted distortion. Relation-Preserving Symmetry Selection (RPSS) then selects symmetric or asymmetric grids to preserve instruction-vision affinities and local spatial relations in GUIs. Across three GUI-capable models, GUI-Q improves mean grounding TargetHit by 8.04 to 12.55 points over the strongest evaluated 4-bit configurations, while reducing static peak graphics processing unit (GPU) memory by 49.9% to 56.2% relative to bfloat16 (BF16). Fixed-output replay with packed 4-bit execution provides 1.7 to 2.8 times model-call speedups. The code will be released.

</details>


### [282] [Audio-Visual Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30171)
*Ruohao Guo,Jiatong Ye,Chenwei Dong,Jinxing Zhou,Dantong Niu,Guangyao Li,Zhenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出音视频重排任务AVR：智能体听懂语音请求、取回合适物体并送达请求者或指定地点，构建含三类查询、八种子类型的数据集，并提出将运动感知空间音频模块融入音视频大模型、辅以多任务指令微调的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务缺乏同时要求语音理解与声源空间定位的物体取送设定，需要能听懂请求并定位说话人以完成交付的新任务与基准。

Method: 构建AVR数据集，覆盖显式目标指定、相对说话者的空间指代、隐含用户意图三类查询及八个子类型；框架将运动感知空间音频模块集成到音视频大语言模型中，编码语音请求中的空间线索并随智能体移动更新；通过多任务指令微调引入语音理解、空间音频感知与视觉定位的辅助监督以改进动作预测。

Result: 在已见与未见环境中建立了基线性能，为后续具身智能与人机交互研究提供基础；代码将公开。

Conclusion: AVR为结合语音理解、空间音频定位与视觉定位的具身物体取送任务提供了数据集与基线框架。

Abstract: We introduce Audio-Visual Rearrangement (AVR), a novel task in which an agent hears a spoken request, retrieves a suitable object, and delivers it to the requesting user or an instruction-specified destination. AVR requires the agent to understand the spoken request and acoustically localize the speaker for delivery. To support this task, we construct a dataset covering three query types: explicit target specification, speaker-relative spatial references, and implicit user intent, spanning eight subtypes with diverse object selection and delivery requirements. Moreover, we propose a framework that integrates a motion-aware spatial audio module into an audio-visual large language model to encode spatial cues from the spoken request and update them as the agent moves. We further incorporate auxiliary supervision for speech understanding, spatial audio perception, and visual grounding through multitask instruction tuning to improve action prediction. Experiments establish baseline performance in both seen and unseen environments, laying the groundwork for future research on embodied intelligence and human-robot interaction. The code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [283] [OmniSenMo: A Unified Multisensory Language Model for Generalizable Human Motion Estimation, Understanding, and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30176)
*Cheng Guo,Qiming Cao,Pu Perry Wang,Pu Wang,Hongfei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniSenMo 以人体骨架为跨传感器的统一参照，将任意传感器子集转化为逐关节 token 并融合，实现对 RGB、深度、LiDAR、mmWave、WiFi 五类传感数据统一建模的人体运动理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的人体运动理解主要依赖 RGB；深度、LiDAR、mmWave、WiFi 可在弱光、遮挡和隐私敏感场景下互补，但现有多传感模型只覆盖部分传感任务，难以从任意传感器子集完成全部任务。核心难点在于异构传感器 token 语义不一致、空间未对齐、多传感配对数据稀缺，且各任务所需时间粒度不同。

Method: 提出 OmniSenMo：以人体骨架作为所有传感器的共同参照，构建 skeleton-grounded 运动表示，把每个传感器转换为逐关节 token（每关节一个），从而按关节逐一融合任意传感器子集，并映射到语言模型从动作捕捉与文本中学到的运动词表；利用自研多传感模拟器生成五种传感器数据进行预训练；采用 Multi-Granularity Observation 为不同任务提供所需的时间帧粒度。

Result: 在 MM-Fi 的 cross-action 划分上，五传感版本在各任务上均优于对比模型：姿态估计 MPJPE 84.1 mm 优于 LEO-Fuse 的 89.0 mm；未见动作识别 69.2% 优于 HoloLLM 的 42.8%。即使不含 RGB，仍优于两个使用 RGB 的方法；在 XRF55 的跨数据集验证中，未见动作识别显著优于 HoloLLM。

Conclusion: 以骨架为共同参照的逐关节 token 化与融合，能有效解决异构多传感的语义不一致与空间错位问题，并在配对数据稀缺条件下实现任意传感器子集上的多任务人体运动理解，且不依赖 RGB。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) estimate, understand, and predict human motion, but mainly from RGB cameras. Depth, LiDAR, mmWave, and WiFi complement RGB under poor lighting, through occlusions, and in privacy-sensitive applications, but existing multisensory models cover only some of these sensing tasks. Covering all of them from any sensor subset is challenging, since the tokens of heterogeneous sensors are semantically inconsistent and spatially misaligned, paired multisensory data are scarce, and the tasks need different temporal granularities. We present OmniSenMo, a multisensory language model that takes the human skeleton as a common reference for all sensors. Its skeleton-grounded motion representation turns each sensor into joint tokens, one per body joint, so that any sensor subset is fused joint by joint and expressed in the motion vocabulary that the language model learned from motion capture and text. We pretrain it on all five sensors generated by our multisensory simulator, and Multi-Granularity Observation gives each task the frames it needs. On a cross-action split of MM-Fi, the same model with all five sensors outperforms the models compared on each task, including LEO-Fuse in pose estimation (84.1 vs. 89.0 mm MPJPE) and HoloLLM in unseen-action recognition (69.2% vs. 42.8%). Even without RGB, it outperforms both methods that use RGB, and in a cross-dataset validation on XRF55, it recognizes unseen actions far better than HoloLLM.

</details>


### [284] [Visual-RFT++: Extending Visual Reinforcement Fine-Tuning to Multimodal Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30193)
*Ziyu Liu,Zeyi Sun,Xiaoyi Dong,Yuhang Cao,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visual-RFT++：将强化微调统一扩展到视觉感知任务与多模态智能体工作流，并引入MAT基准，在细粒度分类、检测、grounding及搜索/编码智能体任务上显著超越SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）已成为大型推理模型（LRM）的核心范式，但如何用RFT把视觉感知、逐步推理与智能体工具调用统一进同一训练范式仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出Visual-RFT++统一框架，将RFT扩展至视觉感知任务和多模态智能体工作流，使LVLM能基于图像推理、对图像施加动作，并用可验证奖励自评输出；同时构建多模态智能体工具基准MAT（含MAT-Search与MAT-Coding）。

Result: 视觉感知任务上大幅超越SFT：单样本细粒度分类+24.3pp，COCO 4-shot +21.0pp，LVIS（数百样本）+15.4pp。智能体任务相对基线提升MAT-Coding +18.6 F1/+13.0 EM，MAT-Search +10.3 F1/+8.7 EM；2Wiki、HotpotQA等多跳QA提升29.3 F1与25.9 EM，展现跨模态泛化。

Conclusion: Visual-RFT++是一个数据高效、奖励驱动的框架，同时强化视觉推理与智能体适应性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) has recently become a central paradigm for advancing Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), as demonstrated by systems such as OpenAI's o3 and the R1 series. Despite significant progress in LLMs, how to leverage RFT to unify visual perception, step-by-step reasoning, and agentic tool use into one coherent training paradigm remains an open challenge. In this work, we present Visual Reinforcement Fine-Tuning++ (Visual-RFT++), a unified framework that extends RFT to both visual perception tasks and multimodal agentic workflows, enabling LVLMs to reason with images, act upon images, and evaluate their own outputs using verifiable reward. With Visual-RFT++, open-source LVLMs not only show significant gains on diverse visual perception tasks, but also acquire agentic abilities such as web searching for real-time information and code-based image manipulation (e.g., cropping, rotation, and analysis). Across visual perception tasks, including fine-grained classification, few-shot detection, reasoning grounding, and open-vocabulary detection, Visual-RFT++ delivers strong gains over SFT, including a +24.3 percentage points improvement in one-shot fine-grained classification and +21.0 percentage points on COCO's 4-shot setting and +15.4 percentage points on LVIS with around several hundred samples. To evaluate agentic behaviors, we introduce the Multimodal Agentic Tool Bench (MAT) with MAT-Search and MAT-Coding. Visual-RFT++ surpasses its baseline by +18.6 F1 / +13.0 EM percentage points on MAT-Coding and +10.3 F1 / +8.7 EM percentage points on MAT-Search. It also shows gains of 29.3 F1 and 25.9 EM percentage points on multi-hop QA benchmarks such as 2Wiki and HotpotQA, demonstrating strong cross-modal generalization. Visual-RFT++ introduces a data-efficient and reward-driven framework that strengthens both visual reasoning and agentic adaptability.

</details>


### [285] [Quantization for Action, Execution for Deployment: Joint Precision and Execution Selection for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30195)
*Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Le Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PTQ4WAM：面向世界动作模型的逐层比特分配与执行路线联合搜索框架，在显存、时延与任务评估预算约束下达成中位99.2%的FP32任务得分、2.36倍加速与71.5%峰值显存下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需在设备内存内反复读取权重以比较数百个候选未来，后训练量化虽降低权重位宽，但更小的模型未必可部署：临时张量可能超出GPU显存上限，且微小预测误差即可改变所选动作。因此存在两个耦合难题——如何找到保持任务性能的比特分配，以及如何在不耗尽昂贵任务试验的前提下判定哪些分配满足真实执行约束。

Method: 提出PTQ4WAM框架，在内存、时延和任务评估预算约束下联合选择逐层比特分配与执行路线。以校准测量驱动候选方案生成，可选引入语言模型智能体辅助提议；先在GPU上对全模型路线做性能剖析，再将有限的任务试验分配给可行方案；并通过融合组重缩放与就地读取视觉历史，使原本不可行的分配变得可部署。

Result: 在来自四个模型族的15个工作负载上，任务得分中位数为对应FP32得分的99.2%；在固定GPU回放上相对FP32的中位加速为2.36倍，峰值分配内存中位下降71.5%。

Conclusion: 将量化比特分配与执行路线选择统一纳入预算感知的搜索，并辅以显存友好的执行优化，可在保持任务性能的同时显著改善世界动作模型的部署效率；代码即将开源。

Abstract: World action models guide robots by predicting the effects of actions or generating commands from observations. A model may compare hundreds of candidate futures before acting, repeatedly reading weights that must fit in device memory. Post-training quantization reduces the number of bits used to store pretrained weights, but a smaller model is not necessarily a deployable one: temporary tensors can exceed graphics processing unit (GPU) memory limits, while even small prediction errors can change the selected action. This creates two coupled challenges: identifying bit assignments that preserve task performance and determining which of them meet real execution constraints, without exhausting costly task trials. We present PTQ4WAM, a framework for choosing layer-wise bit assignments and execution routes under memory, latency and task-evaluation budgets. Calibration measurements guide candidate proposals, optionally with a language model agent. We profile full-model routes on the GPU before spending task trials on feasible assignments. Fusing group rescaling and reading visual history in place can make an otherwise infeasible assignment deployable. Across 15 workloads from four model families, the median task score is 99.2% of the corresponding 32-bit floating-point (FP32) score. On fixed GPU replays, the median speedup over FP32 is 2.36 times and the median reduction in peak allocated memory is 71.5%. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [286] [ToWo-VTON: Take-Off Wear-On Virtual Try-On via Topology-aware Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30203)
*Zhenchen Wan,Tianxi Chen,Runqi Lin,Tianyi Bo,Ce Chen,Jiaxin Huang,Yanwu Xu,Tongliang Liu,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ToWo-VTON提出"两阶段(脱衣-穿衣)"范式，将人体结构估计与服装合成解耦，并构建BFDC数据集与Garment Boosting Attention，在上下装及全身虚拟试衣中显著降低KID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTON方法在上装表现良好，但在下装与全身场景下，遇到复杂遮挡和大幅轮廓变化时仍效果不佳，主要受源服装干扰与结构引导不足影响。

Method: 提出ToWo范式：Take-Off阶段先生成紧身人体代理作为结构引导，Wear-On阶段基于该先验合成目标服装，从而削弱源服装干扰；构建Body-Fitting DressCode (BFDC)数据集，通过body-aware扩散与VLM过滤生成上/下/全身的人体代理监督；在扩散与MLLM引导两类骨干上实例化，扩散模型用人体代理作person输入微调，MLLM版本增加Garment Boosting Attention (GBA)保留服装细节。

Result: 在VITON-HD与DressCode上优于所评估基线，扩散实现与MLLM实现KID分别降低26.1%与58.6%；BFDC训练与Take-Off/Wear-On策略在各类服装上生成更协调的肢体布局与服装边界。

Conclusion: 结构估计与服装合成解耦是提升下装与全身试衣鲁棒性的有效途径，配合带结构监督的专用数据集可获得更一致的轮廓与细节保真度。

Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to synthesize garment transfer without physical trials. Although recent methods perform well on upper-body garments, they still struggle in lower-body and full-body scenarios with complex occlusions and large silhouette changes. To address this limitation, we introduce ToWo (Take-Off Wear-On), a two-stage paradigm that decouples body structure estimation from garment synthesis. Take-Off first generates a tight-fitting body proxy that provides estimated structural guidance, and Wear-On then synthesizes the target garment conditioned on this prior. This decoupling reduces source-garment interference, supporting garment placement under substantial silhouette variations. Built on this paradigm, we propose ToWo-VTON, a unified topology-aware framework with structure-aware supervision. We construct the Body-Fitting DressCode (BFDC) dataset via body-aware diffusion and VLM-based filtering to provide body-proxy supervision across upper-, lower-, and full-body cases. We instantiate ToWo-VTON on both diffusion-based and MLLM-guided backbones. For diffusion-based models, we fine-tune on BFDC using the body proxy as the person input. For the MLLM-guided model, we use native backbone conditioning for the body proxy and target garment, and add Garment Boosting Attention (GBA) to preserve garment details. Experiments on VITON-HD and DressCode demonstrate improvements over the evaluated baselines, with KID reductions of 26.1% and 58.6% for diffusion-based and MLLM-guided implementations, respectively. The combined BFDC-training and Take-Off/Wear-On strategy also produces more coherent limb layouts and garment boundaries across the evaluated garment categories.

</details>


### [287] [Benchmarking Geospatial Foundation Models under Calibrated Label Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30215)
*Mollah Md Saif,Showrin Rahman,Anindya Majumder,Sowad Rahman,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在标签不完美条件下微调地理空间基础模型的基准协议，将四类空间结构化标签误差校准到相同标签mIoU并同施加于所有模型与种子，用配对对比与±3分等价边界判定编码器优劣；在9个编码器、6种解码头和10个数据集上发现卫星/网络预训练DINOv3在8/9数据集上等价，预训练数据并不能保证稳健性，应按不完美标签下的精度选编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感标签常来自地图、旧产品或仓促标注，存在错位、粗糙、轮廓松散或不完整；然而地理空间基础模型的基准测试却把标签当作正确，导致从业者在标签不完美时无法判断该选哪个编码器。

Method: 提出面向不完美标签微调的基准协议：定义四类空间结构化误差，校准至相同标签mIoU并等同施加于每个模型和种子；所有对比采用配对设计，以±3个点的等价边界和最小可检测效应判读；在9个编码器、6个解码头、10个数据集上实施。

Result: 探索性读取（匹配学习率）下，卫星预训练与网络预训练DINOv3编码器在9个数据集中8个上处于等价边界内；多光谱GFM与ImageNet ResNet-50约在一半数据集上等价；同等标签质量下随机偏移类标签代价最小；在其他解码头下，结论在FLAIR-2上大多保持，在HLS Burn Scars上依赖解码器；5对真实标签中4对上真实标签的代价高于匹配的模拟误差类型。

Conclusion: 没有哪种预训练数据能使编码器持续更稳健，因此应依据编码器在不完美标签下的精度而非预训练数据类型来选择。

Abstract: Earth-observation labels from maps, older products or hurried annotation arrive misaligned, coarse, loosely outlined or incomplete. Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) are nevertheless benchmarked with labels taken as correct. A practitioner therefore cannot tell which encoder to choose when the labels are imperfect. We present a benchmark protocol for fine-tuning under such labels. Four spatially structured error types are calibrated to equal label mIoU and applied identically to every model and seed. Every contrast is paired and read against an equivalence margin of $\pm 3$ points and a minimum detectable effect. We apply it to nine encoders, six decoder heads and ten datasets. In an exploratory reading at a matched learning rate the satellite-pretrained and web-pretrained DINOv3 encoders are equivalent within the margin on 8 of 9 datasets. Multispectral GFMs and an ImageNet ResNet-50 are equivalent within the margin on about half of them. Labels with random offsets cost the least at equal label quality. Under other decoder heads the verdicts mostly hold on FLAIR-2 and are decoder dependent on HLS Burn Scars. On four of five real label pairs the real label costs more than its matched simulated type. No choice of pretraining data makes an encoder consistently more robust, so the encoder is better chosen by its accuracy under imperfect labels.

</details>


### [288] [RoomTokens: Masked Diffusion over Structured Tokens for Indoor Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30219)
*Xiaohao Sun,Angel X. Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出切片式结构化 token 表示 RoomTokens（编码语义类别、实例颜色身份、朝向场）与掩码离散扩散模型 MDIRT，将场景生成为离散网格，在尊重房间几何约束下采样家具布局，3D-FRONT 上布局保真度与物理一致性优于序列/向量式与语义地图式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 室内场景生成表示存在两难：以对象向量序列建模的方法隐式学习空间约束，碰撞与越界只能靠损失函数或后处理纠正；语义地图方法显式表达占用，但丢失实例身份与方向结构。需要一种同时保留语义、实例、高度结构与朝向并遵从房间几何的表示。

Method: 1) RoomTokens：切片式结构化 token 表示，每个 token 存储语义类别、实例颜色身份与 facing field（朝向场），使场景可作为离散网格生成，同时保留实例分组、朝向估计与 3D 范围预测线索。2) MDIRT：RoomTokens 上的掩码离散扩散模型，通过共享跨切片主干联合去噪语义、实例颜色、朝向三个通道；建筑类 token 被钳制为显式平面图约束，从而使采样布局符合房间几何。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上，RoomTokens + MDIRT 相较序列/向量方法与基于语义地图的方法，在布局保真度和物理一致性（碰撞、越界等）上均有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化空间 token 是离散 3D 场景合成的可行且实用的表示选择；显式结构化表示配合离散扩散可在约束满足与属性结构保留之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Generating 3D indoor scenes requires a representation that captures object semantics, instance identity, height structure, and orientation while respecting room geometry. Methods that model scenes as sequences of object vectors learn spatial constraints implicitly, leaving collisions and boundary violations to losses or post-processing; semantic-map methods make occupancy explicit, but collapse instance and directional structure. We introduce RoomTokens, a sliced structured-token representation in which each token stores semantic class, instance-color identity, and facing field. This representation allows a scene to be generated as a discrete grid while preserving cues for instance grouping, orientation estimation, and 3D extent prediction. To learn the RoomToken distribution, we propose MDIRT (Masked Diffusion over RoomTokens), a masked discrete diffusion model that denoises semantic/instance-color/facing channels jointly with a shared cross-slice backbone. Architectural tokens are clamped as explicit floor- plan constraints, so the model samples furniture layouts that respect room geometry while retaining instance and object-attribute structure. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that RoomTokens with MDIRT improve layout fidelity and physical consistency over sequence/vector- and map-based methods, suggesting structured spatial tokens as a practical choice for discrete 3D scene synthesis.

</details>


### [289] [EditGRN: Inversion-Free Image Editing by Source-Target Generative Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30224)
*Jiahuan Wang,Jianan Chen,Jian Han,Rui Li,Guangming Lu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EditGRN，一个无需训练、无需反演的离散空间图像编辑框架，通过双分支与轨迹级源-目标生成同步，在保留背景的同时实现语义连贯的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导图像编辑存在“保留源内容”与“实现连贯目标语义”之间的固有权衡。现有无需反演的离散编辑器依赖事后概率修正，导致局部 token 级调整与全局连贯语义生成之间脱节。

Method: EditGRN 采用双分支设计：源分支构建源参考，目标分支生成目标图像，两者沿整条轨迹同步。利用 GRN 从任意二值损坏中恢复干净 token 的能力，源分支在各精化步直接构造损坏源状态以提取编辑特征，无需恢复特定源生成轨迹；目标分支以相同损坏调度同步，在模型内部融合对应源上下文与可编辑表示（intra-model fusion）。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上，于多种编辑任务中相对连续与离散基线取得背景保持与文本对齐方面的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 沿整条轨迹同步源上下文与目标生成，可忠实保留背景并产生与周边场景无缝融合的高质量编辑；该范式为离散生成模型下的编辑提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing faces a persistent trade-off between preserving source content and realizing coherent target semantics. We explore this challenge through training- and inversion-free image editing based on discrete generative models. Existing inversion-free discrete editors primarily rely on post-hoc probability correction, creating a disconnect between local token-level adjustments and globally coherent semantic generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce EditGRN, a training- and inversion-free framework with trajectory-wide source-target generative synchronization in discrete space. EditGRN adopts a dual-branch design---the source branch constructs source references and the target branch generates the target image-enabling trajectory-wide synchronization between the two. Exploiting GRN's ability to recover clean tokens from arbitrary binary corruptions, the source branch directly constructs corrupted source states at each refinement step to extract useful editing features, without recovering a specific source-generation trajectory. The target branch synchronizes with the source branch through the same corruption schedule, fusing the corresponding source context with editable representations intra-model. By synchronizing source context with target generation along the entire trajectory, our approach faithfully preserves the background while producing high-quality edits that blend seamlessly with the surrounding scene through intra-model fusion. PIE-Bench show state-of-the-art performance on background preservation and text alignment across diverse editing tasks against continuous and discrete baselines.

</details>


### [290] [PixelOne: From Options to Pixels in a Single Forward for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30235)
*Kanghui Tian,Shuai Dong,Yuhan Zhu,Congcong Wang,Ji Zhao,Tianxiang Jiang,Zhixiong Zhang,Shengyuan Ding,Yi Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Yu Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixelOne，从搜索式教师模型将空间知识蒸馏到学生模型，在原生分辨率截图上单次前向预测点击坐标，PixelOne-Pro-35B-A3B在四个基准上达到83.8%平均准确率，平均延迟0.43秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI接地模型（如GUI-Decisions、GUI-Swift）用固定全局分箱加局部细化在单次前向中预测坐标，但截图下采样会丢失定位小控件所需的细节，且固定分箱在更高分辨率下覆盖的像素范围过大。

Method: 教师模型利用VideoJEV的文本选项打分器选择行与列并逐步放大候选区域进行搜索；学生继承教师参数，通过空间蒸馏匹配教师在探索区域上的分布并结合框监督训练；推理时同一决策头选择视觉token，轻量像素头在其中回归点击位置，全部在一次前向完成；另引入点击对齐损失，使目标框内像素得分高于框外。

Result: 在四个基准上，PixelOne-Pro-35B-A3B取得83.8%的平均准确率，为所评估的单次前向接地模型中最高，平均每目标延迟0.43秒。

Conclusion: 通过空间蒸馏将搜索式定位能力压缩到单次前向的架构中，可在原生分辨率下兼顾高精度与低延迟的GUI点击定位。

Abstract: GUI grounding requires precise clicks at low latency. Recent models such as GUI-Decisions and GUI-Swift predict coordinates in one forward pass using fixed global bins with local refinement. However, screenshot downsampling removes detail needed to locate small controls, while fixed bins cover larger pixel ranges at higher resolutions. We introduce PixelOne, which transfers spatial knowledge from a search teacher to a student that predicts clicks directly from screenshots at native resolution. The teacher uses VideoJEV's text-option scorer to select rows and columns and progressively zoom into candidate regions. The student inherits the teacher's parameters and matches its distributions over explored regions through spatial distillation alongside box supervision. At inference, the same decision head selects a visual token, and a lightweight pixel head predicts the click within it, all in one forward pass. A click-aligned loss encourages pixels inside the target box to score above those outside. Across four benchmarks, PixelOne-Pro-35B-A3B achieves 83.8% mean accuracy, the highest among the evaluated single-forward grounders, with a mean latency of 0.43 s per target.

</details>


### [291] [AIGI-26: A Benchmark for AI-Generated Image Detection Across Diverse Modern Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30236)
*Qishuai Ding,Yifeng Wang,Shichuang Xie,Yanli Ren,Guorui Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出AIGI-26基准（253,200张图像，覆盖26个最新开源/闭源生成器）与多视角取证检测网络MAFDNet，在仅1.04M参数下取得88.5%准确率和94.9%平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGI检测基准多依赖早期生成系统，内容多样性有限且伪影明显，无法充分反映当代高保真生成图像带来的检测挑战。

Method: 构建AIGI-26基准以提供新颖、多样、异构的生成源；提出MAFDNet：用固定取证算子提取纹理、局部预测、重采样与色度互补响应，经分层编码器以多尺度均值/标准差汇总；并行频谱分支捕捉傅里叶对数幅度及径向频带、方向轴上的邻频相位变化；融合局部不一致性与全局频谱组织特征进行真伪判别。

Result: 在12,660张AIGI-26图像上训练，于240,540张测试集上达到88.5%准确率与94.9%平均精度，每张256×256输入仅需1.04M参数和1.39 GFLOPs；在DailyBench与Treasure-37上展现有效的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 面向现代高保真生成图像，构建更具挑战性的AIGI-26基准，并以轻量化的多视角取证融合网络MAFDNet实现高精度检测与良好泛化，兼顾性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Existing AI-generated image (AIGI) detection benchmarks predominantly rely on earlier synthesis systems with limited content diversity and conspicuous artifacts, failing to fully reflect the detection challenges posed by high-fidelity images from contemporary models. We introduce AIGI-26, a benchmark of 253,200 images covering 26 recent open- and closed-source generators. Its novel, diverse, and heterogeneous sources provide a basis for systematically evaluating detection methods on modern generated imagery. Additionally, to capture subtle statistical discrepancies across complementary forensic views, we propose the multi-aspect forensic detection network (MAFDNet). Its fixed forensic operators expose complementary texture, local prediction, resampling, and chromatic responses, summarized by a hierarchical encoder using multiscale means and standard deviations. In parallel, a spectral branch captures Fourier log-magnitude and neighboring-frequency phase variation across radial bands and directional axes. Fusing these descriptors combines local inconsistencies with global spectral organization for authenticity detection. Trained on 12,660 AIGI-26 images, MAFDNet achieves 88.5% accuracy and 94.9% average precision on the 240,540-image test set, with only 1.04M parameters and 1.39 GFLOPs per $256\times256$ input. MAFDNet also demonstrates effective cross-dataset generalization on DailyBench and Treasure-37.

</details>


### [292] [TriSegAgent: Context-Efficient 3D Annotation with MLLMs via Tri-Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30240)
*Qingqiao Hu,Jialin Li,Meilong Xu,Kehan Qi,Yipeng Zhang,Lin Tian,Hongwei Bran Li,Klaus Mueller,Xiaoling Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriSegAgent：以三正交平面（tri-planes）作为3D体数据的token高效2D观测表示，让通用MLLM智能体在3D体积分割中执行"预测-检查-点击"迭代修正闭环，并配可视化执行器将2D判断转化为精确体素级3D操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式分割将标注转为"检查预测并点击修正"的视觉判断循环，天然适合通用MLLM智能体；但MLLM仅接受2D输入，智能体须以2D方式观察3D体数据。由于循环中需多次调用MLLM，该观测必须token紧凑以降低每次调用成本，同时又需保留足够细节以显示3D预测中的错误区域——二者存在张力。

Method: 选用在体积中一点相交的三张正交平面（tri-planes）作为观测表示，从理论与实验两方面论证其在上下文效率与错误区域可辨识性之间的折中；在此基础上构建TriSegAgent：MLLM仅在tri-planes上判断预测，visual harness将每次判断转译为代码形式的3D动作，把点击落在精确体素上并判定是否接受3D预测；并构建含35个公开医学数据集、3种成像模态、690个ROI的评测基准。

Result: 使用前沿MLLM时，整体Dice较最强基线提升约10个百分点，在小于3cm的靶标上领先更明显；使用flash级MLLM时，仍优于基于前沿MLLM的近期3D分割智能体。

Conclusion: tri-planes是一种上下文高效且信息充分的3D-to-2D观测接口，可使通用MLLM智能体在低token成本下可靠地完成3D交互式分割迭代细化，并在跨模态医学ROI上取得显著性能优势。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation models turn a few clicks into a segmentation prediction across domains, so annotators only decide where the prediction still needs correction. Annotation thus becomes a loop of inspecting the prediction and clicking again. Each step of this loop is a visual judgment, which suits general-purpose multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents. In this work, we focus on letting these agents run this loop on 3D volumes. However, the MLLM in such an agent takes only 2D images as input, so the agent must observe the 3D volume in 2D. Since the loop calls the MLLM many times, this observation must be token-compact enough to keep every call cheap, yet informative enough to show error regions in the 3D prediction. We meet both needs with tri-planes, the three orthogonal planes that cross at one point in the volume. We show, both theoretically and empirically, that tri-planes are context-efficient yet keep sufficient detail to show error regions in the 3D prediction. Building on tri-planes, we introduce TriSegAgent, an MLLM agent that refines the 3D prediction iteratively. Within the agent, the MLLM only judges the prediction on tri-planes, while a visual harness turns each judgment into a 3D action in code. To make the loop reliable, the harness places each click at an exact voxel in the volume and checks whether to accept the 3D prediction. To evaluate the agent, we build a diverse benchmark of 690 regions of interest (ROIs) from 35 public medical datasets across three imaging modalities. With frontier MLLMs, TriSegAgent reaches an overall Dice about 10 points above the strongest baseline, and its lead is even larger on targets below 3 cm. With flash-level MLLMs, it outperforms a recent 3D segmentation agent built on a frontier MLLM.

</details>


### [293] [OCCA-Seg: Object and Channel Context Aggregation for Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30267)
*Gang Yang,Si Chen,Quan Zhou,Jiangchuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对象与通道上下文聚合（OCCA）方案，含OCAM、CCAM与聚合模块AM，通过对象级和通道级上下文建模提升遥感图像语义分割精度，在LoveDA、UAVid、ISPRS Potsdam和Vaihingen上取得有竞争力的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像存在场景复杂、目标多尺度等问题，导致语义分割精度受限；作者观察到同一目标内的像素共享类别标签，且特征通道与目标类别相关，因此可从对象级与通道级两个维度挖掘上下文信息。

Method: OCCA由三部分组成：对象上下文注意力模块（OCAM）通过聚合每个像素所属对象类别的上下文来表示该像素；通道上下文注意力模块（CCAM）以目标类别为条件建模通道间的上下文依赖；聚合模块（AM）融合OCAM与CCAM的上下文信息，并用于加权与调制对象上下文。

Result: 在LoveDA、UAVid、ISPRS Potsdam、Vaihingen等多个公开遥感数据集上，相比现有方法取得有竞争力的性能提升。

Conclusion: 对象级与通道级上下文的联合聚合能有效增强分割模型的判别能力，是改善复杂遥感场景语义分割的一种有效方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation, which assigns a class label to each pixel, is a key task in computer vision. However, semantic segmentation of remote sensing images (RSIs) remains challenging because of complex scenes and objects at multiple scales, which limits segmentation accuracy. To address these challenges, we propose a context aggregation scheme called object and channel context aggregation (OCCA), consisting of three components: object contextual attention module (OCAM), channel contextual attention module (CCAM), and aggregation module (AM). Firstly, motivated by the observation that each pixel shares the class label of the object to which it belongs, object-level context represents each pixel through aggregating context from its corresponding object class. Secondly, because feature channels are correlated with object categories, channel-wise context models channel-wise contextual dependencies conditioned on object categories. Finally, the context information from OCAM and CCAM are fed into AM and used to weight and modulate the object context, enhancing the discriminative ability of the segmentation model. The experiments on several public remote sensing datasets including LoveDA, UAVid, ISPRS Potsdam, and Vaihingen datasets demonstrate competitive improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [294] [RigidAnchor: Cross-View Geo-Localization for UAV via Iterative Planar Rigid Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30278)
*Bo Wang,Dekang Zhu,Jian Li,Zhipeng Meng,Bo Wang,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位（U-S CVGL）重新表述为求解两幅异尺寸图像间的平面刚体变换（2D平移+平面内旋转），提出迭代式框架RigidAnchor，同时估计位置与航向，精度不再受瓦片网格限制。在Bearing-UAV-90K上位置误差降低78.6%、航向误差降低69.7%，HSR@15提升19.06。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有U-S CVGL方法多将任务视为图像检索：将无人机图像与各卫星瓦片分别编码为描述子并排序，返回最佳瓦片中心。其输出只能落在预定义网格上，精度受瓦片尺寸限制，且不产生航向。单次回归位姿虽可摆脱网格，但缺乏事后修正的机会。

Method: 观察发现卫星瓦片以固定间距、尺度与朝向铺设在图上，相邻瓦片拼接成度量一致的连续卫星块，而正下视无人机视图与之仅相差一个平面刚体变换。据此将任务重构为求解两幅不同尺寸图像间的该变换，位置与航向由此成为同一几何量的组成部分。RigidAnchor迭代求解该变换：网络编码两幅图像并构建多层级相关性金字塔；每次迭代用当前估计映射的每个航拍单元在金字塔中索引，将所有单元描述子池化为单一隐状态，再由两个解耦头分别预测平移增量与旋转增量，其中旋转采用复合（compose）而非相加。

Result: 在Bearing-UAV-90K上，跨视角平均位置误差较Bearing-UAV降低78.6%，航向误差降低69.7%，HSR@15提升19.06个点。

Conclusion: 将U-S CVGL建模为连续卫星块与无人机视图间的平面刚体变换并迭代求解，可在摆脱瓦片网格约束的同时联合输出位置与航向，且迭代机制保留了对估计的事后修正能力，显著优于检索式基线。

Abstract: A UAV that flies without GNSS has to estimate its absolute position and heading without any external signal. Most existing methods for UAV-to-satellite cross-view geo-localization (U-S CVGL) treat the task as image retrieval: they compress the UAV image and each satellite tile into separate descriptors, rank the tiles by their similarity, and return the center of the best tile. The output can therefore only lie on a pre-defined grid, its accuracy is limited by the tile size, and no heading is produced. Regressing the pose in a single pass removes the grid, but it leaves no chance to correct the estimate afterwards. We observe that satellite tiles are not independent samples. They are laid on the map at a fixed spacing, scale and orientation, so neighboring tiles join into one continuous satellite block with a consistent metric, and a nadir UAV view of that block differs from it by a planar rigid transform only, i.e. a 2D translation plus an in-plane rotation. We therefore reformulate U-S CVGL as solving for this transform between two images of different sizes. Position and heading then become parts of one geometric quantity, and the accuracy is no longer limited by the tile grid. We propose RigidAnchor, which solves the transform iteratively. The network encodes the two images and builds a multi-level correlation pyramid between them. At each iteration, it indexes the pyramid with every aerial cell carried through the current estimate, pools the descriptors of all cells into one hidden state, and lets two decoupled heads predict a translation increment and a rotation increment, the latter being composed rather than added. On Bearing-UAV-90K, the cross-view mean errors are 78.6% lower in position and 69.7% lower in heading than Bearing-UAV, and HSR@15 increases by 19.06 points. Code is available at https://github.com/wwsource/RigidAnchor.

</details>


### [295] [AdvMTO: Multimodal and Temporal Adversarial Object Attacks via 2D Gaussian Splatting for BEV-Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30283)
*Zhiyang Cui,Hao Tan,Ruonan Li,Shibin Wu,Junjian Zhang,Hong'an Lin,Zhaoquan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AdvMTO，一种基于 2D Gaussian Splatting 的统一多模态、多时刻对抗物体攻击框架，同时抑制相机与 LiDAR 融合检测器及时序 BEV 检测器对持续存在对抗物体的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 BEV 3D 检测器的对抗物体攻击多仅针对单一模态：或仅在当前时刻协调相机与 LiDAR 观测，或主要在相机流中建模时序持续性。由于缺乏对当前与历史观测的统一构建，检测器累积的证据无法被完全抑制，攻击效果受限。

Method: 采用 2DGS，以带朝向的 surfel 统一编码物体的外观、几何与不透明度，并由此渲染相机图像与扫描线式 LiDAR 回波。通过检测器原生处理流程构建当前与历史输入，对融合检测器在每个原生扫描源中重新生成 LiDAR 回波；对每种检测器优化有界外观扰动，并使梯度通过历史 BEV 记忆反向传播以作用于时序相机检测器；对融合检测器，沿局部法线进行有界几何细化，从而同时扰动两种传感模态。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上，对三种 BEV 检测器均有效抑制了交通锥的检测。时序消融实验进一步表明，检测器原生的历史建模对攻击构建与优化均具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 统一建模当前与历史观测、并同时扰动多模态输入，是提升 BEV 3D 检测器对抗物体攻击持续性与有效性的关键；2DGS 提供了可微且紧凑的统一表示载体。

Abstract: Adversarial object attacks pose a threat to BEV-based 3D object detectors in autonomous driving. Existing attacks typically target a single modality, coordinate camera and LiDAR observations at the current timestamp, or model temporal persistence mainly in camera streams. Without a unified construction of current and historical observations, attacks can leave part of the detector's accumulated evidence unsuppressed, limiting their effectiveness. In this paper, we propose AdvMTO, a 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) framework for suppressing detection of persistent adversarial objects. Its oriented surfels encode appearance, geometry, and opacity in one object state, from which camera images and scanline LiDAR returns are generated. AdvMTO then constructs current and historical inputs through detector-native processing, regenerating LiDAR returns in each native source sweep for fusion detectors. We optimize bounded appearance perturbations for each detector, propagating gradients through historical BEV memory for temporal camera detectors. For fusion detectors, bounded geometry refinement along local normals further perturbs both sensing modalities. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate effective suppression of traffic-cone detection across three BEV detectors. Temporal ablations further highlight the importance of detector-native history modeling for both attack construction and optimization.

</details>


### [296] [Uncovering Separable and Composable Skill Mechanisms in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30290)
*Miaoyu Li,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多技能可解释性框架DISCO，以"可分性"与"可组合性"为技能定位的核心准则，通过Separable Head Selection选取技能专属注意力头，并首次设计相应度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉推理技能内部机制定位工作忽视了机制识别的可分性与可组合性，既未区分机制对其他技能的影响，也未评估机制能否组合支撑复杂任务。

Method: 提出DISCO框架：识别阶段采用Separable Head Selection，依据注意力头在各技能上的贡献差异挑选技能专属头；评估阶段首次设计可分性与可组合性的量化指标。

Result: 在3个VLM与4项基础视觉推理技能上验证有效；可分性在三模型上分别超出最强基线24.80、18.38、13.75分，且三模型上可组合性均为最优。

Conclusion: 可分性与可组合性应作为技能定位的重要准则；DISCO能实现更精准的诊断与干预，并以少量机制组合覆盖更广能力。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to localize the internal mechanisms underlying visual reasoning skills in vision-language models. These methods largely overlook separability and composability in both mechanism identification and evaluation. We propose separability and composability as two important desiderata for skill localization. Separability requires identified mechanisms to contribute primarily to their target skills while having limited effects on other skills, enabling precise diagnosis and targeted intervention. Composability requires skill mechanisms to be combinable to support complex tasks, allowing a small set of mechanisms to account for broader capabilities. In this work, we propose a multi-skill interpretability framework DISCO, built around these two properties. For identification, we propose Separable Head Selection, which selects skill-specific attention heads based on their contributions across skills. For evaluation, we are the first to design metrics for measuring separability and composability of identified heads. Extensive experiments on three VLMs and four fundamental visual reasoning skills demonstrate the effectiveness of DISCO. It achieves the highest separability, outperforming the strongest baseline by 24.80, 18.38, and 13.75 points across the three models, respectively, while also achieving the highest composability on all three models.

</details>


### [297] [PinGround: Pinning Queries to Points for Parallel Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30304)
*Shuxuan Li,Jialiang Kang,Yanghao Zhou,Ziye Zhu,Zhilin Zhao,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出锚点式并行解码方法，将实例定位建模为"锚点→最近实例"的独立查询，并用前缀树共享前缀实现并行解码，在密集场景取得最高21.9倍加速且F1损失不超过5.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM通过文本接口把检测框按序列逐一写出，但同一查询的多个实例本质上是无序集合，不存在序列依赖；因此解码步数随实例数线性增长，密集场景代价最高。而朴素并行解码的各条流彼此不可见，会产生实例重复与漏检。

Method: 在每个查询中写入一个点作为空间锚，要求模型输出该点局部半径内最近的匹配实例（若无则输出NONE）；由于各锚定查询条件于图像且相互独立，可将其视为共享图像与查询前缀的前缀树叶子节点并行解码，训练也在同一树上进行。另引入由粗到细的锚点仅在检出区域细化、重复框抑制保证一实例一框，以及集合级强化学习直接优化set F1。仅修改提示与注意力掩码，不改动模型架构。

Result: 在3种VLM与12个基准（检测、OCR、版面与指代）上，方法F1最多比同数据训练的序列解码低5.5；加速比随实例密度增长，密集场景达21.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过将无序集合定位重构为独立锚点查询并借助前缀树并行解码，可在基本保持序列解码精度的前提下大幅降低密集场景的推理成本，且无需改变模型架构。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can localize through their text interface, grounding open-vocabulary objects, complex referring expressions, text lines and layout regions with strong semantic understanding. They write the boxes one after another as a single sequence, although the instances of a query, unlike the words of a sentence, form an unordered set with no sequential dependence. The number of decoding steps therefore grows with the number of instances, which makes dense scenes the most expensive. Decoding the instances in parallel avoids this growth, but streams that cannot see each other duplicate some instances and miss others. We propose \method, which writes a single point into each query as a spatial anchor and asks for the matching instance nearest to that point within a local radius, or \none. Given the image, the anchored queries are independent, so they are decoded in parallel as leaves of a prefix tree that shares the image and the query, and the model is trained on the same tree. Coarse-to-fine anchors refine only the regions where instances are found, duplicate suppression keeps one box per instance, and set-level reinforcement learning optimizes the set F1. Only the prompt and the attention mask change, not the architecture. On three different VLMs and 12 benchmarks covering detection, OCR, layout and referring, \method is at most 5.5 F1 below sequential decoding trained on the same data, while its speedup grows with instance density and reaches 21.9$\times$ on dense scenes.

</details>


### [298] [SafeRank-ReID: Candidate-Conditioned Re-Ranking under Identity Conflicts for Multimodal Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30314)
*Yizhi Wang,Zixuan Li,Zhuwen Chen,Feiwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SafeRank-ReID，一个以候选条件建模的"安全重排序"框架，用于多模态行人重识别，解决"视觉质量正常但身份错误"的冲突输入导致的负融合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ReID方法在查询级(query-level)估计模态可靠性，难以识别那些视觉质量正常却对应错误身份的冲突输入；这类输入会获得高置信度、推广无关图库候选，造成负融合(negative fusion)。

Method: 1) 候选条件证据仲裁机制：对每个"模态-候选"对融合单模态检索证据、输入质量与跨模态一致性，输出信任与冲突logits并归一化为模态权重，使支持强度随模态和候选变化；2) 候选集精化模块：用自注意力联合建模候选表示，依据候选间竞争关系预测残差分数以修正初始融合排序；3) 查询级回退门控：对精化后仍不确定的结果，由整体冲突水平与跨模态不确定性估计插值系数，自适应混合融合分数与最可靠单模态分支分数。

Result: 在RGBNT201与MSVR310数据集上，保持有竞争力的干净输入检索性能，同时在行人和车辆基准上提升了对身份冲突(Identity Conflicts)的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将可靠性估计从查询级细化到候选级、并结合候选集精化与不确定性触发的单模态回退，可有效抑制多模态ReID中的负融合，在保证常规检索精度的同时增强冲突场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal Person Re-Identification (ReID) exploits complementary identity cues from different sensing modalities to mitigate the performance degradation caused by challenging imaging conditions. Existing methods typically estimate Modality Reliability at the query level, making it difficult to identify conflicting inputs that retain normal visual quality but correspond to an incorrect identity. Such inputs may receive high confidence, promote irrelevant gallery candidates, and lead to negative fusion. To address this issue, we propose SafeRank-ReID, a candidate-conditioned safe re-ranking framework for Multimodal Person Re-Identification (ReID). To capture the varying support of the same modality for different candidates, we introduce a candidate-conditioned evidence arbitration mechanism that integrates unimodal retrieval evidence, input quality, and cross-modal consistency to estimate trust and conflict logits for each modality-candidate pair. Together, these logits contribute to the normalized modality weights, allowing support strength to vary across modalities and candidates while reducing the contribution of potentially unreliable evidence. A candidate-set refinement module subsequently applies self-attention to jointly model candidate representations and predicts residual scores from their competitive relations, thereby refining the initial fused ranking. For fusion results that remain uncertain after refinement, a query-level fallback gate estimates an interpolation coefficient from the overall conflict level and cross-modal uncertainty, adaptively combining the fused scores with those of the most reliable unimodal branch. Experiments on RGBNT201 and MSVR310 show that SafeRank-ReID maintains competitive clean-input retrieval while improving robustness to Identity Conflicts across person and vehicle benchmarks.

</details>


### [299] [Video Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30316)
*Bardia Safaei,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoGCD 框架，将广义类别发现（GCD）从静态图像扩展到视频动作识别，通过分区混合专家、新类平衡损失与时间最优传输损失提升新类识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GCD 方法在静态图像上有效，但在视频场景下因时间动态性、更大的类内差异以及缺乏时间一致性而导致性能下降，因此需要针对视频的 GCD 方法。

Method: VideoGCD 集成三个组件：(i) 分区混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）模块，促进已知类与新类的专家专门化；(ii) 新类平衡损失（novel-class balancing loss），缓解对已知类的偏置；(iii) 时间最优传输损失（temporal optimal transport loss），促进跨帧路由的一致性。

Result: 在三个视频基准上，VideoGCD 相较现有 GCD 方法显著提升性能，尤其在新类上表现突出。

Conclusion: 结合时间建模与专家专门化对视频 GCD 至关重要。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to categorize unlabeled data from both known and unknown classes using labeled data from only the known classes. While recent GCD methods have shown promise on static images, their performance degrades in videos due to temporal dynamics, larger intra-class variation, and the lack of temporal consistency in existing approaches. We introduce VideoGCD, a framework tailored for video action recognition under the GCD setting. Our method integrates (i) a partitioned Mixture-of-Experts module that encourages specialization for known and novel categories, (ii) a novel-class balancing loss to reduce bias toward known classes, and (iii) a temporal optimal transport loss that promotes consistent routing across frames. Experiments on three video benchmarks demonstrate that VideoGCD significantly improves performance over existing GCD methods, particularly on novel categories. Our findings highlight the importance of combining temporal modeling and expert specialization for video GCD.

</details>


### [300] [Accuracy Is Not Evidence: Measuring and Training Evidence Dependence in Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30320)
*Zijie Jin,Shuai Zhao,Haoran Tao,Chaomin Huang,Liwen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据依赖"(evidence dependence)这一指标，用于衡量视频时序定位模型的答案在多大程度上依赖事件帧本身，而非"盲答"或拟合标注风格；并给出可微的审计-训练框架，使模型在保持准确率的同时真正依赖事件证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频时序定位中Recall at IoU将正确答案一视同仁，但正确答案可能来自真正定位事件、盲答（不看视频）或拟合某一标注风格，现有评测无法区分。作者希望量化并改善模型对事件证据的依赖程度。

Method: 固定答案审计：分别编辑标注区间内的帧，或作为安慰剂编辑同一视频中等量的区间外帧，比较两者对标注答案似然的下降之差，得到模型自身似然差可微的依赖度量。以此为训练目标，并加入安慰剂对比与安慰剂下降低上界约束，防止模型学会对任意编辑敏感；在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上进行SFT与IoU-reward RL实验。

Result: SFT与三个用IoU-reward RL训练的公开checkpoint将准确率提升9–20个百分点，但部分源于盲答与拟合单一标注风格，其证据依赖并不高于基座模型。加入该目标后，五个基座模型的证据依赖均上升，且受干净监督权重调控；与IoU-reward训练结合可在保持准确率增益的同时提升依赖。掩蔽事件帧使模型更常漏检或答错，掩蔽等量其他帧则不会；查询指向另一事件时依赖随之转移，并在第二个数据集与patch scrambling（训练中未见过的编辑）上成立。

Conclusion: Recall at IoU不足以反映模型是否真正利用事件证据；所提审计与训练目标能正交地提升证据依赖而不牺牲准确率，并在未见编辑与跨数据集上泛化。

Abstract: In video temporal grounding, a correct interval can come from finding the event, answering blind or fitting one annotation style, and Recall at IoU scores them alike. We measure evidence dependence, how much the answer rests on the event's frames. Our fixed-answer audit edits either the frames inside the annotated interval or, as a placebo, as many frames elsewhere in the same video, and compares the two drops in the annotated answer's likelihood. Supervised fine-tuning and three public checkpoints trained with IoU-reward reinforcement learning raise the accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 9 to 20 points, partly by answering blind and by fitting one annotation. Yet their answers rest on the event no more than the base model's, and the same holds for IoU-reward training on our data at the audit's frame rate. This comparison is a difference of the model's own likelihoods, so we can train on it. A placebo contrast and a bound on the placebo drop rule out learning to react to any edit. With them, evidence dependence rises in all five base models we train, and the weight on clean supervision controls how much. Added to IoU-reward training, the objective raises evidence dependence while keeping the accuracy gain. Masking the event's frames makes the trained models decline or miss the event more often than their base; masking as many other frames does not. The dependence moves to another event when the query names it, and holds on a second dataset and under patch scrambling, an edit never used in training.

</details>


### [301] [TAED: Trajectory-Aligned Encoder--Decoder Distillation for Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30326)
*Zilong He,Zhangchi Hu,Hebei Li,Yijun Pan,Yifan Zhao,Xiangyu Yu,Yansong Peng,Xinghao Wang,Dongsheng Jiang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轨迹对齐编解码蒸馏框架TAED，解决DETR蒸馏中师生查询对应不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在采用编码器查询选择的DETR中，教师与学生独立选择Top-K提议，同排名查询可能来自不同空间位置、对应不同目标，导致基于排名的逐层监督不可靠，定位知识难以有效蒸馏到紧凑DETR。

Method: TAED包含三部分：(1) 编码器特征蒸馏，对齐归一化多尺度特征以增强学生表示并增加来自同一编码位置的师生查询对；(2) 源索引查询对齐，配对同一编码位置的原生查询，并仅保留教师最终定位更可靠且更准确、且对应同一目标的配对；(3) 轨迹对齐定位蒸馏，维持上述对应关系，利用教师提议及对应解码层框预测指导学生细化，且不引入额外查询。

Result: 在COCO上，TAED使DINO-R18与两阶段Deformable DETR-R18分别提升2.5和1.7 AP；在D-FINE与ECDet上的进一步增益表明其适用于近期实时DETR，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 基于源索引与轨迹一致的查询对应是DETR蒸馏的关键；TAED在多种架构（含实时DETR）上通用有效，且不增加推理成本。

Abstract: Distilling localization knowledge into compact detection transformers (DETRs) requires consistent teacher-student query correspondence across decoder layers. However, in DETRs with encoder-based query selection, the teacher and student select top-K proposals independently. Same-rank queries can originate from different spatial locations and correspond to different objects, making rank-based layer-wise supervision unreliable. To address this problem, we propose Trajectory-Aligned Encoder-Decoder Distillation (TAED), a framework for knowledge transfer between DETRs of different capacities. Encoder feature distillation matches normalized multi-scale features to strengthen student representations and increase the number of teacher-student query pairs from the same encoder locations. Source-indexed query alignment pairs native queries from the same encoder locations and retains pairs associated with the same object for which the teacher provides reliable and more accurate final localization than the student. Trajectory-aligned localization distillation maintains these correspondences and uses teacher proposals and corresponding decoder-layer box predictions to guide student refinement, without introducing additional queries. On COCO, TAED improves DINO-R18 and two-stage Deformable DETR-R18 by 2.5 and 1.7 AP, respectively. Further gains on D-FINE and ECDet demonstrate applicability to recent real-time DETRs at no additional inference cost.

</details>


### [302] [Draft Forcing: Target-guided Speculative Drafting and Learned Verifier for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30328)
*Chenkai Xu,Yuji Ren,Zihui Liu,Yijie Jin,Yuhao Shen,Xin Wang,Yajing Chen,Keyang Luo,Shengju Qian,Litong Gong,Tiezheng Ge,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Draft Forcing 通过特征级草稿与在策略训练的验证器，将自回归视频扩散模型的分块生成加速约1.6倍且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散支持逐块流式生成，但每个块都调用大模型代价高；现有块级投机解码存在两点缺陷：(1) 现成草稿模型偏离目标轨迹，导致历史误差累积；(2) 或省略验证，或依赖解码后的美学打分而非与目标的一致性验证。

Method: 提出 Draft Forcing 框架，耦合目标引导的草稿与在策略训练验证器。草稿端采用特征级草稿，以已提交块中持久化的多层目标隐状态为条件，并用多方面损失将其输出对齐目标轨迹；验证端提出在策略训练的特征空间验证器，预测经CDF校准的逐帧相对RMSE，训练时上下文从随机混合退火至推理对齐混合，无需VAE解码即可门控候选。

Result: 在14B Self-Forcing 目标上，VBench 取得1.63× DiT 与1.57× 端到端加速，质量损失极小；与正交KV压缩结合可达1.93× DiT（1.82×端到端）。可迁移加速 MiniMax-H3 的流式变体，超过50%帧由草稿生成且质量具竞争力。

Conclusion: 特征级对齐草稿与在策略特征空间验证相结合，可有效解决投机解码中的轨迹漂移与验证代价问题，实现高质量、可组合、可迁移的流式视频扩散加速。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable streaming synthesis chunk by chunk, yet invoking the large model for every chunk remains costly. While chunk-level speculative decoding can offload chunks to a lightweight draft model, existing approaches suffer from two limitations: (1) an off-the-shelf draft diverges from the target's trajectory, compounding errors in history; and (2) they either omit verification or rely on costly post-decode aesthetic scores rather than verifying consistency against the target. We address both with Draft Forcing, a framework coupling target-guided drafting with an on-policy trained verifier. Specifically, to align the draft for high acceptance, we opt for feature-level drafting, conditioning it on multi-layer target hidden states persisted from committed chunks, whose outputs are pushed to match target trajectories with multi-aspect losses. For verification, we propose an on-policy trained feature-space verifier predicting CDF-calibrated frame-wise relative RMSE against the target. It is trained on speculative rollouts as contexts anneal from random to inference-aligned mixtures, and gates proposals without VAE decoding. On the 14B Self-Forcing target, Draft Forcing achieves $1.63\times$ DiT and $1.57\times$ end-to-end speedups on VBench with minimal quality loss, and up to $1.93\times$ DiT ($1.82\times$ end-to-end) when combined with orthogonal KV compression. It generalizes to accelerate a streaming variant of MiniMax-H3, generating over $50\%$ draft frames with competitive quality.

</details>


### [303] [AirVT-15k: A Query-Diverse UAV Video-Text Dataset and Query-Adaptive Evidence Alignment Network for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30339)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiangyang Jia,Ting Gan,Xu Zhang,Shuquan Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无人机视频-文本检索基准 AirVT-15k（15,504 视频、155,040 双语描述）及侧重局部证据选择与时序差分建模的检索方法 AirFocus-CST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV-TVR 需同时匹配场景上下文与细微物体变化：如“港口布局”类查询依赖全局场景上下文，而“船只离港”类查询依赖局部功能线索与运动状态；现有基准虽提供多样化航拍描述，却未在同一视频内区分不同证据需求。

Method: 构建 AirVT-15k：每段视频配 10 条互补中英双语描述，覆盖场景、物体状态与时间变化。方法 AirFocus-CST：选取与查询相关的帧与图像块，编码时序特征差分，并将由粗到细交互（CFI）与最优传输对齐（OTA）相结合。

Result: 平均 mean recall 在 AirVT-15k 上达 63.67%，在 CapERA 上达 24.41%，分别超出最强基线 2.96 与 1.91 个百分点。

Conclusion: 同一视频内不同查询的证据需求差异值得显式建模；局部帧/块选择加时序差分与 CFI+OTA 对齐可有效提升 UAV 视频-文本检索性能，新基准为该方向提供评测支撑。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle video-text retrieval (UAV-TVR) requires matching language to both scene context and subtle object changes. A harbor-layout query relies on global scene context, whereas a departing-vessel query requires local functional cues and motion states. Existing benchmarks provide diverse aerial descriptions but give limited attention to distinct evidence needs within the same video. We introduce AirVT-15k, a benchmark of 15,504 UAV videos and 155,040 captions, with ten complementary bilingual captions per video describing scenes, object states, and temporal changes. We also propose AirFocus-CST, which selects query-relevant frames and patches, encodes temporal feature differences, and combines coarse-to-fine interaction (CFI) with optimal-transport alignment (OTA). Average mean recall reaches 63.67% on AirVT-15k and 24.41% on CapERA, improving over the strongest baselines by 2.96 and 1.91 percentage points, respectively. The dataset and code are available at https://chenxi000233.github.io/AirVT-15k-site/.

</details>
