### [304] [Shift by Analogy for Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30342)
*Zanwu Liu,Bo Li,Guanglin Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Shift by Analogy (SBA)，通过"移位-匹配"流程为未见属性-对象组合构建视觉代理，从而让未见组合"被看见"，在三个基准上优于强 CZSL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合零样本学习（CZSL）需识别训练中未出现的属性-对象对。未见对仅有语义描述而无观测到的视觉参照，导致语义与视觉空间对应不足。作者希望为未见组合构造可匹配的视觉参照，弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 核心为"加减加"规则：在两个已见图像-文本残差间提取属性变化，并将其迁移到源对象上，作用于目标对象的一个参考残差，从而把目标自身的文本特征向其视觉中心移位；再经可学习细化生成代理特征用于匹配查询图像。训练时每个目标被排除在其支持集之外，用其他对的参考构造识别。辅以类比库与同对象注意力扩展候选构造，并通过联合学习融合代理匹配、语义证据与基元证据。

Result: 视觉中心分析表明未见组合的语义-视觉对应显著改善；在三个标准基准的闭世界与开放世界评测中，SBA 一致优于强 CZSL 基线。

Conclusion: 通过类比移位显式构造未见组合的视觉代理，可有效缓解 CZSL 中未见对缺乏视觉参照的问题，且方法通用、在闭世界与开放世界设定下均具优势。

Abstract: Compositional zero-shot learning recognizes attribute-object pairs absent from training. An unseen pair has a semantic description but lacks an observed visual reference. We propose Shift by Analogy (SBA) to make unseen compositions visually seen through a shift-then-match process. Its core add-subtract-add rule transfers an attribute change between two seen image-text residuals on a source object to a reference residual on the target object. This shifts the target's own text feature toward its visual center; learned refinement produces a proxy for matching query images. During training, each target is excluded from its supports, and recognition is learned using references constructed from other pairs. An analogy library and same-object attention extend this construction across candidates, while joint learning integrates proxy matching with semantic and primitive evidence. Visual-center analysis confirms substantially improved correspondence for unseen compositions. Experiments across three standard benchmarks show that SBA consistently outperforms strong CZSL baselines under both closed- and open-world evaluation.

</details>


### [305] [SceneCast: Learning Indoor Scene Synthesis from Curated Synthetic Videos with LLM Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30345)
*Ziyang Zong,Canming Xia,Haoran Xu,Guang Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneCast 是一个无需人工标注、基于大规模无标签视频训练的 3D 室内场景合成框架，并通过 LLM 奖励的强化学习与可控视频数据筛选，在场景质量上超越现有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 室内场景合成方法高度依赖人工标注数据集，成本高且泛化能力受限；因此作者希望利用大规模、无标注的网络视频作为训练来源，但面临两个挑战：视频仅提供部分视角，模型需外推未见 3D 区域且易出错；在线原始视频未经整理，条件不可控、质量参差。

Method: 方法包含两部分。其一，设计基于 Transformer 的模型顺序预测物体，并用强化学习微调，由 LLM 充当空间常识评估器，通过思维链推理和可执行代码的几何检查提供奖励信号，以修正物体配置。其二，提出数据筛选方法 trim，利用可控视频生成模型生成训练视频，并通过多准则质量评估与基于熵的加权策略仅保留高质量的前缀片段。

Result: 在筛选后的合成视频集上评估，该无标注方法在场景质量上优于当前最先进的监督方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明无需人工标注、仅借助筛选后的生成视频与 LLM 空间常识奖励，即可实现高质量 3D 室内场景合成，为降低标注依赖并提升泛化性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: 3D indoor scene synthesis requires generating objects to form a complete and coherent scene. Existing approaches rely heavily on manually annotated datasets, leading to high costs and limited generalization. We present SceneCast, an annotation-free framework that trains on large-scale, unlabeled videos, addressing two key challenges: (1) Videos provide only partial views of a scene, requiring the model to extrapolate unseen 3D regions, which is inherently error-prone; (2) Raw videos available online are uncurated, suffering from uncontrolled conditions and variable data quality. First, we design a Transformer-based model that predicts objects sequentially and is fine-tuned with reinforcement learning (RL), where an LLM acts as a spatial common-sense evaluator. The LLM provides reward signals through chain-of-thought reasoning and code-executable geometric checks to refine object configurations. Second, we introduce \trim, a data curation approach that generates training videos with controllable video generation models. Crucially, it retains only high-quality prefix clips via multi-criteria quality assessment with an entropy-based weighting strategy. Evaluated on curated synthetic video sets, our annotation-free approach outperforms state-of-the-art supervised methods in scene quality.

</details>


### [306] [NNS-GS: Non-Local Neural Scattering for Relightable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30351)
*Shiyu Liang,Yang Gao,Lili Wang,Aimin Hao,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NNS-GS：面向高斯泼溅的非局部神经散射建模，结合几何引导的局部反射，实现可重光照的半透明物体三维资产重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半透明物体的重光照困难在于次表面散射使光照变化具有全局耦合性：某一表面位置的光照变化会通过散射影响其他区域的外观。现有基于高斯（Gaussian）的散射模型通常仅从局部特征预测散射，难以刻画这种非局部依赖关系。

Method: 提出非局部神经散射模块，在光空间中学习可见光表面区域对视图空间着色点的贡献，并以区域漫反射响应与空间关系为条件；反射模块提供这些漫反射响应，并利用额外几何引导捕捉高光与锐利阴影边界；采用基于锚点的渐进训练策略，先建立漫反射-散射基座，再引入镜面反射，并对各分量分别监督，以减少分量混叠、实现可控散射外观。

Result: 在标定点光源条件下，合成场景与真实半透明场景的平均PSNR分别较现有方法提升2.47 dB与0.81 dB；额外合成对比表明，在调节散射衰减时NNS-GS更贴近参考结果。

Conclusion: 将非局部散射建模与几何引导的局部反射解耦并结合渐进训练，可有效提升半透明物体高斯表示的重光照保真度与散射外观可控性。

Abstract: Creating relightable 3D assets of translucent objects is challenging because changing the illumination at one surface location can affect the appearance elsewhere through subsurface scattering. Existing Gaussian-based scattering models typically predict scattering from local features, making this dependence difficult to capture. We present Non-Local Neural Scattering for Gaussian Splatting (NNS-GS), which learns non-local scattering together with geometry-guided local reflection. The scattering module learns how illumination from light-visible surface regions in light space contributes to the shading point in view space, conditioned on regional diffuse responses and spatial relationships. The reflection module supplies these diffuse responses and uses additional geometric guidance to better capture specular highlights and sharp shadow boundaries. To reduce component mixing and support controllable scattering appearance, anchor-based progressive training establishes the diffuse-scattering base before introducing specular reflection and supervises the components separately. Under calibrated point lighting, NNS-GS improves mean PSNR over existing methods by 2.47 dB and 0.81 dB on the synthetic and real translucent scenes, respectively. Additional synthetic comparisons further show that NNS-GS better matches the reference when adjusting scattering attenuation.

</details>


### [307] [STAR: Structure-Aware Temporal Action Recovery for Point Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30354)
*Yawar Rehman,Min Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAR——结构感知的时序动作恢复框架，用于点监督时序动作定位，通过角色建模与提案重排改善伪标签质量，在四个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督时序动作定位在视频数据快速增长背景下需求迫切；现有方法依赖片段级动作响应生成伪标签，但存在边界模糊、提案过度延长、时序结构薄弱的问题。

Method: 1) 在基础S模型上引入时序差分交互注意力模块，捕捉前向与后向片段变化；2) 学习start/core/end角色的内部时序结构建模；3) 通过全点提案排序（All point proposal ranking）与基于Rolebank的结构重排抑制邻居跨越和结构不一致提案；4) 检测器向STAR反馈监督，将高质量检测预测以soft的start/core/end/action/background目标回传，进一步精炼伪标签。

Result: 在四个标准基准上验证有效性（I3D特征）：THUMOS'14 AVG@0.1:0.7 达67.2%，GTEA 58.1%，BEOID 74.6%，ActivityNet-1.3 AVG@0.5:0.95 达31.7%，多项基准上优于以往点监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知的角色建模、提案重排与检测器反馈闭环，STAR有效缓解了点监督伪标签的边界模糊与结构不一致问题，提升了时序动作定位性能。

Abstract: Point supervised temporal action localization is becoming the need of time in this era of ever growing and non-stop video data. Existing method mainly rely on snippet level action response to generate pseudo labels. However, these labels often suffer from boundary ambiguity, over extended proposals, and weak temporal structure. To address these issues, we propose STAR, a structure-aware Temporal Action Recovery framework for point supervised temporal action localization. STAR works by enhancing the base S model with temporal difference interaction with attention module to capture forward and backward snippet changes, and introduces the learning of start, core, and end roles to model the internal temporal structure of actions. In addition, STAR further improves the pseudo label generation through All point proposal ranking and Rolebank based structural re-ranking, which suppress neighbor crossing and structurally inconsistent proposals. Finally, temporal action detector to STAR feedback supervision transfers high quality detector prediction back to the STAR model through soft start, core, end, action, and background targets. This helps to further refine the pseudo labels. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks shows the effectiveness of STAR. By using I3D features, STAR achieves 67.2% at AVG@0.1:0.7 on THUMOS'14, 58.1% on GTEA, 74.6% on BEOID, and 31.7% at AVG@0.5:0.95 on ActivityNet-1.3, outperforming previous point supervised methods across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [308] [Boosting zero-shot learning through neuro-symbolic integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30358)
*Francesco Manigrasso,Fabrizio Lamberti,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLPN 将零样本分类表述为视觉-语义嵌入空间中的原型匹配，并用逻辑张量网络（LTN）把类层级与高层归纳偏置作为可微逻辑公理注入训练，在 AWA2 与 SUN 的 GZSL 基准上取得最优性能且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少探索如何利用神经-符号（NeSy）技术在训练过程中注入先验知识以增强零样本学习（ZSL）能力；作者希望把符号知识表示与推理同深度网络的学习能力结合，以缓解对已见类的过早过拟合。

Method: 提出模糊逻辑原型网络（FLPN）：在视觉-语义嵌入空间中以原型匹配方式完成分类，并用 NeSy 损失训练。具体地，借助 Logic Tensor Network（LTN）将一阶逻辑语言落地为实张量间的可微运算，从而把背景知识（类与宏类的分类层级、以及鲁棒的高层归纳偏置）作为逻辑公理融入训练；这些偏置可处理类级属性中的例外情况，并约束同类图像间的相似性。此外，FLPN 同时学习类级原型与属性级原型，后者通过针对卷积或 Transformer 骨干分别设计的注意力机制获得。

Result: 在 GZSL 基准 AWA2 与 SUN 上达到 state-of-the-art 性能，匹配或超过对比算法，且额外计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 以 LTN 形式注入逻辑公理与高层归纳偏置是提升零样本识别的有效途径，可在几乎不增加算力成本的前提下显著改善泛化表现，并适用于不同骨干网络。

Abstract: Zero-shot learning (ZSL) aims to train deep neural networks to recognize objects from unseen classes, starting from a semantic description of the concepts. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) integration refers to a class of techniques that incorporate symbolic knowledge representation and reasoning with the learning capabilities of deep neural networks. However, to date, few studies have explored how to leverage NeSy techniques to inject prior knowledge during the training process to boost ZSL capabilities. Here, we present Fuzzy Logic Prototypical Network (FLPN) that formulates the classification task as prototype matching in a visual-semantic embedding space, which is trained by optimizing a NeSy loss. Specifically, FLPN exploits the Logic Tensor Network (LTN) framework to incorporate background knowledge in the form of logical axioms by grounding a first-order logic language as differentiable operations between real tensors. This prior knowledge includes class hierarchies (classes and macroclasses) along with robust high-level inductive biases. The latter allow, for instance, to handle exceptions in class-level attributes and to enforce similarity between images of the same class, preventing premature overfitting to seen classes and improving overall performance. % CHECK: original sentence had no verb ("Both class-level and attribute-level prototypes through an attention mechanism ...") FLPN learns both class-level and attribute-level prototypes, the latter through an attention mechanism specialized for either convolutional- or transformer-based backbones. FLPN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the GZSL benchmarks AWA2 and SUN, matching or exceeding the performance of competing algorithms with minimal computational overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/FrancescoManigrass/FLPN

</details>


### [309] [REACT: Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30366)
*Hesen Yang,Yuxue Liu,Yanzhe Chen,Weibo Zhao,Junwei Su,Dan Hu,Shenggan Cheng,Xuanlei Zhao,Wenchao Zhou,Junhua Wang,Lansong Diao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出上下文引导的专家组装方法REACT，按神经元对相似输入的响应进行分组，实现稀疏执行下的MoE化上循环（upcycling），在ImageNet-1K上以仅37.5%的FFN参数获得更优生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密前馈网络中神经元分组无影响（全部被评估），但稀疏执行下每个输入只访问少数专家，任意划分会保留预训练权重却割裂了输入本需一起使用的计算。因此upcycling应按神经元对相似输入的响应来组织。

Method: REACT（Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories）：利用冻结的去噪轨迹将输入上下文与预训练神经元响应关联。先对时间步做中心化以应对不同噪声水平的分布漂移，识别上下文；用其关联响应把完整神经元分配给路由专家；同一上下文初始化路由条目，使专家内容与目标输入对齐；用全局激活统计选择常激活的共享专家；最后通过逐层重构与端到端自适应精调初始化。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上生成质量更优，仅激活原FFN参数的37.5%，每token总激活参数减少27.6%。

Conclusion: 以响应一致性驱动的神经元分组策略可有效将稠密FFN转化为稀疏专家结构，在显著降低激活计算量的同时保持乃至提升生成性能。

Abstract: Dense feed-forward networks are unaffected by how their neurons are grouped: every neuron is evaluated. Sparse execution makes this grouping consequential, because each input can access only a few experts. An arbitrary partition can therefore retain pretrained weights while separating computations that an input needs together. We argue that upcycling should organize neurons according to their responses to similar inputs. To establish this correspondence, we introduce a context-guided upcycling approach REACT, Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories, that uses frozen denoising trajectories to connect input contexts with pretrained neuron responses. We first identify contexts after timestep-wise centering to account for distribution shifts across noise levels, then use their associated responses to allocate intact neurons to routed experts. The same contexts initialize the corresponding router entries, aligning expert contents with their intended inputs, while global activation statistics select an always-active shared expert. Layer-wise reconstruction and end-to-end adaptation refine this initialization for sparse execution. On ImageNet-1K, our approach achieves better generation quality while activating only 37.5% of the original FFN parameters and reducing total per-token activated parameters by 27.6%.

</details>


### [310] [Mind the Gaps: State-Guided Supervision Allocation for Object Detection Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30369)
*Jiehui Xie,Zhengxun Jin,Jiahao Zhang,Yuxuan Luan,Dongshen Han,Caiyan Qin,Sung-Ho Bae,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出状态引导的监督分配框架SGSA，将目标检测数据集蒸馏建模为数据集级渐进式监督分配问题，在MS COCO和PASCAL VOC上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测数据集蒸馏需在有限图像预算下同时保留分类与定位信号；现有方法侧重单图信息量，未显式考虑在整个蒸馏数据集上累积的监督，导致监督冗余与类别-尺度覆盖不均。

Method: 将检测数据集蒸馏表述为数据集级渐进监督分配问题：设立类别-尺度监督配额，维护记录已选合成图像所累积检测器预测的全局状态；依据目标配额与累积监督之间的剩余差距，驱动自适应目标采样、检测器引导的画布精炼及基于边际增益的候选选择，促使各图像提供互补监督。

Result: MS COCO上在0.25%–1.0%三个图像预算下，较匹配的OD3基线提升3.73–4.06 mAP；PASCAL VOC上三个预算下较最强评估基线提升4.53–6.90 mAP50。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了在有限图像预算下，由累积检测器反馈引导的数据集级监督分配的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in dataset distillation have enabled large-scale training datasets to be compressed into compact synthetic sets. However, dataset distillation for object detection remains challenging, as it requires preserving diverse classification and localization signals under limited image budgets. Existing approaches often emphasize individual-image informativeness without explicitly accounting for supervision accumulated across the distilled dataset, potentially leading to redundant supervision and uneven class-scale coverage. To address this limitation, we propose State-Guided Supervision Allocation (SGSA), a framework that formulates object detection dataset distillation as a dataset-level progressive supervision allocation problem. SGSA establishes class-scale supervision quotas and maintains a global state that tracks detector predictions accumulated from previously selected synthetic images. The remaining gaps between the target quotas and accumulated supervision guide adaptive object sampling, detector-guided canvas refinement, and marginal-gain-based candidate selection, encouraging complementary supervision across images. On MS COCO, SGSA outperforms a matched OD3 baseline by 3.73-4.06 mAP points across three image budgets (0.25%-1.0%). On PASCAL VOC, SGSA outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline by 4.53-6.90 mAP50 points across three image budgets. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of dataset-level supervision allocation guided by accumulated detector feedback under limited image budgets.

</details>


### [311] [ColonoWorld: Camera-Controlled Full-Procedure Colonoscopy Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30372)
*Jihwan Hong,Sunghwan Steve Cho,Ping Shu Ho,Joo Seong Kim,Bokyung Kim,Nam-Joon Kim,Simon See,Ji Won Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ColonoWorld 是一个以相机轨迹与结肠粗3D管状结构为条件、基于活体视频训练的结肠镜世界模型，可从整段结肠管生成从盲肠到直肠的完整检查过程，并按指定位置与时间放置息肉、器械和冲洗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜导航系统正从"引导内镜医师"走向"自主操控镜体"，错误运动可能损伤患者，因此开发与评估这类系统需要结肠镜世界模型。该模型须满足三点：遵循指令的镜体运动、保持结肠解剖结构一致、呈现病灶与操作事件。现有方案存在两类缺陷：通用场景训练的相机控制生成器在运动下丢失结肠结构；内镜生成器则缺乏相机控制。

Method: 提出 ColonoWorld：以相机轨迹（commanded camera motion）和结肠的粗3D管状（tube）几何为双重条件，在活体（in vivo）视频上训练。给定整段结肠管，可生成完整的盲肠至直肠检查序列，并可泛化到未见过的结肠形态；同时支持在指定位置与时间插入息肉、器械与冲洗等元素。

Result: 相比当前最先进的视频生成器，相机轨迹误差降低32%，FVD降低29%。

Conclusion: ColonoWorld 作为可控的结肠镜世界模型，在运动可控性与视觉真实性上均超越现有方法，为自主结肠镜导航系统的开发与评测提供基础；代码与标注将公开。

Abstract: Navigation systems in colonoscopy are advancing from guiding the endoscopist to steering the scope autonomously. Since an incorrect motion can injure the patient, developing and evaluating these systems calls for a world model of colonoscopy. Such a model must follow a commanded scope motion, keep the colon anatomy consistent, and show lesions and procedural events. Yet camera-controlled generators trained on general scenes lose the colon's structure under motion, and endoscopic generators offer no camera control. We present ColonoWorld, a colonoscopy world model conditioned on a camera trajectory and a coarse 3D tube of the colon, trained on in vivo videos. From a whole-colon tube, it generates a full cecum-to-rectum procedure, even for unseen colon shapes. It also places polyps, instruments, and irrigation at specified locations and times. ColonoWorld outperforms state-of-the-art video generators, reducing camera trajectory error by 32% and FVD by 29%. Our code and annotations will be released at https://aidaslab.github.io/ColonoWorld.

</details>


### [312] [BASE: A Backend-Agnostic Streaming Embodiment Layer for Full-Duplex Conversational Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30388)
*Chenye Yang,Jin Jiang,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BASE——一个后端无关的流式具身层，仅以对话模型输出的语音为接口，将实时全双工对话系统转化为具备全身手势、倾听反馈与轮次切换的3D虚拟人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D虚拟人方法将动作生成器与某一对话模型紧耦合（读取内部隐状态或联合训练语音与动作），导致对话模型变更即需重训动作模块、无法接入仅暴露音频的云端API，并纠缠了角色、音色与动作风格。

Method: 1) 仅由音频驱动的对话状态管理器，在同一时间轴上处理说话、倾听、空闲、打断与主动轮次，角色/音色/动作风格/3D外观可独立设定；2) 将离线2秒flow-matching手势模型改为滑窗续接推理，并采用重叠前瞻集成消除接缝抖动；3) Drift-Aware History Perturbation (DAHP)：向真值训练历史注入类推理漂移，缓解自生成运动历史误差累积。

Result: 在BEAT2上流式BASE达到FGD 0.342，与紧耦合的MIBURI（0.333）相当；强制选择中82%的观察者更偏好其身体动作。DAHP在单GPU上约50分钟即可缩小流式差距，并可迁移至第二个生成器。无需重训即可实时驱动级联流水线、三个开源全双工模型及一个云端API。

Conclusion: 解耦语音接口即可实现与紧耦合方案相当甚至更受偏好的流式3D具身，且具备强通用性；剩余流式差距主要源于运动历史误差累积而非未来语音缺失。

Abstract: Many end-to-end spoken dialogue models can now converse in real time and full duplex, and turning them into 3D avatars requires full-body gestures, listening feedback, and turn-taking. Existing methods couple the motion generator tightly to one dialogue model by reading its internal states or training speech and motion jointly. The motion module must then be retrained whenever the dialogue model changes, cannot be attached to cloud APIs that expose only audio, and entangles role, voice, and motion style. We propose BASE, a backend-agnostic streaming embodiment layer whose only interface to the dialogue model is its emitted speech. First, a conversation state manager driven solely by audio handles speaking, listening, idling, barge-in, and proactive turns on one timeline, while role, voice, motion style, and 3D appearance are set independently. Second, to run an offline 2 s flow-matching gesture model on live audio, we reformulate inference as sliding-window continuation with overlapped look-ahead ensembling, which removes seam jitter. Third, we show that the remaining streaming gap comes from errors accumulating in the self-generated motion history rather than missing future speech; our Drift-Aware History Perturbation (DAHP) reduces this gap in about 50 min on one GPU by injecting inference-like drift into ground-truth training histories, and transfers to a second generator. On BEAT2, streaming BASE reaches an FGD of 0.342, on par with the tightly coupled MIBURI (0.333), and viewers prefer its body motion over MIBURI's in 82% of forced-choice judgments. Without retraining, the same layer drives a cascaded pipeline, three open full-duplex models, and a cloud API in real time.

</details>


### [313] [LiquidGazeNet: Towards Event-Based Near-Eye Gaze Estimation under Low-Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30392)
*Himanshu Kumar,Sanjay Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiquidGazeNet：纯事件相机+极性感知脉冲潜伏期编码+LTC循环模块的低光夜间注视估计框架，跨被试/跨场景平均误差3.75°/2.41°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光夜间近眼注视估计中，传统强度帧的质量与有效时间分辨率下降，难以支撑可靠估计；作者希望仅依赖事件数据完成注视估计。

Method: 将正/负极性事件分通道表示，采用指数衰减时间面保留像素级时间新近性；共享卷积编码器逐窗提取空间特征；两层LTC（液态时间常数）启发式循环单元对短时序进行因果整合；训练采用屏幕坐标回归与中心参照方向一致性构成的混合损失。

Result: 在Gaze-FELL数据集上按跨被试与跨场景协议评估，平均角度误差分别为3.75°与2.41°；并通过事件表示、时序模块、窗口时长与计算量消融验证各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 极性感知的时间新近性编码结合循环时序整合，在所评估的低光夜间条件下对纯事件注视估计有效。

Abstract: Near-eye gaze estimation under low-light nighttime conditions is challenging because the quality and effective temporal resolution of conventional intensity frames can be reduced. This paper presents LiquidGazeNet, an event-only gaze-estimation framework that combines polarity-aware spike-latency encoding with a custom LTC-based recurrent module. Positive- and negative-polarity events are represented in separate channels, while exponentially decayed time surfaces retain per-pixel temporal recency across successive event windows. A shared convolutional encoder extracts spatial features from each window, and a two-layer recurrent module composed of LTC-inspired cells integrates them causally over short temporal sequences. The network is trained using a hybrid objective that combines screen-space coordinate regression with centre-referenced directional consistency. The framework is evaluated on Gaze-FELL under cross-subject and cross-scene protocols and is further assessed through event-representation, temporal-module, window-duration, and computational ablations. LiquidGazeNet achieves mean errors of 3.75 degrees and 2.41 degrees under cross-subject and cross-scene evaluation, respectively. The results indicate that polarity-aware temporal-recency encoding, together with recurrent integration, is effective for event-only gaze estimation under the evaluated low-light nighttime conditions.

</details>


### [314] [GeLiFiSH: Recursive LiDAR Keypoint Estimation with Ground and Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30402)
*Nikolaos Stathoulopoulos,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeLiFiSH：一种无学习、扫描率下递归估计持久关键点的滤波器，结合地面分割与动目标分割，实现跨传感器、跨会话高可重复的LiDAR关键点与回环检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR配准与回环依赖稀疏关键点，需跨会话、视角与传感器保持可重复性；但基于累积子图的检测器逐子图独立处理导致延迟且重复观测不融合，基于高度的检测器依赖估计地面致误差传播，动态物体还会留下鬼影被误判为静态结构。

Method: 提出GeLiFiSH（几何LiDAR滤波+序贯假设检验）：高度-结构检测器运行于滚动体素地图；检测结果以位置、协方差与存在概率初始化轨迹，经序贯概率比检验（SPRT）接受或剔除；地面分割提供传感器无关的高度参考；动目标分割在插入地图前剔除动态点。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI、HeLiMOS、HeLiPR（覆盖5种LiDAR、多会话）上，GeLiFiSH平均关键点可重复性最高；仅用关键点的跨会话配准召回率第二，仅次于提取点数为其4倍的ISS；仅一组参数、无需传感器高度先验，地面与动目标分割模块平均性能均优于基线。

Conclusion: 通过滚动体素图上的序贯假设检验融合重复观测，并以传感器无关地面参考和动目标剔除消除误差源，可在扫描率下在线获得持久、可重复的关键点，且具备跨传感器泛化能力。

Abstract: LiDAR registration and loop closure often rely on sparse keypoints, which must remain repeatable across sessions, viewpoints, and sensors. To handle differing scan patterns, detectors typically operate on accumulated submaps, which introduces latency and processes each submap independently, so repeated observations are never fused. Height-based detectors also reference structure to an estimated ground, so its inconsistency across sessions propagates into the keypoints. Moreover, dynamic objects leave ghost trails that can be mistaken for static structure. We present GeLiFiSH (Geometric LiDAR Filtering with Sequential Hypothesis testing), a learning-free filter that recursively estimates persistent keypoints at scan rate. A height-structure detector runs on a rolling voxel map, and detections initialize tracks with a position, covariance, and existence probability, accepted or rejected by a sequential probability ratio test. Ground segmentation provides a sensor-agnostic height reference, and moving object segmentation removes dynamic points before map insertion. On SemanticKITTI, HeLiMOS, and HeLiPR, spanning five LiDARs and multiple sessions, GeLiFiSH attains the highest average keypoint repeatability among the compared detectors and the second-highest cross-session registration recall from keypoints alone, behind ISS which extracts four times as many keypoints. With one parameter set and no sensor-height prior, both ground and moving object segmentation modules outperform their baselines on average.

</details>


### [315] [Spatial-Zero: Self-Evolving Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30424)
*Yutong Li,Haoyu Zhao,Daoguo Dong,Ziyi Ye,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spatial-Zero是一个零外部数据的VLM自进化训练框架，通过自主空间探索同时解决"可靠生成有效空间任务"与"提供准确监督"两大难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理要求VLM理解度量距离、三维关系与视角相关几何，而现有训练通常依赖外部人工策展数据，成本高且难扩展。作者希望实现完全自治、零数据的空间能力自我进化。

Method: 提出Proposer-Coder-Oracle-Solver闭环：Proposer指定物体配置与问题；Coder将之编译为可执行场景程序，渲染多视角图像并输出显式场景几何；Geometric Oracle校验空间约束、可见性与答案唯一性，并从执行几何中导出真值标签；这些标签用于监督Solver，并产生难度反馈，验证结果反过来引导后续任务生成，形成迭代自进化。

Result: 在六个空间推理基准、三个VLM骨干上均获一致提升；迭代三次后Qwen3.5-9B平均准确率提升3.23个百分点；以Qwen3.5-4B为骨干时亦优于现有VLM自进化方法。

Conclusion: 执行式场景程序+几何Oracle的自动验证为空间推理提供了可靠、可扩展的零数据自监督来源，闭环迭代可稳定累积空间能力，无需外部标注数据。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires a vision-language model~(VLM) to understand metric distances, three-dimensional relationships, and viewpoint-dependent geometry. Learning these capabilities typically requires externally curated training data. This paper introduces Spatial-Zero, a VLMs' training framework that can self-evolve through autonomous spatial exploration with zero data. Spatial-Zero addresses two key challenges in autonomous spatial exploration: reliably generating valid spatial interaction tasks and providing accurate supervision for learning from these tasks. Specifically, a proposer specifies object configurations and questions, and a Coder turns them into executable scene programs that produce multi-view images and explicit scene geometry. A Geometric Oracle checks spatial constraints, visibility, and answer uniqueness and derives ground-truth labels from executed geometry; these labels supervise the Solver and ground difficulty feedback, while validation outcomes guide subsequent task generation. Across six spatial reasoning benchmarks and three VLM backbones, Spatial-Zero yields consistent gains through iterative self-evolution. After three iterations, Qwen3.5-9B improves average accuracy by 3.23 percentage points; with Qwen3.5-4B, Spatial-Zero also outperforms existing VLM self-evolution methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/YutongLi2024/Spatial-Zero

</details>


### [316] [Decompose and Describe: Narrowing the Diagnosis-to-Report Gap in 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30458)
*Zhisong Wang,Wanxing Chang,Weiwei Cao,Fenghe Tang,Bowen Shi,Ruifeng Yuan,Yong Xia,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"诊断到报告差距"（diagnosis-to-report gap）现象：MedVLMs在诊断VQA上的表现优于对同一CT生成报告的表现。为此提出DeCAD，将CT报告生成逐步分解为诊断、区域文本、区域视觉三个层级，可缩小该差距并在CT-RATE与Merlin上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动3D CT报告生成可支持放射科工作流，但医学视觉语言模型常遗漏诊断阳性发现。作者希望探究模型的诊断能力是否能迁移到自由形式报告生成，并解释、缩小二者之间的性能落差。

Method: 在五个MedVLMs上对比诊断VQA与同批CT报告生成的宏F1，定义diagnosis-to-report gap；发现常规Report Adaptation多数模型有效但无法弥合差距。提出DeCAD（Decompose CT Analysis and Describe）：先识别阳性发现再写报告，将诊断与描述组织为区域任务，并将每个任务限制在对应区域的视觉token上，分诊断级、区域文本级、区域视觉级三层逐步分解。

Result: 诊断级与区域文本级分解在五个MedVLMs上均提升报告级诊断性能；区域视觉分解收益有限，但在无医学VQA预训练的标准VLM上前两级仍有效，且配合区域级对比预训练编码器时区域视觉分解带来额外增益。完整DeCAD在CT-RATE与Merlin上达SOTA；分析显示诊断阶段CT token注意力更高，诊断输出与最终报告对参考阳性发现的召回率均优于Report Adaptation。

Conclusion: 显式地组织诊断过程可提升诊断性报告质量并有助于缩小诊断到报告差距；区域视觉层级的价值依赖编码器的区域级预训练质量。

Abstract: Automated 3D CT report generation could support radiology workflows, yet medical vision-language models (MedVLMs) often omit diagnostic findings from generated reports. We ask whether the diagnostic ability of these models carries over to free-form reporting. Across five MedVLMs, diagnostic VQA achieves higher macro-F1 than reports generated from the same CT studies and evaluated on the same disease categories. We call this task-dependent discrepancy the diagnosis-to-report gap. Conventional Report Adaptation improves reporting for most models but does not close the gap. To investigate and narrow it, we introduce DeCAD (Decompose CT Analysis and Describe), which progressively decomposes CT report generation at diagnostic, regional textual, and regional visual levels. The model identifies positive findings before writing a report, organizes diagnosis and description into regional tasks, and restricts each task to visual tokens from the corresponding region. Diagnostic and regional textual decomposition improve report-level diagnostic performance across the five MedVLMs, whereas regional visual decomposition provides limited additional gains. In a standard VLM without medical VQA pretraining, the first two levels remain effective; regional visual decomposition also provides further gains with a region-level contrastively pretrained encoder. Full DeCAD achieves state-of-the-art performance on CT-RATE and Merlin. Analyses show greater CT-token attention during diagnosis and higher recall of reference-positive findings in both the diagnostic output and final report than with Report Adaptation. These results show that explicitly organizing diagnosis improves diagnostic reporting and helps narrow the diagnosis-to-report gap.

</details>


### [317] [LiveMirror: Real-Time Diffusion Virtual Try-On on a Consumer GPU and Its Self-Supervised Adaptation to Indian Ethnic Wear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30461)
*Jigyansha Jeevan Rout,Sukanta Kumar Sabut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveMirror 是一个在单张 16 GB 消费级 GPU 上运行的实时摄像头虚拟试衣镜：核心是异步关键帧试衣，生成不阻塞显示，显示帧由光流对新关键帧进行形变，关键帧只通过交叉淡化替换，实现 720p/25 FPS 相机帧率显示、每 370–490 ms 更新一次新试衣结果；同时通过大腿遮盖掩码、自监督配对与仅注意力微调修正模型对民族服饰（kurta、sherwani）的长度误判，LPIPS 近似减半，并以约 1/20 推理成本在聚合指标上超过 FASHN VTON 1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式虚拟试衣（VTO）模型单图耗时数百毫秒到数秒，部署只能离线运行或退化为需要每件商品预制骨骼 3D 资产的 AR 镜，难以支撑实时摄像头场景；此外，在 VITON-HD 与 DressCode 上训练的模型存在文化盲区，会把及膝的 kurta 或 sherwani 渲染成及腰上衣或露腿连衣裙。

Method: 1) 异步关键帧试衣：显示线程不等待生成，每个显示帧为最新生成关键帧的光流形变，关键帧仅经交叉淡化替换，测得残余闪烁；2) 逐阶段延迟预算分析，并评估步数精简、一致性 LoRA、微型解码器、编译等加速手段对印花保真度的影响；3) 使用大腿遮盖掩码、从 24.9k 张模特电商图自动构建自监督配对、对 CatVTON 进行仅注意力微调（3.2 GPU 小时）以适配民族服饰；4) 两遍 hem-aware 掩码去除下摆以下被错误重着色的裤子，且训练零成本；镜面中下摆直接取自上一关键帧。

Result: 720p 显示达 25 FPS 相机帧率，新试衣每 370–490 ms 到达；民族服饰感知误差近似减半（IndoFashion LPIPS 0.137→0.077，Atlas 0.135→0.073，每个数据源各 200 对留出配对），西式上装无退化；两遍 hem-aware 掩码使下摆以下长裤错染的残余失败降至 3–4% 以内；适配模型在民族服饰各聚合指标上全面优于 FASHN VTON 1.5，推理成本约低 20 倍，权重可直接接入镜像系统。

Conclusion: 以异步关键帧+光流形变解耦生成与显示，可在单卡消费级 GPU 上实现实时扩散试衣镜；轻量自监督适配与掩码设计即可消除民族服饰的长度与下摆失效模式，并具备优于商用基线的性价比，模型权重可直接部署于该镜像。

Abstract: Diffusion virtual try-on (VTO) models produce convincing garment transfers from a single product photograph, but at hundreds of milliseconds to seconds per image every deployment today is offline or falls back to AR mirrors that need a rigged 3D asset per product. We present LiveMirror, a live-camera try-on mirror that renders a flat garment photograph onto a moving person with a diffusion model on one 16 GB consumer GPU. Its core is asynchronous keyframe try-on: generation never blocks the display, every displayed frame is an optical-flow warp of the latest generated keyframe, and keyframes are replaced only through a cross-fade. The display runs at camera rate (25 FPS at 720p) while a new try-on arrives every 370-490 ms; we measure the residual flicker, give a stage-by-stage latency budget, and report which accelerations (step count, consistency LoRA, tiny decoder, compilation) keep garment prints intact. We then show that the same GPU fixes the model's biggest cultural blind spot: try-on models trained on VITON-HD and DressCode render a knee-length kurta or sherwani as a torso-length top or a dress over bare legs. A thigh-covering mask, self-supervised pairs built automatically from 24.9k on-model e-commerce photographs, and 3.2 GPU-hours of attention-only fine-tuning of CatVTON halve the perceptual error on ethnic wear (LPIPS 0.137 to 0.077 on IndoFashion, 0.135 to 0.073 on Atlas; 200 held-out pairs per source) with no regression on Western tops. A two-pass hem-aware mask then removes the remaining failure, recoloured trousers below the hem, on all but 3-4% of pairs at no training cost; in the mirror the hem comes from the previous keyframe for free. The adapted model beats FASHN VTON 1.5 on every aggregate ethnic-wear metric at about $20\times$ lower inference cost, and its weights drop straight into the mirror.

</details>


### [318] [Struct2Det: Grounding Category Structure in Visual Evidence for Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30469)
*Kaixuan Chang,Huabing Zhou,Yanduo Zhang,Tao Lu,Liwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Struct2Det，利用类别结构（部件及其关系图）分三阶段引导少样本检测：关系结构编码、部件接地检测、验证引导精化，并构建工程图纸符号数据集InduSym。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本检测器难以区分外观相似但局部部件不同的类别，因为少量样本难以揭示决定类别身份的差异；已有类别描述说明了部件与关系，却无法确认这些部件是否出现在图像中。

Method: 三阶段框架：1）关系结构编码（Relational Structure Encoding）将类别图映射为部件嵌入与全局条件；2）部件接地检测（Component-Grounded Detection）用部件查询检索多尺度图像证据，并结合全局条件调制类别文本嵌入进行初始检测；3）验证引导精化（Verification-Guided Refinement）将候选区域与最高分类别期望的部件匹配，在类别分数接近且匹配可靠时用匹配对修正区域特征后由共享检测器重预测。类别结构同时作用于文本嵌入与区域特征两路分类输入。另构建InduSym数据集：674张工程图纸、275个类别、88,912个符号实例及每类结构图。

Result: 在InduSym上新类AP较最强基线提升6.02点，在Digitize-PID上提升9.68点，两数据集基类AP均为最高；消融显示少样本增益来自正确类别结构、图像级检索与候选验证；在少样本自然物体检测和零样本汉字识别上亦有提升。

Conclusion: 将类别结构显式引入并贯穿检测与验证环节，可有效提升少样本/零样本检测中细粒度相似类别的判别能力，且结构信息需与图像证据结合才能发挥作用。

Abstract: Few-shot detectors struggle to distinguish categories that look alike but differ in local components, because a few examples rarely reveal which differences determine category identity. Category descriptions specify components and relations but do not establish their presence in an image. We propose Struct2Det, which uses category structure to guide detection and verify candidates in three stages. Relational Structure Encoding maps category graphs to component embeddings and a global condition. Component-Grounded Detection uses component queries to retrieve multi-scale image evidence that, with the global condition, modulates category text embeddings for initial detection. Verification-Guided Refinement matches candidate regions with components expected by their top-scoring categories; when class scores are close and matches are reliable, matched pairs correct region features before the shared detector predicts again. Category structure informs both classification inputs: text embeddings integrate image-wide component evidence, while region features incorporate candidate-specific matches. We also construct InduSym, with 88,912 symbol instances across 275 categories from 674 engineering drawings and a structural graph per category. Struct2Det improves few-shot-category AP over the strongest baseline by 6.02 points on InduSym and 9.68 on Digitize-PID, with the highest base-category AP on both. InduSym ablations show few-shot gains from correct category structure, image-level retrieval, and candidate verification. Further tests show gains in few-shot natural-object detection and zero-shot Chinese character recognition.

</details>


### [319] [Inclusion of obstacles in ground point classification in ALS data for terrain models used for analysis of vehicle traversability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30471)
*Mikael Lundbäck,Erik Wallin,Sima Mohtasami,Martin Servin,Mattias Nyström,Henrik Persson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文评估多种ALS点云地面滤波算法在森林环境（含障碍物计入地面类）下的性能，发现算法可行但高度依赖参数，CSF算法地形模型RMSE最优（平均0.08米）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林场景下ALS点云分类因地形类型、传感设备、飞行参数及点密度差异大而困难；近年点密度显著提升使面向低密度点云的既有算法可能失效；且多数算法无法将巨石等障碍物与灌木、下层植被等非障碍物区分开。研究面向越野机械路径规划与导航，需将障碍物纳入地面类以生成可用地形模型。

Method: 在将障碍物归入地面类的设定下，对多种ALS地面点分类算法进行对比评估；点云层面采用Precision、Recall、F1-score，地形模型层面采用RMSE及差异图（maps of differences）作为评价指标。

Result: 多种算法表现良好，但性能高度受参数设置影响；地形模型RMSE最优为CSF算法，平均RMSE为0.08米。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，现有算法可经调整后将障碍物纳入地面类；参数选择对性能影响显著，需针对性调优。

Abstract: Classification of ALS point clouds has been studied for decades, but it remains challenging in forest contexts due to the great variety of terrain types, sensor equipment, flight parameters, and resulting point densities. Point cloud density has increased significantly in recent years, and existing algorithms, developed for lower density point clouds, may no longer be effective. It is beyond the scope of most existing algorithms to classify obstacles, such as boulders, while also distinguishing non-obstacles, such as bushes and undergrowth. We evaluate the performance of different algorithms for ground point classification in ALS data in a context where obstacles are included in the ground class, resulting in terrain models useful for offroad machinery route planning and navigation. The evaluation is performed using metrics directly on the point clouds (Precision, Recall, $F_1$-score) and on resulting terrain models (RMSE and maps of differences). The results show that there are several algorithms that perform well, but performance is highly affected by the parameters used. The best results in terms of terrain model RMSE are obtained with the CSF-algorithm, with an average RMSE of 0.08 meters. The results indicate that existing algorithms can be adapted to include obstacles in the ground class under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [320] [Target-Part Preference Optimization for Part-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30472)
*Zishan Qin,Rui Li,Zeyu Zhang,Sevvandi Kandanaarachchi,Lina Yao,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究能否用基于DPO的偏好微调，在有限部件标注数据上、不改架构地适配预训练的部件感知3D生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部件感知3D生成需全局合理且部件完整、边界清晰，但高质量部件分解数据稀缺，难以支撑大规模有监督微调。

Method: 用不同随机种子生成候选并对照GT几何指标排序，挑选跨种子质量差距最大的(object, target-part)样本；每个样本存一对共享同一干净GT组合潜变量、仅高斯噪声不同的种子流轨迹。由FlowDPO导出参考相对的target-block偏好损失，将两条轨迹与冻结参考模型比较；同时以target block与完整部件集上的GT流匹配损失提供绝对监督，仅更新DiT。另设人工排序对照与合成裁剪构造的受控loser目标。

Result: 三种训练集规模中，中等规模在留出Objaverse上最优，在多部件聚合指标上超过预训练生成器与已报道的直接有监督微调基线；对配对顺序、目标部件覆盖度及成对与GT监督平衡敏感；跨数据集在分布偏移下增益不一致。

Conclusion: DPO式偏好微调可在有限部件标注下有效提升部件感知3D生成，但效果依赖数据规模与监督配比，且跨域泛化有限。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation requires not only globally plausible objects but also complete, well-separated semantic parts, yet high-quality part decompositions remain too scarce to support large-scale supervised fine-tuning. We study whether preference fine-tuning based on Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) can adapt a pretrained part-aware generator from limited part-annotated data without architectural changes. We rank candidates generated with different random seeds against ground-truth (GT) geometric metrics, and select the (object, target-part) examples with the largest across-seed quality gap. Each selected example stores a seed pair whose two flow trajectories share one clean GT compositional latent and differ only in Gaussian noise. We derive a reference-relative target-block preference loss from FlowDPO that compares the two trajectories against a frozen reference model. GT flow-matching losses on the target block and complete part set provide absolute supervision, while only the diffusion transformer (DiT) is updated. Human-ranked controls test an alternative ordering source, and synthetic cropping provides controlled loser targets that are incomplete by construction. Among the three training-set scales, the medium-scale setting gives the strongest result on held-out Objaverse and outperforms both the pretrained generator and the reported direct supervised fine-tuning baseline on the multi-part aggregate. Further analysis shows sensitivity to pair ordering, target-part coverage, and the balance between pairwise and GT supervision. Cross-dataset experiments show less consistent gains under distribution shift.

</details>


### [321] [Test-Time Latent Computation to Improve Visual Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30480)
*Chen Yang,Zheng Fang,Wenqi Fan,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练解码方法Lobals，通过在潜空间中重新前向传播视觉注意力最强的连续层窗口，让MLLM在测试时"回看"视觉证据，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM研究表明测试时推理能力可通过潜空间额外计算增强，由此引出问题：MLLM能否在测试时通过潜空间额外计算改善视觉感知。

Method: 先验证MLLM中存在稳定、可复用、在推理时重新前向而不破坏正常生成的层；据此识别对视觉token注意力最强的连续层窗口，并对其进行重新前向传播，实现潜空间中的视觉证据回访；整个方法无需训练。

Result: 该潜空间回访策略在多种多模态设置下提升性能，包括通用视觉-语言任务、视觉幻觉任务以及以视觉为中心的任务。

Conclusion: 测试时在潜空间进行额外计算（层窗口重放）可有效增强MLLM的视觉感知，且无需额外训练，为多模态推理时计算扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent work on Large Language Models (LLMs) suggests that reasoning capabilities can be enhanced at test time not only through longer token-level reasoning traces, but also through additional computation in latent space. This raises a natural question for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs): can additional computation in latent space improve visual perception at test time? In this work, we first verify that MLLMs contain stable reusable layers that can be re-forwarded at inference time without disrupting normal generation. Based on this observation, we propose \textbf{Lo}ok \textbf{Ba}ck in the \textbf{L}atent \textbf{S}pace (\textbf{Lobals}), a training-free decoding method that identifies the contiguous layer window with the strongest visual-token attention and re-forwards through it, allowing the model to revisit visual evidence in latent space. We demonstrate that this latent-space revisit strategy can improve MLLMs' performance across diverse multimodal settings, including general vision-language tasks, visual hallucination tasks, and vision-centric tasks.

</details>


### [322] [Do learned image captioning metrics generalize? Auditing duplicated samples in human-judgment benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30488)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Jin Li,Chunlong Xia,Longjiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了最新三代图像描述评价指标（Polaris、Nebula、Spica）与人类判断的相关性主张，发现训练/测试集存在像素级重复样本、相关性主要由"最低分检测"项贡献，并提出更严格的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式图像描述指标以与人类判断的相关性作为泛化性主张，但训练与测试数据可能重叠，导致相关性被高估。作者希望系统检验这一主张是否成立。

Method: 对 Polaris、Nebula、Spica 三个人类判断集做像素级图像匹配，检测训练-测试重复；通过受控重训练（Pearl、DENEB）量化重复样本对指标的提升；将 Kendall τ 分解为"最低分检测项"与"其余排序项"；提出新协议：仅用不含测试样本训练的模型排序指标、报告两项指标、并用配对检验比较指标。

Result: Spica 训练集包含约三分之二的 Polaris 测试集且评分相同；Pearl 的增益仅限重复样本并随 epoch 增长，DENEB 增益不足 1 分；Kendall τ 的主要部分来自近乎饱和的最低分检测项；近期测试集无法稳定区分 Polos、DENEB、Pearl，而冻结特征上的岭回归可匹敌或超过三者；新协议下零样本视觉-语言评判器在三个新测试集上排名第一，微调评判器在三个早期基准上排名第一。

Conclusion: 现有学习式指标的报告相关性因数据重复与指标分解结构而被显著高估；应采用排除测试样本训练、双项报告与配对检验的协议，并公开了过滤列表与代码。

Abstract: Learned image captioning metrics are trained and tested on human judgments, so their reported correlation is a claim about generalization. We audit this claim for the three most recent collections of such judgments, Polaris, Nebula, and Spica, matching images at the pixel level. First, test samples reappear in training sets: the Spica training set contains about two-thirds of the Polaris test set, with identical human ratings. Second, controlled retraining shows that duplicates can inflate a metric: for Pearl, the gain is confined to the duplicated samples and grows with every epoch; for DENEB, it stays below one point. The gain depends on the learner and on how often a training set repeats a sample. Third, Kendall's $τ$ over all samples is the sum of two terms, detecting captions with the lowest human score and ranking the others; the first supplies most of the reported correlation and is nearly saturated. The recent test sets cannot order Polos, DENEB and Pearl consistently, and a ridge regression on frozen features matches or exceeds all three. Fourth, we propose a protocol that ranks a learned metric only with a model trained without test samples, reports both terms, and compares metrics by paired tests. Under it, zero-shot vision--language judges rank first on the three recent test sets, and a fine-tuned judge ranks first on the three earlier benchmarks. The same analysis applies to any evaluator learned from human judgments; filter lists and code are released at https://github.com/morninghut/ic-dataset-audit .

</details>


### [323] [When Do Spatial Plans Help Text-to-Image Generation? Causal Control, System Utility, and Text Responsiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30514)
*Lu Han,Jingyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 空间规划（生成的布局）对生成器的增益高度依赖两点：文本基线已能达到的能力，以及布局条件的精度/宽松度。文本在匹配算力下不劣于布局纠错；固定规则布局主要贡献是"分离已在正确顺序出现的对象"；而用更精确的自然语言指令（最小间距）可替代紧凑布局条件获得大幅增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清"给生成器提供空间规划"这一做法的真实收益来源：是模型从文本中无法获得的信息，还是仅仅因为布局条件更精确？作者希望通过受控实验区分"文本能力上限"与"条件精度"两个因素，从而判断布局/规划模块的必要性。

Method: 受控对比实验，三层设计：（1）18 个配对的训练种子，比较"纠正预测位置与尺寸"的布局条件与纯文本条件（训练算力匹配）；（2）在预训练生成器上比较固定规则布局与同一 text-only adapter，考察双物体任务成功率；（3）在 288 个新任务族上引入"最小间隔（minimum-gap）"指令，分别在纯文本、宽松间距区域、紧凑间距区域三种条件下测试，并做基线图像诊断（230/288 基线图像物体与方向正确但缺失所需间隔）。

Result: 位置/尺寸纠错使空间 IoU 提升 0.030，但纯文本在匹配算力下不劣（non-inferior）。固定规则布局相对 text-only adapter 将双物体任务成功率提升 12.50 个百分点，增益主要来自"把已按正确顺序出现的对象分开"。最小间隔指令在 text-only adapter 上提升 37.15 点，在宽松间距区域下最多仅 0.69 点，在紧凑间距区域下为 6.94 点。

Conclusion: 布局条件的收益并非来自信息本身，而取决于模型能否从文本中获得等价能力，以及条件是否提供了文本难以表达的精度（如紧凑区域的精确间隔）。当给出足够精确的指令时，文本通路可在很大程度上替代空间规划；反之，宽松布局条件几乎无增益。因此评估布局/规划模块应与忠实的文本基线在匹配算力下对照。

Abstract: A generator can follow a spatial plan, but the benefit of supplying that plan depends on what the model can already do from text. In a controlled study with 18 paired training seeds, correcting predicted positions and sizes improves spatial intersection-over-union by 0.030, yet direct text is non-inferior at matched training compute. In a pretrained generator, a fixed rule layout improves two-object task success over the same text-only adapter by 12.50 percentage points, mostly by separating objects that already appear in the correct order. We then ask whether a more precise instruction can achieve the same separation across conditioning paths. On 288 new task families, adding a minimum-gap instruction improves success by 37.15 points with the text-only adapter, by at most 0.69 points with widely spaced regions, and by 6.94 points with tightly spaced regions. Under tight regions, 230 of 288 baseline images have the correct objects and direction yet miss the required gap, ruling out a high baseline success rate as the sole explanation for the weak text response.

</details>


### [324] [Field-Level Crop Classification via Similarity-Induced Supervision from Imperfect Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30515)
*Raul Lomonte Figueiredo,João Rebouças Dorea,Dário Augusto Borges Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需新地面调查的流程，从有偏的公共土地覆盖产品中构建大尺度作物分类模型：用相似度聚类把标注从样本级提升到组级以降低语义不一致，用光学影像分割田块后在块内多数投票以降低预测的空间不一致；在巴西两个市镇SAR时序作物制图上平均类别精度最高提升10.14个百分点，少数类作物受益明显，且导出面积与IBGE/PAM官方统计一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带地区持久云覆盖限制光学观测，SAR可全生育期连续观测；但另一障碍是标签质量：国家级土地覆盖产品存在系统性偏差，低估稀有作物，直接在其上训练会继承并放大误差。因此需要无需新野外调查或人工纠错的弱监督大尺度作物制图方法。

Method: 双机制弱监督框架。①语义一致性：以相似度聚类将SAR时序组织为高一致性簇，噪声标签仅用于为每个簇指派最具代表性的类别，把监督从样本级转为组级；②空间一致性：用光学影像分割模型勾绘田块边界，对块内像元预测做多数投票聚合，得到空间连贯的分类图。

Result: 两个研究区簇纯度分别达89.0%和91.2%；在Campo Verde（马托格罗索）与Luís Eduardo Magalhães（巴伊亚）两市镇，平均类别精度较噪声基线最高提升10.14个百分点，少数类作物提升显著；马托格罗索与巴伊亚州级分析无需重新调参即表现稳定；导出市镇作物面积与模型从未观测的IBGE/PAM官方统计相符。

Conclusion: 组级监督与田块级投票相结合，可有效抑制公共土地覆盖产品标签的语义与空间不一致，实现无可新地面调查的跨区域可迁移大尺度SAR作物制图，并与独立官方统计相互印证。

Abstract: Regional-scale crop mapping is critical for food production monitoring, yet remains challenging in tropical regions, where persistent cloud cover limits optical imagery. Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) enables continuous observation throughout the agricultural cycle, but a second obstacle is label quality: national-scale land cover products exhibit systematic biases that underestimate less prevalent crops, and training directly on them inherits and amplifies their errors. This work proposes a methodology to build large-scale crop classification models from imperfect public land cover products, requiring no new field campaign, ground survey, or manual label correction. The approach combines two mechanisms. The first reduces the semantic inconsistency of the labels by shifting supervision from the sample level to the group level: a similarity-based clustering model organizes SAR time series into coherent groups, and noisy labels are used only to assign each group its most representative class. The resulting clusters are highly pure with respect to the reference classes (89.0% and 91.2% in the two study regions). The second reduces the spatial inconsistency of the predictions: a segmentation model delineates crop field boundaries from optical imagery, and patch-level predictions are consolidated by majority voting within each field, yielding spatially coherent maps. Evaluated in two Brazilian municipalities, Campo Verde (Mato Grosso) and Luís Eduardo Magalhães (Bahia), the approach consistently outperforms the noisy baseline in mean class accuracy, with gains of up to 10.14 percentage points, especially for minority crops. A state-scale analysis across Mato Grosso and Bahia shows stable behavior without further model adjustment, and the derived municipal crop areas are consistent with official IBGE/PAM statistics, an independent estimate the model never observes.

</details>


### [325] [GeoRead3D: Grounding Part Descriptions for 3D Pointing and Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30519)
*Zhangcheng Hou,Kevin Tonkich,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出几何读出监督GRS与系统GeoRead3D，用点证据的加权质心来定位部件，弥合分割掩码重叠与部件几何任务之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言常通过部件指代物体（如指出某部件，或旋转任意姿态物体使两个命名部件处于给定方向），这两类任务都需要从物体中心指向部件中心的方向，而非部件自身朝向。常规做法是分割部件并取掩码质心，但当部件相对其到物体中心的距离较"分散"时，高掩码重叠并不能很好地预测指向方向；而语言引导的部件分割以掩码重叠为训练与评测目标，因此其输出与任务所需的部件几何之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出几何读出监督（GRS）：学习"点证据"——对观测点的权重，其加权质心给出部件位置；通过质心的位置来训练这些权重，并对重定向指令所用部件额外施加方向监督。系统GeoRead3D实现该监督：由语言模型将每个描述映射到已知部件名；双路部件证据（Dual-Route Part Evidence）将精炼的分割证据与直接预测的点证据结合以定位部件。

Result: 在PartNeXt与Artiverse上，GeoRead3D在74.1%与60.0%的查询中指向误差在22.5°以内，分别比最强基线高5.8与7.3个百分点；重定向成功率分别比最强基线高10.4与7.8个百分点。且仅用单个冻结编码器与单一输入姿态即可获得提升。

Conclusion: 以质心位置与方向为监督的点证据学习，可有效替代仅依赖掩码重叠的范式，显著提升部件指向与重定向任务性能，且不依赖多视角或多编码器。

Abstract: Language often refers to an object by its parts, as in asking to point to a part or to turn an arbitrarily posed object so that two named parts lie in given directions. Both tasks need the direction from the object's center to the part's center, not the part's own orientation. A natural approach segments the part and takes the centroid of its mask. However, high mask overlap predicts the pointing direction poorly when a part is spread out relative to its distance from the object center. Language-guided part segmentation is trained and evaluated by mask overlap, which leaves a gap between the masks it produces and the part geometry that these tasks use. We close this gap by learning point evidence, weights over the observed points whose weighted centroid gives the part's location. Geometric Readout Supervision (GRS) trains these weights through the centroid's position and adds direction supervision on the parts used in reorientation commands. GRS improves both tasks even with a single frozen encoder and a single input pose. We implement this supervision in GeoRead3D, a system for pointing and reorientation from part descriptions. A language model maps each description to a known part name. Dual-Route Part Evidence combines refined segmentation evidence with directly predicted point evidence to locate each part. On PartNeXt and Artiverse, GeoRead3D points within $22.5^\circ$ for 74.1% and 60.0% of queries, 5.8 and 7.3 percentage points above the strongest baselines. Its reorientation success exceeds the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 7.8 points.

</details>


### [326] [VeriOrigin: A Proactive Image Protection Framework for Provenance, Tamper Localization, and Lossless Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30523)
*Jiale Chen,Wei William Wang,Junnan Yin,Chongyang Shi,Li Dong,Yuanman Li,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一图像保护框架VeriOrigin，基于可逆CNN编码器XOnet，同时实现鲁棒溯源、高精度篡改定位与无损信道下的原始图像完美恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用水印方法难以在复杂失真下同时兼顾鲁棒的来源追溯（provenance）、高精度篡改定位以及无损信道中原始图像的完美恢复，缺乏统一且综合性强的保护框架。

Method: 脱离传统流（flow）模型架构，设计基于CNN的可逆编码器XOnet，由Xnet与Onet两个标准CNN水印子网络通过跨残差预测（cross residual prediction）实现可逆性；采用两阶段训练策略，先优化水印分支、再优化篡改定位分支；推理时对载体图像依次执行水印嵌入与篡改残差嵌入。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，VeriOrigin实现了精确的篡改定位与强鲁棒性，同时保证用于完整性认证的无损载体恢复。

Conclusion: VeriOrigin为通用水印提供了一种全面、统一的图像保护方案，验证了用CNN可逆编码器替代flow架构的可行性，兼具溯源、定位与无损恢复能力。

Abstract: Versatile watermarking, which seamlessly integrates provenance and tamper detection, has recently attracted widespread attention. In this paper, we propose \textbf{VeriOrigin}, a comprehensive and unified image protection framework that not only robustly achieves provenance and highly precise tamper localization under various complex distortions, but also guarantees perfect recovery of the original cover in lossless channels. To realize this framework, we depart from conventional flow based architectures. Instead, we design \textbf{XOnet}, a CNN based invertible encoder. XOnet comprises two standard CNN based watermarking sub networks, Xnet and Onet, which achieve reversibility through cross residual prediction. Based on XOnet, we train the proactive protection scheme via a two stage strategy: first optimizing the watermarking branch, followed by the tamper localization branch. During inference, the cover image undergoes sequential watermarking and tamper residual embedding. Extensive experimental results on four datasets demonstrate that VeriOrigin achieves precise tamper localization and strong robustness, while guaranteeing lossless cover recovery for integrity authentication. Project page is available at https://veriorigin.github.io/

</details>


### [327] [Assembly-JEPA: Using World Models for Fine-Grained Industrial Image Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30530)
*Michael Hollmer,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于JEPA自监督世界模型的工业装配验证框架，将装配正确性判定建模为前后观测的预测性世界建模任务，在自建数据集上AUROC达86.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业装配自动验证面临视觉变化大、缺陷标注数据稀缺、错误粒度细等难题；现有基于目标检测、CAD模型或监督分类的流程难以跨产品与装配配置泛化。

Method: 以JEPA世界模型LeWorldModel为起点，研究架构需如何适配细粒度装配操作验证；将装配验证形式化为对装配过程"前—后"观测的预测性世界建模任务，采用自监督联合嵌入预测架构，而非依赖检测或分类头。

Result: 在工业装配任务实验中可可靠检测细粒度错误装配状态与动作—状态不匹配，并在新引入的装配验证数据集上取得86.9%的AUROC。

Conclusion: JEPA式预测表征可作为稳健且数据高效的工业装配验证基础；未来可进一步提升跨产品泛化与细粒度判别能力。

Abstract: Automated verification of industrial assembly processes remains a challenging problem due to substantial visual variability, limited availability of labeled defect data, and the fine-grained nature of many assembly errors. Existing approaches often rely on object detection, CAD models, or supervised classification pipelines, which can struggle to generalize across changing products and assembly configurations. In this work, we propose a novel self-supervised assembly verification framework based on Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs). Starting from the JEPA-based world model LeWorldModel, we investigate how the architecture must be adapted to verify the correctness of fine-grained assembly operations. To this end, we formulate assembly verification as a predictive world modeling task operating on before-and-after observations of assembly processes. Experimental results on industrial assembly tasks demonstrate that the proposed approach reliably detects both fine-grained incorrect assembly states and action-state mismatches, achieving an AUROC of 86.9% on a newly introduced assembly verification dataset. These results indicate that JEPA-based predictive representations constitute a promising foundation for robust and data-efficient industrial assembly verification.

</details>


### [328] [ReDiffDet: Rotation-equivariant Diffusion Model for Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30531)
*Zeyu Ding,Yong Zhou,Jiaqi Zhao,Hancheng Zhu,Wen-Liang Du,Rui Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDiffDet，将旋转目标检测建模为旋转等变去噪扩散过程，用二维高斯分布表示有向框，并设计条件编码器与对齐解码器，显著超越扩散模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型用于有向目标检测存在三大问题：任意旋转的目标需要编码方向信息；随机初始化框难以准确定位密集排列和极端长宽比目标；有向框与图像特征之间存在错位。

Method: 1) 将有向框表示为二维高斯分布作为去噪范式基础，并证明在该表示与模型框架下反向过程具有旋转等变性；2) 设计带条件框的条件编码器，避免框在整个图像中随机放置；3) 提出对齐解码器实现有向框与图像特征的对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明ReDiffDet性能良好，且显著优于扩散模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过旋转等变扩散建模、条件编码与特征对齐设计，可有效解决扩散模型在有向目标检测中的方向编码、随机框定位与特征错位问题。

Abstract: The diffusion model has been successfully applied to various detection tasks. However, it still faces several challenges when used for oriented object detection: objects that are arbitrarily rotated require the diffusion model to encode their orientation information; uncontrollable random boxes inaccurately locate objects with dense arrangements and extreme aspect ratios; oriented boxes result in the misalignment between them and image features. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReDiffDet, a framework that formulates oriented object detection as a rotation-equivariant denoising diffusion process. First, we represent an oriented box as a 2D Gaussian distribution, forming the basis of the denoising paradigm. The reverse process can be proven to be rotation-equivariant within this representation and model framework. Second, we design a conditional encoder with conditional boxes to prevent boxes from being randomly placed across the entire image. Third, we propose an aligned decoder for alignment between oriented boxes and image features. The extensive experiments demonstrate ReDiffDet achieves promising performance and significantly outperforms the diffusion model baseline.

</details>


### [329] [BEND: Spectral Bregman Losses for Multi-Image Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30532)
*Mohamed-Hicham Leghettas,Markus Püschel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEND：通过在Bregman散度族中双层搜索谱域散度以构造去学习损失，单模型一次性抹除SD上48张可验证记忆图像，遗忘更彻底且生成质量保持得更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型会记忆并近乎逐字复现训练图像，带来版权与隐私风险。数据去学习需在不重训的前提下抹除特定样本影响，且是双向目标：既要彻底遗忘目标，又要保持其余生成质量。已有方法使用平方L2扩散损失（最多加手工频率掩码），但任何Bregman散度在数学上都是有效选择，平方L2只是最简单的一例，这一族尚未被探索。

Method: 首个在该族中搜索的方法：通过双层优化循环，为去学习任务拟合一个谱域Bregman散度，使损失函数本身被塑形以兼顾彻底遗忘与质量保护；同时将记忆图像重定向至其保留图像集的均值（ReTrack则重定向至单个近似副本）。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion上，单个BEND模型一次性抹除全部48张可验证记忆基准图像；遗忘程度不低于逐目标、顺序及联合训练的ReTrack（SSCD 0.084 vs 0.094–0.120），质量接近去学习前水平（CLIP-IQA 0.81 vs 0.64–0.69）。在CelebA-HQ上三项指标均优于SOTA，在MNIST与CIFAR-10上持平或超越。

Conclusion: 将损失函数本身作为可学习对象（在Bregman散度族中谱域搜索）是提升扩散模型数据去学习双向性能的有效途径，且支持多目标同时抹除。

Abstract: Diffusion models memorize training images and reproduce them almost verbatim when prompted, creating copyright and privacy liabilities. Data unlearning erases the influence of specific training samples from a trained model without retraining, and is two-sided: it must forget the targets while preserving generation quality on everything else. Prior methods use the squared-L2 diffusion loss, at most with a hand-set frequency mask; however, it is known that every Bregman divergence is a mathematically valid choice and the squared-L2 is only the simplest example. BEND is the first to search this family: it fits a spectral Bregman divergence to the unlearning task through a bilevel loop, so the loss itself is shaped to forget thoroughly while protecting quality. It also redirects a memorized image to the mean of its retain images, where ReTrack, the state of the art, redirects it to a single near-copy. On Stable Diffusion, a single BEND model erases all 48 verifiably memorized benchmark images at once. It forgets at least as much as per-target, sequential, and jointly trained ReTrack (SSCD 0.084 versus 0.094-0.120), at the quality of the model before unlearning (CLIP-IQA 0.81 versus 0.64-0.69). BEND also outperforms the state of the art on CelebA-HQ in all three measures of the benchmark, and matches or exceeds it on MNIST and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [330] [Where Compositionality Hides: Shared-Content Interference in Vision-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30538)
*Chimata Anudeep,Harsh Vardhan Poddar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出均值擦除（mean-erasure）：将冻结图像嵌入投影至远离两条候选标题均值的方向，可在不改变图内标题排序的前提下使 Group Score 等于 Text Score，在 Winoground 上将 CLIP ViT-B/32 的 Group Score 从 9% 提升到 31%，表明大部分 Group 损失来自跨图比较而非语义绑定能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对组合基准（如 Winoground）要求图像偏好其匹配标题、标题也偏好其匹配图像。Group 决策失败可能源于"标题偏好错误"，也可能仅由跨图比较（偏置）造成，而 Group Score 将两者混合。要在"重训编码器"与"改评分规则"之间做选择，必须把这两种失效来源分离。

Method: 1) 定义 mean-erasure：对每张冻结图像嵌入，沿两张归一化候选标题均值方向做投影擦除；2) 理论证明该投影保持所有图内标题排序，并使 Group Score 恒等于基线 Text Score；3) 在 Winoground、ColorSwap 上对五个 CLIP/SigLIP 检查点验证；4) 进行分数分解，分析 Image 失败中正确联合分配已高于交换分配、仅因两图间偏移而失败的比例；5) 对比固定校正、学习式双线性打分器、以及基于检索标题估计方向的方案（2 条与 16 条标题）。

Result: Winoground 上 mean-erasure 将 CLIP ViT-B/32 的 Group Score 由 9% 提升至 31%，五个 CLIP/SigLIP 检查点提升 21–27 个百分点；ColorSwap 上颜色-物体绑定亦获相似增益。分数分解显示约 60% 的 Image 失败中正确联合分配得分已高于交换分配，仅因两图间偏移而失败。固定校正与学习式双线性打分器增益不足 1 点；基于检索标题方向的方法在 2 条标题时约 +5 点，在 16 条标题时低于基线。

Conclusion: 在重训编码器之前，应先测量 mean-erasure 可恢复的 Group 损失；若增益仅停留在基线 Text Score 水平，则说明瓶颈在跨图比较偏移而非编码器语义绑定，超出该水平的增益必须来自改变标题自身的决策。

Abstract: Paired compositional benchmarks such as Winoground ask each image to prefer its matching caption and each caption to prefer its matching image. A failed Group decision can come from a wrong caption preference or from the cross-image comparison alone, and Group Score merges the two. Choosing between retraining the encoder and changing the scoring rule therefore requires separating them. We study mean-erasure, which projects each frozen image embedding away from the mean of the two normalized candidate captions. We prove that the projection preserves every within-image caption ranking and makes Group Score equal baseline Text Score. On Winoground, mean-erasure raises CLIP ViT-B/32 Group Score from 9% to 31%, and five CLIP and SigLIP checkpoints gain 21 to 27 percentage points. ColorSwap shows a similar gain for color-object binding. A score decomposition shows that about 60% of Image failures already score the correct joint assignment above the swapped one and fail only on an offset between the two images. Fixed corrections and a learned bilinear scorer gain under one point. A direction estimated from retrieved captions gains about five points with two captions and falls below baseline with sixteen. Before retraining an encoder for paired retrieval, measure the Group loss that mean-erasure recovers: gains beyond baseline Text Score require changing the caption decisions themselves.

</details>


### [331] [SVUBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30541)
*Yuxuan Liang,Bohan Zhang,Haiyang Yu,Bocheng Hu,Ke Niu,Xu Li,Zhengtao Yao,Yan Wen,Haotian Chen,Xiaolei Chen,Jiacong Wang,Zhe Liu,Jingqun Tang,Benlei Cui,Fan Shi,Xiangyang Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SVUBench，一个包含 12,131 个问题和 62,881 个答案点、覆盖 10 项主动式任务的综合流式视频理解基准，并提出 Proactive Response Score（PRS）从正确性、及时性、历史一致性、内容质量四方面联合评估模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式流式视频理解要求模型解读正在发生的事件、及时响应并检索相关历史证据，但缺乏系统性评测手段，现有基准难以同时衡量"及时性"与"历史回溯"能力。

Method: 构建 SVUBench：涵盖感知、理解、推理三大层次的 10 项主动任务，采用模型辅助标注+人工校验；设计两种互补交互模式——Basic（从视频起始进行持续主动响应）与 Trajectory（流式过程中任务激活后需回溯历史并持续更新响应）；引入 Duration-Scaled Challenge，在四个视频时长层级上评测；提出 PRS 指标，配合任务自适应答案窗口，联合评估答案正确性、响应及时性、历史一致性与内容质量。

Result: 通过大量实验与分析，揭示了现有模型在主动式流式视频理解上的优势与局限。

Conclusion: SVUBench 提供了系统性评测框架，明确了现有模型的能力边界，并为主动式流式视频理解的后续研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Proactive streaming video understanding requires models to interpret unfolding events, respond timely, and retrieve relevant historical evidence. To systematically evaluate these capabilities, we introduce SVUBench, a comprehensive proactive streaming video benchmark comprising 12,131 questions and 62,881 answer points across ten proactive tasks spanning Perception, Understanding, and Reasoning. Constructed through model-assisted annotation and human verification, SVUBench introduces two complementary interaction modes. Basic evaluates sustained proactive responses from video onset, while Trajectory requires historical recovery and continuous response updates following task activation during streaming. A Duration-Scaled Challenge further evaluates these capabilities across four video duration tiers. We develop the Proactive Response Score (PRS) with task-adaptive answer windows to jointly assess answer correctness, response timeliness, historical consistency, and content quality. Extensive experiments and analysis reveal the strengths and limitations of existing models, highlighting directions for advancing proactive streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [332] [RAPID: Codec-Aware Video Decoding for Efficient Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30557)
*Yuna Lee,Yulhwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出编解码感知框架 RAPID，利用压缩码流中的参考帧与随机访问点信息，加速长视频的帧解码与帧选择，在小时级视频上实现最高 4.9x 的 TTFT 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频高效理解方法主要优化帧选择与 LLM 端，忽视了视频解码开销——解码甚至可能比 LLM prefill 更耗时；且现有帧选择方法需密集解码候选帧并打分，预处理成本高。

Method: RAPID 为编解码感知框架，含两部分：RAPID-Dec 利用参考帧信息与随机访问点，跳过不必要帧并并行解码保留帧，同时保证目标帧精确重建（H.264/AVC）；RAPID-Sel 以关键帧作为视频片段的粗粒度代表，避免现有方法所需的密集候选帧解码与打分。

Result: RAPID-Dec 相比最快基线在 H.264/AVC 视频上解码加速最高 3.7x；RAPID-Sel 在精度相当情况下预处理加速最高 3.0x；二者结合在小时级视频上相较使用最快基线解码器的 SOTA 帧选择方法实现 4.9x TTFT 加速。

Conclusion: 视频解码是长视频 VLM 推理中被忽视的关键瓶颈，直接利用压缩码流元数据可在不损失重建精度的前提下显著降低解码与选择开销，端到端提升长视频理解的推理效率。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) can process hour-long videos, but the large number of input frames incurs substantial inference overhead. Existing approaches to efficient long-video understanding largely overlook video decoding, which can take longer than LLM prefill. We propose RAPID (Reference- and Access-Point-Informed Decoding), a codec-aware framework that exploits information embedded in compressed bitstreams to reduce both frame decoding and selection costs. RAPID consists of two components. RAPID-Dec exploits reference-frame information and random access points to skip unnecessary frames and decode the retained frames in parallel while preserving exact reconstruction of the target frames. It achieves up to a 3.7x decoding speedup over the fastest baseline on H.264/AVC videos. RAPID-Sel uses keyframes as coarse representatives of video segments, avoiding the dense candidate decoding and scoring required by existing frame selection methods. It achieves comparable accuracy with up to a 3.0x preprocessing speedup. Together, RAPID achieves a 4.9x time to first token (TTFT) speedup on hour-long videos over a state-of-the-art frame selection method using the fastest baseline decoder.

</details>


### [333] [JONES-19: A Cultural Image Dataset Based on The Grammar of Ornament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30558)
*Linh Pham,Stuart Shieber,David Alvarez-Melis,Alexandros Haridis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JONES-19是一个基于《装饰法则》开放档案构建的1901幅装饰图案图像数据集，涵盖19种人类文化，并提供分类基准与误分类分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为计算机视觉分类任务及机器学习与艺术设计交叉研究提供具有挑战性的基准，填补人类设计制品图像数据集（区别于自然场景与常见物体）的空白。

Method: 基于Owen Jones《装饰法则》(1856)开放获取档案整理并标注数据；数据集特性包括小样本量、类间不平衡、依赖线条纹理/浮雕/色彩的细粒度判别；建立基线分类基准，并通过误分类错误模式分析类间关系（尤其文化相似性）。

Result: 构建了含1901幅图像、19个文化类别的数据集；基线实验验证其适用于训练分类器，并揭示误分类模式所反映的类间及文化相似性关系。

Conclusion: JONES-19可作为视觉识别、数据高效学习、艺术史研究、建筑风格分析与文化遗产等多领域基准，数据集已公开于Hugging Face。

Abstract: We introduce JONES-19, a high-quality image dataset documenting 1,901 ornament designs belonging to nineteen human cultures. The images and their annotations are based on an open access archive of The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1856), by Owen Jones. The dataset poses numerous challenges as a benchmark for computer vision classification tasks and for research at the intersection of machine learning and art and design: a small sample size, image samples of human-designed artifacts rather than common objects in-context or natural scenes, imbalanced class distribution, and image distinctions based on fine details involving line patterns, reliefs, and colors. As a design-inspired dataset, JONES-19 can serve as a benchmark for various research fields, such as visual recognition, data-efficient learning, art-historical research, architectural style analysis, and cultural heritage. This paper describes the curation of the JONES-19 dataset and reports a baseline classification benchmark that evaluates the suitability of the dataset for training classifiers and exposes insights into inter-class relationships--particularly cultural similarities--by examining patterns in misclassification errors. The dataset and its accompanying documentation are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/harvardseas-cultural-ornaments/JONES-19.

</details>


### [334] [Seeing Is Locating: Spatial Awareness and Geographic Bias in Decision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30564)
*Wilson Wongso,Hada Melino Muhammad,Xiachong Lin,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeoDecisions 多模态基准，评估决策模型的空间感知能力与地理偏见，发现空间感知依赖视觉、存在显著地理偏见，并提出免训练的位置先验融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型（输出候选标签概率分布而非生成文本）越来越多被用作与真实地理位置相关输入的分类器和评估器，但其能否利用空间上下文、预测是否存在地理偏见尚不清楚。

Method: 构建 GeoDecisions 多模态基准，包含两项任务：(1) 地理感知图像分类；(2) 在客观与主观主题上的地点评分。将 6 个决策模型与有监督任务专用模型对比；并提出一种免训练方法，将决策模型与位置先验结合。

Result: 空间感知依赖视觉：仅凭文本坐标不足以对日常照片和卫星图像分类；存在显著地理偏见：日常照片上开源决策模型虽准确率更高，但错误的空间聚集性高于有监督模型；在全部 5 个主观主题上，所有决策模型均对低社会经济条件地区给出更低评分。

Conclusion: 决策模型尚不具备可靠的空间感知且存在系统性地理偏见；所提免训练位置先验融合方法可在不显著增加性能偏见的前提下提升空间感知能力。

Abstract: Decision models, which output probability distributions over candidate labels instead of generating text, are increasingly used as classifiers and evaluators for inputs associated with real-world locations. However, it remains unclear whether they can leverage spatial context and whether their predictions are geographically biased. We introduce GeoDecisions, a multimodal benchmark for evaluating the spatial awareness and geographic bias of decision models. GeoDecisions comprises two tasks: (1) geo-aware image classification, and (2) location rating on objective and subjective topics. Evaluating six decision models against supervised task-specific models, we find that spatial awareness requires vision: textual coordinates alone provide insufficient context for classifying everyday photos and satellite images. We also find substantial geographic bias. On everyday photos, despite achieving higher accuracy, open-weight decision models exhibit more spatially clustered errors than supervised models. Moreover, across all five subjective topics, every decision model gives lower ratings to locations with lower socioeconomic conditions. Finally, we propose a training-free method that combines decision models with a location prior to improve their spatial awareness without substantially increasing performance bias. Our code is available at https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/GeoDecisions.

</details>


### [335] [Reliable Recursive Memory for Causal Multi-scale Video Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30568)
*Tong Wang,Wenbo Peng,Yaolei Qi,Yuting He,Yutong Xie,Guanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STREAM是一种用于视频息肉分割（VPS）的流式框架，将会议工作中的因果多尺度聚合（CMA）从固定片段扩展到在线流，通过递归槽记忆（RSM）以恒定开销提供长程历史，并用可靠性感知槽写入（RASW）抑制局部退化帧的污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPS中息肉与黏膜相似、尺度变化快，需要超越短片段的历史信息；而模糊、高光与遮挡往往只退化帧的局部区域。现有方法以整帧形式保存历史，代价随历史长度增长，且局部退化内容会传播到后续预测。

Method: 提出STREAM流式框架：（1）Recursive Slot Memory（RSM）将多尺度特征及软前景/背景线索压缩为固定数目的槽，当前特征读取槽信息并输入CMA，实现恒定代价的长程历史；（2）Reliability-Aware Slot Writing（RASW）从当前帧估计像素级质量图，并以局部误差监督，用于控制哪些位置写入RSM及各槽的更新程度。

Result: 在SUN-SEG与CVC-ClinicDB上达到SOTA，在最难子集上将会议模型的Dice从81.3%提升至85.7%，运行速度48 FPS，状态内存恒定0.32 MB。

Conclusion: 以恒定状态内存实现长程历史建模与抗退化写入，STREAM在精度、速度与内存间取得良好平衡，适用于在线结肠镜视频息肉分割。

Abstract: Video polyp segmentation (VPS) is essential for computer-aided colonoscopy, as it localizes and tracks polyps frame by frame. However, VPS remains challenging because polyps resemble the mucosa and change quickly in scale, which calls for history beyond a short clip, and because blur, specular highlights, and occlusion often degrade only part of a frame. Existing methods keep history as whole frames, so their cost grows with the history length, and a partly degraded frame carries its degraded content to later predictions. To address these issues, we propose STREAM, a streaming framework for VPS that extends the causal multi-scale aggregation (CMA) of our conference work from a fixed clip to an online stream. STREAM introduces a Recursive Slot Memory (RSM) that summarizes multi-scale features and soft foreground and background cues into a constant number of slots, which the current features read and pass to CMA, supplying long-range history at a constant cost. Furthermore, we design Reliability-Aware Slot Writing (RASW), which estimates a pixel-level quality map from the current frame, supervised by its local error, and uses it to control which locations are written into RSM and how much each slot is updated. Experiments on SUN-SEG and CVC-ClinicDB demonstrate that STREAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the Dice of the conference model from 81.3% to 85.7% on the hardest subset while running at 48 FPS with a constant state of 0.32 MB. Code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/STREAM.

</details>


### [336] [Write, Evolve, Read: Streaming Cameras into a Persistent World State for 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30573)
*ZiAn Wang,Tianyi Wang,Zhihao Chen,Manni Cui,Junkai Chen,ZhiJin Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Junfei Wu,Huanyu Zhang,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersistWorld 将相机帧流式写入以场景为锚的持久 3D 世界状态，并通过演化器预测任意查询时刻的状态，从而解决 4D 占据预测中"已见空间保持不住"的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 占据预测需预测数秒后的 3D 语义占据。由于场景变化缓慢而车辆移动，未来空间多为已观测区域。作者把未来划分为不变、变化、未见三类空间，发现现有基于相机的世界模型主要短板在"不变空间"——无法保持已见内容，而非预测变化。

Method: 将模型已见内容作为状态：PersistWorld 把相机帧流写入锚定于场景的持久 3D 世界状态，每帧只写入一次，避免从历史帧反复重建状态；该状态与预测任务端到端联合学习，并由一个演化器（evolver）沿自车轨迹任意查询时刻预测未来状态。

Result: 在 Occ3D-nuScenes 上，1–3 s 将最佳相机世界模型的 IoU 提升 19 点，8 s 内提升 18–24 点，同时运行速度提升两倍以上。

Conclusion: 以"持久、单次写入的场景锚定状态"替代逐帧重建，是提升相机端 4D 占据预测（尤其不变空间保持）的有效且高效的范式。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting predicts the 3D semantic occupancy of a driving scene seconds ahead. Over a few seconds, the scene changes little while the vehicle moves, so most of the space ahead has already been seen. Sorting the future into unchanged, changed, and unseen space, we find that current camera-based world models fall short on the unchanged part: they fail to hold on to what they have already seen, rather than to forecast what will change. We therefore make what the model has seen its state. PersistWorld streams camera frames into a persistent 3D world state anchored in the scene, writing each frame once instead of rebuilding the state from past frames. The state is learned end to end with the forecast, and an evolver predicts it at any queried time along the ego trajectory. On Occ3D-nuScenes, PersistWorld raises the IoU of the best camera-based world model by 19 points at 1-3 s and by 18 to 24 points up to 8 s, while running more than twice as fast. Code and models: https://github.com/wangzian828/PersistWorld. Project page: https://wangzian828.github.io/PersistWorld

</details>


### [337] [A Search Worth a Billion Pixels: Explaining Gigapixel Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30578)
*Danilo Danese,Angela Lombardi,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WaFR（Wavelet Find-and-Refine），一种模型无关的扰动式归因方法，通过自适应分层搜索与稀疏多尺度回归，在计算开销随证据空间分布变化的前提下实现细粒度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动式归因方法无需访问模型内部表示，但扩展到高分辨率图像时面临扰动粒度与计算成本的权衡：细粒度扰动需要大量模型前向评估，而粗粒度掩码又可能遗漏小而关键的证据。

Method: WaFR 结合小波变换、自适应分层搜索与稀疏多尺度回归；仅对影响预测的区域进行选择性细化，从而将计算成本与证据的空间分布耦合，而非与像素数线性相关。

Result: 在每边 8192 像素的受控定位实验中，WaFR 的 Pointing Game 得分不低于 .995，且在相同前向次数预算下依然成立；在千兆像素全切片图像上，中位前向次数仅 176，并在所评测的扰动类方法中取得最高的平均 patch 级 AUROC（.949）。

Conclusion: WaFR 在保持细粒度归因精度的同时显著降低高分辨率场景的计算成本，适用于超大图像（如病理全切片）的可扩展归因分析。

Abstract: Perturbation-based attribution methods explain model predictions without accessing their internal representations, but scaling them to high-resolution images requires balancing perturbation granularity and computational cost. Fine-grained perturbations require many model evaluations, whereas coarse masks may overlook small but relevant evidence. We introduce Wavelet Find-and-Refine (WaFR), a model-agnostic attribution method combining adaptive hierarchical search with sparse multiscale regression. By selectively refining regions that influence the prediction, WaFR maintains fine-grained localisation with a computational cost that depends on the spatial distribution of the evidence. As image resolution increases to 8,192 pixels per side, WaFR maintains a Pointing Game score of at least .995 in controlled localisation experiments, including at matched forward-pass budgets. On gigapixel whole-slide images, it requires a median of 176 forward passes and achieves the highest mean patch-level AUROC among the evaluated perturbation-based methods (.949).

</details>


### [338] [Foundation Model and Radiomics Distance Scores for Post-Hoc Segmentation Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30582)
*Simon Johannes Joham,Gianluca Guglielmo,Mateusz Kozinski,Martin Urschler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对医学图像分割模型的“事后失效检测”，提出两种基于马氏距离的失效分数（放射组学形状特征与掩膜感知的冻结基础模型特征），在无需配准或模型内部信息的前提下，与 Atlas-RCA 及成对 Dice 基线表现相当；并指出按站点内排序评估才能反映真实逐例失效，建议同时报告全局与站点内两种评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的分割模型在临床数据分布偏移时会静默失效，且多为黑箱、缺乏质量控制。已有事后失效检测方法依赖额外回归/分割模型、配准或模型内部访问，代价高且配准本身也可能静默失败。

Method: 构建带标签参考集，计算测试样本到参考集的马氏距离作为失效分数：其一基于预测掩膜的放射组学形状特征；其二基于冻结 DINOv3 基础模型特征，通过将掩膜叠加到图像上使特征“掩膜感知”，并对掩膜区域加权池化 patch 特征。在多中心前列腺与海马 MRI 上测试成像协议偏移与人群偏移。

Result: 在站点内按预测失效风险排序病例时，两种分数均与基于配准的 Atlas-RCA 和基于集成的成对 Dice 基线具有竞争力，且无需可能静默失败的配准，也无需访问单个预测。此外发现，将全部测试病例合并为单一排序（既往做法）会奖励“按站点难度排序”，使检测器站点排序好但漏检站点内失效却仍看似强。

Conclusion: 站点内评估能度量真实的逐例失效；两种评估方案奖励不同行为，推荐同时报告全局与站点内评估。代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models can fail silently when clinical data differs from training data. Deployed models rarely ship with quality control, and are often black boxes. Prior methods that work in this post-hoc failure detection setting still need a separate regression or segmentation model, registration, or access to model internals. We instead propose two failure scores based on the Mahalanobis distance to a labeled reference set. The first uses radiomic shape features of the predicted mask. The second uses frozen foundation model (FM) features, made mask-aware by overlaying the mask on the image, encoding it with a frozen DINOv3, and pooling patch features with more weight on the mask region. We test on multi-site prostate and hippocampus MRI under imaging-protocol and population shift. Ranking cases by predicted failure risk within each site, both scores are competitive with the registration-based (Atlas-RCA) and ensemble-based (pairwise Dice) baselines, without registration that can fail silently or access to individual predictions. Our results show that pooling all test cases into one ranking, as prior work does, rewards ordering sites by difficulty, so a detector can rank sites well, miss failures within them, and still look strong. Within-site evaluation measures true per-case failure. The two evaluation schemes reward different behavior, so we recommend reporting both. The code is available at https://github.com/imigraz/fm-radiomics-failure-detection.

</details>


### [339] [DARE: Dual-Level Adversarial Learning with Domain-Aware Regularization for Whole Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30589)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Haichuan Dong,Manning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DARE：一种面向病理全切片图像（WSI）分类的即插即用无监督域自适应框架，通过patch级与slide级双重对抗对齐、教师-学生自适应伪标签以及域感知注意力掩码正则化，缓解跨医院域偏移导致的性能下降与负迁移。
