<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI因扫描设备、染色方案与组织制备差异存在显著域偏移，跨源应用模型性能大幅下降。现有UDA在自然图像上有效，但病理领域探索不足：WSI超高分辨率及切片间/切片内异质性使传统UDA方法不稳定、易发生负迁移。

Method: 提出DARE框架：(1) 在教师-学生架构中引入自适应伪标签，为目标域提供稳定监督；(2) 对patch级与slide级嵌入同时进行对抗对齐，联合优化两级语义表征实现跨域对齐；(3) 引入域感知注意力掩码正则化注意力机制，提升跨域泛化能力。整体为即插即用设计。

Result: 在四个公开WSI数据集上，DARE在多种域偏移设定下均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DARE通过双重对抗学习与域感知正则化有效解决WSI跨域自适应中的不稳定性与负迁移问题，验证了多层级对齐在计算病理学UDA中的有效性。

Abstract: Pathological whole slide images (WSIs) exhibit substantial domain shifts across hospitals due to variations in scanning devices, staining protocols, and tissue preparation procedures, leading to significant performance degradation when models are applied to data from different sources. Although unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) has shown strong performance in transferring models from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain in natural images, it remains relatively underexplored in computational pathology. The ultra-high resolution of WSIs and their pronounced inter-slide and intra-slide heterogeneity pose unique challenges for domain adaptation, often rendering existing UDA methods unstable and susceptible to negative transfer. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-level Adversarial learning with domain-aware REgularization (DARE), a plug-and-play UDA framework for WSI classification. DARE incorporates adaptive pseudo-labeling within a teacher-student architecture to provide stable supervision for the target domain, alongside adversarial alignment of both patch-level and slide-level embeddings. Together, these components enable the joint optimization of semantic representations at both levels for cross-domain alignment. We further introduce domain-aware attention masking to regularize attention and improve cross-domain generalization. Extensive experiments on four public WSI datasets demonstrate that DARE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across various domain shifts.

</details>


### [340] [HOSE: Hierarchical Orthogonal Subspace Erasure for Concept Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30592)
*Xiaoqi Wei,Li Li,Kejiang Chen,Hanzhou Wu,Xinpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层正交子空间擦除（HOSE），在参数更新时将其投影到与通用知识正交的子空间以消除对非目标概念的干扰，并针对U-Net分层异质性设计差异化子空间投影，实现有害概念擦除与图像质量间的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型可能生成有害或敏感内容，带来安全隐患。模型编辑通过在最小参数更新下修改特定概念是高效手段，但扩散模型中概念表征相互纠缠，直接修改会破坏非目标概念；同时U-Net的分层异质性使统一策略难以平衡擦除效果与图像质量。

Method: 1）正交子空间擦除：将参数更新投影到与通用知识正交的子空间，抑制对非目标概念的干扰；2）分层自适应机制：考虑U-Net架构的分层异质性，为不同层构建差异化的子空间投影，以精确适配模型层次结构，缓解统一约束下擦除与质量的固有权衡。

Result: 相较未编辑模型，有害内容生成降低约80%；虽存在一定图像质量下降，但在质量保持上优于其他基于编辑的基线，且在有害内容抑制与质量损失的权衡上显著更优；对抗鲁棒性优于现有SOTA方法，表明目标概念被更彻底地擦除。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 正交子空间约束结合分层自适应投影能有效缓解概念纠缠与U-U-Net分层异质性带来的干扰，在有害概念擦除、图像质量保持与对抗鲁棒性之间取得更好的平衡，为扩散模型安全编辑提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate harmful or sensitive content, such as pornographic or violent imagery, raising serious security concerns. Model editing has recently emerged as an efficient approach to mitigate this issue. It modifies pretrained models with minimal parameter updates to alter specific concepts while preserving overall model capabilities. However, due to the entangled representations of different concepts in diffusion models, direct modifications inevitably disrupt non-target concepts. Meanwhile, U-Net's hierarchical heterogeneity hinders unified strategies from balancing erasure and quality. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Orthogonal Subspace Erasure (HOSE). This method projects parameter updates into the subspace orthogonal to general knowledge, thereby eliminating interference with non-target concepts. Considering the hierarchical heterogeneity of the U-Net architecture, we further propose a hierarchical adaptive mechanism, constructing differentiated subspace projections for different layers to precisely adapt to the model's hierarchical structure, thereby resolving the inherent trade-off between erasure and image quality in unified constraints. Experimental results demonstrate the proposed method reduces harmful content generation by 80% compared with the unedited model. While it inevitably incurs some image quality degradation, it still outperforms other editing-based baselines in terms of quality preservation, and achieves a significantly better trade-off between harmful content suppression and quality loss than these baselines. Notably, our approach demonstrates higher adversarial robustness than existing SOTA method, indicating that the target concepts have been more thoroughly erased. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sevenqiiu/HOSE.

</details>


### [341] [Advancements in Diabetic Retinopathy Classification Using Novel Multi-Stacked U-Net Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30593)
*Ameya Uppina,S Navaneetha Krishnan,Talluri Krishna Sai Teja,Joe Dhanith P R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究UNET及其堆叠架构变体在糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）五分类任务上跨多个数据集的性能，并得出软结论：堆叠数为三层时性能最佳，继续增加层数会导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病引起的眼部并发症即糖尿病视网膜病变，会造成血管结构异常、导致视力丧失。对大规模糖尿病人群进行筛查的需求催生了计算机辅助的全自动DR诊断。CNN在基于眼底图像的DR分类上表现良好，但高质量标注数据有限、图像质量参差不齐以及类别不平衡使构建可靠模型面临挑战。

Method: 采用UNET架构及其堆叠（Stacked）变体，在多个数据集上对DR眼底图像进行五分类（0级为无DR，4级为增殖性DR），比较不同堆叠层数下UNET的性能表现。

Result: 跨多数据集的对比研究表明，UNET架构在三层堆叠时性能最佳，堆叠层数进一步增加则导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 堆叠UNET在适度堆叠（三层）时可获得最优DR分类效果，过度堆叠反而有害；该结论为"软结论"，仍需更多验证。

Abstract: Diabetes leads to several health complications throughout the body, with the complication in the eyes being Diabetic Retinopathy. It leads to damaging or abnormal changes in the blood vessels' structure, causing vision loss. The need for massive screening of a large population of diabetic patients has created a necessity for computer-aided, fully automatic diagnosis of DR. CNNs have shown great promise in the classification of DR based on the Fundus images. Building a reliable model faces considerable challenges due to the limited availability of high-quality annotated datasets and issues with varying image quality and class imbalances. This paper aims to demonstrate the UNET, and its Stacked Architecture variant and their performance across several datasets. This paper has compared and studied the UNET architecture's performance across multiple stacks and has come to a soft conclusion that the architecture performs its best at three stacks, and any more leads to deterioration of its performance. The model aims at classifying the images into five categories, 0 to 4, where 0 is no DR, and 4 is proliferative DR.

</details>


### [342] [ViDiHand2: Unifying Video Diffusion Priors and Hand Geometry for 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30597)
*Yuxi Wang,Yufei Liu,Chengkai Jin,Qingyue Chen,Wenqi Ouyang,Tianyi Wei,Kerui Ren,Siyuan Huang,Zhiqi Shen,Hongsheng Li,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViDiHand2：将VDM手部深度估计从直接回归改为细粒度几何约束求解，通过几何提升、片段级射线拟合与置信门控融合实现鲁棒度量3D手部运动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一视角人类视频学习机器人操作需要准确的度量3D手部运动；现有基于预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）的方法直接从整帧视频回归被遮挡手部的深度，其依赖外观的深度回归在未见过相机上泛化差。

Method: 提出几何驱动框架ViDiHand2：1）Fine-Grained Geometric Lifting与Clip-Level Ray Fitting，通过强制像素对齐2D关节与MANO手部度量结构之间的几何一致性、并利用片段内时序观测来恢复度量深度；2）Confidence-Gated Fusion，依据定位置信度自适应融合几何提升关节与MANO预测，提升遮挡与图像截断下的鲁棒性；3）增强VDM骨干以更好捕获时空表征，输出上述两步所需的逐帧2D关节与MANO预测。

Result: 在7个公开数据集上一致优于18个基线；相较各指标最优基线，野外场景绝对关节误差降低42%，检测失败降低8.82倍，运行速度57 FPS。

Conclusion: 以几何约束求解替代外观深度回归可显著提升跨相机泛化与遮挡鲁棒性，在精度、失败率与效率上均取得优势；代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from egocentric human videos requires accurate metric 3D hand motion. Recent methods leverage pretrained video diffusion model (VDM) priors to recover occluded hands directly from full-frame videos, but their appearance-based depth regression generalizes poorly to unseen cameras. In this work, we propose ViDiHand2, a geometry-driven framework that reformulates VDM-based hand depth estimation from direct regression into fine-grained geometric constraint solving. Specifically, we introduce Fine-Grained Geometric Lifting and Clip-Level Ray Fitting, which recover metric depth by enforcing geometric consistency between pixel-aligned 2D joints and the metric structure of MANO hands, while leveraging temporal observations across the clip. Since the lifting relies on these 2D joints, to improve robustness under occlusion and image truncation, we further develop Confidence-Gated Fusion to adaptively combine geometrically lifted joints with MANO predictions according to localization confidence. Finally, we enhance the VDM backbone to better capture spatial-temporal representations and produce the per-frame 2D joint and MANO predictions that both steps rely on. Extensive experiments on seven public datasets demonstrate that ViDiHand2 consistently outperforms 18 baselines, reducing in-the-wild absolute joint error by 42% and detection failures by 8.82x compared with the best baseline on each metric, while running at 57 FPS. The code is available at https://github.com/NTUYWANG103/ViDiHand.

</details>


### [343] [GSDet: Gaussian Splatting for Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30602)
*Zeyu Ding,Jiaqi Zhao,Yong Zhou,Wen-liang Du,Hancheng Zhu,Rui Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSDet将定向目标检测重构为3D高斯溅射问题，用随机初始化并优化的3D高斯表示定向目标，投影为2D高斯后转为定向框，摆脱了预定义锚框或查询的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代定向检测器依赖预定义候选（CNN的锚框、Transformer的查询），难以有效捕获空间信息，限制了检测性能。

Method: 由图像特征构建3D特征空间，在其内部署3D高斯表示定向目标；将3D高斯投影到图像平面得到2D高斯并转换为定向框；采用含3D高斯采样的解码器优化随机初始化高斯的均值、各向异性协方差与置信度；支持自适应控制与推理阶段动态调整高斯数量。

Result: 在3个数据集上，配合自适应控制，AP₅₀在DIOR-R提升0.7%、DOTA-v1.0提升0.3%、DOTA-v1.5提升0.55%，优于主流检测器。

Conclusion: 以高斯溅射替代预定义候选的建模范式在定向目标检测中有效，且高斯数量可动态可调，兼具性能与灵活性。

Abstract: Oriented object detection has advanced with the development of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transformers. However, modern detectors still rely on predefined object candidates, such as anchors in CNN-based methods or queries in transformer-based methods, which struggle to capture spatial information effectively. To address the limitations, we propose GSDet, a novel framework that formulates oriented object detection as Gaussian splatting. Specifically, our approach performs detection within a 3D feature space constructed from image features, where 3D Gaussians are employed to represent oriented objects. These 3D Gaussians are projected onto the image plane to form 2D Gaussians, which are then transformed into oriented boxes. Furthermore, we optimize the mean, anisotropic covariance, and confidence scores of these randomly initialized 3D Gaussians, using a decoder that incorporates 3D Gaussian sampling. Moreover, our method exhibits flexibility, enabling adaptive control and a dynamic number of Gaussians during inference. Experiments on 3 datasets indicate that GSDet achieves AP$_{50}$ gains of 0.7\% on DIOR-R, 0.3\% on DOTA-v1.0, and 0.55\% on DOTA-v1.5 when evaluated with adaptive control and outperforms mainstream detectors.

</details>


### [344] [Towards an Armenian Epigraphic Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30603)
*Chahan Vidal-Gor{è}ne,Arpine Avetisyan,Edita Matevosyan,Seda Kirakosyan,Victoria Khurshudyan,Pollet Samvelian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文介绍 Corpus Inscriptionum Armenicarum Electronicum，将十卷亚美尼亚铭文印刷总集《Divan hay vimagrut'yan》转为开放数字语料库，采用版面检测、OCR 与视觉语言模型生成初步结构化释读，并由专家在校对平台复核；初步结果显示字母识别可靠，残余错误集中于编辑符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《Divan hay vimagrut'yan》（1960–2017）收录近7600条亚美尼亚铭文记录，其中不少铭文近数十年已损毁或消失，仅存于纸本之中；而亚美尼亚铭文学界尚缺乏希腊、拉丁、格鲁吉亚铭文已有的同类数字资源，故有必要建立数字化语料库以保存与开放这批材料。

Method: 将十卷本《Divan》数字化：先以版面检测（layout detection）定位各条记录，再结合 OCR 与视觉语言模型（vision-language model）生成每条记录的首版结构化释读，随后由专家在校对平台（proofreading platform）上人工校订。

Result: 首批校订结果表明字母识别具有可靠性，残余错误主要集中在编辑符号（editorial signs）上；语料库同时被定位为古典亚美尼亚语与中亚美尼亚语的"银标准数据"（silver data）来源，可供语言模型与计算语言学研究使用。

Conclusion: 该电子语料库为亚美尼亚铭文的长期记录与保存提供了可持续平台，并为古典/中亚美尼亚语的计算语言学研究及语言模型训练提供高质量数据资源。

Abstract: The Divan hay vimagrut'yan (1960-2017), the printed corpus of Armenian inscriptions, comprises nearly 7,600 records; a growing number of the inscriptions they describe, damaged or destroyed in recent decades, survive only in its pages. Armenian epigraphy nevertheless lacks a digital resource of the kind available for Greek, Latin or Georgian. This paper presents the Corpus Inscriptionum Armenicarum Electronicum, which turns the ten volumes of the Divan into an open digital corpus. Layout detection, OCR and a vision-language model produce a first structured reading of each record, which specialists then check on a proofreading platform. First corrections show that letters are read reliably and that residual errors concentrate in the editorial signs. The corpus is designed as a lasting platform for documenting Armenian inscriptions and as a source of silver data in Classical and Middle Armenian for language models and computational linguistics.

</details>


### [345] [Tri-DNO: Trilateral Diffusion Noise Optimization for 3D Human Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30607)
*Paul Hinzer,Niklas Gard,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Tri-DNO：一种对预训练多模态 HOI 扩散模型的初始噪声进行迭代优化的扩散噪声优化策略，在不重训练模型的前提下生成接触紧实、穿透更少、手部位置合理的抓握，实验在 GRAB 数据集上提升了抓取质量，且无额外训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 人-物交互（HOI）生成对 AR/VR 与角色动画很重要，其中手部抓握的物理合理性尤为关键：接触不足或穿模等几何不一致会严重破坏真实感。扩散模型虽在 HOI 合成上有进展，但保证手-物交互的物理合理性仍有挑战。

Method: Tri-DNO 不改动也不重训练生成模型，而是将其视为黑盒，在采样阶段迭代优化扩散生成器的初始噪声（初始潜变量/噪声种子），以接触质量、穿透程度与手部位置合理性为约束引导采样，从而得到紧实的手-物接触并抑制穿透、保持手部位置真实。

Result: 在 GRAB 数据集上，相比基线在抓取质量（接触、穿透）指标上取得提升；不增加模型训练，且相对已有方法未增加计算开销。

Conclusion: 噪声空间优化是提升预训练 HOI 扩散模型物理合理性的高效、免训练途径，并可应用于实际媒体制作场景。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D human object interactions (HOI) is essential for applications, such as augmented or virtual reality (AR/VR) or character animation of interactive entertainment. A particular challenge lies in the faithful generation of hand grasps in human object interaction scenes, where even minor geometric inconsistencies, such as insufficient contact or interpenetration, can significantly compromise realism. While diffusion-based generative models have shown promising results in HOI synthesis, ensuring physically plausible hand-object interactions remains challenging.
  We present Tri-DNO, a diffusion noise optimization strategy to sample from a pretrained multimodal HOI diffusion model, improving hand-object contact. Rather than modifying or retraining the generative model, our approach iteratively optimizes the initial noise of the diffusion generator, thereby generating firm hand object contact, while mitigating penetration and keeping the hand position realistic. Experiments on the GRAB dataset demonstrate improvements over the baseline in grasp quality without additional model training or increased computational cost compared to existing methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of our approach to practical media production scenarios.

</details>


### [346] [CineAct: Interaction-State Agents for Playable, Branching Film Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30613)
*Junyan Lin,Jianqi Ma,Shujie Duan,Hanxin Zhu,Zhihao Hu,Jinming Liu,Shuai Li,Jun Zhang,Xin Jin,Chang Wen Chen,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineAct：一种"状态优先"的智能体框架，用LLM负责推理、冻结的图像/视频生成器仅作渲染器，实现目标驱动的交互式长视频生成，并配套多空间基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源视频生成器单次仅能生成数秒至十余秒片段；延长视频的方法要么依赖预设脚本的智能体系统，要么从上段末帧自回归推进，导致离屏状态丢失、跨段幻觉累积。用户无法给定明确目标、选择实现路径，并让该选择在后续片段中持续生效。

Method: 研究"目标锚定的交互式视频生成"任务：单目标可有多种有效实现路径，用户选定路径须在画面中执行且后果跨片段持续。CineAct 中：交互前由 Architect 以空间布局、相机、资产库与场景图初始化世界；每轮先推导计划中的状态变化，用图像生成器生成目标末帧并严格验证，再由 FL2V 模型合成中间运动。

Result: 在两类多空间基准（长程步骤执行、开放意图分支可采纳性）上，显式世界状态建模相较纯像素基线提升多步推演一致性（自动指标+11.4、人工+23.1 点），并使命中同一目标的配对分支成功率提升+30.8 点。

Conclusion: 将推理与渲染解耦、以显式世界状态为核心，可有效解决交互式长视频生成中的状态遗忘与幻觉累积问题，并支持用户意图在多片段间的持久化。

Abstract: Open video generators such as MiniMax-H3 produce short clips of only several to a dozen seconds per run. To synthesize longer sequences, existing methods either rely on agentic systems following preset scripts, or roll out autoregressively from the previous clip's last frame, losing off-screen states and accumulating hallucinations across turns. Consequently, users still cannot take an explicit goal, choose how to accomplish it, and see that choice persist across subsequent clips. We study goal-grounded interactive video generation, where one goal admits multiple valid approaches, the user's chosen approach must be executed on screen, and its consequences must persist across clips. To this end, we propose CineAct, a state-first agentic framework in which language-model agents handle reasoning while frozen image and video generators serve purely as renderers. Before interaction, the Architect initializes the world as a spatial layout with cameras, an asset library, and a scene graph; at each turn, the system deduces the planned state change, generates a target end frame with an image generator and rigorously verifies it, and synthesizes intermediate motion with an FL2V model. We further introduce a multi-space benchmark covering two suites: long-horizon step execution and open-intent branching admissibility. Experiments show that explicit world-state modeling boosts multi-step rollout consistency over the pixel-only baseline (+11.4 automated, +23.1 human points) and raises paired branching success toward a shared goal by +30.8 points.

</details>


### [347] [Auxiliary Clues Aware's Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30617)
*Qigong Lei,Qingsong Wang,Wang Lin,Qianyi Yang,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AuxCA强化学习框架，通过辅助线索依赖(ACD)度量与细粒度优势加权，使MLLM在推理时无需显式绘图即可学到可迁移的辅助线构造策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何问题求解(GPS)是检验MLLM复杂推理的重要测试，尤其是需要输入图中不存在的辅助构造时；显式绘图方法受制于图像生成质量不稳定与级联误差，而现有隐式注意力机制主要关注可见元素，难以支撑需要额外几何构造的推理。

Method: 构建AuxCA（Auxiliary-Clues-Aware GRPO）强化学习框架：引入辅助线索依赖(ACD)量化辅助视觉线索对token生成的影响，并将其以细粒度优势加权方式融入GRPO；同时加入辅助激励奖励，鼓励在正确解中有效使用辅助几何线索；推理阶段不生成显式图形。

Result: 在依赖辅助线的平面几何问题上，AuxCA-8B优于显式绘图基线Bagel-Canvas，并与领先的闭源模型表现相当；在解析几何、立体几何及外部基准上的提升表明所学策略可迁移至训练域之外。

Conclusion: 无需显式绘图的隐式辅助构造策略学习是可行且有效的，ACD引导的GRPO优势加权加辅助激励奖励能够缓解显式绘图的不稳定性与级联误差，并具备跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving (GPS) serves as a rigorous test of the complex reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), particularly when solutions require auxiliary constructions absent from the input diagram. However, explicit diagramming methods suffer from unstable image generation quality and cascading errors, while existing implicit attention mechanisms primarily focus on visible elements, limiting their ability to support reasoning that requires additional geometric constructions. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{AuxCA} (Auxiliary-Clues-Aware GRPO), a reinforcement learning framework that helps models learn transferable auxiliary-construction strategies that can be applied beyond the training domain without explicit diagram generation at inference time. Specifically, we introduce Auxiliary Clues Dependency (\textbf{ACD}) to quantify the influence of auxiliary visual clues on token generation and integrate it into GRPO through fine-grained advantage weighting. Furthermore, we incorporate an auxiliary incentive reward to encourage the effective use of auxiliary geometric clues in correct solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AuxCA-8B outperforms the explicit-plotting baseline Bagel-Canvas and achieves competitive performance with leading proprietary models on auxiliary-dependent plane geometry problems. Improvements on analytic geometry, solid geometry, and external benchmarks further suggest that the learned strategies can transfer beyond the training domain. Code is available at https://github.com/fukua23/AuxCA

</details>


### [348] [Motion on Demand: Adaptive Optical Excitation for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30641)
*Yingtao Li,Chaoran Feng,Haoran Jiang,Zhenyu Tang,Wangbo Yu,Zhankuo Xu,Jianbin Zhao,Li Yuan,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EvGaze，一种受蝇眼控制启发的自适应棱镜旋转事件相机系统，通过多模态学习控制器与逐事件运动补偿，在更少事件下恢复更多边缘并提升特征跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机仅响应亮度变化，边缘可见性依赖垂直于边缘的图像运动；旋转楔形棱镜可引入受控运动以显现静态或慢速边缘，但固定转速无法适应变化运动条件——自然运动已充分时额外光学激励会产生冗余事件与对齐误差。

Method: 受蝇类控制中快速haltere前馈与较慢视觉反馈的分工启发，构建自适应调节棱镜转速的事件相机系统。采用学习型多模态控制器融合亮度、事件与惯性观测，其中快速惯性通路在视觉更新间隙细化转速决策；并利用实测编码器角度在单个事件时间戳上补偿棱镜引起的图像运动，以保持变速下的几何一致性。

Result: 在EvMotion上，相较全速棱镜旋转恢复更多边缘且事件减少39%；在DSEC模拟光调制下达到0.73特征跟踪成功率（原生事件0.55，180 RPM为0.67），同时比360 RPM少产生26%事件；真机实验验证在线闭环自适应，相对全速旋转平均事件率降低17%。

Conclusion: 自适应、前馈-反馈结合的光学运动调制可在事件效率与几何一致性之间取得更优折衷，验证了仿生多模态控制在事件相机主动感知中的有效性。

Abstract: Event cameras respond only to brightness changes, so whether an edge is visible depends on image motion perpendicular to it---a motion that constantly shifts with camera and scene dynamics. Rotating a wedge prism in front of the lens introduces controlled image motion to reveal static or slow edges. However, a fixed rotation speed cannot adapt to changing motion conditions: when natural motion is already sufficient, additional optical excitation generates redundant events and introduces unnecessary alignment errors. Inspired by the division of labor between fast haltere feedforward and slower visual feedback in fly gaze control, we present EvGaze, an event camera system that adaptively regulates prism rotation speed. EvGaze employs a learned multimodal controller that integrates luminance, event, and inertial observations, with a fast inertial pathway that refines speed decisions between visual updates. To preserve geometric consistency during speed changes, we compensate for prism-induced image motion at individual event timestamps using measured encoder angles. On EvMotion, EvGaze recovers more edges than full-speed prism rotation with 39% fewer events. On DSEC with simulated optical modulation, it reaches 0.73 feature-tracking success (compared with 0.55 on native events and 0.67 at 180 RPM) while generating 26% fewer events than 360 RPM. Real-device experiments further demonstrate online closed-loop adaptation, reducing average event rates by 17% relative to full-speed rotation.

</details>


### [349] [Beyond Naming Accuracy: Template Composition and Species-Name Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30642)
*Junle li,Bochang zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定模板数量与名词构成的前提下，分类器的"一致性排名"会随具体句子的措辞而翻转，因此基于少量模板得出的稳定性结论不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察显示 BioCLIP2 在4个摄影模板下比 BioCLIP 2.5 Huge 更少改变预测物种，但在80个模板库上排名反转；这一对比无法区分反转是由模板数量还是模板措辞造成的，本文即要解答该问题。

Method: 将模板数量固定为4，在80模板库与另一组24个经审阅的摄影模板池的所有四模板子集上比较两分类器，图像与候选物种名保持固定；随后在来自此前未使用 iWildCam 序列的5,487帧上检验两种排名是否成立，并对4,356个"两 photo + 两 photograph"子集构造同时95%区间。

Result: 在24模板审阅池整体上 Huge 更一致，但其19.09%的四模板子集在 iWildCam 上偏向 BioCLIP2，Rare Species 上为5.10%；在5,487帧新数据中，1,526个子集的同时95%区间支持 Huge 分歧更低，43个子集支持 BioCLIP2。

Conclusion: 即使提示数量与名词混合方式相同，句子的具体选择也能决定哪个分类器显得更一致，说明仅凭小模板集判定视觉-语言分类器稳定性存在方法学风险。

Abstract: Vision-language classifiers identify species by matching images to text prompts containing species names. BioCLIP2 changes its predicted species less often than BioCLIP 2.5 Huge across four photographic templates, but the ranking reverses across a bank of 80 templates. This comparison leaves open whether the reversal depends on the number of templates or their wording. We hold the count at four and compare the classifiers on every four-template subset of the 80-template bank and of a second pool of 24 reviewed photographic templates, keeping images and candidate names fixed. Huge is more consistent across the reviewed pool as a whole, yet 19.09% of its four-template subsets favor Bio-CLIP2 on iWildCam and 5.10% do so on Rare Species. We then test whether both rankings hold on 5,487 frames from previously unused iWildCam sequences. Among 4,356 subsets containing two photo and two photograph templates, simultaneous 95% intervals support lower disagreement for Huge in 1,526 subsets and for BioCLIP2 in 43. Even with the same prompt count and noun mixture, the choice of sentences can determine which classifier appears more consistent.

</details>


### [350] [Part-wise Certified State Estimation of Articulated Objects from Passive Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30646)
*Orion Desbonnet,Kei Higuchi,Alex Sykes,Ayush Deshmukh,Victor Leve,Joao Moura,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于被动双目视觉的铰接物体（如折叠纸盒）分部件状态估计与几何认证方法，精度可比最优模型拟合方法，且无需深度、无需接近真值的初始估计和物体掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造机器人操作薄壁铰接物体时需监测其铰接状态、位姿及半形变部件形状。学习型估计器泛化好但对薄部件精度差；模型拟合精度高但依赖准确深度、接近真值的初值和干净掩码。

Method: 在双目图像中检测CAD模型定义的角点，将铰接模型拟合到这些角点；将几何认证从刚性物体扩展到铰接物体，按部件判定拟合结果是否可信（因拟合对遮挡部件也会输出状态）；将无标注视频上的认证估计作为伪标签以改善仿真到现实迁移。

Result: 7个仿真物体中5个上，认证部件定位精度比最强学习基线（提供深度与真值掩码）高3–5倍；真实纸盒端板中位角点误差为尺寸的2.1%，较所有学习基线低至少40%，也低于以真值附近初始化的基于深度的拟合。

Conclusion: 分部件几何认证可在被动双目下实现高精度铰接状态估计，并在不可靠拟合时给出可信度，可用于自动生成训练标签。

Abstract: Robots in manufacturing often handle thin, hinged objects known from CAD models that change shape during the task, like folding cartons. Monitoring such tasks requires estimating the object's articulation state, its pose and the shape of any semi-deformable part. Learning-based estimators generalise across objects but lose precision while handling thin parts. Model-based fitting is precise but often needs accurate depth, a close initial estimate and a clean object mask. We present part-wise certification of an articulated object's state, estimated from passive stereo. Our estimator rivals the precision of the best model-based fitting method without using depth, a close initial estimate or a mask. We detect the corners that the CAD model defines in both stereo images and fit the articulated model to them. Geometric certificates, which we extend from rigid to articulated objects, then tell whether to trust each part, because the fit returns a state even for hidden parts. Certified estimates on unlabelled video become labels that improve sim-to-real transfer. On five of seven simulated objects, we locate the parts we certify three to five times more precisely than the strongest learned baseline on the same readings, given depth and true masks. On a real folding carton, our median corner error on the end panels is 2.1% of the carton's size, at least 40% lower than every learned baseline and also lower than a depth-based fit initialised near the true state.

</details>


### [351] [Zhengrong: World Models with Pole-Based Action-Response Factorization for Direct Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30666)
*Yu Zhang,Jingrui Li,Congyu Wang,Yuhang Cheng,Wenbo Chen,Xiaobo Zhang,Jing Zou,Yuxi Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Zhengrong-PoleWM世界模型，用"初始条件锚点+自由响应+延迟响应"的极点基分解刻画动作效应的时序结构，在四个图像目标控制基准上以90.83%宏观平均成功率超越最强基线6.08个百分点，并保持毫秒级规划延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉世界模型无需递归隐状态推演即可预测候选动作序列的效果，但未明确刻画动作效应的时序结构（如初始状态锚定、因果性、动作交互与有界性），限制了预测的准确性与规划效率。

Method: 将隐轨迹分解为初始条件锚点项、自由响应项以及对动作"新息"的延迟响应项；用非线性网络把视觉历史与因果动作前缀映射为响应系数；由共享的稳定共轭极点库决定各响应随时间的演化；并推导精确重锚定恒等式，证明表示平移后的轨迹尾部需引入额外基方向Ψ_κ(s)。

Result: Cube、TwoRoom、PushT、Reacher四基准上，epoch 20、两个训练种子下宏观平均成功率90.83%±0.47%，超最强基线QQWorld 6.08个百分点；按验证损失选检查点达91.21%±1.00%。首epoch无重启正则的Cube消融中，神经与极点响应头分别比通用直接预测器高20.67与23.34个百分点。A100上30步交叉熵法完整搜索中位延迟74–87 ms（均值77.5 ms），较Fast-LeWM降低15.8%，较自回归LeWM加速7.1倍。

Conclusion: 极点基时序分解在保持直接预测模型效率的同时显著提升离线图像目标控制的成功率，并满足因果性、初始锚定与有界响应等结构性约束，为高效视觉世界模型提供了可解释且计算友好的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Direct visual world models predict the effects of candidate action sequences without recursive latent rollout, yet they leave the temporal structure of action effects unspecified. We introduce Zhengrong (Zhengrong-PoleWM), a world model that decomposes a latent trajectory into an initial-condition anchor, a free response, and delayed responses to action innovations. Nonlinear networks map visual history and causal action prefixes to response coefficients. A shared bank of stable conjugate poles determines how each response evolves with elapsed time. This factorization preserves nonlinear interactions across actions, anchors predictions exactly at the initial state, prevents future actions from affecting earlier predictions, and yields bounded responses to finite control plans. We derive an exact re-anchoring identity that identifies the additional basis direction $Ψ_κ(s)$ needed to represent shifted trajectory tails. Across four offline image-goal control benchmarks (Cube, TwoRoom, PushT, and Reacher), Zhengrong-PoleWM achieves $90.83\% \pm 0.47\%$ macro-average success over two training seeds at epoch 20, exceeding the strongest baseline (QQWorld) by 6.08 percentage points. Selecting checkpoints by validation loss yields $91.21\% \pm 1.00\%$. In a controlled first-epoch Cube study without restart regularization, neural and pole response heads outperform a generic direct predictor by 20.67 and 23.34 percentage points, respectively. On an NVIDIA A100 GPU, median latency for a full 30-iteration cross-entropy method search ranges from 74 to 87 ms across the four tasks. The mean of these medians is 77.5 ms, a 15.8% latency reduction relative to Fast-LeWM and a $7.1\times$ speedup over autoregressive LeWM.

</details>


### [352] [DriveDreamer-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of Driving Perception and Agentic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30671)
*Dingyi Yao,Tingdong Yu,Guosheng Zhao,Chaojun Ni,Xiaofeng Wang,Jiming Mao,Guan Huang,Lihui Peng,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveDreamer-RSI：通过感知学习与数据构建之间的反馈回环，实现可递归自我改进的驾驶感知数据生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控生成可扩充驾驶感知训练数据，但仅靠视觉真实性不足以保证学习收益；生成数据须随感知模型演化，同时适配"生成什么"与"如何构建"。

Method: 外层循环Gap-Guided Target Adaptation：依据当前感知差距生成带标签视频，训练后更新目标；内层循环用视觉-语言智能体通过观测与执行反馈迭代编辑场景；跨轮次用Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement从执行经验与下游学习结果提炼可复用的构建策略。

Result: nuScenes验证集上，以约一半合成帧数超过数据集重放基线4.09 mAP与4.13 NDS。

Conclusion: 将生成数据构建与感知学习闭环耦合、并沉淀可复用策略，可显著提升数据效率与感知性能。

Abstract: Controllable generation can expand training data for driving perception, but visual realism alone does not ensure learning gains. Effective generation must adapt both what data to generate and how to construct them as the perception model evolves. In this paper, we present DriveDreamer-RSI, a framework for recursive self-improvement of driving perception and agentic data generation through feedback between perception learning and data construction. Its outer loop implements Gap-Guided Target Adaptation, generating labeled videos from current perception gaps and updating targets after training. Its inner loop uses a vision-language agent to iteratively edit scenes through observation and execution feedback. Across rounds, Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement derives reusable construction advice from execution experience and downstream learning outcomes. On the nuScenes validation set, DriveDreamer-RSI surpasses the dataset-replay baseline by 4.09 mAP and 4.13 NDS points using approximately half the synthetic frames.

</details>


### [353] [Optimising VLM Harnesses to Localise Errors in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30673)
*Ankit Yadav,Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Arpit Garg,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Harness Search and Merge (HSM)，通过搜索可执行评估程序自动构建 VLM harness，用于生成视频中的错误定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型可定位生成视频中的细微错误，但其效果高度依赖于工作流（harness）设计：选帧、工具/模型调用、预测融合方式都会显著影响结果，而人工设计成本高且非最优。

Method: HSM 在可执行评估程序空间上自动搜索 harness：由 LLM 编排器在随机训练子集上独立改进候选 harness，将发现记录于记忆中，并以构造性方式合并互补候选。

Result: 在 Spotlight 基准上取得 0.234 ± 0.012 F1，优于最强基准策略（0.173 ± 0.016）；在未见过的视频生成器上仍保持领先（0.213 ± 0.008 vs. 0.140 ± 0.003）。在 IPV-Bench 上定位性能强，且误报率低于现有优化基线。

Conclusion: 对完整 VLM harness 进行搜索可优于现有评估策略，可迁移至未见生成器，并在真实视频上降低误报。

Abstract: Identifying subtle errors in generated videos is an important step toward automated video generation, where errors must be detected to assess and refine generated clips. Recent work shows that pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) can localise such errors, but their effectiveness depends strongly on the workflow used: choices about which frames to inspect, which tools and model calls to make, and how to combine predictions can lead to substantially different results. We study VLM harnesses for video error localisation. Instead of relying on manually designed harnesses, we propose Harness Search and Merge (HSM), an algorithm that automatically constructs them by searching over executable evaluation programs. An LLM orchestrator refines candidate harnesses independently on random training subsets, records findings in a memory, and constructively merges complementary candidates. We evaluate HSM on Spotlight, a recently proposed benchmark for localising errors in generated videos. HSM achieves 0.234 +- 0.012 F1, exceeding the strongest benchmark strategy (0.173 +- 0.016), and retains its lead on an unseen video generator (0.213 +- 0.008 versus 0.140 +- 0.003). On IPV-Bench, it achieves strong localisation performance with lower false-alarm rates than existing optimisation baselines. Together, these results show that searching over complete VLM harnesses can outperform existing evaluation strategies, transfer to an unseen video generator, and reduce false alarms on real videos.

</details>


### [354] [Speculate in the Right Order: Efficient Self-Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30704)
*Yihan Chen,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将"生成顺序"识别为自推测解码的新优化维度，提出免训练的 Speculation-Aware Ordering（SAO），用成对条件TV统计离线构建有利于推测的生成顺序，提升自回归图像生成的解码效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自推测解码（同一模型并行起草+串行验证）研究主要集中于改进起草与验证策略，而随机顺序自回归建模引入了生成顺序这一额外自由度，其与推测效率的关系尚未被分析和利用。

Method: 理论上分析生成顺序如何影响自推测解码效率，归纳出两个关键因素：决定token被拒绝概率的"草稿-目标失配"（draft-target mismatch），以及在一轮推测中越早拒绝代价越高的"早拒绝代价"（early-rejection cost）。据此提出SAO——一种免训练的离线生成顺序优化框架：不通过昂贵的推测解码来评估候选顺序，而用简单的成对条件TV（total variation）统计构造带拒绝代价感知的目标函数，从而高效离线构建推测友好的生成顺序。

Result: 在不同骨干网络与不同推测宽度下的广泛实验表明，SAO提升了自推测解码效率，并额外带来生成质量的提升；与现有自推测解码方法结合时还能提供互补的效率增益。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是自回归图像生成中高效自推测解码的一个新优化维度，通过离线、免训练的TV统计驱动顺序设计即可同时改善效率与质量。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive image generation by leveraging the same model for parallel drafting and sequential verification, without requiring an additional draft model. While existing methods primarily focus on improving drafting and verification strategies, recent advances in random-order autoregressive modeling introduce an additional degree of freedom: the generation order. In this work, we theoretically analyze how generation order affects self-speculative decoding efficiency, identifying two key factors: draft-target mismatch, which determines token rejection probabilities, and early-rejection cost, which reflects the higher cost of rejecting tokens earlier in a speculative round. Motivated by these insights, we propose Speculation-Aware Ordering (SAO), a training-free framework for offline optimization of generation orders. Instead of evaluating candidate orders through costly speculative decoding, SAO uses simple pairwise conditional-TV statistics to formulate a rejection-cost-aware objective and efficiently construct speculation-friendly generation orders offline. Extensive experiments across different backbones and speculative widths demonstrate that SAO improves decoding efficiency, with the additional benefit of improved generation quality. Furthermore, SAO provides complementary efficiency gains when combined with existing self-speculative decoding methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to identify generation order as a new optimization dimension for efficient self-speculative decoding in autoregressive image generation.

</details>


### [355] [Intra-Class Diversity Loss for Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30707)
*Wei Zhou,BaXian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出类内多样性损失（ICD），通过在与分类无关的正交方向上最大化类内差异，提升行人重识别的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练与测试身份不重叠，且类内变化大，现有ReID损失多关注类内紧凑与类间分离，忽略了对泛化有用的类内差异信息。

Method: 基于表示与类权重向量的几何关系，将表示分解为沿类权重方向的分量与正交分量；构造类别权重正交的多样性学习目标，在同一身份样本的正交分量间拉大差异，并与分类损失联合作为正则项，无需改动特征提取网络。

Result: 在三个常用ReID数据集上的大量实验验证了ICD的有效性，并显示其与现有度量学习损失具有互补性。

Conclusion: ICD为行人ReID引入了可解释的类内多样性正则化，可作为即插即用模块增强现有框架的泛化性能。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) aims to retrieve images of the same pedestrian across different cameras. However, the non-overlapping identities between training and testing sets, together with large intra-class variation, make it challenging to learn generalizable pedestrian representations. Most existing ReID loss functions mainly focus on intra-class compactness and inter-class separation, which may overlook useful intra-class differences. To address these issues, we propose a novel Intra-Class Diversity (ICD) loss that aims to maximize intra-class differences in classification-irrelevant directions. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to introduce this class-weight-orthogonal diversity learning objective for person ReID. Specifically, based on the geometric relationship between the learned representation and its corresponding class-weight vector, we decompose each representation into the component along the class-weight direction and its orthogonal component. This decomposition provides a clear and interpretable geometric basis for modeling intra-class diversity. ICD then encour- ages larger differences among the orthogonal components of samples belonging to the same identity, while the classification loss maintains identity discrimination. The proposed ICD loss can be incorporated into existing ReID frameworks as an additional regularization term without modifying the feature extraction network. Extensive experiments on three widely used ReID datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ICD and its complementarity with existing metric learning losses.

</details>


### [356] [Co-speech Gesture Attribute Annotation with 3D Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30725)
*Frances Yung,Muhammad Naufilr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用人体网格恢复（HMR）得到的3D骨架，提出几何规则法与SL-GCN图卷积网络两种方法，自动化标注10种手势物理属性，并在GESRes数据集2,020个手势上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随言语手势（co-speech gestures）承载语义信息，但在视频中人工标注其物理属性耗时费力，亟需自动化标注手段以提高效率。

Method: 基于SAM 3D Body的单帧人体网格恢复（HMR）估计全身3D骨架，采用两种途径：一是AI辅助的几何规则方法，二是图卷积网络（SL-GCN）；在GESRes数据集的2,020个手势上与专家标注进行一致性比对。

Result: 对于高度、手臂旋转、运动速度等物理定义明确的属性，两种方法达到与专家标注的一致性可与人类标注者之间的一致性相媲美；对于需更高层多模态语境推理才能标注的属性，性能仍较低。

Conclusion: 规则方法所需标注数据显著更少、完全透明且可适配不同标注体系，是数据驱动方法实用且可解释的补充。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures convey meaning alongside speech, but annotating their physical attributes in video is labour-intensive. We propose two approaches to automate annotation of ten gesture attributes using human mesh recovery (HMR), which estimates a full 3D body skeleton from a single video frame: AI-assisted geometrical rules and a graph convolutional network (SL-GCN). Both use pose estimates from SAM 3D Body, evaluated on 2,020 gestures from the GESRes dataset. For physically defined attributes such as height, arm swivel and movement speed, both methods achieve agreement with expert annotation comparable to that between human annotators. For attributes whose annotation requires higher-level multimodal contextual inference, performance remains lower. The rule-based approach requires substantially less annotated data, is fully transparent, and can be adapted to different annotation schemes, offering a practical and interpretable complement to data-driven methods

</details>


### [357] [Same Seed, Different Picture? Deciding Early at Low Resolution for Seed-Faithful Few-Step Flow Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30728)
*Huiran Duan,Weilun Feng,Yuqi Li,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的少步采样器 Decide-then-Render (DtR)：在低分辨率下少数步确定构图，再用种子自身噪声重建全分辨率状态并渲染细节，从而在提速 11-25× 的同时显著保持与 50 步参考采样的种子一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步采样器虽能加速大型文生图流模型，但同一噪声下生成的图像与标准 50 步采样器不再一致，主体、属性与布局都会改变，而非仅细节差异，即“种子保真度”丢失。作者将这一现象归因于采样起始阶段：当噪声水平仍高于 0.9 时，初始噪声的粗粒度成分首先决定粗结构，而少步采样器在最初几步就跨越该区间，其到 50 步参考的距离中有 25-52% 由这几步贡献。

Method: 基于“该决策是粗粒度的”这一观察，提出免训练采样器 DtR：(1) 先用若干低分辨率步骤确定构图（半分辨率下用同一粗噪声可做出近乎相同的决策，成本约三分之一）；(2) 用种子自身的噪声重建全分辨率状态；(3) 再渲染细节。

Result: 在 4 个大型模型上，DtR 比参考采样器快 11-25×；在每个加速比下，其与参考的接近程度及 ImageReward 评分均优于所有对比方法，包括按预算校准的少步调度与已发表的多分辨率采样器。在 FLUX.1-dev 上约 8× 加速内仍为最忠实采样器，优于缓存类加速器；用户研究中 86% 的成对比较认为 DtR 样本更接近参考。

Conclusion: DtR 以“先粗后细”的解耦思路有效恢复少步采样的种子保真度，并具备跨模型、跨模态（视频）的迁移性，且蒸馏模型可作为其渲染器。

Abstract: Few-step samplers make large text-to-image flow models fast, but from the same noise they no longer generate the image of the standard 50-step sampler. Subjects, attributes and layout change, not just fine detail. We trace this loss of seed fidelity to the start of sampling. The coarse part of the initial noise decides coarse structure first, while the noise level is still above 0.9. Few-step samplers cross this range in their first steps, which account for 25-52% of their distance to the 50-step reference. Because this decision is coarse, a half-resolution run from the same coarse noise can make nearly the same decision at a third of the cost. We therefore propose Decide-then-Render (DtR), a training-free sampler that decides the composition in a few low-resolution steps, rebuilds the full-resolution state with the seed's own noise and renders the details. On 4 large models, DtR runs 11-25$\times$ faster than the reference sampler. At each speed-up, it is closer to the reference and rated higher by ImageReward than every compared sampler, including few-step schedules calibrated per budget and published multi-resolution samplers. On FLUX.1-dev, it remains the most faithful sampler down to about $8\times$, ahead of caching accelerators. A user study on this model found DtR's samples closer to the reference in 86% of pairwise trials. DtR transfers to a held-out model and to video, and distilled models can serve as its renderers. Project link: https://duanhuiran.github.io/DtR

</details>


### [358] [EgoDepth-Bench: Benchmarking Metric Depth for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30734)
*Yuan Gao,Dongchen Wei,Xinyang Lin,Shuhua Cao,Xueliang Guo,Zhuohan Li,Yihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoDepth-Bench，一个面向第一视角操作场景的大规模度量深度基准，含941,233个同步样本、37个环境，提供六路标定RGB视图与原生鱼眼视角下的度量深度，并以毫米级标定验证几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角（egocentric）操作场景缺乏同时具备度量精度、多视角同步与跨环境泛化评估能力的大规模深度基准，难以对单目、立体与多视角模型的深度精度及预测覆盖率（含手部与物体）进行严格评测。

Method: 构建EgoDepth-Bench：每样本含六路标定RGB视图与原生鱼眼视角度量深度，深度由五个主动红外立体模块重建；用标定球杆（ball-bar）验证几何精度；提供环境留出（environment-held-out）划分以评估未见环境泛化；在单目、立体与多视角模型上同时评测深度精度与预测覆盖率。

Result: 球杆标定中位误差1.27 mm，达毫米级精度；在未见环境上微调MapAnything使度量AbsRel从0.708降至0.091，尺度对齐AbsRel从0.327降至0.072。

Conclusion: EgoDepth-Bench可提供有效监督信号，并支持对第一视角操作中度量与相对深度估计的严格评测；项目主页公开评测代码与交互式RGB-深度序列。

Abstract: We introduce EgoDepth-Bench, comprising 941,233 synchronized samples collected across 37 distinct physical environments. Each sample combines six calibrated RGB views with metric depth in the native fisheye view, reconstructed from five active-infrared stereo modules. A calibrated ball-bar confirms millimeter-level geometric accuracy, with a median error of 1.27 mm on a known sphere spacing. Environment-held-out splits enable evaluation of generalization to unseen environments. Across monocular, stereo, and multi-view models, the benchmark measures both depth accuracy and prediction coverage, including on hands and objects. Fine-tuning MapAnything on EgoDepth-Bench reduces metric AbsRel from 0.708 to 0.091 and scale-aligned AbsRel from 0.327 to 0.072 on unseen environments. These results demonstrate that EgoDepth-Bench provides effective supervision and enables rigorous evaluation of metric and relative depth estimation in egocentric manipulation. The project page provides the evaluation code and interactive RGB-depth sequences.

</details>


### [359] [Learning Dense Head-Object Contact Estimation with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30751)
*Daniel Sungho Jung,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HECO框架，利用合成数据实现密集的头部-物体接触估计，通过文本语义上下文应对多样接触模式、通过遮挡感知的amodal修复推断被头发遮挡的真实接触区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部在人的认知与感知中至关重要但易受外部冲击，故理解头部接触对人机安全意义重大。然而密集头部-物体接触估计研究几乎空白，核心障碍是缺乏头部-物体接触数据。已有工作表明合成数据在小数据场景下可作为强有力训练数据，提示其可用于本任务。

Method: 提出HECO（HEad COntact estimation）。针对两大挑战：(1) 头-物交互高度多样导致接触区域与模式差异大——引入文本特征中的语义上下文，编码头部分区与交互物体之间的语义关系；(2) 头发严重遮挡真实接触区导致难以直接目视——引入遮挡感知接触学习，借助amodal inpainting从图像可见信息外推头部接触。

Result: 使用自建合成数据集训练HECO，实现了头部-物体接触估计，克服了接触数据缺失的问题；代码将开源。

Conclusion: 合成数据结合语义文本上下文与遮挡感知amodal学习，可有效支撑密集头部-物体接触估计，为无真实接触数据条件下的该任务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Head serves a critical role in human cognitive and perceptual functions while remaining vulnerable to external impacts, making the understanding of head contact essential for human safety. Despite its importance, dense head-object contact estimation remains largely unexplored, primarily due to the absence of head-object contact data. Recently, it has been shown that synthetic data can serve as powerful training data in limited data scenario, implying the potential of synthetic data in head-object contact estimation. There are two core challenges for dense head-object contact estimation. First, head-object interactions are highly diverse, resulting in substantial variations in contact regions and patterns across different objects and interaction types. Second, head contact regions are often heavily occluded by hair, making direct visual observation of the actual contact regions difficult. To address these issues, we introduce HEad COntact estimation (HECO) with synthetic data. To handle diverse head-object interactions, we leverage semantic context from text features that encode semantic relationships between head regions and interacting objects. To resolve hair occlusion issue of actual head contact regions, we introduce occlusion-aware contact learning that learns to infer head contact beyond visible information from images via amodal inpainting. As a result, we enable head-object contact estimation with HECO trained with our synthetic dataset, overcoming the issue of absence of contact data. The codes will be released.

</details>


### [360] [Screening for nonrigidity in structure from motion using projected reprojection residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30769)
*Íñigo Cirauqui-Viloria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于重投影误差"排列结构"的SfM变形诊断方法，先剔除可由点位/相机微调与简单时空趋势解释的残差成分，再度量其与固定时空模式的对齐度；在四组合成管状数据中检出全部8例含运动数据，优于基于误差幅度的统计量（5–6例），并在外观与流程变化下20个重建变体中19个保持阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏SfM假设场景静态，但存在变形时仍可能得到看似合理的重建；现有的重投影误差汇总指标（如幅值统计）丢失了误差在点与视图之间的分布/排列信息，无法揭示与变形相关的不一致性。因此需要一个仅依赖单次重建结果、可作为筛查证据的诊断量。

Method: 1) 先对残差做"解释性"去除：消除可由小的三维点与相机参数调整所解释的成分，以及随时间变化的简单空间趋势；2) 对剩余残差度量其与预设的固定空间模式和时间模式的对齐程度；3) 仅使用一次估计得到的SfM重建（相机、3D点、图像观测），输出筛查性证据而非物理变形估计；4) 在四组合成管状数据家族上评估，并以"其他三族静态得分的最高值"作为各族截断阈值，再迁移到跨外观与重建流程的20个变体上检验。

Result: 在四组合成管状数据家族中，该诊断检出全部8例含预设运动的数据，而基于重投影误差幅度的汇总统计仅检出5或6例；以其他三族静态得分的最高值作阈值时，得分最高的静态数据也会被判为阳性（存在假阳性）；采用相同阈值，在覆盖外观变化与重建流程变化的20个重建变体中19例保持阳性检出，另有1例数值性弃判。

Conclusion: 重投影误差在点与视图间的排列方式能揭示仅靠误差幅度无法发现的不一致性，可作为基于单次估计重建的实用筛查手段，用于标记"看似合理"的重建以供进一步检查。局限：四个数据家族在开发阶段已知，迁移变体复用了其中两族，因此结论限于该设定下，诊断不提供物理变形量估计。

Abstract: Sparse structure from motion (SfM) assumes a static scene, yet deformation may still yield plausible reconstructions. Reprojection-error summaries discard how errors are arranged across points and views. We propose a diagnostic that first removes residual components explainable by small point and camera adjustments and simple spatial trends that change over time. It then measures alignment with fixed spatial and temporal patterns. The diagnostic uses one estimated SfM reconstruction and provides screening evidence rather than a physical deformation estimate. Across four synthetic tubular families, the diagnostic detected all eight acquisitions with prescribed motion, compared with five or six for summaries of reprojection-error magnitude. Each family's cutoff was the highest static score among the other three families, so the static acquisition with the highest score also screened positive. With the same cutoffs, the diagnostic retained positive detections in 19 of 20 reconstruction variants spanning changes in appearance and reconstruction pipeline, with one numerical abstention. These findings show that the arrangement of reprojection errors across points and views can reveal inconsistencies that their magnitudes alone miss. The four families were known during development, and the transfer variants reused two of them. Within this setting, the diagnostic offers a practical way to flag a plausible reconstruction for closer inspection using only its estimated cameras, 3D points and image observations.

</details>


### [361] [GRAIN: Shared-Budget Complementary Re-observation for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30770)
*Chengxuan Zhou,Shaoyang Zhang,Rui Qian,Juntao Cheng,Feng Liu,Wanru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAIN 提出跨图像统一的裁剪调用分配与有界输出准入框架，基于可比的“分辨率闭合增益”，在 CPU 上预先完成集合级裁剪规划，在 102 个 AP@100 设置中的 100 个上优于调用数对齐的切片方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放大裁剪图上重跑冻结检测器可在免训练条件下恢复小目标与拥挤目标，但每次裁剪都需额外一次检测调用；现有切片与自适应裁剪流程对每张图像孤立规划裁剪，忽视了图像集合内检测需求的显著差异。

Method: 1) 以边界安全的裁剪内部区域上“跨图像可比的 resolution-closure 增益”为统一目标，融合跨图像调用分配与有界输出准入；2) 粗假设携带分辨率亏空，裁剪对其内部正区域内所含假设闭合该亏空的几何比例，并用乘法残差在单调子模代理中对已覆盖证据打折；3) 用前缀和与全局最大堆在 CPU 上、裁剪推理前完成集合级规划；4) 推理后用 Positive-Region Fusion 将空间准入与规划的内部支撑对齐，再进行类平衡 top- 选择。

Result: 在 COCO、CrowdHuman、VisDrone-DET 上跨五个家族共 17 个冻结检测器，GRAIN 在 102 个 AP@100 设置中有 100 个优于调用数匹配的切片方法，并迁移至 AI-TOD-v2；RF-DETR-S 在 CrowdHuman 上以约八分之一的裁剪调用接近两裁剪切片；固定预算下代理排序的 Spearman 相关为 0.95–0.99，扫描法达到精确代理最优的至少 98%。

Conclusion: 跨图像统一的裁剪预算分配与几何分辨率闭合证据建模，可在不重训练的前提下高效提升小/拥挤目标检测，且规划开销低、与冻结检测器解耦；代码将公开。

Abstract: Re-running a frozen detector on enlarged image crops recovers small and crowded objects without retraining, yet every crop costs an extra detector call. Existing slicing and adaptive-cropping pipelines plan crops per image in isolation, even though inspection demand varies widely across an image collection. We present GRAIN, which unifies cross-image call allocation and bounded-output admission around cross-image comparable resolution-closure gains over boundary-safe crop interiors. Coarse hypotheses carry resolution deficits, crops close a geometric share of those deficits for hypotheses enclosed in their interior positive region, and multiplicative residuals discount covered evidence within a monotone submodular surrogate. Prefix sums and a global max-heap execute this collection-wide plan on CPU before crop inference, and Positive-Region Fusion aligns post-inference spatial admission with the planned interior support before class-balanced top-
  selection. Across 17 frozen detectors from five families on COCO, CrowdHuman, and VisDrone-DET, GRAIN beats call-matched tiling in 100 of 102 AP@100 settings and transfers to AI-TOD-v2. With RF-DETR-S on CrowdHuman, it nearly matches two-crop tiling using one eighth of the crop calls. At fixed budgets, the surrogate orders candidate plans with Spearman correlations of 0.95 to 0.99 while the sweep attains at least 98% of the exact surrogate optimum. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [362] [Reconstructing 3D+t Cardiovascular Flow From Sparse Planar Velocity Fields With Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30775)
*Viraj Patel,Deblina Bhattacharjee,Katharine Fraser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出平面感知神经场框架，从稀疏平面测量（仅含面内两分量速度）学习连续3D+t心血管速度场，并考察物理约束的作用；结果表明MLP优于传统RBF，物理约束需与流场特性匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏平面测量重建时间分辨三维（3D+t）心血管流场十分困难：每个平面仅能观测到体积的一小部分上的两个面内速度分量，缺乏完整三维信息。

Method: 构建平面感知的神经场框架：以空间与时间坐标为输入，通过逐平面投影（per-plane projection）学习连续三维速度场，进而引入物理约束进行对比研究；在主动脉与动脉瘤计算数据集，以及由真实心脏4D Flow MRI扫描重采样得到的平面上评估。对比对象包括普通MLP、物理约束SIREN（PC-SIREN）与经典径向基函数（RBF）插值，并进一步考察扫描配置、扫描层数与空间分辨率的影响。

Result: 两个计算数据集上，普通MLP精度均优于RBF插值。主动脉数据：PC-SIREN将rRMSE降至0.3756，优于普通MLP的0.4273与RBF的0.7256，且在MRI数据所有解剖分区中均最准确。动脉瘤数据：普通MLP最准确，rRMSE为0.07–0.16，而PC-SIREN为0.55–0.71。

Conclusion: 连续神经场可由稀疏平面测量重建体数据心血管流场；物理约束的效果依赖于流场类型，必须与具体流动特性相匹配，并非普遍增益。

Abstract: Reconstructing time-resolved three-dimensional (3D+t) cardiovascular flow from sparse planar measurements is challenging because each plane observes only the two in-plane velocity components, on a small part of the volume. We propose a plane-aware neural-field framework that learns a continuous 3D velocity field from space and time coordinates through a per-plane projection, and then investigate the potential benefits of physics constraints. We evaluate the framework on computational aortic and aneurysm datasets, and on planes resampled from a real cardiac 4D Flow MRI scan. A plain multi-layer perceptron (MLP) was more accurate than classical radial basis function (RBF) interpolation on both computational datasets. For the aorta, a physics-constrained SIREN (PC-SIREN) reduced the rRMSE further to $0.3756$, compared with $0.4273$ for the plain MLP and $0.7256$ for RBF, and it was the most accurate model in every anatomical segment of the MRI data. For the aneurysms the plain MLP remained the most accurate model, with an rRMSE of $0.07$ to $0.16$ against $0.55$ to $0.71$ for PC-SIREN. Further experiments investigated scanning configuration, number of scans, and spatial resolution. These findings show that continuous neural fields can reconstruct volumetric cardiovascular flow from sparse planar measurements, and that physics constraints have to be matched to the flow.

</details>


### [363] [AvatarAnyone: Agentic Reconstruction of Mesh-Based Layered Avatars from YouTube Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30777)
*Aymen Mir,Peter Wonka,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Rıza Alp Güler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AvatarAnyone 提出用 AI 智能体为每个视频定制优化流程，从随意 YouTube 视频重建分层网格化身（每件衣物/鞋履为独立网格叠加于身体网格之上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随意 YouTube 视频中人物在服装类别、服装类型和层叠方式上差异大，每种组合都需要不同的重建策略，单一固定的分层重建流程无法通用。

Method: 使用 AI 智能体为每个人重建规划定制优化：智能体预测服装类别、服装类型与层叠关系，据此选择初始化并分阶段安排优化，从而输出分层网格（身体网格 + 各衣物层）。

Result: 在约 150 个 YouTube 视频上展示结果；在四个数据集上，该方法在可动画单目重建任务上优于现有基于网格的方法。分层网格支持虚拟试穿、换装渲染、标准布料模拟器做衣物层模拟、接触建模，并可直接导入 Blender、Unreal Engine 与 MuJoCo 等机器人仿真器。

Conclusion: 分层网格表示相较融合单曲面或高斯表示，为试穿、布料仿真与机器人仿真等下游应用提供了可用的拓扑与物理交互能力，且智能体驱动的按需规划有效解决了服装多样性带来的流程不通用问题。

Abstract: We introduce AvatarAnyone, a method for reconstructing avatars from casual YouTube videos as layered meshes, with each garment and footwear as a separate mesh layered on top of the body mesh. In such videos, people differ in garment classes, clothing types, and layers, and each combination needs a different reconstruction. A single fixed layered reconstruction pipeline therefore fails. AvatarAnyone solves this problem with AI agents that plan a custom optimization for each person's reconstruction. The agents predict the garment classes, the clothing type, and the layering. They use these predictions to choose an initialization and stage the optimization. Because the avatars are layered meshes, they support applications such as virtual try-on and cloth simulation. We change the clothing layers of an avatar and render it in different garments and poses. We can also simulate a clothing layer as cloth over the animated body with a standard cloth simulator. Most existing avatars are one fused surface or a set of Gaussians and cannot do this. We load the avatars directly into Blender, Unreal Engine, and robotics simulators such as MuJoCo. As the avatars are meshes, they support contact modeling with other objects. We show results from around 150 YouTube videos. On four datasets, AvatarAnyone outperforms existing mesh-based methods for animatable monocular reconstruction. Our project page is available at https://miraymen.github.io/avataranyone.

</details>


### [364] [BridgeMVS: Bridging Multi-View Stereo and Monodepth via Bidirectional Dynamic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30787)
*Jianfei Jiang,Haochen Yu,Qiankun Liu,Rui Yang,Hongyuan Liu,Xianglong Meng,Jiansheng Chen,Huimin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMVS级联多阶段MVS框架，通过双向动态融合（BDF）桥接单目深度特征与多视图代价体，实现MDE与MVS互为增强，在DTU、T&T、ETH3D达到SOTA并在T&T两榜单排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MVS依赖显式几何提供度量深度，但在特征匹配不可靠区域失效；MDE提供稳健结构先验却存在尺度模糊。现有混合方法多为单向利用单目线索，二者互增强未被充分挖掘。

Method: 统一级联多阶段MVS框架，在各阶段引入BDF模块执行mono-to-cost与cost-to-mono双向动态更新：单目结构特征引导代价体细化，多视图几何证据重校准单目表示以提升度量尺度一致性；融合表示由粗到细逐级传播，并施加阶段级相对尺度MDE监督与度量尺度MVS监督；BDF为即插即用模块。

Result: 在DTU、Tanks-and-Temples、ETH3D上取得SOTA，同时提升单目深度估计精度；在Tanks-and-Temples Intermediate与Advanced榜单均排名第一。

Conclusion: 双向动态融合有效实现MDE与MVS互补增强，可作为通用模块嵌入现有级联MVS框架。

Abstract: Multi-View Stereo (MVS) and Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) provide complementary cues for dense 3D reconstruction. While MVS leverages explicit geometry for metric depth, it struggles in challenging regions where feature matching is unreliable. Conversely, MDE provides robust structural priors but typically yields scale-ambiguous results. Existing hybrid methods mainly exploit monocular cues in a unidirectional manner, leaving the mutual enhancement between MDE and MVS insufficiently explored. To address this limitation, we propose BridgeMVS, a unified cascaded multi-stage MVS framework that bridges monocular depth features and multi-view cost volume representations through Bidirectional Dynamic Fusion (BDF). At each stage, BDF performs dynamic mono-to-cost and cost-to-mono updates, where monocular structural features guide cost volume refinement for more robust MVS depth estimation, while multi-view geometric evidence recalibrates monocular representations to improve their metric-scale consistency. Furthermore, the fused monocular and multi-view representations are progressively propagated from coarse to fine stages and constrained by stage-wise relative-scale MDE supervision and metric-scale MVS supervision, enabling cross-branch interactions to benefit subsequent high-resolution depth estimation. In addition, BDF is designed as a plug-and-play module that can be conveniently integrated into existing cascaded MVS frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BridgeMVS achieves state-of-the-art performance on the DTU, Tanks-and-Temples, and ETH3D benchmarks, while simultaneously boosting the accuracy of monocular depth estimation. Notably, BridgeMVS ranks first on both the Tanks-and-Temples Intermediate and Advanced benchmarks. The source code is available at \href{https://github.com/JianfeiJ/BridgeMVS}{https://github.com/JianfeiJ/BridgeMVS}.

</details>


### [365] [PestWeave: Linking Semantic Modeling and Evidence-Guided Text Modulation through Visual Prototypes for Fine-Grained Pest Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30796)
*Xueheng Li,Yu Wang,Yuxiang Shen,Wen Xue,Rui Li,Jie Zhang,Chengjun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态框架PestWeave，通过视觉原型连接内容自适应的视觉组织与文本融合，提升复杂背景下农业害虫识别的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 害虫外观多样、背景复杂，仅靠视觉难以识别；引入文本物种知识可互补，但复杂背景会遮蔽判别性视觉线索，且文本描述可能包含当前图像不支持的信息，导致跨模态融合失效。

Method: 视觉通路在RWKV架构上引入语义token分组与三类辅助token（聚类原型token、全局token、寄存器token），实现内容自适应扫描与上下文建模并保留空间结构；特征相似token被重排至相邻扫描位置。这些原型进一步用于文本调制：评估视觉对语义成分的支持度，调控各成分相对贡献与整体融合强度。

Result: 在多个害虫识别数据集上的大量实验验证了PestWeave的有效性，并显示其在实际害虫管理中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 基于视觉原型的自适应视觉组织与受控文本融合可有效缓解背景干扰与文本-图像不对齐问题，为多模态害虫识别提供可行方案。

Abstract: Agricultural pests threaten crop yield and quality, making accurate pest recognition essential for targeted management and sustainable agriculture. Their diverse appearances complicate recognition and motivate multimodal approaches incorporating complementary species knowledge from text. However, complex backgrounds can obscure discriminative visual cues, while textual descriptions may contain information unsupported by the current image, limiting effective cross-modal fusion. To address these challenges, we propose PestWeave, a multimodal framework connecting content-adaptive visual organization with text fusion through visual prototypes. Its visual pathway augments a Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV) architecture with semantic token grouping and three types of auxiliary tokens, enabling content-adaptive scanning and contextual modeling while preserving spatial structure. Feature-similar tokens are reordered into adjacent scanning positions, while cluster prototype, global, and register tokens provide group-level summaries, global context, and additional interaction capacity, respectively. These prototypes also guide text modulation by assessing visual support for semantic components and regulating their relative contributions and overall fusion strength, reducing interference from unsupported descriptions. Extensive experiments on multiple pest recognition datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of PestWeave and its potential for practical pest management.

</details>


### [366] [Go Far, Look Less: Active Multi-Room Reconstruction with Persistent Goals and Selective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30802)
*Jiawei Jiang,Haonan Zhou,Wendi Hu,Jiabei Hu,Yina Xiang,Kaiyue Zhou,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROAM-GS是一个免训练的多房间主动3D重建框架，用“持久目标+端点采样”策略在极少视角下实现完整几何与高保真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多房间场景下现有主动重建失效：贪心的下一最佳视角打分不给远处房间的“通行路径”任何收益，并过度精修近处不可消除的残差；而密集建图者虽能覆盖远处房间却需数千帧流式输入。

Method: (1) 每次决策联合选择持久目标及通往其的可变时域前缀，仅在端点处采集；(2) 表面重建记忆通过投影更新后的缺陷与观察方向对位姿打分；(3) 持久目标以剩余价值为通行过程赋分，并在抵达不再带来可测提升时释放；(4) 全程免训练、无需逐场景调参。

Result: HSSD平均覆盖率由74%提升至93%；HM3D由47%提升至96%，PSNR提升11.7 dB；在保持单房间Replica保真度的同时，以173×更少视角匹配90分钟密集建图效果。

Conclusion: 将稀疏视角预算建模为“目标选择+长视距端点采样”，可有效解决多房间主动重建中的短视与样本低效矛盾，实现跨房间的SOTA重建与单房间性能无损。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction aims to allocate a sparse view budget to recover complete geometry and high-fidelity renderings. However, existing methods degrade sharply in multi-room homes: myopic next-best-view scoring gives transit no credit for distant rooms and over-polishes irreducible nearby residuals, whereas dense mappers reach far rooms only by streaming thousands of frames. We propose ROAM-GS, a training-free active multi-room reconstruction framework that couples persistent goals with selective views: each decision selects a goal and a variable-horizon prefix toward it, capturing only at its endpoint. A Surface Reconstruction Memory scores poses by projecting post-update defects and viewing directions. Persistent goals credit transit with their remaining value and are released once arrivals stop measurably improving them. Without per-scene tuning, ROAM-GS achieves state-of-the-art multi-room reconstruction while preserving single-room Replica fidelity, raising mean coverage from 74% to 93% on HSSD and from 47% to 96% (+11.7 dB PSNR) on HM3D, and matching 90-minute dense mapping using $173\times$ fewer views.

</details>


### [367] [Can Multimodal Fusion MakeSense for Embodied Vision in Degraded Sensing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30836)
*Cheng-Ru Chou,Shih-Yu Lai,Pei-Lin Chiang,Shou-Po Tsai,Jing-Zhe Huang,Fukuharu Tanaka,Hirozumi Yamaguchi,Bing-Yu Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Hsun-Ping Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MakeSense多模态融合框架：私有流保留各传感器证据，经共享瓶颈token融合，并用全视图/子集视图配对训练惩罚"传感器越多误差越大"的负融合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉系统依赖互补传感器，但即使支持任意传感器子集的模型也存在负融合：增加传感器反而使预测误差上升。需在架构与训练层面同时抑制该问题。

Method: 私有流(per-sensor private streams)保留传感器特有证据，通过共享瓶颈token(shared bottleneck tokens)进行信息整合；训练时对同一退化观测配对全视图与子集视图，对"传感器更多却误差更高"施加惩罚；在几何允许时，距离读出仅用测距量，其余预测使用全部可用输入；辅以轻量适配器与逐数据集训练。

Result: 五数据集配对测试可辨识有益/有害的传感器增补；在MM-Fi上降低负融合；经轻量适配器与逐数据集训练，同一设计在MMAUD无人机定位、V2X-Real协同3D检测、RELI11D小样本姿态迁移、Humanoid-Everyday指令预测上均获提升；基于冻结主干输出的世界模型在较长预测时域上优于线性基线。

Conclusion: MakeSense通过结构隔离与配对训练有效缓解负融合，具备跨人类活动识别、空中系统、机器人与自动驾驶的通用性，并可作为世界模型的可复用冻结主干。

Abstract: Embodied vision systems rely on complementary sensors to perceive and act as conditions change. Even models that accept arbitrary sensor subsets can exhibit negative fusion: adding a sensor increases prediction error. We propose MakeSense, a multimodal fusion framework that preserves sensor-specific evidence in private streams and combines information through shared bottleneck tokens. Training pairs full and subset views of the same degraded observations, penalizing higher error with more sensors. Where geometry permits, the distance readout uses range measurements alone, while the rest of the prediction draws on all available inputs. Paired tests across five datasets reveal helpful and harmful sensor additions. MakeSense reduces negative fusion on MM-Fi. With lightweight adapters and per-dataset training, the same design improves UAV localization on MMAUD, cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Real, few-shot pose transfer to RELI11D, and command prediction on Humanoid-Everyday, where a world model trained on the frozen core's outputs forecasts future fused states more accurately than a linear baseline at longer tested horizons. MakeSense supports embodied sensing across human activity, aerial systems, robotics, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [368] [Certified Defense against Adversarial Patches on Traffic Signs via Mask-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30840)
*Rudra Saxena,Vishal Dev Verma,Rohan Sharma,Palani Thanaraj K*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出掩码感知训练，使PatchCleanser在交通标志识别上的认证精度从4.8%提升至60.0%，真实对抗补丁下精度达85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PatchCleanser在交通标志上认证精度极低（GTSRB上仅1.7%–4.8%），原因是单个掩码即可遮挡区分标志类别的中心小符号，导致防御与常规训练分类器配合时几乎无法认证任何样本。

Method: 掩码感知训练：用防御自身掩码集合中随机抽取的成对掩码对图像进行遮挡后训练分类器，并对覆盖标志中心的掩码施加采样偏置；防御、掩码集与认证流程均保持不变，因而认证保证得以保留。

Result: 对ImageNet预训练的ResNet-50，认证精度由4.8%升至60.0%；在真实逐图像对抗补丁攻击下，防御后的ResNet-50达到85.7%，而原始PatchCleanser配合常规训练ResNet-50为74.6%。

Conclusion: 分类器训练方式与掩码分布相匹配是释放认证防御潜力的关键，掩码感知训练在不改变认证保证的前提下显著提升交通标志识别的可认证性与实际鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adversarial patches are a practical threat to traffic-sign recognition: a small sticker on a sign can change a classifier's decision. PatchCleanser is a certified defense that applies two rounds of masking and, for every image it certifies, provably returns the correct label for any patch that fits inside one mask. We show that on traffic signs the defense certifies almost nothing when paired with a normally trained classifier (1.7% to 4.8% certified accuracy on GTSRB), because a single mask can hide the small central symbol that distinguishes sign classes. We propose mask-aware training, which trains the classifier on images occluded by random pairs drawn from the defense's own mask set, with a sampling bias toward masks over the sign's centre. The defense, mask set and certification procedure are unchanged, so the guarantee is preserved. For an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, mask-aware training raises certified accuracy from 4.8% to 60.0%. Under real per-image adversarial patches, the defended ResNet-50 reaches 85.7% accuracy, versus 74.6% for the original PatchCleanser with a normally trained ResNet-50.

</details>


### [369] [DyLoMo-DiT: Dynamic Looped Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30844)
*Lernie Li,Yuanliu Liu,Pengfei Xiong,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyLoMo-DiT，通过权重共享循环复用中层并配合稀疏专家提升DiT的参数与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT依赖增加参数量和计算量来扩展容量；MoE变体虽扩展参数容量但每token计算固定，仍需大量参数存储。作者希望在不增加参数的前提下加深网络并动态分配计算。

Method: 核心是参数复用而非新增：对中间层进行权重共享的循环重复应用；同时用稀疏专家在不同token间分配参数容量；并按噪声水平动态调整循环次数（低噪声步骤分配更多pass）。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，匹配参数与计算预算时，FID较DiT降低11-24%，较DiT-MoE降低36-56%；文本到图像生成中，缩放版DyLoMo-DiT的GenEval分数超过FLUX.1（0.714 vs 0.673），活跃参数不足其十分之一（1.05B vs 12B）。

Conclusion: 参数复用（循环共享）+稀疏专家+噪声自适应的计算分配能更高效地利用参数与计算预算；为低噪声步分配更多pass可带来额外增益。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) scale model capacity by increasing both parameter count and computation. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants expand parameter capacity while keeping per-token computation roughly fixed, but still require substantial parameter storage. In this paper, we propose Dynamic Looped MoE DiT (DyLoMo-DiT), which reuses parameters rather than adding them: a weight-shared loop repeatedly applies the middle layers, while sparse experts allocate parameter capacity across tokens. This design deepens the network without adding parameters, freeing the parameter budget for other uses, such as more experts or more distinct layers. It also allocates compute dynamically, varying the number of passes across noise levels. Experiments show that DyLoMo-DiT makes more effective use of parameter and compute budgets, with further gains from assigning more passes to low-noise steps. On ImageNet $256\times256$, DyLoMo-DiT reduces FID by 11-24% relative to DiT and 36-56% relative to DiT-MoE at matched parameter and compute budgets. For text-to-image generation, a scaled DyLoMo-DiT achieves a higher GenEval score than FLUX.1 (0.714 vs. 0.673), with less than one tenth of its active parameters (1.05B vs. 12B).

</details>


### [370] [Age and Gender Estimation from Face and Ocular Smartphone Images via Identity Transfer and Ordinal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30850)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Jose Maria Buades,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究人脸/眼部身份识别表征向性别与年龄估计的迁移性，并提出有序损失提升年龄估计的1-off准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究身份识别预训练表征能否迁移至人脸与眼部的性别、年龄估计任务，并检验眼部区域能否替代整脸。

Method: 采用三种不同复杂度的CNN，分别在人脸或眼部身份识别上预训练；以固定特征+SVM和端到端微调两种方式评估；数据集为Adience的11,299张正面智能手机图像，遵循其五折协议与主体不重叠验证；并引入利用年龄组有序性的ordinal loss。

Result: 域匹配的身份预训练在端到端训练中优于ImageNet预训练与随机初始化；融合双眼区域可缩小与整脸的差距，年龄任务尤为明显。性别准确率最高达整脸88.1%、双眼组合82.9%；年龄估计眼部为59.0%精确、93.1% 1-off，整脸为57.6%和95.2%。

Conclusion: 身份识别表征可有效迁移至性别与年龄估计，ordinal loss在各架构与输入区域上稳定提升1-off准确率；眼部年龄准确率据称达到Adience上的最高水平。

Abstract: We investigate the transferability of identity-recognition representations to gender and age estimation from face and ocular images. Three CNNs of varying complexity, pre-trained for face or ocular identity recognition, are evaluated using fixed features with SVMs and end-to-end adaptation. Experiments use 11,299 frontal smartphone images from Adience, following its predefined five-fold protocol and subject-disjoint validation. Results show that domain-matched identity pre-training outperforms direct ImageNet and scratch initialisation in end-to-end training, while combining both ocular regions narrows the gap with full-face estimation, especially for age. We additionally introduce an ordinal loss that exploits the ordering of age groups and consistently improves 1-off accuracy across architectures and input regions. Across the evaluated configurations, best gender accuracies reach 88.1% for the face and 82.9% for combined ocular regions, while age estimation reaches 59.0% exact and 93.1% 1-off accuracy for ocular images, versus 57.6% and 95.2% for the full face. To our knowledge, these are the highest ocular age accuracies reported on Adience.

</details>


### [371] [CableMoE: Harm-Regularized Mean-Residual Expert Fusion for Transmission-Corridor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30859)
*Shima Salehi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CableMoE：对四个冻结分割专家做后验混合，以可用性感知概率平均为基线、加校准残差与损害惩罚，在输电走廊语义制图任务上取得84.906% mIoU、98.140%总体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LiDAR与图像引导预测器集成的有效性强，但用可学习的router（路由网络）替换强集成，会损害稀有电气资产的识别；需要一种稳健的事后融合方式。

Method: 四项冻结分割专家的后验混合（MoE）；可部署预测为围绕availability-aware概率平均的校准残差，并引入harm penalty惩罚对“均值已正确分类点”的额外损失；用匹配的消融组件研究验证各设计选择；数据划分为3个zone用于拟合、2个互斥zone用于残差强度选择、router未触碰的t5b5用于最终报告。

Result: 无约束学习混合相较概率平均损失1.032 mIoU；harm正则化在固定容量下恢复0.359点；去除多余逐点gate后精度与稳定性略升。最终84.906% mIoU、98.140%总体精度，较SPT +10.000点、较概率平均 +0.263点，三个随机种子均优于后者；pylon/conductor/structural-cable/insulator四类macro-IoU由SPT 84.517%、概率平均97.153%提升至98.144%。

Conclusion: 结果支持“保守残差组合”的设计理念，而非宣称新的分割骨干网络或普适SOTA；核心贡献在于降低集成替换风险的稳健后验融合框架。

Abstract: Semantic mapping of transmission corridors benefits from heterogeneous LiDAR and image-guided predictors, but replacing a strong ensemble with a learned router can damage rare electrical assets. We introduce CableMoE, a post-hoc mixture of four frozen segmentation experts. Its deployable prediction is a calibrated residual around availability-aware probability averaging, while a harm penalty discourages extra loss on points that the mean already classifies correctly. A matched component study shows why each design choice is needed: an unconstrained learned mixture loses 1.032 mIoU points relative to probability mean, harm regularization restores 0.359 points at fixed capacity, and removing an unnecessary point-wise gate slightly improves accuracy and stability. With three zones for fitting, two disjoint zones for residual-strength selection, and router-untouched t5b5 for final reporting, CableMoE obtains 84.906% mIoU and 98.140 % overall accuracy. This is +10.000 points over SPT and +0.263 over probability mean; all three seeds improve the latter. Across pylon, conductor, structural-cable, and insulator classes, macro-IoU rises from 84.517% for SPT and 97.153% for probability mean to 98.144 %. The results support conservative residual composition, not a claim of a new segmentation backbone or universal state of the art

</details>


### [372] [XFreq: Geometry-Aligned Cross-View Residual Learning for Sparse 3D Ultrasound Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30869)
*Yuhuan Lu,P. Ellen Grant,Davood Karimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XFreq：一种几何对齐的频率分解框架，从稀疏2D超声切片重建3D体数据，低频解码器重建平滑结构、高频解码器结合查询到的2D特征预测残差细节，并由门控模块调节残差贡献，在胎儿脑与CETUS心脏数据集上取得最优PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D超声对解剖评估有价值，但2D切片广泛可得、3D体数据获取受限，故需从稀疏2D切片重建3D。稀疏切片导致体数据大部分未被观测，即使已知采集位姿也难以直接重建。现有方法或插值观测强度、或将学习到的2D图像特征融入3D重建，但二者在"平滑结构"与"残差细节"预测中的互补作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出XFreq框架：利用已知切片位姿与有限足迹（finite footprints），在3D位置查询2D图像特征，与splat强度表示及采样几何互补。采用双解码器频率分解：低频解码器从splat表示预测平滑3D场；高频解码器额外引入查询的2D特征预测残差细节。引入可学习门控模块，依据几何与跨切片特征变化调控残差贡献。在自建胎儿脑数据集与公开CETUS心脏数据集上评估，切片由3D体数据重采样，采用五折交叉验证。

Result: 在多数设置下于两个数据集上取得最高PSNR与SSIM；在CETUS上使用32张输入切片达到26.82 dB PSNR。消融实验验证了局部特征查询、几何线索与残差预测的各自贡献，且增益通常随输入切片增多而增大。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，几何对齐的跨视角特征融合对从稀疏2D切片重建3D超声体数据具有重要价值；频率分解与门控残差机制能有效兼顾平滑结构与细节恢复。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) ultrasound provides valuable volumetric context for anatomical assessment, motivating reconstruction from widely available two-dimensional (2D) slices. Sparse slices leave much of the volume unobserved, even when their acquisition poses are known. Existing methods interpolate observed intensities or incorporate learned 2D image features into 3D reconstruction, but their complementary roles in predicting smooth structure and residual details remain less explored. We introduce XFreq, a geometry-aligned framework for sparse 3D ultrasound reconstruction. It uses known slice poses and finite footprints to query 2D image features at 3D locations, complementing splatted intensities and sampling geometry. A low-frequency decoder predicts a smooth 3D field from the splat representation, while a high-frequency decoder additionally incorporates the queried 2D features to predict residual details. A learned gating module regulates the residual contribution based on geometry and cross-slice feature variation. We evaluate XFreq on an in-house fetal brain dataset and the public CETUS cardiac dataset, using slices resampled from 3D ultrasound volumes and fivefold cross-validation. Among the compared methods, XFreq achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM in most settings across both datasets, reaching 26.82 dB PSNR on CETUS with 32 input slices. Ablation studies further show the contributions of local feature querying, geometric cues, and residual prediction, with gains generally increasing as more input slices are used. Our findings highlight the value of geometry-aligned cross-view feature integration for reconstructing 3D ultrasound volumes from sparse 2D slices.

</details>


### [373] [A High-Resolution UAV Benchmark Dataset and Baseline Evaluation for Teak Crown Segmentation in Western Ghats Tropical Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30888)
*Anand S Sahadevan,Sunita Arya,Anup Kumar Das,Bimal Kumar Bhattacharya,S Manthira Moorthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TeakTreeNet数据集与评测协议（6391个224×224、2cm分辨率柚木树二值分割标注无人机patch），并系统基准测试U-Net、PSPNet、DeepLabV3、SegFormer，SegFormer取得SOTA（F1 0.938±0.002，IoU 0.968±0.001，Acc 0.986±0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率无人机影像的柚木树精准分割对可持续种植园管理至关重要，但该任务缺乏专用基准数据集与统一评测协议，导致方法间难以直接比较。

Method: 构建原生2cm分辨率的二值柚木树分割数据集与评测协议；在scratch（从零训练）与ImageNet预训练两种设置下，以三随机种子可复现实验，对U-Net、PSPNet、DeepLabV3、SegFormer四种主流架构进行严格基准测试，并开展边界描绘等综合分析。

Result: SegFormer刷新SOTA：F1 0.938±0.002、IoU 0.968±0.001、Accuracy 0.986±0.001，优于所有预训练CNN；且从零训练的SegFormer即可超过ImageNet预训练基线；Transformer在密集种植场景下边界描绘能力更优。

Conclusion: 域自适应训练已足够，无需大规模预训练开销；SegFormer在精度、效率与可部署性间取得最佳平衡，适合柚木种植园的实际林业监测，TeakTreeNet为后续方法提供可复现的统一比较平台。

Abstract: Accurate teak-tree segmentation from high-resolution drone imagery is critical for sustainable plantation management, yet lacks dedicated benchmarks. We introduce TeakTreeNet, a comprehensive dataset and evaluation protocol for binary teak-tree segmentation at native 2cm resolution. Four state-of-the-art architectures: U-Net, PSPNet, DeepLabV3, and SegFormer were rigorously benchmarked across scratch and ImageNet-pretrained settings with three-seed reproducibility. SegFormer establishes new SOTA performance with F1 0.938 +/- 0.002 (Teak), IoU 0.968 +/- 0.001 (Teak), and Accuracy 0.986 +/- 0.001, outperforming all pretrained CNNs. Domain-adaptive training suffices, as the scratch SegFormer outperforms ImageNet pretrained baselines, avoiding large-scale pretraining overhead. Comprehensive analysis reveals Transformer's superior boundary delineation for dense plantation scenarios. TeakTreeNet provides researchers with 6391 expertly annotated drone patches (224x224) and establishes reproducible evaluation protocols, enabling direct comparison of future methods. Our benchmark demonstrates SegFormer's optimal balance of accuracy, efficiency, and deployability for operational forestry monitoring of teak plantations for India's western tropical forests.

</details>


### [374] [Speak When You See It: Proactive Task Assistants Should Wait for Evidence, Not the Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30895)
*Bohe Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出逐步物理任务基准主要奖励"知道何时"而非"看见什么"，形式化为 video-blind frontier，并提出 Evidence-Contrastive Training 使模型真正依赖视频证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式助手需每几秒判断步骤是否完成、是否出错。但现有基准（Ego-MC-Bench、QICD）可能被仅凭时间的策略轻易"攻破"：一个完全不看视频、仅按 25s 报警、30s 确认步骤的计时器即可超越所有已发表结果，说明基准存在时间捷径，亟需揭示并突破。

Method: 1) 构造 video-blind 计时器基线，量化完成准确率与错误 F1 的权衡，定义 video-blind frontier；2) 检验所有已发表 per-step 结果与 12 个零样本模型是否落入该前沿；3) 提出 Evidence-Contrastive Training (ECT)：在基准自身问题上微调，将正样本与同一录像、同一时间戳的负样本配对，使耗时无法区分正负；在 209 个 QICD 视频上以约 15 GPU 小时训练 2B 模型。

Result: 计时器在 Ego-MC-Bench 上错误 F1 达 0.373，超过所有已发表结果（≤0.27）。ECT 训练的 2B 模型在四个随机种子下于两个基准上均跨越前沿；无适配迁移到 Ego-MC-Bench 时，对无错步骤的及时确认率为 59%，而同等骨干、使用约 25k 合成反事实与真实录像训练的已发表模型仅 30%。等时长的测试对中，区分已完成/未完成步骤的准确率达 90–92%（指令典型时长基线为 65%）；重缩放帧间时间戳几乎无影响；替换为同一食谱的其他录像使完成准确率下降约三分之二。

Conclusion: 现有基准存在严重时间捷径，ECT 能有效迫使模型依赖视频证据，显著突破 video-blind 前沿并具备跨基准迁移与时间鲁棒性，代码与权重将公开。

Abstract: Proactive assistants that guide people through physical tasks must decide, every few seconds, whether a step is complete and whether a mistake has occurred. We find that two benchmarks for this task largely reward knowing when rather than seeing what: a timer that never looks at the video, raising an alarm at 25 s and confirming each step at 30 s, reaches a mistake F1 of 0.373 on Ego-MC-Bench, above every published result (at most 0.27). We formalize this as the video-blind frontier, the best trade-off between completion accuracy and mistake F1 that policies reach from the elapsed time, the instruction and training statistics alone: every published per-step result on Ego-MC-Bench lies inside it, and so do all twelve zero-shot models we run on QICD. To move beyond it, we introduce Evidence-Contrastive Training (ECT): fine-tune on the benchmark's own questions, and pair its positives with negatives from the same recording at the same elapsed time, so that elapsed time cannot separate the two sides of a pair.
