Trained on 209 QICD videos in about 15 GPU-hours, a 2B model with ECT crosses the frontier on both benchmarks in all four seeds; transferred to Ego-MC-Bench without adaptation, it confirms 59% of mistake-free steps in time, against 30% for a published model with the same backbone trained on about 25k synthetic counterfactuals and real recordings. Its decisions follow the video beyond what timing explains: at equal elapsed time it tells a finished step from an unfinished one in 90-92% of test pairs, where the instruction's typical duration reaches 65%, rescaling the time stamps between the frames leaves it essentially unchanged, and swapping in another recording, of the same recipe where one exists, cuts its completion accuracy by two thirds. The code and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [375] [World Models as Native Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30898)
*Qinyue Li,Weijie Qiao,Qingyi Tao,Zerui Gong,Zhe Yang,Quanzhou Li,Taolin Zhang,Jinglin He,Zhonghua Wu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Native LAM：将预训练视频扩散Transformer同时用作IDM与FDM，以可学习动力学寄存器作为唯一因果桥接，在仅运动差异的反事实时序对上训练，提取紧凑潜在动作；并据此构建VLA预训练范式PAWlicy，在多个基准与真机上取得领先或竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构视频（第一视角人类视频、多机器人录制）蕴含丰富动力学，但或缺动作标注、或缺乏共享动作空间；现有LAM从零训练IDM/FDM，需在像素重建与潜空间容量/精度间权衡。预训练视频世界模型虽能生成高保真动力学，却以密集、跨层纠缠方式表示。作者希望以最小改动从世界模型中提取可迁移的紧凑潜在动作。

Method: 1) Native LAM：复用单个预训练视频扩散Transformer兼作IDM和FDM，可学习dynamics registers作为二者间唯一因果桥；训练数据为仅在运动上不同的反事实时序对，使寄存器将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑native latent action。2) PAWlicy：以Native LAM作为大规模视频上的动力学教师，为VLA策略做动作预训练；区别于world action models，训练/推理中不生成视频。

Result: 在LARY基准上，动作分类与控制回归均优于现有LAM；无需迁移目标即可在新机器人、新场景及人手到机器人间重放相同运动。3.8B模型PAWlicy在RoboTwin 2.0（域随机化示范训练）达93.6%平均成功率，为VLA中最高；RoboDojo上各评测维度均具竞争力，其Precision与榜单领先条目相当；真机上两项任务均优于pi_0.5基线。

Conclusion: 预训练视频世界模型可被视为LAM并以极小改动提取紧凑潜在动作；由此得到的Native LAM在表征质量与跨具身/跨场景迁移上表现优越，且PAWlicy作为不生成视频的动力学教师式VLA预训练范式有效。两阶段均仅使用公开数据和公开世界模型，具备可复现性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Large and diverse video sources, such as egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot recordings, carry rich dynamics but come either without actions or without a shared action space. Latent action models (LAMs) can recover these dynamics, but they train their inverse- and forward-dynamics models (IDM, FDM) from scratch, trading off pixel reconstruction against latent capacity and accuracy. Pretrained video world models natively generate high-fidelity dynamics, yet represent them densely, entangled across all layers. Viewing the world model as a LAM, we show that these dynamics can be extracted with minimal changes to the model. Native LAM operates one pretrained video diffusion transformer as both IDM and FDM, with learnable dynamics registers as the only causal bridge between the two, and trains on counterfactual temporal pairs that differ in motion alone, so that the registers distill a video's dynamics into a compact native latent action. We further propose a pretraining paradigm for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, Pretraining Actions from World models (PAWlicy), in which Native LAM serves as a dynamics teacher on large-scale video; unlike world action models, it never generates video. Native latent actions outperform existing LAMs on the LARY benchmark in both action classification and control regression, and replay the same motion on new robots, in new scenes, and from human hands to robots, although no transfer objective is used in training. With a 3.8B model, PAWlicy reaches a 93.6% average success rate on RoboTwin 2.0 trained with domain-randomized demonstrations, the highest among VLAs. On RoboDojo, it is competitive on every dimension we evaluate, and its Precision, evaluated by us, is on par with the leading leaderboard entries. On real robots, it improves over the pi_0.5 baseline on both tasks. Both stages use only public data and a publicly released world model.

</details>


### [376] [GlanceNav: Efficient Vision-and-Language Navigation with Streaming Memory and Counterfactual Rewind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30900)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Danh Vinh Le,Tuyen Pham Le,Quan Nguyen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GlanceNav，一种基于混合 MLLM 的流式视觉语言导航器：Gated DeltaNet 递归状态贯穿整条轨迹，注意力层仅保留短窗口与关键帧库，实现恒定代价的步进计算与整回合训练；并配套 Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe) 强化学习，通过回退到最接近目标的轨迹点、由专家修正少量动作并按结果变化分配信用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 导航策略在每个决策点重新编码历史帧、且在孤立决策上训练，导致计算冗余且可用历史极短；同时其强化学习依赖整回合单一标量奖励，错误学习效率低。作者追问：能否只编码每帧一次、每步恒定代价，同时保留关键记忆并高效从错误中学习。

Method: 1) 混合 MLLM 架构：Gated DeltaNet 层以递归状态承载全回合历史；注意力层保留最近观测的短窗口，并维护一个由"帧对递归状态的改变量"筛选出的关键帧小库。2) 恒定大小记忆使每步计算固定，且训练时使用与推理相同记忆，可整回合训练；记忆可在常数时间内复制。3) CoRe 强化学习：将漂移的 rollout 回退至最接近目标的时刻，由专家修正少量动作，再以结果变化对决策进行信用分配。

Result: 仅在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 训练集上训练、强化学习使用 1,000 条指令，4B 参数 GlanceNav 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE val-unseen 上成功率分别达 71.9% 与 73.6%；在 R2R-CE 上较最强的不使用外部数据的单 RGB 方法高 9.1 个点，单步耗时 104 ms。

Conclusion: 单次编码 + 恒定大小混合记忆的流式设计，可在固定每步开销下维持长程有效历史；配合基于记忆回退的反事实信用分配，显著提升 VLN 的成功率与训练效率，验证了"恒定代价记忆 + 回合级强化学习"路线的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become strong policies for vision-and-language navigation (VLN). However, most of them encode a sample of past frames again at every decision and are trained on isolated decisions, so computation is redundant and the usable history is short. They also learn from their own mistakes through outcome rewards that give one scalar to a whole episode. We ask whether a navigator can encode each frame only once, at a constant cost per step, and still remember what matters and learn efficiently from its mistakes. We present GlanceNav, a streaming navigator built on a hybrid MLLM. Its Gated DeltaNet layers carry a recurrent state through the whole episode, while its attention layers keep a short window of recent observations and a small bank of keyframes, selected by how much each frame changes the recurrent state. This constant-size memory keeps the cost per step fixed and allows training on whole episodes with the same memory as at inference. Because the entire memory can also be copied in constant time, we further propose Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe), a reinforcement learning stage that rewinds a drifting rollout to its closest approach to the goal, lets an expert correct a few actions, and credits decisions by the resulting change in outcome. Trained only on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE training splits, with 1,000 instructions for reinforcement learning, a 4B GlanceNav reaches success rates of 71.9% and 73.6% on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen. On R2R-CE, it outperforms the strongest previous single-RGB method trained without external data by 9.1 points, while running at 104 ms per step.

</details>


### [377] [Replay Before You Reply: Second-Look Prompting for Unified Multimodal Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30909)
*Yicheng Zhang,Xun Zhang,Jingyuan Ma,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Second-Look Prompting (SLP)：将图像-问题对在输入中重复一遍，使副本中的每个 token 都能在因果注意力下看到完整输入，从而获得统一的多模态编码，并在 12 个基准上普遍提升 VLM 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 采用因果注意力，token 只能关注其之前的上下文；因此先出现的模态只能在部分观测下编码，无法利用完整的图文信息，导致多模态理解不统一。

Method: SLP 是一种无需训练的方法：在输入中重复图像-问题对。在重复副本中，所有视觉与文本 token 均可关注到完整输入，形成统一多模态编码。分析揭示两个互补机制：问题引导第二张图像的注意力位置；新增视觉 token 充当 prefill scratchpad，其隐状态保留与答案相关的图文信息（即使替换为空白图像仍能提升推理）。

Result: 在 12 个基准上，SLP 提升了所有评估模型的总体平均分，涵盖 9 个开源模型（3B–72B）与 3 个专有前沿模型，其中 Gemini 3 Flash 在 MATH-Vision 上提升达 5.6 分。

Conclusion: 输入呈现方式是一种简单而有效的提升 VLM 性能的手段；重复输入带来的收益既源于完整上下文可见性，也源于额外视觉 token 提供的计算型 scratchpad。

Abstract: Solving multimodal tasks requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to form a unified understanding of the image and the question text. However, under causal attention in current VLMs, a token can attend only to what precedes it. Whichever modality comes first is therefore encoded under partial observation of the input, uninformed by the rest of the multimodal context. We introduce Second-Look Prompting (SLP), a training-free method that repeats the image-question pair in the input. In the repeated copy, every visual and textual token can attend to the complete input, yielding a unified multimodal encoding of the pair. Moreover, the added visual tokens contribute computation beyond re-exposing the image: even a blank image in their place can improve reasoning. Across 12 benchmarks, SLP improves the overall average score of every evaluated model, spanning nine open-source models (3B-72B) and three proprietary frontier models, with gains reaching 5.6 points for Gemini 3 Flash on MATH-Vision. Our analysis reveals two complementary mechanisms: the question guides where the second image attends, and the added visual tokens act as a prefill scratchpad whose hidden states retain answer-relevant information from the image and question. These findings highlight input presentation as a simple yet effective way to improve VLM performance.

</details>


### [378] [Do Waste Classifiers Recognise Items They Have Never Seen? Object-Disjoint Evaluation on Household and Medical Waste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30911)
*Nusrat Jahan,Mst. Eshrat Jahan Esha,Masuma Sadia,Saad Ahmed,Md Zahim Hassan,S M Jahangir Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 BanglaWaste-23 数据集（4,160 张图像、172 个物体、23 个家庭与医疗废物类别），采用按物体不重叠的五折交叉验证评估 11 个 ImageNet 预训练模型和 2 个从头训练模型，指出现有随机划分评估高估性能，主张以未见物体评估为准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾自动分类研究常在随机图像划分上报告近乎完美的准确率，但随机划分会把同一件物品的照片同时放入训练集和测试集，导致数据泄漏，无法反映模型对从未见过物品的识别能力；此外，从头训练模型在医疗废物漏检上的风险缺乏量化。

Method: 构建 BanglaWaste-23 数据集，图像级标注物理物体身份；对比评估 11 个 ImageNet 预训练模型与 2 个从头训练模型（紧凑自定义网络、MobileNetV3-L），采用物体不重叠的五折交叉验证与随机划分两种协议，并在达卡的第二份数据集上做跨数据集测试。

Result: 未见物体上最佳预训练模型宏平均 F1 达 98.8%，六个模型统计上并列；随机划分将从头训练模型的准确率高估 15–19 个百分点；从头训练模型漏检 11–13% 医疗物品，预训练模型低于 1%；在达卡数据集上预训练模型准确率下降 15–22 个百分点。

Conclusion: 分类器应在未见物体上评估，随机划分结果仅可作为性能上界；预训练迁移对医疗废物安全识别尤为关键。数据集发布于 Zenodo，代码见 https://github.com/saadbaust/BanglaWaste-23。

Abstract: Automatic waste classification supports source separation, which enables recycling and keeps hazardous medical items out of household waste. Waste classification studies often report near-perfect accuracy on random image splits, which put photographs of one item in both training and test sets. To measure how well classifiers recognise items they have never seen, we built BanglaWaste-23, with 4,160 images of 172 objects in 23 household and medical waste classes from Bangladesh. Every image also records which physical item it shows. We evaluated 11 ImageNet-pretrained models and two models trained from scratch (a compact custom network and MobileNetV3-L) using object-disjoint five-fold cross-validation and a random split. On unseen objects, the best pretrained model reached a macro-averaged F1 score of 98.8%, and six models were statistically tied. The random split overstated from-scratch accuracy by 15 to 19 percentage points. The from-scratch models also missed 11 to 13% of medical items, against under 1% for pretrained models. On a second dataset from Dhaka, pretrained accuracy fell by 15 to 22 points. Classifiers should therefore be evaluated on unseen objects, with random-split results read as an upper bound. The data are on Zenodo and the code at https://github.com/saadbaust/BanglaWaste-23.

</details>


### [379] [FGSCalib: Targetless Camera-LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration from Foreground Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30935)
*Marcell Kégl,Csaba Benedek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FGSCalib：利用场景中任意运动物体的前景一致性，无需标定靶、无需初始外参，实现固定式相机-激光雷达对的无靶在线重标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定安装的相机-LiDAR基础设施需周期重标定，但使用标定靶会使设备停止服务，因此需要无靶、免初始外参的重标定方法。

Method: 假设场景中存在任意运动物体（不依赖类别）；各传感器独立将运动前景与静态背景分离；将相机前景掩膜转换为边界邻近距离场，在选定帧对上以两阶段模拟退火搜索外参，使LiDAR前景点投影与相机前景边界一致；支持旋转式与非重复扫描（Livox）LiDAR。

Result: 仿真21个精确真值场景中中位误差5.3 cm/0.19°；真实数据72个场景（8个场景中9对传感器，参考值由43次人工标定平均得到）在18 cm/1.8°容差下，取五次运行中最低代价结果，51/72达标（Livox 20/24，旋转式31/48），优于五个基线；中位误差9.5 cm/0.9°，单次人工标定为7.3 cm/0.7°。

Conclusion: FGSCalib证明仅凭无类别运动前景即可实现免初值、免标定靶的相机-LiDAR重标定，精度接近单次人工标定，尤其显著优于Livox场景的基线方法；代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR pairs installed as fixed infrastructure need periodic recalibration, but a calibration target takes the installation out of service. We present FGSCalib, a targetless method that calibrates a stationary camera-LiDAR pair from the objects moving in its view. We only assume that arbitrary moving objects are present in the scene: each sensor separates the moving foreground from the static background, without relying on object classes. With the correct extrinsic, the LiDAR foreground points project onto the camera foreground. FGSCalib turns the camera mask into a boundary-proximity field and finds the extrinsic by two-stage simulated annealing over selected frame pairs. It needs no measured initial extrinsic (random starts give similar results) and works with both rotating and non-repetitive LiDARs. We evaluate it on 21 simulated scenarios with exact ground truth and on 72 real ones (nine camera-LiDAR pairs in eight scenes), with a reference averaged from 43 manual calibrations. In simulation the median error is 5.3 cm and 0.19°. On real data we use a tolerance of 18 cm and 1.8°, met by 93% of single manual calibrations. Keeping the lowest-cost of five runs, FGSCalib meets it in 51 of the 72 scenarios: in 20 of 24 with the Livox, where the strongest of five baselines reaches 11, and in 31 of 48 with the two rotating LiDARs, where only one baseline estimate does. Its median error is 9.5 cm and 0.9°, against 7.3 cm and 0.7° for a single manual calibration. The code and the dataset are available online.

</details>


### [380] [SIAMBA: A Pseudo-Siamese Mamba-based Deep Learning Network for Multimodal Flood Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30938)
*Muhammad Fasih Ishfaq,Giulio Weikmann,Lorenzo Bruzzone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出伪孪生Mamba网络SIAMBA，融合Sentinel-1 SAR与Sentinel-2多光谱双时相影像进行洪水变化检测，在OMBRIA基准上取得81.98% IoU和93.57% OA的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时相多光谱与SAR联合洪水变化检测对灾害快速响应至关重要：Sentinel-2提供判别性光谱观测，Sentinel-1提供不受天气影响的结构信息。但联合使用需在建模时序证据的同时处理跨模态异质性，云污染、光照变化、成像几何与斑点噪声、地物响应异质等因素均使变化检测复杂化。

Method: SIAMBA为伪孪生Mamba架构：采用模态专属编码器，结合双池化SE重校准与选择性状态空间处理以建模长程空间上下文；设计门控跨模态变化模块，在多个语义层级提取绝对、有符号与相对对数比差异；并通过注意力精化跳跃连接支持渐进式多尺度解码。

Result: 在OMBRIA基准（含独立测试集及法国、阿尔巴尼亚、圭亚那、东帝汶四个地理多样子集）上，独立测试集达到81.98% IoU与93.57% OA，为对比方法中最高；四个空间不重叠区域子集上的平均IoU与OA同样最高。

Conclusion: 将选择性状态空间上下文建模与门控多尺度差异学习相结合，可有效提升多地理场景下的洪水变化制图性能。

Abstract: Flood change detection from bi-temporal multispectral and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images is essential for rapid disaster response. In this context, Sentinel-2 multispectral imagery provides discriminative spectral observations, whereas Sentinel-1 SAR provides weather-independent structural information. Using these modalities jointly requires modelling temporal evidence while accounting for cross-modal heterogeneity. In particular, change detection is complicated by cloud contamination, illumination variability, acquisition geometry and speckle, and heterogeneous land-cover responses. To address these challenges, we propose SIAMBA, a pseudo-siamese Mamba-based network for bi-temporal flood change detection from paired Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 observations. SIAMBA uses dedicated modality-specific encoders with dual-pooling squeeze-and-excitation recalibration and selective state-space processing for long-range spatial context. A gated cross-modal change module derives absolute, signed, and relative log-ratio differences at multiple semantic levels, and attention-refined skip connections support progressive multi-scale decoding. Experiments are carried out on OMBRIA benchmark, which includes an independent test set and geographically diverse subsets in France, Albania, Guyana, and Timor. SIAMBA achieves 81.98% IoU, and 93.57% OA on the independent test set, reaching the highest values in the considered comparison. It also provides the highest unweighted average IoU and OA across four regional subsets spatially disjuncted. These results indicate that combining selective state-space contextual modelling with gated multiscale discrepancy learning effectively improves flood change mapping across the evaluated geographic settings.

</details>


### [381] [Diffutoon-2: Toon Shading with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30945)
*Yuze Sun,Zhongjie Duan,Yingda Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diffutoon-2 提出基于视频扩散框架的卡通（toon）渲染方法，通过“动画重建”方式学习风格化，无需跨域训练对或外部风格参考，性能优于通用与专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卡通渲染扩散方法多依赖精心构造的源-风格训练对或额外视觉参考；Diffutoon 虽借结构引导与上色规避了这些需求，但受限于图像扩散骨干、短时窗运动模块与重复重叠窗口去噪，效率与一致性欠佳。

Method: 构建 Diffutoon-2 视频扩散框架，采用两个互补模块在冻结视频骨干的条件下，分别从轮廓（线稿）与模糊颜色重建动画片段；推理时将真实视频中提取的同类条件联合用于引导生成。

Result: 在定量指标与人工评测上均优于通用及领域专用基线方法。

Conclusion: 以动画重建为监督信号可有效替代跨域配对训练，实现高质量视频卡通化渲染并兼顾时序一致性。

Abstract: Toon shading renders objects with a flat, stylized appearance. Diffusion models open a new route to this task by transforming photorealistic videos into an anime style. However, existing approaches often rely on curated source and style training pairs or additional visual references. Diffutoon avoids these requirements through structure guidance and colorization, but builds on an image diffusion backbone with short-window motion modules and repeated overlapping-window denoising. We present Diffutoon-2, a video diffusion framework that learns toon shading through animation reconstruction. Two complementary modules independently reconstruct animation clips from outlines and blurred colors, with the video backbone frozen. At inference, the modules jointly guide generation using the same types of conditions extracted from photorealistic footage, requiring neither cross-domain training pairs nor external style references. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Diffutoon-2 outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific baselines, across quantitative metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [382] [Graph2Scene: Relation-Aware Text-to-Image Generation with Graph-Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30964)
*Peizheng Li,Xin Ai,Guanghui Cheng,Zikang Dong,Qiange Wang,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Graph2Scene：免训练的图结构注意力引导框架，将场景图（节点=元素、边=关系）转为选择性注意力，并按场景复杂度自适应控制时长、渐进松弛，显著提升复杂提示词中的对象关系生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型的注意力控制方法主要解决"元素出现在哪里"（区域定位），却忽略"元素之间如何交互"。描述关系的relation token常把高注意力分配到无关区域，导致交互缺失或错误；同时过度约束又会损害生成自由度。

Method: 提出Graph2Scene，一种无需训练的框架，将场景图作为扩散生成的核心控制信号：以节点表示元素、边表示关系，把元素—关系依赖转化为选择性注意力引导——既将每个元素锚定到对应区域，又引导相关元素间的关系交互；并依据场景复杂度自适应调整控制持续时间，在去噪过程中渐进松弛图引导以避免过度约束。

Result: 在T2I-CompBench与ConceptMix上组合式生成（尤其对象关系）明显提升；ConceptMix上关系准确率从0.59提升至0.84（对比最强的基于FLUX.1的区域控制基线）；LongBench-T2I上对长且复杂提示词亦具优势。

Conclusion: 以场景图为控制信号的选择性注意力引导，是提升复杂提示词组合式生成的有效途径，且免训练、可与现有扩散模型（如FLUX.1）结合，在关系正确性与长提示词场景上均有收益。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models still struggle with complex prompts containing many elements and interactions. Existing attention-control methods mainly focus on controlling where elements appear, but often ignore how objects interact with each other. As a result, relation tokens describing interactions between elements may assign high attention to irrelevant regions, leading to missing or incorrect interactions. In this paper, we propose Graph2Scene, a training-free framework that treats scene graphs as a core control signal for diffusion generation. It represents elements as nodes and their relations as edges, and translates these element-relation dependencies into selective attention guidance. It grounds each element to its corresponding region and guides relational interactions among the involved elements. To achieve appropriate relation control without over-constraining the generation process, Graph2Scene further adapts the control duration to scene complexity and progressively relaxes graph guidance during denoising. Experiments on T2I-CompBench and ConceptMix show clear improvements in compositional generation, especially for object relations. On ConceptMix, Graph2Scene improves relationship accuracy from 0.59 to 0.84 over the strongest FLUX.1-based regional-control baseline. Results on LongBench-T2I further show its advantage on long and complex prompts.

</details>


### [383] [Parsing Visual Latents for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31021)
*Zhuoying Yang,Yichen Guo,Kathy Jia,Zichang Su,Ayesha Kashif,Nange Hai,Jeffrey Ming Han Li,Zhewen He,Silvio Kempf,Ni Jiachen,Junjie Yang,Muhammad Hamza,Zakaria El Ouahidi,Gabriel Azpitarte,Manling Li,Yi Fang,Shuhong Liu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LatentParse 是一个免训练框架，利用分析型 VLM 通过预定义工具解析 CLIP、DINOv3、VGGT 的潜特征，生成显式的空间关系文本报告注入上下文，从而提升多视图空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图空间推理需要从2D观测推断物体与相机的3D关系，依赖跨视图对应与场景几何，而 VLM 无法可靠地提取这些信息。预训练视觉编码器（CLIP 关联物体与图像区域、DINOv3 跨视图匹配语义、VGGT 编码多视图几何）的潜特征已包含此类信息，但以灰度特征图直接放入上下文时 VLM 无法有效利用。

Method: 免训练框架 LatentParse：由一个 analyst VLM 使用预定义工具探测三种编码器特征——定位物体、跨视图跟踪、测量视图间运动；再将结果蒸馏为简洁文本报告，与图像一同输入目标 VLM 回答问题。

Result: 在 MMSI-Bench、MV-RoboBench、SPAR-Bench 上，五个 VLM 相对仅图像基线平均提升 0.7–2.4 个百分点；MMSI-Bench 上 Qwen3.8-27B 从 34.4% 提升至 39.5%，相机相关问题最大提升 19.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，潜特征一旦被解析为关系证据，即可在无需训练的情况下有效支持 VLM 的空间推理。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning across multiple views requires inferring the 3D relations between objects and the camera from 2D observations, which depends on cross-view correspondence and scene geometry that vision-language models (VLMs) do not reliably extract. Pretrained vision encoders already capture this information in their latent features, with CLIP linking objects to image regions, DINOv3 matching semantics across views, and VGGT encoding multi-view geometry. However, VLMs do not benefit from these features when we present them directly in context as grayscale feature images. We therefore propose LatentParse, a training-free framework that parses latent features into explicit spatial evidence for VLMs. In LatentParse, an analyst VLM probes the features of all three encoders with predefined tools that locate objects, track them across views, and measure motion between views. It then distills the results into a concise text report, which is provided together with the images to the VLM to answer the question. We evaluate LatentParse on MMSI-Bench, MV-RoboBench and SPAR-Bench, where it improves all five VLMs over the photo-only baseline by 0.7-2.4 percentage points on average. On MMSI-Bench, it raises Qwen3.8-27B from 34.4% to 39.5%, with the largest improvements of 19.2 percentage points on the camera-related questions. These results suggest that latent features become useful for spatial reasoning once they are parsed into relation evidence, without training. Code is available at https://github.com/Harvard-AI-and-Robotics-Lab/LatentParse.

</details>


### [384] [OmniV-Bench: Can MLLMs Comprehend Immersive Spatio-temporal Scenes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31022)
*Liu Yang,Huiyu Duan,Yixuan Gao,Sirui Liu,Lu Liu,Zhaolin Cai,Ke Gu,Xiongkuo Min,Xiaoping Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OmniV-Bench基准与OmniV-Harness智能体框架，用于评测并增强多模态大模型对全景视频（ODV）时空信息的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频（ODV）具备360°全视场与动态时空信息，是VR/AR与具身智能的重要数据形式；然而现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对ODV沉浸式时空信息的理解能力尚缺乏系统评测与研究。

Method: 构建OmniV-Bench：含4,123段多场景高质量ODV及8,923个人工标注问答对，覆盖通用、空间、时空理解三大类共11项细粒度任务；并提出OmniV-Harness智能体框架，通过视角引导的证据初始化与任务相关证据获取（自适应调用工具）增强ODV理解。

Result: 基准实验表明，ODV的时空复杂性对现有MLLM构成显著挑战；引入OmniV-Harness后，模型的沉浸式时空推理能力得到一致性提升。

Conclusion: ODV时空理解是当前MLLM的薄弱环节，OmniV-Bench提供了系统评测手段，OmniV-Harness为提升该能力提供了有效方案；两者将于论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Omnidirectional videos (ODVs) capture dynamic environments with a full $360^\circ$ field of view, providing rich spatial context and temporal dynamics for applications such as virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), and embodied intelligence. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made great progress in spatial and temporal understanding, yet their ability to comprehend the immersive 360 spatio-temporal information conveyed by ODVs remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we first introduce OmniV-Bench, a Benchmark designed to comprehensively evaluate the capabilities of MLLMs on spatio-temporal Omnidirectional Video understanding. OmniV-Bench contains 4,123 high-quality ODVs collected from diverse scenes, along with 8,923 human-annotated question-answer pairs spanning 11 fine-grained tasks across three categories, i.e., general, spatial, and spatio-temporal understanding. Extensive benchmark experiments reveal that the spatio-temporal complexity of ODVs poses significant challenges for current MLLMs. To address these limitations, we further propose OmniV-Harness, an agentic harness that enhances ODV understanding through viewpoint-guided evidence initialization and task-relevant evidence acquisition with adaptive tool use. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that OmniV-Harness consistently improves immersive spatio-temporal reasoning ability of MLLMs. Both OmniV-Bench and OmniV-Harness will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [385] [WildAnySplat: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31023)
*Richard Shaw,Arthur Moreau,Athanasios Papaioannou,Thomas Tanay,Zhensong Zhang,Eduardo Pérez-Pellitero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildAnySplat：面向野外多视图图像的前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，通过师生蒸馏与双边网格实现瞬态鲁棒、外观一致的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外多视图图像常因瞬态遮挡、光照变化和相机ISP存在多视不一致性，现有前馈重建方法在此条件下质量显著下降，亟需一种既保持前馈快速推理又能抵抗上述干扰的重建方案。

Method: 提出师生框架：教师网络处理干净图像，学生网络处理对应野外输入，教师提供干净监督以引导学生学习一致的规范表示。数据上采用混合策略，结合真实配对采集、合成增强与优化式重建生成的伪干净标签以弥合域差。架构上使用transformer并配DPT头分别预测相机位姿、深度与高斯；额外预测逐视图瞬态掩码以重加权光度监督、抑制不可靠高斯；并引入双边网格模块建模逐图像颜色变换，恢复规范辐射表示。

Result: 在具有挑战性的野外基准上，WildAnySplat在瞬态遮挡与外观变化下的鲁棒性优于现有方法，同时保留前馈高斯泼溅的快速推理特性。

Conclusion: 师生蒸馏结合瞬态掩码与双边网格外观建模，可有效将干净监督迁移至野外输入，使前馈3DGS在真实场景中实现鲁棒且高效的新视角合成。

Abstract: We present WildAnySplat, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that enables robust novel-view synthesis from in-the-wild multi-view images. Unlike controlled captures, real-world images often contain multi-view inconsistencies caused by transient occluders, illumination changes, and camera ISP, which degrade the quality of existing feed-forward reconstruction methods.
  To address this challenge, we introduce a student-teacher framework that learns transient-robust and appearance-consistent 3D representations. A teacher network operates on clean images, while a student processes the corresponding in-the-wild inputs. The teacher provides clean supervision to guide the student toward a consistent canonical representation. Since paired clean and in-the-wild data are scarce, we use a hybrid data strategy, combining real paired captures, synthetic augmentations, and pseudo-clean labels generated from optimization-based reconstructions, bridging the domain gap between synthetic and real-world data.
  We adopt a transformer architecture with dedicated DPT heads for camera pose, depth, and Gaussian prediction. We additionally predict per-view transient masks to reweight photometric supervision and suppress unreliable Gaussians. To account for cross-view appearance variation, we incorporate a bilateral grid module that models per-image color transformations and enables recovery of a canonical radiance representation.
  Experiments on challenging in-the-wild benchmarks show that WildAnySplat improves robustness to transient occlusions and appearance variation while retaining the fast inference of feed-forward Gaussian Splatting.

</details>


### [386] [Token Pruning Needs a Protected Sink Token: Coherent Spatial Aggregation in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31025)
*Julian Wyatt,James Willoughby,Allison Clement,Irina Voiculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Monte Carlo Token Pruning (MCTP)，在前向传播中显式移除token以估计其对预测的影响，用于刻画预训练ViT对token剪枝的响应随深度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作只比较不同的token缩减算法，而未研究模型本身如何响应剪枝、以及这种响应如何随模型深度变化。

Method: MCTP通过蒙特卡洛方式在推理时显式移除token，度量每个token对预测结果的影响，从而得到各深度下的token重要性排序，并引入“一致性聚合(Coherent Aggregation)”指标，应用于DINOv3与SigLIP 2等近期预训练模型。

Result: DINOv3等多数模型在各深度保持相近的token重要性排序（高一致性），而SigLIP 2不具备；该一致性依赖高范数“sink”token得以保留：有prefix的模型由设计保留prefix token，而无prefix的SigLIP 2在patch token上形成sink，易被剪枝移除。移除DINOv3的prefix token会使其预测与一致性崩溃；对SigLIP 2仅保护5个token，可将目标框外的token影响降低最多62%。

Conclusion: MCTP提供了一种事后评估预训练ViT对剪枝响应的方法，并表明token缩减方法应保留“sink”token。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are a predominant architecture for computer vision, with token reduction emerging as a popular paradigm to improve their efficiency. Prior work compares reduction algorithms, but not how models respond to them, nor how their response changes with model depth. We propose Monte Carlo Token Pruning (MCTP), which estimates each token's impact on the prediction when tokens are explicitly removed during the forward pass. Applying MCTP to recent pretrained models, we observe that most, such as DINOv3, maintain a similar ranking of token importance at every depth (high Coherent Aggregation), while SigLIP 2 does not. This coherence requires predictions to survive pruning, which depends on keeping high-norm "sink" tokens: prefix tokens are kept by design, while prefix-free SigLIP 2 forms them on patch tokens, which pruning may remove. We show that in removing DINOv3's prefix tokens, its predictions and coherence collapse, while protecting only 5 tokens for SigLIP 2 cuts the token impact outside the object's bounding box by up to 62%. Beyond this, MCTP offers a post-hoc method to evaluate how a pretrained ViT responds to pruning, and suggests that token reduction methods should keep "sinks".

</details>


### [387] [Déjà VAE: When Do Autoencoder Traces Reveal Generated Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31036)
*Saugat Pandey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 分析潜空间扩散模型输出的"似曾相识"(déjà vu)重建痕迹何时能揭示生成图像，并揭示基准测试中匹配协议等缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型输出已经过一次自编码器，第二次往返几乎不改变图像，这一性质支撑了基于重建的生成图像检测器，但此前缺乏对其何时有效何时失效的系统分析。

Method: 冻结的视觉基础模型免训练读取重建痕迹（在中间层最强）；识别痕迹归属于生成器精确解码器；仅用真实照片及其自编码器副本训练检测器。

Result: 在Synthbuster上，SD 1.3/1.4/2/XL的精确解码器在5%虚警率下捕获其生成器94-100%图像，而近似同版本解码器仅7-42%；仅用真实照片训练的检测器在GenImage上与16.2万生成图训练的检测器相当(+2.3点，CI[0.9,3.7])并可融合互补；一次缩放+JPEG重存或轻度模糊使其下降19-27点。

Conclusion: 重建痕迹检测有效但脆弱；Chameleon基准可由图像宽度直接解出；Synthbuster中真实与生成图像尺寸匹配方式可将检测器比较结果反转(+4.4 vs -9.7点)。

Abstract: A latent diffusion model's output has already passed through its autoencoder, so a second round trip changes it very little. This "déjà vu" underlies reconstruction-based detectors of generated images; we analyse when it reveals a generated image and when it does not. Read by a frozen vision foundation model without training, the trace is strongest in intermediate layers, and, unlike pixel-level reconstruction error, it treats blur as a sign of a real image. It belongs to the generator's exact decoder: on Synthbuster, the exact decoders of Stable Diffusion 1.3, 1.4, 2 and XL are the strongest of eight autoencoders and catch 94-100% of their generators' images at a 5% false-alarm rate (SDXL's version identified post hoc), while near-identical versions catch 7-42%. A detector trained only on real photographs and their autoencoder copies matches one trained on 162k generated images on GenImage (+2.3 points, 95% CI [0.9, 3.7]) and complements it in fusion, but one resize with JPEG re-save, or a mild blur, costs it 19-27 points. Two lessons concern benchmarks. The in-the-wild benchmark Chameleon can be solved from image width alone. And on Synthbuster, how the real photographs are matched to the generated images' size decides whether our detector leads or trails (+4.4 or -9.7 points): the matching protocol alone can reverse a detector comparison. Code and per-image scores: https://github.com/iamsaugatpandey/deja-vae

</details>


### [388] [Distill the Correction, Not the Rewrite: Hindsight Twins for Self-Improving Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31048)
*Jiaze Li,Jingyang Chen,Wenhao Zhao,Haoran Xu,Zhenlong Yuan,Dianmo Sheng,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Hindsight Twins (HinT)：用同一修订模板分别填入"预期修正"与"观测错误"构造双教师，仅蒸馏二者速度差并在槽位外对齐 token 状态，从而在保持基模型水平的评审偏置的同时获得更强对齐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法依赖视觉-语言评审器改写后的提示训练自教师，但对齐提升有限，且会把评审器对光照、场景等非目标细节的"无意添加"一并吸收。原因有二：(1) 提示本身已指明期望修正，有用修订必须超越失败片段、重写整段场景，新增细节淹没修正；(2) 评审器虽将错误定位到单一片段，自教师却以整段改写为条件，使目标修正与无意添加相互纠缠。

Method: HinT 将评审器的修订模板分别填充"预期修正"与"观测错误"生成一对孪生条件，锚定在用户原始提示上，只蒸馏两者之间的速度差（velocity difference），以抵消模板中与修正无关的内容。考虑到文本编码器会把单个槽位的编辑扩散到所有其他 token，HinT 进一步在槽位之外对齐孪生分支的 token 状态。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image-2512、SD3.5-M 与 FLUX.2-klein 三个模型上，HinT 将评审器引入的偏置率保持在基模型水平，同时对 Aligning 增益匹配或超过既有自蒸馏方法；在十二轮自训练中持续改进，而对比式基线出现衰减。

Conclusion: 通过"差分蒸馏 + 槽位外状态对齐"的双重设计，HinT 解耦了目标修正与评审器的无意添加，实现低偏置、可持续累积的自改进对齐。

Abstract: Text-to-image models can learn from their own mistakes by distilling a self-teacher conditioned on a vision-language critic's revised prompt. However, such self-teachers barely improve alignment, and both they and their hindsight-contrast variants absorb the critic's unintended additions to the scene, such as lighting or scenery. We attribute this to two factors: (1) the prompt already specifies the desired fix, so a useful revision must go beyond the failed span, often rewriting the entire scene and adding details that overshadow the fix; (2) although the critic localizes the error to a single span, self-teachers condition on the rewrite, entangling the intended fix with unintended additions. Motivated by these insights, we propose Hindsight Twins (HinT), which fills the critic's revision template once with the intended fix and once with the observed error and distills only their velocity difference, anchored at the user's prompt. Because text encoders spread the edit of a single slot to all other tokens, HinT further aligns the twins' token states outside the slot. Across Qwen-Image-2512, SD3.5-M, and FLUX.2-klein, HinT keeps the rate of critic-induced bias at the base-model level while matching or exceeding the alignment gains of prior self-distillation, and it keeps improving over twelve rounds of self-training where contrastive baselines decay.

</details>


### [389] [UMAMI: A Multimodal Benchmark Dataset for Evidence-Grounded Dietary Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31068)
*Safal Thapaliya,Jiatan Huang,Debapriya Chatterjee,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出UMAMI多模态数据集与基准，将独立采集的食物图像、营养档案与受访者健康/摄入记录关联，并设计三项诊断任务，揭示多模态系统在食物记录选择与营养规则应用上的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭图像识别食物无法获得营养信息，视觉相似食物的营养档案可能不同，选错食物记录会导致决策错误；且满足营养约束并不代表食物与个人摄入相关。评估这些挑战需要将食物图像、营养记录与个体摄入历史相连接。

Method: 构建UMAMI（Unified Multimodal Assessment of Meals and Intake）数据集与基准，链接独立采集的食物图像、营养档案及受访者健康与摄入记录；据此设计三项诊断任务：视觉营养问答、个性化食物推荐、条件感知的食物替换；并采用配对实例与输入控制考察视觉身份和受访者语境对决策的影响。

Result: 实验发现系统在食物记录选择与营养规则应用两环节均存在错误；更高的规则遵从度并不必然提升与后续记录摄入的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 需评估多模态系统如何选择并使用结构化证据来支持饮食决策；数据集、代码与看板已公开。

Abstract: Dietary decisions require identifying a food's nutrient content and considering individual dietary needs, but recognizing food from an image alone does not provide this information. Visually similar foods can have different nutrient profiles, so selecting the wrong food record can lead to incorrect decisions even when nutrient rules are applied correctly. Furthermore, satisfying nutrient constraints does not necessarily establish a food's relevance to a person's intake. Evaluating these challenges together requires connecting food images, nutrient records, and individual intake histories. To support this evaluation, we introduce UMAMI (Unified Multimodal Assessment of Meals and Intake), a multimodal dataset and benchmark linking independently collected food images with nutrient profiles and respondent health and intake records. Using this dataset, we construct three diagnostic tasks covering visual nutrient question answering, personalized food recommendation, and condition-aware food substitution. Paired instances and input controls examine how visual identity and respondent context affect decisions. Our experiments identify errors in both food-record selection and nutrient-rule application, while showing that higher rule compliance does not necessarily improve alignment with subsequent recorded intake. These findings highlight the need to evaluate how multimodal systems select and use structured evidence for dietary decisions. The dataset, code, and dashboard are available at https://umami-benchmark.github.io.

</details>


### [390] [Leakage-Safe Auditing of Attribution-Driven Data Curation in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31096)
*Malay Phadke,Akshay Gokhale*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将“归因驱动的数据筛选”拆解为可分别检验的三个命题（异常检测、危害估计、干预效用），并在3D CTA动脉瘤分类器上以患者不重叠、保留标识符的审计方式检验第一与第三命题；发现输入数据损坏（232个全零数组）虽可被检出，但删除170个损坏训练阳性并不改善甚至略降低泛化（AUC 0.9617 vs 0.9652），说明输入损坏不必然意味着删除有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归因图（如GradCAM++、Integrated Gradients）常被用来发现“异常样本”进而据此清洗医疗影像训练集，但“异常”到“有害”再到“删除有益”之间存在未被验证的逻辑跳跃；同时历史研究中的子群划分可能因记录与文件按位置拼接而存在身份错配与数据泄漏问题，因此需要显式检验这些环节。

Method: 将归因驱动数据治理形式化为三个可独立检验的论断；对3D卷积CTA动脉瘤候选分类器进行患者不重叠、保留标识符的审计；在1,201个阳性patch中发现232个严格全零数组（含170个训练阳性），并推翻一个历史上基于无标识记录按位置关联的321-patch“注意力失效”子群；在测试集上比较GradCAM++与Integrated Gradients的空间集中性及两者排序一致性；以5个配对训练种子比较删除170个损坏训练阳性与全量训练的AUC差异，并做剔除34个全零测试阳性、以及预算匹配的随机删除对照实验。

Result: 测试集上GradCAM++的空间集中性对所有候选类别均位于中心区域，反转了历史模式；GradCAM++与Integrated Gradients对阳性样本呈负的秩相关一致性，限制了解剖解释的方法独立性。删除170个损坏训练阳性后AUC为0.9617±0.0044，全量训练为0.9652±0.0039（差值−0.0035，95% CI −0.0082至0.0007）。剔除34个全零测试阳性反而扩大而非缩小该差距，反驳“空白记忆”解释；预算匹配的随机删除对照表明差异并非单纯来自训练集规模减小。

Conclusion: 输入损坏并不必然意味着删除能改善泛化；归因驱动的医疗影像数据治理应显式包含泄漏控制、样本身份保持与干预效用检验，而非仅凭异常检测结果就修改训练集。

Abstract: When can an attribution-derived anomaly justify changing a medical-imaging training set? We formulate attribution-driven curation as three separately testable claims: anomaly detection, harmfulness estimation, and intervention utility. We test the first and third using a patient-disjoint, identifier-preserving audit of a 3D convolutional classifier for computed tomography angiography aneurysm candidates. We identify 232 exactly zero-valued arrays among 1,201 positive patches, including 170 training positives, and invalidate a historical 321-patch attention-failure subgroup whose identifier-free records were joined to files by position. On the held-out test set, GradCAM++ spatial concentration is central for every candidate class, reversing the historical pattern. GradCAM++ and Integrated Gradients show negative rank-order agreement for positives, limiting method-independent anatomical interpretation. Across five paired training seeds, removing the 170 corrupted training positives yields AUC 0.9617 +/- 0.0044 versus 0.9652 +/- 0.0039 for full-data training (difference -0.0035; 95% CI -0.0082 to 0.0007). Excluding 34 raw-zero test positives widens rather than closes the gap, countering a blank-memorization explanation. Budget-matched random-removal controls suggest the result is not simply an effect of reducing training size. Input corruption does not necessarily imply that deletion improves generalization. Our framework makes leakage control, sample identity, and intervention testing explicit in attribution-driven medical-imaging data curation.

</details>


### [391] [CrownFusion: 3D Dental Crown Generation Using Geometry Images and Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31128)
*Johan Ziruo Ye,Xingguang Yan,Søren Hauberg,Angel X. Chang,Hao Zhang,Peter Lempel Søndergaard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrownFusion——基于几何图像（CrownImage）表示的潜扩散模型，从周围牙列条件生成符合解剖形态的3D牙冠设计，并发布FDI 16 Crown数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统牙冠生成方法多基于点云，受限于空间分辨率，易产生过度平滑的伪影，且难以充分利用成熟的图像生成架构；同时单个病例存在多种有效设计方案，缺乏对应的评价指标。

Method: 将牙冠表面坐标编码为2D网格形式的几何图像（CrownImage），以远高于点云的空间分辨率保留精细几何细节；采用以周围牙列联合学习嵌入为条件的潜扩散模型，概率性地生成具患者特异形态变化的牙冠；并提出基于与对颌牙邻接程度（proximity）的咬合适配度量。

Result: 定量实验与含专家评价的定性研究验证了方法有效性；模型可产生解剖合理且具形态多样性的牙冠设计；同时发布包含11,513例标注口内扫描的FDI 16 Crown Dataset。

Conclusion: 几何图像表示与潜扩散建模相结合，可有效缓解3D牙冠生成的平滑伪影并支持多解设计评估，为牙科CAD生成式研究提供方法与数据基础。

Abstract: We introduce CrownFusion, a latent diffusion model to generate 3D dental crown designs from geometry images, a 2D-grid encoding of surface coordinates. Departing from traditional approaches that use point clouds, our "CrownImage" representation not only unlocks the full power of image-based generative architectures, but also preserves fine geometric detail by operating at far higher spatial resolutions than point clouds to alleviate smoothing artifacts in the 3D generation. Conditioned on jointly learned embeddings of the surrounding dentition, our probabilistic diffusion model produces anatomically plausible crowns with morphological variations tailored to individual patient cases. To accommodate multiple valid designs, we propose an occlusal fit metric based on proximity to the antagonist teeth. We evaluate our method through quantitative experiments and qualitative studies involving expert testimonials. We also release the FDI 16 Crown Dataset, comprising 11,513 annotated intra-oral scans, to support future research in dental CAD.

</details>


### [392] [SRCWDNet: Semantic-Relational Cross-Window Dual-Graph Network for Multi-Label Remote Sensing Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31135)
*Fareeha Amir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SRCWDNet，通过跨窗口注意力、双标签图模块与神经分类器链联合建模视觉上下文与标签相关性，在MLRSNet和China-MAS50k上取得98.93%/96.53%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签遥感场景分类面临空间-光谱模式复杂、极端类别不平衡以及标签间依赖建模不足等问题。

Method: SRCWDNet包含三个模块：跨窗口注意力模块（借助可学习全局token捕获局部与全局视觉上下文）；双标签图模块（自适应融合统计共现图与学习到的语义嵌入图以进行标签关系推理）；神经分类器链（顺序预测标签以利用标签间依赖）。训练采用非对称图一致性损失，评估采用五折交叉验证。

Result: 在MLRSNet与China-MAS50k上分别达到98.93%和96.53%的准确率，F1分数为0.9365和0.8723，优于CNN、Transformer及轻量级基线。

Conclusion: 联合建模视觉注意力与自适应标签相关性可显著提升多标签遥感场景分类性能，尤其在长尾类别分布下效果明显。

Abstract: Multi-label remote sensing scene classification remains a challenging task due to complex spatial-spectral patterns, extreme class imbalance, and inter-label dependencies that are often insufficiently modeled. To address these challenges, we introduce a Semantic-Relational Cross-Window Dual-Graph Network (SRCWDNet), which integrates three key modules: (1) a Cross-Window Attention module that captures local and global visual context through learnable global tokens; (2) a Dual Label-Graph module that adaptively combines statistical co-occurrence graphs and learned semantic-embedding graphs to facilitate label-relational reasoning; and (3) a Neural Classifier Chain that sequentially predicts labels to exploit inter-label dependencies. Evaluated using five-fold cross-validation and trained with an Asymmetric-Graph-Consistency loss, SRCWDNet achieves accuracies of 98.93% and 96.53%, with corresponding F1-scores of 0.9365 and 0.8723 on the MLRSNet and China-MAS50k datasets, respectively, outperforming CNN, transformer, and lightweight baselines. These findings demonstrate that jointly modeling visual attention and adaptive label correlations can substantially improve multi-label remote sensing scene classification, particularly under long-tailed class distributions.

</details>


### [393] [HoloSplat: Real-Time Token Streaming for Feed-Forward Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31140)
*Chaofan Li,Qihan Ding,Shihan Wang,Zhehao Shen,Boyu Shen,Quanyu Chen,Haoyu Wu,Lan Xu,Xin Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloSplat：面向人体自由视角视频的实时流式框架，将前馈重建器在Gaussian头之前拆分，服务端token压缩+客户端token恢复，实现50 Mbit/s下多路实时传输与轻量端解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自由视角人体视频流需逐帧重建、严格带宽预算内传输，并在轻量客户端GPU上解码。现有逐帧优化或前馈推断方法产生巨量3D Gaussian数据，超出实际流式传输上限；而激进压缩又严重损害渲染质量。

Method: 将前馈重建器在其Gaussian头之前切分，构建多码率流式编解码：服务端采用不透明度引导的token剪枝、基于PCA的降维，以及对token锚点做无损几何编码；客户端将特征重建超网络融合进Gaussian头以补偿压缩损失；并提出神经会话校准（NSC），在预热阶段用传入相机视角在线快速微调Gaussian头，零逐帧开销地解决新相机参数与不同采集条件带来的质量下降。

Result: 在4块RTX 5090上、50 Mbit/s下平均32.2 FPS；RTX 3050 Ti笔记本GPU上每帧解码为3D Gaussians耗时22.1 ms；传输数据量为未压缩float32 Gaussians的1/22.8；校准解码使PSNR提升1.050 dB。

Conclusion: 通过在Gaussian头前拆分重建器并采用端到端多码率编解码与在线会话校准，HoloSplat在严格带宽下兼顾了实时性、轻量端可解码性与渲染质量，为人体体视频流式传输提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Live free-viewpoint video streaming of humans enables immersive telepresence and interactive XR applications. However, this requires reconstructing each frame in real time, transmitting the data within strict bandwidth budgets, and decoding it on lightweight client GPUs. Existing approaches, whether relying on per-frame optimization or feed-forward inference, generate massive 3D Gaussian payloads that exceed practical streaming limits, while aggressive compression severely degrades rendering quality. In this work, we present HoloSplat, a real-time streaming framework for human-centric volumetric video. HoloSplat splits a feed-forward reconstructor before its Gaussian head, introducing a multi-rate streaming codec driven by server-side token compression and client-side token restoration. On the server side, we apply opacity-guided token pruning, PCA-based dimensionality reduction, and lossless geometry coding for token anchors. On the client side, a feature reconstruction hypernetwork is fused into the Gaussian head to recover compression-induced quality loss. Furthermore, we introduce Neural Session Calibration (NSC), which rapidly fine-tunes the Gaussian head online using incoming camera views during a warm-up phase. This resolves quality degradation from novel camera parameters and varying capture conditions with zero per-frame overhead. In our evaluations, HoloSplat averages 32.2 FPS on four RTX 5090 GPUs at 50 Mbit/s. It decodes each frame into 3D Gaussians in 22.1 ms on an RTX 3050 Ti laptop GPU, transmitting 22.8$\times$ less data than uncompressed float32 Gaussians, while calibrated decoding improves PSNR by 1.050 dB.

</details>


### [394] [Head Anything: Correspondence-Guided Joint Generation of 3D Heads from Few Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31143)
*Umut Kocasari,Bartłomiej Baranowski,Richard Shaw,Eduardo Pérez-Pellitero,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Head Anything 将预训练的单图3D生成器的原生3D隐空间改造为联合生成器，通过跨图像对应注意力适配器在少量多视角/多表情图像间共享信息，显著提升头部网格细节与跨帧一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D生成器把每张图视为独立新物体，对同一人的多张图会各自独立地"幻觉"未见区域，无法利用其他图像的信息。同时，直接在稀缺的3D头部数据上学习图像间的全局注意力十分困难。

Method: 不训练多图先验，而是把预训练的单图生成器（具备原生3D隐空间）改造成对所有图像联合去噪的生成器；引入轻量级 correspondence-attention 适配器，使每个3D token 只关注其他图像中的对应点。对应关系由将神经参数化头部模型拟合到生成器自身的单图输出获得，测试时无需真值；并在真值与拟合两种对应上训练，使适配器对拟合误差具备鲁棒性。每个网格保持与自身图像的像素对齐，并从其他图像借用未见区域。

Result: 在头部扫描数据上，Head Anything 相比最佳图像到3D生成器将头部 Chamfer 距离降低32%，跨帧不一致性降低47%，其网格还提升了人脸跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 通过在单图3D生成器的隐空间中注入基于参数化头模对应的跨图像注意力，可在数据有限条件下实现多图联合的高质量头部重建，同时兼顾像素对齐与跨视角一致性，并服务于下游的人脸跟踪任务。

Abstract: Large image-to-3D generators recover detailed heads from a single photograph but treat every image as a new object. For several images of one person, they hallucinate unseen regions independently without using information from the other images. We present Head Anything, which generates detailed head meshes from a few images with changing expressions and viewpoints. Instead of training a multi-image prior, we turn a pretrained single-image generator with a native 3D latent space into a joint generator that denoises all images together. Since global attention between images is hard to learn from scarce 3D head data, lightweight correspondence-attention adapters let every 3D token attend only to its corresponding points in the other images. We obtain correspondences by fitting a neural parametric head model to the generator's own single-image outputs, requiring no ground truth at test time. Training on both ground-truth and fitted correspondences makes the adapters robust to fitting errors. Each mesh stays pixel-aligned with its own image and borrows the unseen regions from all others. On head scans, Head Anything lowers the head Chamfer distance of the best image-to-3D generator by 32% and its cross-frame inconsistency by 47%, and its meshes improve face tracking. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [395] [Tokens as Locality-Preserving Anchors: Scalable Novel View Synthesis with Target-Adaptive Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31156)
*Sagnik Ghosh,Aswin C. Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级路由器预测源token的重要性，使Transformer视图合成解码器只关注固定预算的top-ranked token，渲染成本不再随源图像数量增长，24张源图下比LagerNVS快6.4倍，PSNR仅低0.68 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的视图合成无需显式3D表示即可复现细节与视角依赖效应，但解码器对每帧都需关注全部源token，渲染开销随源图像数量线性增长。光栅化可通过剔除视锥外图元来规避此问题，而源token缺乏显式位置与范围，无法以同样方式剔除。

Method: 核心观察是源token实为空间锚点，各自编码场景局部区域，因此任意视角下解码器注意力仅集中于少数token。据此设计轻量路由器，以源token与目标视角为输入预测token重要性排名；解码器仅关注固定预算的top-ranked token。路由器以解码器注意力为监督信号，并用非最大抑制（NMS）降低冗余token的排名。

Result: 在24张源图像、约两个视角token量的设置下，相比LagerNVS渲染速度提升6.4倍，PSNR仅下降0.68 dB。单次排名适用于任意预算，可在渲染时动态调整预算。

Conclusion: 通过将源token视为可排序的空间锚点并以注意力监督学习路由器，实现了与源图像数量解耦的解码开销，在精度小幅损失下获得显著加速，且支持推理时灵活切换计算预算。

Abstract: Transformer-based view synthesis models render each target viewpoint from the source images, without an explicit 3D representation, which lets them reproduce fine detail and view-dependent effects. However, their decoder attends to every source token for every frame, so rendering slows with each source image. Rasterization avoids this by culling primitives that the viewpoint does not see, but source tokens cannot be culled this way, as they have no explicit position or extent. Our key insight is that source tokens are nonetheless spatial anchors: each encodes a local region of the scene, so the decoder's attention for any viewpoint concentrates on a few of them. A lightweight router predicts these tokens from the source tokens and the viewpoint, and the decoder attends only to a fixed budget of top-ranked tokens, so its cost no longer depends on the number of source tokens. We supervise the router with the decoder's attention, using non-maximum suppression to demote redundant tokens. With 24 source images and two views' worth of tokens, our model renders 6.4 times faster than LagerNVS, at a PSNR only 0.68 dB lower. One ranking serves every budget, so the budget can change at render time.

</details>


### [396] [SPROUT: Predicting Objective-Grounded Prompt Utility for Efficient Text-to-Image Preference Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31163)
*Kangping Hu,Akshay Mehra,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出提示词选择框架 SPROUT，用纯文本模型预测目标锚定效用（DGPO 梯度的范数），通过随机接受规则构建批次，减少偏好微调中生成候选图像的计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型偏好微调的计算主要花在生成候选图像上，但训练提示词通常均匀随机采样，导致大量生成的图像对模型对齐提供的信息量过低，浪费算力。因此需要研究每次迭代中如何挑选最有用的提示词。

Method: 将提示词选择分解为效用分数、效用估计器和批次选择三部分。提出 SPROUT：用一个纯文本模型对提示词池中所有提示词（含未见过的）预测效用分数，该模型在每次迭代后用迄今观测到的所有效用重新拟合；再用随机接受规则把预测转为批次，从而不额外生成训练之外图像。对 DGPO，目标锚定效用定义为目标函数在该提示词图像组上产生的梯度范数。

Result: 在 GenEval 上用 DGPO 微调 Stable Diffusion 1.5，SPROUT 约用 44% 的迭代次数达到均匀采样的最终得分，且学习曲线下面积优于均匀采样和基于方差的提示采样器 CGPO。效率提升在四个未参与训练的留出奖励模型下依然成立，方法排序在 DPG-Bench 和 T2I-CompBench++ 上同样保持。

Conclusion: 通过预测目标锚定效用来选择提示词，可在不增加图像生成开销的前提下显著提升偏好微调的计算效率，且效果对奖励模型和评测基准具有泛化性。

Abstract: Preference tuning of text-to-image diffusion models spends most of its compute generating candidate images, yet training prompts are typically sampled uniformly at random, wasting compute generating images that provide too little signal for model alignment. To best utilize the available compute, we study prompt selection, where at each iteration we identify the most useful prompts for preference tuning. We separate prompt selection into a utility score, an estimator of that utility score, and batch selection. Within this framework, we propose Selection by PRedicting Objective-grounded UTility (SPROUT). The utility score is predicted on every prompt in the pool, including unseen prompts, by a text-only model, refit after every iteration on all utilities observed so far. A randomized acceptance rule then turns these predictions into a batch, so no images are generated beyond those used for training. For direct group preference optimization (DGPO), the objective-grounded utility is the norm of the gradient that the objective produces from a prompt's group of images. Fine-tuning Stable Diffusion 1.5 with DGPO on GenEval, SPROUT matches the final score of uniform sampling in about 44% of the iterations and improves the area under the learning curve with respect to both uniform sampling and CGPO, a recent variance-based prompt sampler. The efficiency gains hold under four held-out reward models never used in training, and the ranking of methods carries over to DPG-Bench and T2I-CompBench++. Our code is available at https://github.com/ADDAPT-ML/SPROUT.

</details>


### [397] [Analyzing and Improving FlowMap Distillation of Autoregressive Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31164)
*Yong-Hyun Park,Guande He,Joon Hyeok Kim,Hansheng Chen,Kaifan Yu,Chaoqi Luo,Xun Huang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析独立AR FlowMap蒸馏的瓶颈，提出反漂移引导与运动感知引导两种互补机制，使1.3B教师蒸馏的4步学生达到14B教师DMD学生水平并保持多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FlowMap蒸馏可直接学习教师去噪轨迹，是少步生成的自然路径；但在自回归视频生成中性能不及DMD，而DMD牺牲样本多样性且依赖大型双向教师。作者希望弄清独立AR FlowMap蒸馏受限的原因并加以弥补。

Method: 提出两种引导机制：(1) 反漂移引导——训练辅助模型模仿学生漂移，作为负向引导分支，帮助AR教师纠正漂移的学生 rollout；(2) 运动感知引导——定位运动 patch，对其选择性施加 skip-layer 引导，降低FlowMap学生学习运动信息的难度。方法可接入π-Flow、MeanFlow与一致性蒸馏。

Result: 在1.3B AR教师上应用于π-Flow、MeanFlow与一致性蒸馏，均提升生成质量并减少30秒长rollout中的漂移；4步π-Flow学生在VBench上与14B教师蒸馏的DMD学生相当，同时保留AR教师的样本多样性。

Conclusion: 纯FlowMap蒸馏可有效用于少步自回归视频生成，无需依赖DMD和大型双向教师，兼顾质量与多样性。

Abstract: FlowMap distillation offers a natural route to few-step generative modeling by directly learning a teacher's denoising trajectories. Yet for autoregressive (AR) video generation, existing few-step models still rely on distribution matching distillation (DMD) to remain competitive, sacrificing sample diversity and requiring a large bidirectional teacher. In this work, we analyze what limits standalone AR FlowMap distillation and introduce two complementary guidance mechanisms to close this gap: (1) anti-drifting guidance, which helps the AR teacher correct drifted student rollouts by training an auxiliary model to mimic student drift and using it as a negative guidance branch; and (2) motion-aware guidance, which makes motion easier for a FlowMap student to learn by locating motion patches and selectively applying skip-layer guidance. Empirically, our approach applies to $π$-Flow, MeanFlow, and consistency distillation with 1.3B AR teachers, improving generation quality and reducing drift over 30 s rollouts. Notably, our 4-step $π$-Flow students match DMD students distilled from 14B teachers on VBench while preserving the sample diversity of their AR teachers, demonstrating that pure FlowMap distillation is effective for few-step autoregressive video generation.

</details>


### [398] [On-Policy Codec Distillation for Efficient All-in-One Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31184)
*Boyang Wang,Kevin Blackburn-Matzen,Oindrila Saha,Matheus Gadelha,Zezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CodecOPSD，用视频编解码器的冗余检测思路剪枝条件token，并以on-policy自蒸馏训练DiT适配器，剪掉约66%条件token而几乎无损，去噪提速2倍、KV缓存缩小至多73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉生成模型将身份、涂鸦、深度图、运动视频等所有条件资源token化并拼接到输入序列，导致条件token主导计算开销；而这些token大多含冗余的时空信息，需要在不损失质量的前提下削减其计算与显存成本。

Method: 1) 基于编解码器的token剪枝：借鉴经典视频编解码器"识别真正新增信息"的机制，丢弃冗余条件token；2) On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）：训练一个DiT adapter，使其在自身策略分布下学习编解码器剪枝模式，避免质量退化；二者合称CodecOPSD。

Result: 在自研视频扩散模型上扩展到16种条件模态，平均剪除66%条件token且质量几乎不降；去噪速度提升2倍，KV cache模式下缓存显存最多减少73%。在其它SOTA预训练图像/视频生成模型上同样有效，且条件越多、帧越长、分辨率越高，收益越大。

Conclusion: 将经典视频编解码器的冗余建模与on-policy自蒸馏结合，可高效剪枝多模态条件token，在保持生成质量的同时显著加速去噪并降低显存，具备良好的可扩展性与跨模型通用性。

Abstract: Modern visual generative models operate on tokens, and every visual asset they receive, whether an identity, a scribble, a depth map, or a motion video, is tokenized and appended to the input token sequence. Consequently, conditioning quickly dominates the computation cost. Yet most of these tokens carry redundant spatiotemporal information. Our key insight is that identifying what is genuinely new is exactly the problem that classical video codecs have long solved. Thus, we first introduce a codec-based token pruning method that discards redundant tokens. Then, we propose an on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) strategy to train a DiT adapter capable of learning the codec pruning pattern well without quality degradation. Together, they form On-Policy Codec Distillation (CodecOPSD). We scale CodecOPSD to 16 condition modalities on our own video diffusion model, where it prunes 66% of the condition tokens on average with almost no quality drop. Therefore, denoising becomes 2x faster, and the cache memory shrinks by up to 73% in KV cache mode. CodecOPSD also extends to other state-of-the-art pretrained image and video generative models, where its benefit grows further with more conditions, longer frames, and higher resolution.

</details>


### [399] [WorldLatent: A Unified 4D Latent Space for World Perception and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31209)
*Jingxi Chen,Yujia Chen,Cornelia Fermuller,Yiannis Aloimonos,Wen-Sheng Chu,Du Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldLatent，将统一的 4D 潜空间作为世界感知与生成的通用表征协议，用多模态 VAE 压缩并融合 4D 外观、几何与动态，使感知与生成作为同一潜空间上的互补去噪过程，在单一训练循环中由单一模型同时掌握感知、生成与编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借鉴 GPT 以共享 token 表征统一理解与生成任务的成功范式，论文指出 4D 时空视觉缺乏共同的表征协议：深度、相机等感知任务与生成任务要么由彼此分离的单向流水线处理，要么由未对齐的单模态空间拼接而成，导致模型碎片化、无法共享优化。

Method: 提出 WorldLatent 框架，设计多模态 VAE 架构，将复杂的 4D 视觉外观、几何与动态压缩并融合为单一结构化潜表征；在该形式化下，感知与生成被建模为同一潜空间上的互补去噪过程，从而用单一生成模型、单一训练循环同时学习两类能力，而非依赖任务专用模型或堆叠的单模态表征。

Result: 在代表性 4D 基准上验证：该融合潜协议在单一模型下实现了专家级零样本 4D 感知、连贯的 4D 生成以及可控编辑。

Conclusion: 研究表明多样化的 4D 任务可在共享表征协议下无缝统一，为通用 4D 空间智能提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Generative Pre-Training (GPT) represents a profound milestone in natural language processing, serving as the foundation for most of today's state-of-the-art large language models. By casting diverse understanding and generation problems into a shared token representation, GPT enables a single architecture and unified optimization loop to master heterogeneous tasks across massive corpora, giving rise to powerful foundation models for natural language. In contrast, 4D spatiotemporal vision lacks a common representational protocol: perception (such as depth and camera estimation) and generation are either handled by separate one-way pipelines or bolted together from unaligned single-modality spaces. In this work, we introduce WorldLatent, a framework that formulates a unified 4D latent space as a common protocol for both world perception and generation. Rather than relying on fragmented, task-specific models or stacked single-modality representations, our framework proposes a multimodal VAE architecture that compresses and fuses complex 4D visual appearance, geometry, and dynamics into a single structured latent representation. Within this formulation, perception and generation operate as complementary denoising processes over the same latent space, enabling one generative model to learn both capabilities simultaneously within a single training loop. Experiments across representative 4D benchmarks verify that our fused latent protocol achieves specialist-level zero-shot 4D perception, coherent 4D generation, and controllable editing from a single model. By demonstrating that diverse 4D tasks can be seamlessly unified under a shared representational protocol, our work provides a scalable foundation for general-purpose 4D spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [400] [LeJEPA Features Are Not Isotropic, and Should Not Be](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31216)
*Richeek Das,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出LeJEPA的SIGReg正则项实际上无法实现各向同性高斯特征的目标，且各向同性高斯目标本身过严（与对齐项冲突），据此提出仅约束模长与方向边缘分布、不强制独立性的PolarReg正则项，在ImageNet-1k上以4.7倍更少epoch达到LeJEPA及其变体精度，并提升最终精度1.8个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeJEPA用SIGReg将特征正则化为各向同性高斯分布，但作者怀疑该正则项在实践中难以达成其目标；同时，LeJEPA的对齐项会把特征模长与方向耦合，而各向同性高斯要求模长与方向独立，二者存在冲突，实际特征并非各向同性。因此需要重新审视正则项的有效性以及各向同性高斯目标本身的合理性。

Method: 1）设计改进版正则项以更好地逼近原目标；2）分析对齐项与SIGReg的冲突，论证自监督编码器不应具有各向同性高斯特征；3）将各向同性高斯正则化重新表述为只约束模长与方向的边缘分布、不强制二者的独立性，得到简洁的PolarReg正则项，并对数据集、批大小和嵌入维度具有超参数鲁棒性。

Result: 改进正则项带来小幅性能提升，例如ImageNet-100上以1.3倍更少epoch达到SIGReg的最终精度。PolarReg在ImageNet-1k上以4.7倍更少epoch匹配LeJEPA及其变体的精度，并将最终精度提升1.8个百分点。

Conclusion: LeJEPA存在两方面问题：正则项偏弱，且各向同性高斯目标过于严格。放弃模长—方向独立性约束、只匹配边缘分布的PolarReg是更可优化、更稳健且更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The SIGReg term in LeJEPA regularizes features to have an isotropic Gaussian distribution. Our first result is to show that this regularizer, in practice, cannot achieve its goal. We design a modified regularizer to enforce the objective better. This leads to slightly improved performance. For example, on ImageNet-100, our modified regularizer reaches the final accuracy of SIGReg in 1.3$\times$ fewer epochs. Our second result argues that self-supervised encoders should not have isotropic Gaussian features. We show that the alignment term in LeJEPA conflicts with SIGReg, because it couples the magnitude of the features with their directions. In contrast, isotropic Gaussian features would entail independent magnitude and directions. Features obtained with LeJEPA are, in fact, not isotropic. So LeJEPA has two issues. Its regularizer is weak, and the isotropic Gaussian target is too strict. Our third result is about reformulating the isotropic Gaussian regularizer to enforce only the marginal distributions of the magnitude and direction, without enforcing independence. This leads to a simple regularization term, PolarReg, that can be optimized reliably across many different datasets, batch sizes and embedding dimensions without any hyper-parameter changes. On ImageNet-1k, our technique matches the accuracy of LeJEPA and its variants in 4.7$\times$ fewer epochs. It improves the final accuracy by 1.8 points.

</details>


### [401] [CTLink: A Text-Prompted Segmentation Model for Linking Radiology Report Findings to 3D Chest CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31222)
*Mohammed Baharoon,Sung Eun Kim,Mahmoud Hussain Alabbad,Lucas Bijnens,Ali Piracha,Reem Alwasiah,Talal Yahyan,Abdulrahman Alamoudi,Mashael Alshehri,Rayan Alsager,Hannah Hancock,Anna M. Hunkemöller,Peter Youssef,Mona A. Alhammad,Afnan Aljumayan,Brecht Van Berkel,Eric Bijnens,Saud Alhussain,Maarten Steyvers,Rolf Symons,Bryan Eelen,Ayed Alaklubi,Maha Buraykan Alshammari,Amirah Fahad Alshammeri,Tomas Gilvydis,Ibrahim AlTurki,Monerah Almohideb,Ali Alburkani,Shahad Alhomaiani,Fatima Jabali,Abdulrhman Aljouie,Daesung Kim,Junha Im,Michael Moritz,Hassan R. Alomaish,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CTLink：面向胸CT报告的文本提示3D分割模型，实现报告所见病灶的像素级定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告仅以文本描述所见，未在影像上标注，读者无法直接看到所报所见对应哪一异常区域，存在文本-图像对应鸿沟。

Method: 文本提示驱动的3D分割模型；训练于4123例平扫胸部CT、9547个已分割报告所见，含新标注数据集ReXGroundingCTv2（1134例、1895个所见，且每个所见的全部实例均被分割）；与VoxTell对比，并在MICCAI 2026 ReXGrounding挑战赛中以7模型集成参赛。

Result: 200例验证集上命中率0.816、实例F1 0.456、平均Dice 0.400，显著优于VoxTell的0.533/0.175/0.234（P<.001）；挑战赛测试集上7模型集成取得48支队伍中最高的命中率0.782与实例F1 0.386；50例由两位独立放射科医师二次分割的测试扫描上，模型与医师间一致性相当或更优。

Conclusion: CTLink可将报告文本所述所见映射为3D分割，性能优于现有公开模型并达到接近人工标注的一致性；弥散性非局灶所见仍是主要局限（其范围在医师间本身标注不一致）。模型权重与数据集均已公开。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings in text only, without visually marking them on the scan, so readers cannot directly see which abnormality each reported finding refers to. We introduce CTLink, a text-prompted 3D segmentation model that segments findings described in a chest CT report. CTLink was trained on 4123 noncontrast chest CT scans with 9547 segmented report findings, including ReXGroundingCTv2, a newly annotated dataset of 1134 scans and 1895 findings with every instance of each finding segmented. On the 200 ReXGroundingCT validation scans, CTLink achieved a hit rate of 0.816, an instance F1 score of 0.456, and a mean Dice score of 0.400, compared with 0.533, 0.175, and 0.234 for VoxTell, a publicly available text-prompted segmentation model (P < .001 for all). On the test set of the MICCAI 2026 ReXGrounding challenge, an ensemble of seven CTLink models achieved the highest hit rate (0.782) and instance F1 score (0.386) of all 48 participating teams. On 50 test scans segmented twice by independent radiologists, CTLink matched or exceeded inter-rater agreement. Diffuse non-focal findings remained the main limitation, partly because their extent is outlined inconsistently even between radiologists. The CTLink model weights are available at https://huggingface.co/rajpurkarlab/CTLink, and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/rajpurkarlab/ReXGroundingCT.

</details>


### [402] [Self-Supervised Dataset Distillation via Mutual Information Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31225)
*Mohammad Hossein Moslemi,Mahdi Beitollahi,Zhimin Mei,Jiale Cai,Bissan Ghaddar,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIPA：将合成图像视为可学习原型，用冻结编码器按特征相似度分配真实样本，通过最大化同一真实图像不同增强视图间交叉分配互信息（并避免塌缩到少数原型）来蒸馏保留增强不变信息的自监督数据，表示质量优于现有方法且蒸馏时间与存储更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集蒸馏（DD）在监督学习中已较成功，但在自监督学习（SSL）中研究不足。SSL 下蒸馏数据须保留学习"跨增强一致表示"所需的信息，现有方法未针对这一增强不变性目标进行优化。

Method: 提出 MIPA（互信息原型对齐）：把合成图像当作可学习原型；用冻结编码器依据特征相似度将真实样本分配到原型；要求同一真实图像的多个增强视图产生稳定的原型分配，以最大化跨视图分配的互信息为目标，并显式抑制平凡解（所有样本塌缩到少数原型）。

Result: 在图像分类基准上，MIPA 生成的合成数据训练出的表示质量高于现有蒸馏方法，同时降低蒸馏过程中的时间与存储开销。

Conclusion: 以"跨增强视图的原型分配互信息"作为 SSL 数据集蒸馏的优化目标是一种高效且有效的途径，可在提升表示质量的同时降低计算与存储成本。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) aims to compress a large dataset into a small synthetic one that remains effective for training neural networks. Despite its success in supervised learning, DD remains underexplored in self-supervised learning (SSL), where distilled data must preserve the information needed to learn representations that remain consistent across augmentations. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mutual Information Prototype Alignment (MIPA), an efficient SSL dataset distillation approach that optimizes synthetic images to preserve augmentation-invariant information. Specifically, MIPA treats synthetic images as learnable prototypes and uses a frozen encoder to assign real samples to them based on feature similarity. A synthetic dataset that preserves such augmentation invariance should yield stable prototype assignments across multiple augmented views of the same real image. MIPA encourages this by maximizing mutual information between cross-view assignments while avoiding trivial solutions in which all real samples are assigned to only a few synthetic prototypes. Evaluations on image classification benchmarks show that synthetic data generated by MIPA yield higher-quality representations than existing distillation approaches while reducing time and storage demands during distillation.

</details>


### [403] [TowerBridge: Bridging the Dual-Encoder Modality Gap via Weight-Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31233)
*Hani Alomari,Md Atabuzzaman,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现CLIP双塔间的"模态间隙"并非检索失败的真正元凶，提出配对统计量定位硬负样本上两塔过度一致的投影方向，经LLM代理在权重空间编程发现共现修正，提炼为闭式配方TowerBridge，在21个CLIP检查点上平均提升COCO检索16.8 RSUM、Flickr30k 6.6，且几乎不损零样本精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP双编码器存在模态间隙，错误匹配（硬负样本）常排在正确匹配之前，学界通常将间隙视为检索失败的主因。但作者观察到事后消除间隙反而在所有测试的CLIP检查点上降低检索性能，说明"间隙"这一诊断是错误的，需要找到真正导致硬负样本占优的机制。

Method: 1) 提出配对统计量，识别两塔在硬负样本上比在匹配对上一致性更高的方向；2) 采用权重空间编程：由统计量引导LLM代理检查两个塔并直接写出闭式权重编辑，无需反向传播；3) 基于硬负样本间隔的一阶分析，将代理反复出现的修正蒸馏、精炼为TowerBridge闭式配方，仅需25,000个带标签样本对、数秒完成计算，无需模型特定调参，不改变架构与推理成本。

Result: 代理在全部13个CLIP检查点上提升COCO测试检索，其中12个选择编辑输出投影。TowerBridge在21个CLIP检查点上平均提升COCO测试检索16.8 RSUM、Flickr30k测试检索6.6（含8个代理未见过的检查点），ImageNet零样本精度下降不超过0.5个百分点；在全部21个检查点上降低hubness，并在其他4/5个族系的双编码器上提升检索。

Conclusion: 检索失败的关键不在模态间隙本身，而在于特定方向上的硬负样本混淆。轻度衰减这些方向可在基本保留间隙的同时提升检索。TowerBridge作为一种轻量、免训练、即插即用的闭式权重修正，具备良好的跨检查点与跨族系泛化性，并可作为LLM代理权重空间编程的有效范例。

Abstract: CLIP-style dual encoders map images and text through two separate towers into a shared embedding space, where the two modalities occupy distinct regions separated by a modality gap. Retrieval fails when wrong matches, or hard negatives, outrank the correct one, and the gap is the usual suspect. Yet removing it post hoc degrades retrieval on every CLIP checkpoint we test. We introduce a paired statistic that identifies directions where the two towers agree more on hard negatives than on matched pairs. Mildly attenuating these directions improves retrieval while largely preserving the gap. We arrive at this correction through weight-space programming: guided by the statistic, an LLM agent inspects both towers and writes closed-form weight edits without backpropagation. Free to edit any component, the agent improves COCO test retrieval on all 13 CLIP checkpoints and edits an output projection in 12 of the 13. We distill and refine its recurring correction into TowerBridge, a closed-form recipe motivated by a first-order analysis of the hard-negative margin. Computed in seconds from 25,000 labelled pairs, it requires no model-specific tuning and leaves the architecture and inference cost unchanged. TowerBridge improves COCO test retrieval by 16.8 RSUM and Flickr30k test retrieval by 6.6 on average across all 21 evaluated CLIP checkpoints, including 8 unseen by the agent, while limiting any drop in ImageNet zero-shot accuracy to 0.5 points. It also reduces hubness on all 21 checkpoints and improves retrieval on 4 of 5 dual encoders from other families.

</details>


### [404] [Let Vision-Language Models Generate Images through Their Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31237)
*JB Kim,Byungwoo Jeon,Kyungmin Lee,Hyeonmo Kang,Cheonbok Park,Shinyoung Joo,Jinwoo Shin,Jeonghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Integral，通过构建统一视觉表示空间，将预训练视觉语言模型改造为同时具备理解与生成能力的统一多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型难以有效整合视觉理解与生成两种能力；直接复用预训练视觉语言模型虽可继承多模态知识，但其知识难以被视觉生成模块有效利用。核心症结在于理解与生成所使用视觉表示的异质性。

Method: Integral 由两个互补组件构成：（1）Abstraction Fusion——聚合冻结视觉编码器的层级特征，构造共享视觉 token；（2）Block-wise MoT——引入轻量生成流，仅在 block 边界处与视觉语言模型交互。二者共同构建跨能力共享的视觉表示空间。

Result: 在保留预训练理解能力的同时获得较强的视觉生成性能。控制实验表明，在重建保真度相当的情况下，采用共享视觉表示相比使用独立视觉表示空间能显著提升生成学习效果。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉语言模型所学表示本身即可为视觉生成提供有效基础，共享表示空间是整合理解与生成的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Recent unified multimodal models aim to combine visual understanding and generation within a single model, yet effectively integrating the two capabilities remains challenging. Leveraging pretrained vision-language models offers a way to build on already learned multimodal knowledge, but making this knowledge effectively accessible to visual generation remains nontrivial. We find that a central challenge lies in the heterogeneous visual representations used for understanding and generation. To address this challenge, we introduce Integral, which converts a pretrained vision-language model into a unified multimodal model by constructing a shared visual representation space across the two capabilities. Integral implements this design through two complementary components. Abstraction Fusion constructs shared visual tokens by aggregating hierarchical representations from the frozen vision encoder, while Block-wise MoT introduces a lightweight generative stream that interacts with the vision-language model only at block boundaries. Our approach effectively preserves the pretrained understanding capability while extending it to strong visual generation. Controlled comparisons further demonstrate that sharing visual representations between understanding and generation substantially improves generative learning compared with using a separate visual representation space, despite comparable reconstruction fidelity. These results suggest that representations learned by pretrained vision-language models already provide an effective foundation for visual generation.

</details>


### [405] [Modern Self-Supervised Vision Transformers Are Pareidolic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31238)
*Zihang Lin,Huaiyuan Qin,Liangli Zhen,Qing Guo,Jun Liu,Yew-Soon Ong,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现自监督ViT（DINOv2/v3）的特征在白噪声上存在空间相干的"空想性"方向子空间，该子空间占据自然图像特征方差的主导，阻碍了物体的自涌现；提出了免训练的Pareidolia Subspace Calibration（PSC）方法进行校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINOv2/v3虽具有更强的稠密特征，但在"物体自涌现"（object emergence）能力上远落后于DINO。作者试图探究这一反差的成因，并寻求无需训练即可修复的方法。

Method: 提出"pareidolic subspace"概念：仅在白噪声输入上测量特征中形成空间相干模式的方向构成子空间；提出PSC，在噪声输入上度量该子空间，再将自然输入特征中该子空间方向缩放至其余方向的方差水平。并通过逐块追踪与受控训练实验定位成因。

Result: PSC在多种物体涌现任务上提升DINOv2/v3：物体发现CorLoc最高+31.7，显著性检测maxF最高+30.2，视频物体分割𝒥最高+59.9；缩小该子空间可稳定恢复物体发现能力，说明能力是被"抑制"而非"丢失"。成因方面，该子空间在所有自监督模型的中间块形成，但仅在使用patch级损失的模型中保持至最后块，受控实验证实仅用image级损失可使其消退。

Conclusion: DINOv2/v3的物体涌现能力被过大的pareidolic子空间所抑制而非丧失；该现象源于patch级损失训练，且可通过免训练的PSC有效校准恢复，为自监督ViT的稠密特征诊断与改进提供了新视角。

Abstract: In human perception, pareidolia is the tendency to perceive a meaningful pattern, such as a face, in a random or ambiguous stimulus.We find a similar phenomenon in modern self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs): on white noise, which contains neither objects nor spatial structure, a few directions of their features still form spatially coherent patterns across the patches, imposed by the features themselves.We call this behavior pareidolia, and the span of these directions the pareidolic subspace.On natural images, it holds most of the feature variance in DINOv2 and DINOv3 but little in DINO.This excess explains why DINOv2 and DINOv3, despite stronger dense features, fall far behind DINO in object emergence, the hallmark ability of self-supervised ViTs to identify the patches of an object on their own.Our experiments further show that scaling down their pareidolic subspaces steadily restores their ability to discover objects, so their object emergence is hindered rather than lost.These findings naturally lead to a training-free calibration: Pareidolia Subspace Calibration(PSC) measures the pareidolic subspace only on a noise input and then calibrates the features of natural inputs by scaling its directions down to the variance level of the remaining directions.Across diverse object emergence tasks, PSC improves DINOv2 and DINOv3 by up to 31.7 CorLoc on object discovery, 30.2 maxF on saliency detection and 59.9~$\mathcal{J}$ on video object segmentation.To identify the cause of pareidolia, we trace it block by block and find that it forms in the middle blocks of every self-supervised model we examine but stays strong to the last block only in models trained with a patch-level loss; controlled training confirms that an image-level loss alone lets it fade.

</details>


### [406] [Lyris: Layout-Guided Long Video Generation with Persistent 3D Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31239)
*Chen Wang,Jiahui Huang,Riccardo de Lutio,Alex Trevithick,Bryan Chu,Jiawei Ren,Laura Leal-Taixé,Shengyu Huang,Lingjie Liu,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Lyris 长视频生成框架：在已观测/已生成区域使用持久点云记忆，在未观测区域由视觉语言模型规划 3D layout，从而实现单张照片出发、可返回、相机受控的长距离场景漫游。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭单张照片生成远超拍摄范围的长距离相机漫游视频，需在"返回"时保持一致。既有方法存在两类缺陷：基于记忆的方法能保持已见内容，但在已见范围之外无锚定，导致相机偏离路径，文本提示只能命名物体而无法指定其位置；layout 条件方法虽提供锚定与精确控制，但依赖人工编写的 layout。根本原因在于：超出已观测范围后，缺少锚定物，除非由人手工编写。

Method: Lyris 使视频模型始终拥有锚定：在已观测或已生成的区域维护持久点云记忆；在未观测区域由视觉语言模型（VLM）自动创作 3D layout。核心洞见是两模型间接口可极其粗糙——每个物体一个带标签的 3D box 即足够 VLM 生成（也便于用户编辑），又足以在沿相机路径渲染后让视频模型遵循。

Result: 可从单张照片生成超过 3 分钟、50 米的漫游，穿越门洞进入原图从未展示的房间。在四个数据集上进行一分钟往返评测，大幅超越 SOTA：相机误差降低 3.8 倍，返回一致性平均提升 22%。

Conclusion: 将"持久点云记忆"与"VLM 自动生成的粗略 3D box layout"结合，可为视频模型在已见与未见区域提供持续锚定，实现长距离、相机可控且返回一致的单图场景漫游，并保持 layout 的可编辑性。

Abstract: Exploring a scene from as little as a single photo requires generating a long, camera-controlled walkthrough that extends far beyond what was captured, yet remains consistent upon return. Memory-based video generation methods preserve what they have already seen, but have no anchor beyond it, where the camera drifts from its path and a text prompt can name objects but not place them. Layout-conditioned methods provide such an anchor and precise control over what appears where, but rely on layouts that are typically authored by hand. Both limitations arise because, beyond what was captured, nothing anchors the video model unless a human authors one. To overcome both, we propose Lyris, a long video generation framework that never leaves the video model without an anchor: a persistent point-cloud memory where the scene has been observed or generated, and a 3D layout that a vision-language model authors where it has not. Our key insight is that the interface between the two models can be surprisingly coarse. A layout of one labeled 3D box per object is simple enough for a vision-language model to author (and for a user to edit), yet expressive enough for a video model to follow once rendered along the camera path. We demonstrate walkthroughs of over three minutes and 50 meters from a single photo, passing through doorways into rooms it never shows. When evaluated on minute-long round trips across four datasets, Lyris outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a wide margin, reducing camera error by 3.8x and improving consistency upon return by 22% on average.

</details>


### [407] [Pruning Autonomous Driving VLAs: What to Preserve for Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31240)
*Seongchan Kim,Soowon Son,Sangoh Kim,Hyunah Ko,Eunbeen Hong,Hyunkoo Lee,Sanghyun Lee,WonJun Moon,Kihong Kim,Dongyeon Son,Woongchan Nam,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoOP结构化剪枝方法，融合推理与轨迹两类目标的重要性排序剪枝VLM，并按去噪步聚合重要性固定选择专家MLP通道；在24%–39.4%参数削减下保持驾驶性能与推理能力，获得1.37倍加速与36.6%峰值显存下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带CoT推理的VLA自动驾驶模型内存与计算开销大；剪枝需同时保住场景理解（推理）与轨迹预测两类能力，而二者对同一VLM结构的重要性分配存在冲突。

Method: 基于Alpamayo-1.5-10B做Taylor重要性分析：发现推理与轨迹目标对VLM结构重要性不同；动作专家中MLP通道跨去噪步排序比query head更稳定，且少数通道贡献大部分重要性。据此提出MoOP：VLM剪枝时融合两目标的重要性排名，专家侧聚合逐步重要性选择固定MLP通道子集。

Result: 参数削减24.0%时，MoOP仅剪VLM在驾驶指标上持平或略优于最强基线，保留94.0%稠密模型LingoQA分数；削减30.0%时退化最小且推理不崩溃；去除93.75%专家MLP通道后总削减39.4%，开环误差几乎不变、闭环具竞争力；端到端推理加速1.37倍、峰值显存降低36.6%，无需剪枝后微调。

Conclusion: 多目标重要性融合与跨步聚合的结构化剪枝可在不微调条件下显著压缩推理型VLA模型，同时避免推理能力崩塌，验证了结构重要性差异分析对剪枝策略设计的指导价值。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving use chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to guide action generation, but incur substantial memory and computational costs. We study structured pruning for these models, focusing on preserving both scene understanding and trajectory prediction. Our analysis of Taylor importance in Alpamayo-1.5-10B shows that reasoning and trajectory objectives assign different importance to the same vision-language model (VLM) structures. In the action expert, MLP channels exhibit more stable importance rankings across denoising steps than query heads, and a small fraction of channels accounts for most of the measured importance. Based on these findings, we propose MoOP, which combines importance ranks from both objectives for VLM pruning and aggregates stepwise importance to select a fixed subset of expert MLP channels. At 24.0% total parameter reduction, VLM-only pruning with MoOP performs comparably to or slightly better than the strongest baselines in driving, while retaining 94.0% of the dense model's LingoQA score, far above all other methods. At a higher reduction of 30.0%, MoOP degrades much less than every other method, as it is the only one whose reasoning does not collapse. Removing 93.75% of expert MLP channels increases total parameter reduction to 39.4%, with nearly unchanged open-loop errors relative to VLM-only pruning and competitive closed-loop performance. The resulting model achieves a 1.37x end-to-end inference speedup and 36.6% lower peak memory than the dense model, without post-pruning fine-tuning.

</details>
